# 中华人民共和国民法典

**分类**：法律  
**法律部门**：民法典  
**公布日期**：2020-05-28  
**生效日期**：2021-01-01

> （2020年5月28日第十三届全国人民代表大会第三次会议通过） 目　　录 第一编　总　　则 第一章　基本规定 第二章　自然人 第一节　民事权利能力和民事行为能力 第二节　监　　护 第三节　宣告失踪和宣告死亡 第四节　个体工商户和农村承包经营户 第三章　法　　人 第一节　一般规定 第二节　营利法人 第三节　非营利法人 第四节　特别法人 第四章　非法人组织

---

## 第一编　总则

### 第一章　基本规定

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条　为了保护民事主体的合法权益，调整民事关系，维护社会和经济秩序，适应中国特色社会主义发展要求，弘扬社会主义核心价值观，根据宪法，制定本法。

**Article 1** This Law is enacted in accordance with the Constitution of the People's Republic of China for the purposes of protecting the lawful rights and interests of civil subjects, regulating civil relations, maintaining social and economic order, meeting the requirements of the development of socialism with Chinese characteristics, and promoting the core socialist values.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条　民法调整平等主体的自然人、法人和非法人组织之间的人身关系和财产关系。

**Article 2** The civil law regulates personal and property relations among natural persons, legal persons, and unincorporated organizations that are equal in status.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条　民事主体的人身权利、财产权利以及其他合法权益受法律保护，任何组织或者个人不得侵犯。

**Article 3** The personal rights, property rights, and other lawful rights and interests of civil subjects are protected by law, and no organization or individual shall infringe upon them.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条　民事主体在民事活动中的法律地位一律平等。

**Article 4** All civil subjects have equal legal status in civil activities.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条　民事主体从事民事活动，应当遵循自愿原则，按照自己的意思设立、变更、终止民事法律关系。

**Article 5** In conducting civil activities, civil subjects shall follow the principle of voluntariness, and establish, modify, or terminate civil legal relations based on their true intentions.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条　民事主体从事民事活动，应当遵循公平原则，合理确定各方的权利和义务。

**Article 6** In conducting civil activities, civil subjects shall follow the principle of fairness, and reasonably determine the rights and obligations of each party.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条　民事主体从事民事活动，应当遵循诚信原则，秉持诚实，恪守承诺。

**Article 7** In conducting civil activities, civil subjects shall follow the principle of good faith, act honestly, and abide by their commitments.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条　民事主体从事民事活动，不得违反法律，不得违背公序良俗。

**Article 8** In conducting civil activities, civil subjects shall not violate the law or contravene public order and good customs.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条　民事主体从事民事活动，应当有利于节约资源、保护生态环境。

**Article 9** In conducting civil activities, civil subjects shall act in a manner that conserves resources and protects the ecological environment.

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条　处理民事纠纷，应当依照法律；法律没有规定的，可以适用习惯，但是不得违背公序良俗。

**Article 10** Civil disputes shall be resolved in accordance with the law. Where the law does not provide for a matter, custom may be applied, except that the custom shall not contravene public order and good customs.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条　其他法律对民事关系有特别规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 11** Where other laws make special provisions on civil relations, those provisions shall be followed.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条　中华人民共和国领域内的民事活动，适用中华人民共和国法律。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 12** The laws of the People's Republic of China shall apply to civil activities within the territory of the People's Republic of China. Where the law provides otherwise, those provisions shall be followed.

### 第二章　自然人

#### 第一节　民事权利能力和民事行为能力

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条　自然人从出生时起到死亡时止，具有民事权利能力，依法享有民事权利，承担民事义务。

**Article 13** A natural person has civil capacity for rights from the time of birth until death, and may enjoy civil rights and assume civil obligations in accordance with law.

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条　自然人的民事权利能力一律平等。

**Article 14** All natural persons have equal civil capacity for rights.

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条　自然人的出生时间和死亡时间，以出生证明、死亡证明记载的时间为准；没有出生证明、死亡证明的，以户籍登记或者其他有效身份登记记载的时间为准。有其他证据足以推翻以上记载时间的，以该证据证明的时间为准。

**Article 15** The time of birth and the time of death of a natural person shall be determined based on the time recorded on the birth certificate or the death certificate, respectively. In the absence of a birth certificate or a death certificate, the time recorded on the household registration or other valid identification registration shall prevail. Where there is other evidence sufficient to overturn the time recorded as above, the time certified by such evidence shall prevail.

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条　涉及遗产继承、接受赠与等胎儿利益保护的，胎儿视为具有民事权利能力。但是，胎儿娩出时为死体的，其民事权利能力自始不存在。

**Article 16** In matters involving the protection of the interests of a fetus, such as inheritance of an estate or acceptance of a gift, the fetus shall be deemed to have civil capacity for rights. However, if the fetus is stillborn at delivery, its civil capacity for rights shall be deemed never to have existed.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条　十八周岁以上的自然人为成年人。不满十八周岁的自然人为未成年人。

**Article 17** A natural person aged 18 or over is an adult. A natural person under the age of 18 is a minor.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条　成年人为完全民事行为能力人，可以独立实施民事法律行为。  
十六周岁以上的未成年人，以自己的劳动收入为主要生活来源的，视为完全民事行为能力人。

**Article 18** An adult is a person with full civil capacity for conduct, and may independently perform civil juristic acts.  
A minor aged 16 or over whose primary source of income is his or her own labor shall be deemed a person with full civil capacity for conduct.

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条　八周岁以上的未成年人为限制民事行为能力人，实施民事法律行为由其法定代理人代理或者经其法定代理人同意、追认；但是，可以独立实施纯获利益的民事法律行为或者与其年龄、智力相适应的民事法律行为。

**Article 19** A minor aged 8 or over is a person with limited civil capacity for conduct, and shall have his or her civil juristic acts performed by his or her legal representative or with the consent or ratification of his or her legal representative. However, such a minor may independently perform a civil juristic act that is purely for his or her benefit or that is appropriate to his or her age and intelligence.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条　不满八周岁的未成年人为无民事行为能力人，由其法定代理人代理实施民事法律行为。

**Article 20** A minor under the age of 8 is a person with no civil capacity for conduct, and shall have his or her civil juristic acts performed by his or her legal representative.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条　不能辨认自己行为的成年人为无民事行为能力人，由其法定代理人代理实施民事法律行为。  
八周岁以上的未成年人不能辨认自己行为的，适用前款规定。

**Article 21** An adult who is unable to recognize his or her own conduct shall be a person having no capacity for civil conduct, and his or her legal representative shall act on his or her behalf to perform civil juristic acts.  
Where a minor aged eight or above is unable to recognize his or her own conduct, the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply.

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条　不能完全辨认自己行为的成年人为限制民事行为能力人，实施民事法律行为由其法定代理人代理或者经其法定代理人同意、追认；但是，可以独立实施纯获利益的民事法律行为或者与其智力、精神健康状况相适应的民事法律行为。

**Article 22** An adult who is unable to fully recognize his or her own conduct shall be a person with limited capacity for civil conduct, and may perform civil juristic acts through his or her legal representative or with the consent or ratification of his or her legal representative; provided that he or she may independently perform civil juristic acts that are purely for his or her benefit or that are appropriate to his or her intellectual or mental health condition.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人的监护人是其法定代理人。

**Article 23** The guardian of a person having no capacity for civil conduct or a person with limited capacity for civil conduct shall be his or her legal representative.

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条　不能辨认或者不能完全辨认自己行为的成年人，其利害关系人或者有关组织，可以向人民法院申请认定该成年人为无民事行为能力人或者限制民事行为能力人。  
被人民法院认定为无民事行为能力人或者限制民事行为能力人的，经本人、利害关系人或者有关组织申请，人民法院可以根据其智力、精神健康恢复的状况，认定该成年人恢复为限制民事行为能力人或者完全民事行为能力人。  
本条规定的有关组织包括：居民委员会、村民委员会、学校、医疗机构、妇女联合会、残疾人联合会、依法设立的老年人组织、民政部门等。

**Article 24** For an adult who is unable to recognize or is unable to fully recognize his or her own conduct, an interested person or a relevant organization may apply to the People's Court for a determination that the adult is a person having no capacity for civil conduct or a person with limited capacity for civil conduct.  
Where a person has been determined by the People's Court as a person having no capacity for civil conduct or a person with limited capacity for civil conduct, upon the application of the person himself or herself, an interested person or a relevant organization, the People's Court may, based on the recovery of his or her intellectual or mental health condition, determine that such adult has been restored to the status of a person with limited capacity for civil conduct or a person with full capacity for civil conduct.  
The relevant organizations referred to in this Article shall include: residents committees, villagers committees, schools, medical institutions, the Women's Federation, the Disabled Persons' Federation, organizations for the elderly lawfully established, the civil affairs department, and the like.

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条　自然人以户籍登记或者其他有效身份登记记载的居所为住所；经常居所与住所不一致的，经常居所视为住所。

**Article 25** A natural person shall take the domicile recorded in the household registration or other valid identity registration as his or her residence; where the habitual residence is inconsistent with the residence, the habitual residence shall be deemed as the residence.

#### 第二节　监护

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条　父母对未成年子女负有抚养、教育和保护的义务。  
成年子女对父母负有赡养、扶助和保护的义务。

**Article 26** Parents shall have the obligation to raise, educate and protect their minor children.  
Adult children shall have the obligation to support, assist and protect their parents.

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条　父母是未成年子女的监护人。  
未成年人的父母已经死亡或者没有监护能力的，由下列有监护能力的人按顺序担任监护人：  
（一）祖父母、外祖父母；  
（二）兄、姐；  
（三）其他愿意担任监护人的个人或者组织，但是须经未成年人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门同意。&thinsp;<sup>[1]</sup>

**Article 27** The parents of a minor child shall be the guardians of the minor child. Where the parents of a minor are deceased or have no ability to be guardians, the following persons who have the ability to be guardians shall act as guardians in the following order:  
(1) paternal grandparents and maternal grandparents;  
(2) elder brothers and sisters; and (3) other individuals or organizations that are willing to act as guardians, provided that the consent of the residents committee, villagers committee or the civil affairs department of the place of the minor's residence is obtained.

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条　无民事行为能力或者限制民事行为能力的成年人，由下列有监护能力的人按顺序担任监护人：  
（一）配偶；  
（二）父母、子女；  
（三）其他近亲属；  
（四）其他愿意担任监护人的个人或者组织，但是须经被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门同意。

**Article 28** For an adult with no capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct, the following persons who have the ability to be guardians shall act as guardians in the following order:  
(1) spouse;  
(2) parents and children;  
(3) other close relatives; and (4) other individuals or organizations that are willing to act as guardians, provided that the consent of the residents committee, villagers committee or the civil affairs department of the place of the ward's residence is obtained.

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条　被监护人的父母担任监护人的，可以通过遗嘱指定监护人。

**Article 29** Where the parents of a ward act as guardians, they may appoint a guardian by will.

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条　依法具有监护资格的人之间可以协议确定监护人。协议确定监护人应当尊重被监护人的真实意愿。

**Article 30** Persons who are legally qualified to be guardians may reach an agreement to determine a guardian. The determination of a guardian by agreement shall respect the true will of the ward.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条　对监护人的确定有争议的，由被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门指定监护人，有关当事人对指定不服的，可以向人民法院申请指定监护人；有关当事人也可以直接向人民法院申请指定监护人。  
居民委员会、村民委员会、民政部门或者人民法院应当尊重被监护人的真实意愿，按照最有利于被监护人的原则在依法具有监护资格的人中指定监护人。  
依据本条第一款规定指定监护人前，被监护人的人身权利、财产权利以及其他合法权益处于无人保护状态的，由被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会、法律规定的有关组织或者民政部门担任临时监护人。  
监护人被指定后，不得擅自变更；擅自变更的，不免除被指定的监护人的责任。&thinsp;<sup>[1]</sup>

**Article 31** Where there is a dispute over the determination of a guardian, the residents committee, villagers committee or the civil affairs department of the place of the ward's residence shall designate a guardian. Where any relevant party disagrees with the designation, it may apply to the People's Court for the designation of a guardian; the relevant party may also directly apply to the People's Court for the designation of a guardian. The residents committee, villagers committee, civil affairs department or the People's Court shall respect the true will of the ward, and designate a guardian from among those who are legally qualified to be guardians in accordance with the principle of the best interests of the ward. Where, before the designation of a guardian in accordance with the provisions of paragraph 1 of this Article, the personal rights, property rights and other lawful rights and interests of a ward are left unprotected, the residents committee, villagers committee, the relevant organization prescribed by law or the civil affairs department of the place of the ward's residence shall act as the provisional guardian. Once a guardian is designated, it shall not be changed without authorization; any unauthorized change shall not relieve the designated guardian of its liability.

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条　没有依法具有监护资格的人的，监护人由民政部门担任，也可以由具备履行监护职责条件的被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会担任。&thinsp;<sup>[1]</sup>

**Article 32** Where there is no person legally qualified to be a guardian, the guardianship shall be assumed by the civil affairs department, or may be assumed by the residents committee or villagers committee of the place of the ward's residence that is qualified to perform guardianship duties.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条　具有完全民事行为能力的成年人，可以与其近亲属、其他愿意担任监护人的个人或者组织事先协商，以书面形式确定自己的监护人，在自己丧失或者部分丧失民事行为能力时，由该监护人履行监护职责。

**Article 33** An adult with full capacity for civil conduct may, through prior consultation with his or her close relatives, or other individuals or organizations willing to act as guardians, determine his or her own guardian in writing, and when he or she loses or partially loses capacity for civil conduct, such guardian shall perform guardianship duties.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条　监护人的职责是代理被监护人实施民事法律行为，保护被监护人的人身权利、财产权利以及其他合法权益等。  
监护人依法履行监护职责产生的权利，受法律保护。  
监护人不履行监护职责或者侵害被监护人合法权益的，应当承担法律责任。  
因发生突发事件等紧急情况，监护人暂时无法履行监护职责，被监护人的生活处于无人照料状态的，被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门应当为被监护人安排必要的临时生活照料措施。

**Article 34** The duties of a guardian shall be to act on behalf of the ward to perform civil juristic acts, and to protect the personal rights, property rights and other lawful rights and interests of the ward.  
Rights arising from the guardian's lawful performance of guardianship duties shall be protected by law.  
Where a guardian fails to perform guardianship duties or infringes upon the lawful rights and interests of the ward, the guardian shall bear legal liability.  
Where, due to an emergency or the like, the guardian is temporarily unable to perform guardianship duties, leaving the ward's life unattended, the residents committee, villagers committee or the civil affairs department of the place of the ward's residence shall arrange necessary provisional living care measures for the ward.

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条　监护人应当按照最有利于被监护人的原则履行监护职责。监护人除为维护被监护人利益外，不得处分被监护人的财产。  
未成年人的监护人履行监护职责，在作出与被监护人利益有关的决定时，应当根据被监护人的年龄和智力状况，尊重被监护人的真实意愿。  
成年人的监护人履行监护职责，应当最大程度地尊重被监护人的真实意愿，保障并协助被监护人实施与其智力、精神健康状况相适应的民事法律行为。对被监护人有能力独立处理的事务，监护人不得干涉。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-15" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第15条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 35** A guardian shall perform guardianship duties in accordance with the principle of the best interests of the ward. A guardian shall not dispose of the ward's property except for safeguarding the interests of the ward. When performing guardianship duties and making decisions concerning the interests of a minor ward, the guardian shall, based on the age and intellectual status of the ward, respect the true will of the ward. When performing guardianship duties, the guardian of an adult ward shall, to the maximum extent, respect the true will of the ward, ensure and assist the ward in performing civil juristic acts that are appropriate to the ward's intellectual or mental health condition. The guardian shall not interfere with matters that the ward is capable of handling independently.

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条　监护人有下列情形之一的，人民法院根据有关个人或者组织的申请，撤销其监护人资格，安排必要的临时监护措施，并按照最有利于被监护人的原则依法指定监护人：  
（一）实施严重损害被监护人身心健康的行为；  
（二）怠于履行监护职责，或者无法履行监护职责且拒绝将监护职责部分或者全部委托给他人，导致被监护人处于危困状态；  
（三）实施严重侵害被监护人合法权益的其他行为。  
本条规定的有关个人、组织包括：其他依法具有监护资格的人，居民委员会、村民委员会、学校、医疗机构、妇女联合会、残疾人联合会、未成年人保护组织、依法设立的老年人组织、民政部门等。  
前款规定的个人和民政部门以外的组织未及时向人民法院申请撤销监护人资格的，民政部门应当向人民法院申请。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-62" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第62条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 36** Where a guardian falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall, upon application by the relevant individual or organization, disqualify the guardian, arrange necessary provisional guardianship measures, and designate a guardian according to law in accordance with the principle of the best interests of the ward:  
(1) committing acts that seriously harm the physical or mental health of the ward;  
(2) being negligent in performing guardianship duties, or being unable to perform guardianship duties and refusing to delegate part or all of the guardianship duties to another person, thereby leaving the ward in a dangerous or distressed condition  
(3) committing other acts that seriously infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of the ward. The relevant individuals and organizations referred to in this Article shall include: other persons legally qualified to be guardians, residents committees, villagers committees, schools, medical institutions, the Women's Federation, the Disabled Persons' Federation, organizations for the protection of minors, organizations for the elderly lawfully established, the civil affairs department, and the like. Where the individuals and organizations other than the civil affairs department specified in the preceding paragraph fail to apply to the People's Court in a timely manner for the disqualification of a guardian, the civil affairs department shall apply to the People's Court.

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条　依法负担被监护人抚养费、赡养费、扶养费的父母、子女、配偶等，被人民法院撤销监护人资格后，应当继续履行负担的义务。

**Article 37** Parents, children, spouses, and the like who are legally obligated to pay child support, alimony, or support for the ward shall continue to perform such obligations after being disqualified as a guardian by the People's Court.

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条　被监护人的父母或者子女被人民法院撤销监护人资格后，除对被监护人实施故意犯罪的外，确有悔改表现的，经其申请，人民法院可以在尊重被监护人真实意愿的前提下，视情况恢复其监护人资格，人民法院指定的监护人与被监护人的监护关系同时终止。

**Article 38** Where the parent or child of a ward, after being disqualified as a guardian by the People's Court, shows genuine repentance, except in cases where he or she has committed an intentional crime against the ward, upon his or her application, the People's Court may, on the premise of respecting the true will of the ward, restore his or her qualification as a guardian as the circumstances may require, and the guardianship relationship between the guardian designated by the People's Court and the ward shall be terminated simultaneously.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条　有下列情形之一的，监护关系终止：  
（一）被监护人取得或者恢复完全民事行为能力；  
（二）监护人丧失监护能力；  
（三）被监护人或者监护人死亡；  
（四）人民法院认定监护关系终止的其他情形。  
监护关系终止后，被监护人仍然需要监护的，应当依法另行确定监护人。

**Article 39** The guardianship relationship shall terminate under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the ward acquires or regains full capacity for civil conduct;  
(2) the guardian loses the ability to be a guardian;  
(3) the ward or the guardian dies  
(4) other circumstances under which the People's Court determines that the guardianship relationship terminates. Where the ward still needs guardianship after the termination of the guardianship relationship, another guardian shall be determined according to law.

#### 第三节　宣告失踪和宣告死亡

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条　自然人下落不明满二年的，利害关系人可以向人民法院申请宣告该自然人为失踪人。

**Article 40** Where a natural person has been missing for two years, an interested person may apply to the People's Court for a declaration that the natural person is a missing person.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条　自然人下落不明的时间自其失去音讯之日起计算。战争期间下落不明的，下落不明的时间自战争结束之日或者有关机关确定的下落不明之日起计算。

**Article 41** The time during which a natural person is missing shall be calculated from the date when he or she loses contact. For a person who goes missing in a war, the missing period shall commence on the date the war ends or on the date determined by relevant authorities as the start of the missing status.

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条　失踪人的财产由其配偶、成年子女、父母或者其他愿意担任财产代管人的人代管。  
代管有争议，没有前款规定的人，或者前款规定的人无代管能力的，由人民法院指定的人代管。&thinsp;<sup>[1]</sup>

**Article 42** The property of a missing person shall be administered by his or her spouse, adult children, parents, or any other person willing to act as the property custodian.  
If a dispute arises over the administration, no person as specified in the preceding paragraph exists, or the person specified therein lacks the capacity to administer, the custodian shall be designated by the People's Court.

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条　财产代管人应当妥善管理失踪人的财产，维护其财产权益。  
失踪人所欠税款、债务和应付的其他费用，由财产代管人从失踪人的财产中支付。  
财产代管人因故意或者重大过失造成失踪人财产损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 43** A property custodian shall properly manage the missing person's property and protect his or her property rights and interests.  
Taxes, debts, and other expenses payable by the missing person shall be paid by the property custodian from the missing person's property.  
If a property custodian causes damage to the missing person's property intentionally or through gross negligence, he or she shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条　财产代管人不履行代管职责、侵害失踪人财产权益或者丧失代管能力的，失踪人的利害关系人可以向人民法院申请变更财产代管人。  
财产代管人有正当理由的，可以向人民法院申请变更财产代管人。  
人民法院变更财产代管人的，变更后的财产代管人有权请求原财产代管人及时移交有关财产并报告财产代管情况。

**Article 44** If a property custodian fails to perform administrative duties, infringes upon the missing person's property rights and interests, or loses the capacity to administer, any interested party of the missing person may apply to the People's Court for replacing the property custodian.  
A property custodian having a legitimate reason may apply to the People's Court for replacement.  
If the People's Court replaces a property custodian, the new custodian shall have the right to request the former custodian to promptly transfer the relevant property and report on the status of the property administration.

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条　失踪人重新出现，经本人或者利害关系人申请，人民法院应当撤销失踪宣告。  
失踪人重新出现，有权请求财产代管人及时移交有关财产并报告财产代管情况。

**Article 45** If a missing person reappears, the People's Court shall revoke the declaration of missing person upon application by the person or any interested party.  
Upon reappearance, the missing person shall have the right to request the property custodian to promptly transfer the relevant property and report on the status of the property administration.

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条　自然人有下列情形之一的，利害关系人可以向人民法院申请宣告该自然人死亡：  
（一）下落不明满四年；  
（二）因意外事件，下落不明满二年。  
因意外事件下落不明，经有关机关证明该自然人不可能生存的，申请宣告死亡不受二年时间的限制。

**Article 46** Any interested party may apply to the People's Court for declaring the death of a natural person under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the person has been missing for four years  
(2) the person has been missing for two years due to an accident. If a natural person goes missing due to an accident and relevant authorities have proven that survival is impossible, the application for declaring death shall not be subject to the two-year restriction.

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条　对同一自然人，有的利害关系人申请宣告死亡，有的利害关系人申请宣告失踪，符合本法规定的宣告死亡条件的，人民法院应当宣告死亡。

**Article 47** If, with respect to the same natural person, some interested parties apply for declaring death while others apply for declaring missing, and the conditions for declaring death as provided for in this Law are met, the People's Court shall declare death.

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条　被宣告死亡的人，人民法院宣告死亡的判决作出之日视为其死亡的日期；因意外事件下落不明宣告死亡的，意外事件发生之日视为其死亡的日期。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 48** For a person declared dead, the date of the People's Court's adjudication declaring death shall be deemed as the date of his or her death. If the declaration is due to missing following an accident, the date of the accident shall be deemed as the date of death.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条　自然人被宣告死亡但是并未死亡的，不影响该自然人在被宣告死亡期间实施的民事法律行为的效力。

**Article 49** If a natural person is declared dead but is not actually deceased, the validity of civil juristic acts performed by that natural person during the period of being declared dead shall not be affected.

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条　被宣告死亡的人重新出现，经本人或者利害关系人申请，人民法院应当撤销死亡宣告。

**Article 50** If a person declared dead reappears, the People's Court shall revoke the declaration of death upon application by the person or any interested party.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条　被宣告死亡的人的婚姻关系，自死亡宣告之日起消除。死亡宣告被撤销的，婚姻关系自撤销死亡宣告之日起自行恢复。但是，其配偶再婚或者向婚姻登记机关书面声明不愿意恢复的除外。

**Article 51** The marital relationship of a person declared dead shall be dissolved from the date of the declaration of death. If the declaration of death is revoked, the marital relationship shall be automatically restored from the date of revocation, except where the spouse has remarried or has made a written declaration to the marriage registration authority indicating unwillingness to restore the relationship.

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条　被宣告死亡的人在被宣告死亡期间，其子女被他人依法收养的，在死亡宣告被撤销后，不得以未经本人同意为由主张收养行为无效。

**Article 52** If a child of a person declared dead was lawfully adopted by another person during the period of the declaration of death, after the revocation of the declaration of death, the person shall not assert the invalidity of the adoption on the ground that his or her consent was not obtained.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条　被撤销死亡宣告的人有权请求依照本法第六编取得其财产的民事主体返还财产；无法返还的，应当给予适当补偿。  
利害关系人隐瞒真实情况，致使他人被宣告死亡而取得其财产的，除应当返还财产外，还应当对由此造成的损失承担赔偿责任。

**Article 53** A person whose declaration of death is revoked shall have the right to request any civil subject who acquired his or her property in accordance with Title VI of this Law to return the property; if return is impossible, appropriate compensation shall be made.  
If an interested party, by concealing the truth, causes another person to be declared dead and thereby acquires that person's property, the interested party shall, in addition to returning the property, bear compensation liability for the losses caused thereby.

#### 第四节　个体工商户和农村承包经营户

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条　自然人从事工商业经营，经依法登记，为个体工商户。个体工商户可以起字号。

**Article 54** A natural person who engages in industrial or commercial operations and is registered according to law shall be deemed as an individual industrial or commercial household. An individual industrial or commercial household may adopt a trade name.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条　农村集体经济组织的成员，依法取得农村土地承包经营权，从事家庭承包经营的，为农村承包经营户。

**Article 55** Members of a rural collective economic organization who obtain the right to contract and manage rural land according to law and engage in household contract operations shall be deemed as a rural contractual household.

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条　个体工商户的债务，个人经营的，以个人财产承担；家庭经营的，以家庭财产承担；无法区分的，以家庭财产承担。  
农村承包经营户的债务，以从事农村土地承包经营的农户财产承担；事实上由农户部分成员经营的，以该部分成员的财产承担。

**Article 56** The debts of an individual industrial or commercial household shall be borne with personal property if operated individually, or with household property if operated by the family; if the nature of the operation cannot be distinguished, the debts shall be borne with household property.  
The debts of a rural contractual household shall be borne with the property of the household engaging in rural land contract operations; if the business is actually operated by some members of the household, the debts shall be borne with the property of such members.

### 第三章　法人

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条　法人是具有民事权利能力和民事行为能力，依法独立享有民事权利和承担民事义务的组织。

**Article 57** A legal person is an organization that has the capacity for civil rights and the capacity for civil conduct, and independently enjoys civil rights and assumes civil obligations according to law.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条　法人应当依法成立。  
法人应当有自己的名称、组织机构、住所、财产或者经费。法人成立的具体条件和程序，依照法律、行政法规的规定。  
设立法人，法律、行政法规规定须经有关机关批准的，依照其规定。

**Article 58** A legal person shall be established according to law. A legal person shall have its own name, organizational structure, domicile, and property or funds. The specific conditions and procedures for the establishment of a legal person shall be in accordance with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations. If a legal person is established subject to approval by relevant authorities as provided for by laws or administrative regulations, such provisions shall be followed.

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条　法人的民事权利能力和民事行为能力，从法人成立时产生，到法人终止时消灭。

**Article 59** The capacity for civil rights and the capacity for civil conduct of a legal person shall commence when the legal person is established and shall cease when the legal person is terminated.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条　法人以其全部财产独立承担民事责任。

**Article 60** A legal person shall bear civil liability independently with all of its property.

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条　依照法律或者法人章程的规定，代表法人从事民事活动的负责人，为法人的法定代表人。  
法定代表人以法人名义从事的民事活动，其法律后果由法人承受。  
法人章程或者法人权力机构对法定代表人代表权的限制，不得对抗善意相对人。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 61** In accordance with the provisions of law or the articles of association of a legal person, the person in charge who represents the legal person in engaging in civil activities shall be the legal representative of the legal person.  
The legal consequences of civil activities conducted by the legal representative in the name of the legal person shall be borne by the legal person.  
Any restriction imposed by the articles of association of the legal person or its governing body on the representative authority of the legal representative shall not be asserted against a bona fide counterparty.

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条　法定代表人因执行职务造成他人损害的，由法人承担民事责任。  
法人承担民事责任后，依照法律或者法人章程的规定，可以向有过错的法定代表人追偿。

**Article 62** Where the legal representative causes harm to another person in the performance of his or her duties, the legal person shall bear civil liability.  
After the legal person bears civil liability, it may, in accordance with the provisions of law or its articles of association, seek recourse against the legal representative who is at fault.

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条　法人以其主要办事机构所在地为住所。依法需要办理法人登记的，应当将主要办事机构所在地登记为住所。

**Article 63** A legal person shall take the place where its principal office is located as its domicile. Where registration of the legal person is required by law, the place where its principal office is located shall be registered as its domicile.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条　法人存续期间登记事项发生变化的，应当依法向登记机关申请变更登记。

**Article 64** Where registered items of a legal person change during its existence, the legal person shall apply to the registration authority for modification of registration in accordance with law.

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条　法人的实际情况与登记的事项不一致的，不得对抗善意相对人。

**Article 65** Where the actual circumstances of a legal person are inconsistent with the registered items, such inconsistency shall not be asserted against a bona fide counterparty.

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条　登记机关应当依法及时公示法人登记的有关信息。

**Article 66** The registration authority shall disclose relevant information on the registration of a legal person in a timely manner in accordance with law.

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条　法人合并的，其权利和义务由合并后的法人享有和承担。  
法人分立的，其权利和义务由分立后的法人享有连带债权，承担连带债务，但是债权人和债务人另有约定的除外。

**Article 67** Where a legal person is merged, the rights and obligations of the legal person shall be enjoyed and borne by the legal person resulting from the merger.  
Where a legal person is divided, the rights and obligations of the legal person shall be enjoyed as joint and several claims and borne as joint and several debts by the legal persons resulting from the division, unless the creditors and debtors agree otherwise.

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条　有下列原因之一并依法完成清算、注销登记的，法人终止：  
（一）法人解散；  
（二）法人被宣告破产；  
（三）法律规定的其他原因。  
法人终止，法律、行政法规规定须经有关机关批准的，依照其规定。

**Article 68** A legal person shall terminate upon occurrence of any of the following causes and completion of liquidation and deregistration in accordance with law:  
(1) the legal person is dissolved;  
(2) the legal person is declared bankrupt  
(3) other causes provided for by law. Where laws or administrative regulations require the approval of a relevant authority for the termination of a legal person, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条　有下列情形之一的，法人解散：  
（一）法人章程规定的存续期间届满或者法人章程规定的其他解散事由出现；  
（二）法人的权力机构决议解散；  
（三）因法人合并或者分立需要解散；  
（四）法人依法被吊销营业执照、登记证书，被责令关闭或者被撤销；  
（五）法律规定的其他情形。

**Article 69** A legal person shall be dissolved under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the period of existence prescribed in the articles of association of the legal person expires or other dissolution events prescribed in the articles of association occur;  
(2) the governing body of the legal person resolves to dissolve it;  
(3) dissolution is necessary due to a merger or division of the legal person;  
(4) the legal person's business license or registration certificate is lawfully revoked, or it is ordered to close down or is abolished  
(5) other circumstances provided for by law.

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条　法人解散的，除合并或者分立的情形外，清算义务人应当及时组成清算组进行清算。  
法人的董事、理事等执行机构或者决策机构的成员为清算义务人。法律、行政法规另有规定的，依照其规定。  
清算义务人未及时履行清算义务，造成损害的，应当承担民事责任；主管机关或者利害关系人可以申请人民法院指定有关人员组成清算组进行清算。

**Article 70** Where a legal person is dissolved, except in the case of a merger or division, the person obligated to liquidate shall promptly form a liquidation panel to carry out liquidation. The members of the executive body or the decision-making body of the legal person, such as directors and council members, shall be the persons obligated to liquidate, unless otherwise provided for by laws or administrative regulations, in which case such provisions shall prevail. Where the person obligated to liquidate fails to perform the liquidation obligation in a timely manner, thereby causing damage, such person shall bear civil liability. The competent authority or an interested person may apply to the People's Court for appointing relevant persons to form a liquidation panel to carry out liquidation.

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条　法人的清算程序和清算组职权，依照有关法律的规定；没有规定的，参照适用公司法律的有关规定。

**Article 71** The liquidation procedures and the functions and powers of the liquidation panel of a legal person shall be governed by the provisions of relevant laws. In the absence of such provisions, the relevant provisions of the company law shall be applied mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条　清算期间法人存续，但是不得从事与清算无关的活动。  
法人清算后的剩余财产，按照法人章程的规定或者法人权力机构的决议处理。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。  
清算结束并完成法人注销登记时，法人终止；依法不需要办理法人登记的，清算结束时，法人终止。

**Article 72** During the liquidation period, the legal person shall continue to exist but shall not engage in activities unrelated to the liquidation. The remaining property of the legal person after liquidation shall be disposed of in accordance with the provisions of the articles of association of the legal person or the resolution of its governing body, unless otherwise provided for by law, in which case such provisions shall prevail. A legal person shall terminate upon the conclusion of liquidation and completion of its deregistration. Where a legal person is not required to undergo registration in accordance with law, it shall terminate upon the conclusion of liquidation.

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条　法人被宣告破产的，依法进行破产清算并完成法人注销登记时，法人终止。

**Article 73** Where a legal person is declared bankrupt, the legal person shall terminate upon completion of bankruptcy liquidation and its deregistration in accordance with law.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条　法人可以依法设立分支机构。法律、行政法规规定分支机构应当登记的，依照其规定。  
分支机构以自己的名义从事民事活动，产生的民事责任由法人承担；也可以先以该分支机构管理的财产承担，不足以承担的，由法人承担。

**Article 74** A legal person may establish branches in accordance with law. Where laws or administrative regulations require the branches to be registered, such provisions shall prevail. Where a branch engages in civil activities in its own name, the legal person shall bear the civil liability arising therefrom. Alternatively, the liability may first be satisfied from the property managed by such branch, and any deficiency shall be borne by the legal person.

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条　设立人为设立法人从事的民事活动，其法律后果由法人承受；法人未成立的，其法律后果由设立人承受，设立人为二人以上的，享有连带债权，承担连带债务。  
设立人为设立法人以自己的名义从事民事活动产生的民事责任，第三人有权选择请求法人或者设立人承担。

**Article 75** The legal consequences of civil activities conducted by a founder for the purpose of establishing a legal person shall be borne by the legal person. If the legal person is not established, the legal consequences shall be borne by the founder. Where there are two or more founders, they shall enjoy joint and several claims and bear joint and several debts. Where a founder engages in civil activities in its own name for the purpose of establishing a legal person and thereby incurs civil liability, a third party shall have the right to elect to demand that the legal person or the founder bear such liability.

#### 第二节　营利法人

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条　以取得利润并分配给股东等出资人为目的成立的法人，为营利法人。  
营利法人包括有限责任公司、股份有限公司和其他企业法人等。

**Article 76** A legal person established for the purpose of obtaining profits and distributing them to its shareholders or other contributors shall be a for-profit legal person.  
For-profit legal persons include limited liability companies, companies limited by shares, and other enterprise legal persons.

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条　营利法人经依法登记成立。

**Article 77** A for-profit legal person shall be established upon registration in accordance with law.

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条　依法设立的营利法人，由登记机关发给营利法人营业执照。营业执照签发日期为营利法人的成立日期。

**Article 78** A for-profit legal person established according to law shall be issued a business license for a for-profit legal person by the registration authority. The date of issuance of the business license shall be the date of establishment of the for-profit legal person.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条　设立营利法人应当依法制定法人章程。

**Article 79** To establish a for-profit legal person, the articles of association of the legal person shall be formulated in accordance with law.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条　营利法人应当设权力机构。  
权力机构行使修改法人章程，选举或者更换执行机构、监督机构成员，以及法人章程规定的其他职权。

**Article 80** A for-profit legal person shall have a governing body.  
The governing body shall exercise the powers of amending the articles of association of the legal person, electing or replacing members of the executive body and the supervisory body, and performing other functions as prescribed in the articles of association of the legal person.

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条　营利法人应当设执行机构。  
执行机构行使召集权力机构会议，决定法人的经营计划和投资方案，决定法人内部管理机构的设置，以及法人章程规定的其他职权。  
执行机构为董事会或者执行董事的，董事长、执行董事或者经理按照法人章程的规定担任法定代表人；未设董事会或者执行董事的，法人章程规定的主要负责人为其执行机构和法定代表人。

**Article 81** A for-profit legal person shall establish an executive body. The executive body shall exercise the functions of convening meetings of the authoritative body, deciding on the business plans and investment schemes of the legal person, deciding on the establishment of the internal management structure of the legal person, and other functions as provided for in the legal person's articles of association. Where the executive body is a board of directors or an executive director, the chairman of the board, the executive director, or the manager shall serve as the legal representative in accordance with the legal person's articles of association. Where no board of directors or executive director is established, the principal person in charge provided by the legal person's articles of association shall serve as its executive body and legal representative.

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条　营利法人设监事会或者监事等监督机构的，监督机构依法行使检查法人财务，监督执行机构成员、高级管理人员执行法人职务的行为，以及法人章程规定的其他职权。

**Article 82** Where a for-profit legal person establishes a supervisory body such as a board of supervisors or a supervisor, the supervisory body shall exercise, according to law, the functions of examining the finances of the legal person, supervising the performance of duties by members of the executive body and senior officers, and other functions as provided for in the legal person's articles of association.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条　营利法人的出资人不得滥用出资人权利损害法人或者其他出资人的利益；滥用出资人权利造成法人或者其他出资人损失的，应当依法承担民事责任。  
营利法人的出资人不得滥用法人独立地位和出资人有限责任损害法人债权人的利益；滥用法人独立地位和出资人有限责任，逃避债务，严重损害法人债权人的利益的，应当对法人债务承担连带责任。

**Article 83** The contributors of a for-profit legal person shall not abuse their rights as contributors to harm the interests of the legal person or other contributors. Where the abuse of contributor rights causes losses to the legal person or other contributors, the abusive contributor shall bear civil liability according to law. The contributors of a for-profit legal person shall not abuse the independent status of the legal person and the limited liability of contributors to harm the interests of the legal person's creditors. Where the abuse of the independent status of the legal person and the limited liability of contributors to evade debts seriously harms the interests of the legal person's creditors, such contributors shall bear joint and several liability for the debts of the legal person.

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条　营利法人的控股出资人、实际控制人、董事、监事、高级管理人员不得利用其关联关系损害法人的利益；利用关联关系造成法人损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 84** The controlling contributor, actual controller, directors, supervisors, and senior officers of a for-profit legal person shall not use their affiliated relationships to harm the interests of the legal person. Where the use of an affiliated relationship causes losses to the legal person, such persons shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条　营利法人的权力机构、执行机构作出决议的会议召集程序、表决方式违反法律、行政法规、法人章程，或者决议内容违反法人章程的，营利法人的出资人可以请求人民法院撤销该决议。但是，营利法人依据该决议与善意相对人形成的民事法律关系不受影响。

**Article 85** Where the convening procedures or voting methods of a meeting of the authoritative body or executive body of a for-profit legal person for making a resolution violate any law, administrative regulation, or the legal person's articles of association, or the content of such a resolution violates the articles of association, a contributor of the for-profit legal person may request the People's Court to revoke the resolution. However, civil legal relationships formed between the for-profit legal person and a bona fide counterparty based on such a resolution shall not be affected.

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条　营利法人从事经营活动，应当遵守商业道德，维护交易安全，接受政府和社会的监督，承担社会责任。

**Article 86** When engaging in business activities, a for-profit legal person shall abide by business ethics, maintain transaction security, accept the supervision of the government and the public, and bear social responsibility.

#### 第三节　非营利法人

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条　为公益目的或者其他非营利目的成立，不向出资人、设立人或者会员分配所取得利润的法人，为非营利法人。  
非营利法人包括事业单位、社会团体、基金会、社会服务机构等。

**Article 87** A legal person established for a public interest purpose or other non-profit purpose, which does not distribute the profits it obtains to its contributors, founders, or members, is a non-profit legal person.  
Non-profit legal persons include public institutions, social organizations, foundations, social service institutions, and the like.

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条　具备法人条件，为适应经济社会发展需要，提供公益服务设立的事业单位，经依法登记成立，取得事业单位法人资格；依法不需要办理法人登记的，从成立之日起，具有事业单位法人资格。

**Article 88** A public institution that meets the conditions for being a legal person and is established to provide public services to meet the needs of economic and social development shall, upon being registered and established according to law, acquire the status of a public institution legal person. Where registration as a legal person is not required according to law, it shall have the status of a public institution legal person as of the date of its establishment.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条　事业单位法人设理事会的，除法律另有规定外，理事会为其决策机构。事业单位法人的法定代表人依照法律、行政法规或者法人章程的规定产生。

**Article 89** Where a public institution legal person establishes a board of directors, the board shall be its decision-making body, unless otherwise provided by law. The legal representative of a public institution legal person shall be appointed in accordance with the provisions of any law, administrative regulation, or the legal person's articles of association.

<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条　具备法人条件，基于会员共同意愿，为公益目的或者会员共同利益等非营利目的设立的社会团体，经依法登记成立，取得社会团体法人资格；依法不需要办理法人登记的，从成立之日起，具有社会团体法人资格。

**Article 90** A social organization that meets the conditions for being a legal person and is established based on the common will of its members for a non-profit purpose such as public interest or the common interests of its members shall, upon being registered and established according to law, acquire the status of a social organization legal person. Where registration as a legal person is not required according to law, it shall have the status of a social organization legal person as of the date of its establishment.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条　设立社会团体法人应当依法制定法人章程。  
社会团体法人应当设会员大会或者会员代表大会等权力机构。  
社会团体法人应当设理事会等执行机构。理事长或者会长等负责人按照法人章程的规定担任法定代表人。

**Article 91** To establish a social organization legal person, the articles of association of the legal person shall be formulated according to law. A social organization legal person shall establish an authoritative body such as a membership assembly or a membership representative assembly. A social organization legal person shall establish an executive body such as a board of directors. The responsible person such as the chairman or president shall serve as the legal representative in accordance with the provisions of the legal person's articles of association.

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条　具备法人条件，为公益目的以捐助财产设立的基金会、社会服务机构等，经依法登记成立，取得捐助法人资格。  
依法设立的宗教活动场所，具备法人条件的，可以申请法人登记，取得捐助法人资格。法律、行政法规对宗教活动场所有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 92** A foundation, social service institution, or the like established with donated property for a public interest purpose and meeting the conditions for being a legal person shall, upon being registered and established according to law, acquire the status of a donation legal person. A religious activity site established according to law and meeting the conditions for being a legal person may apply for registration as a legal person and acquire the status of a donation legal person. Where any law or administrative regulation provides for religious activity sites, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条　设立捐助法人应当依法制定法人章程。  
捐助法人应当设理事会、民主管理组织等决策机构，并设执行机构。理事长等负责人按照法人章程的规定担任法定代表人。  
捐助法人应当设监事会等监督机构。

**Article 93** To establish a donation legal person, the articles of association of the legal person shall be formulated according to law. A donation legal person shall establish a decision-making body such as a board of directors or a democratic management organization, and shall also establish an executive body. The responsible person such as the chairman of the board shall serve as the legal representative in accordance with the provisions of the legal person's articles of association. A donation legal person shall establish a supervisory body such as a board of supervisors.

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条　捐助人有权向捐助法人查询捐助财产的使用、管理情况，并提出意见和建议，捐助法人应当及时、如实答复。  
捐助法人的决策机构、执行机构或者法定代表人作出决定的程序违反法律、行政法规、法人章程，或者决定内容违反法人章程的，捐助人等利害关系人或者主管机关可以请求人民法院撤销该决定。但是，捐助法人依据该决定与善意相对人形成的民事法律关系不受影响。

**Article 94** A donor shall have the right to inquire of the donation legal person about the use and management of the donated property and to make comments and suggestions, and the donation legal person shall provide prompt and truthful replies. Where the procedures for making a decision by the decision-making body, executive body, or legal representative of a donation legal person violate any law, administrative regulation, or the legal person's articles of association, or the content of such a decision violates the legal person's articles of association, the donor or other interested persons, or the competent authority may request the People's Court to revoke the decision. However, civil legal relationships formed between the donation legal person and a bona fide counterparty based on such a decision shall not be affected.

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条　为公益目的成立的非营利法人终止时，不得向出资人、设立人或者会员分配剩余财产。剩余财产应当按照法人章程的规定或者权力机构的决议用于公益目的；无法按照法人章程的规定或者权力机构的决议处理的，由主管机关主持转给宗旨相同或者相近的法人，并向社会公告。

**Article 95** When a non-profit legal person established for a public interest purpose is terminated, the residual property shall not be distributed to its contributors, founders, or members. The residual property shall be used for public interest purposes in accordance with the provisions of the legal person's articles of association or the resolution of its authoritative body. Where it cannot be disposed of in accordance with the provisions of the legal person's articles of association or the resolution of its authoritative body, the competent authority shall arrange for its transfer to a legal person with the same or similar purposes and make a public announcement.

#### 第四节　特别法人

<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条　本节规定的机关法人、农村集体经济组织法人、城镇农村的合作经济组织法人、基层群众性自治组织法人，为特别法人。

**Article 96** The organ legal persons, rural collective economic organization legal persons, urban and rural cooperative economic organization legal persons, and basic-level self-governing mass organization legal persons provided for in this Section are special legal persons.

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条　有独立经费的机关和承担行政职能的法定机构从成立之日起，具有机关法人资格，可以从事为履行职能所需要的民事活动。

**Article 97** An organ with independent funds and a statutory body assuming administrative functions shall, as of the date of its establishment, have the status of an organ legal person and may engage in civil activities necessary for performing its functions.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条　机关法人被撤销的，法人终止，其民事权利和义务由继任的机关法人享有和承担；没有继任的机关法人的，由作出撤销决定的机关法人享有和承担。

**Article 98** Where an organ legal person is abolished, the legal person shall terminate, and its civil rights and obligations shall be enjoyed and assumed by the succeeding organ legal person. Where there is no succeeding organ legal person, they shall be enjoyed and assumed by the organ legal person that made the abolition decision.

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条　农村集体经济组织依法取得法人资格。  
法律、行政法规对农村集体经济组织有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 99** A rural collective economic organization shall acquire the status of a legal person according to law.  
Where any law or administrative regulation provides for rural collective economic organizations, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条　城镇农村的合作经济组织依法取得法人资格。  
法律、行政法规对城镇农村的合作经济组织有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 100** An urban or rural cooperative economic organization shall acquire the status of a legal person according to law.  
Where any law or administrative regulation provides for urban or rural cooperative economic organizations, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条　居民委员会、村民委员会具有基层群众性自治组织法人资格，可以从事为履行职能所需要的民事活动。  
未设立村集体经济组织的，村民委员会可以依法代行村集体经济组织的职能。

**Article 101** A residents committee or a villagers committee has the legal person status of a grassroots self-governing mass organization and may engage in civil activities necessary for the performance of its functions.  
Where no rural collective economic organization has been established, the villagers committee may, according to law, perform the functions of the rural collective economic organization on its behalf.

### 第四章　非法人组织

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条　非法人组织是不具有法人资格，但是能够依法以自己的名义从事民事活动的组织。  
非法人组织包括个人独资企业、合伙企业、不具有法人资格的专业服务机构等。

**Article 102** An unincorporated organization is an organization that does not have the status of a legal person but is able to engage in civil activities in its own name according to law.  
Unincorporated organizations include sole proprietorship enterprises, partnership enterprises, and professional service institutions without legal person status, among others.

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条　非法人组织应当依照法律的规定登记。  
设立非法人组织，法律、行政法规规定须经有关机关批准的，依照其规定。

**Article 103** An unincorporated organization shall be registered in accordance with the provisions of law.  
Where the formation of an unincorporated organization is subject to approval by the relevant authority according to laws or administrative regulations, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条　非法人组织的财产不足以清偿债务的，其出资人或者设立人承担无限责任。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 104** Where the property of an unincorporated organization is insufficient to satisfy its debts, its contributors or founders shall bear unlimited liability. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条　非法人组织可以确定一人或者数人代表该组织从事民事活动。

**Article 105** An unincorporated organization may designate one or more persons to represent the organization in engaging in civil activities.

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条　有下列情形之一的，非法人组织解散：  
（一）章程规定的存续期间届满或者章程规定的其他解散事由出现；  
（二）出资人或者设立人决定解散；  
（三）法律规定的其他情形。

**Article 106** An unincorporated organization shall be dissolved under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the period of duration specified in its constitutive documents expires or another ground for dissolution specified in its constitutive documents occurs;  
(2) its contributors or founders decide to dissolve it  
(3) any other circumstance provided by law.

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条　非法人组织解散的，应当依法进行清算。

**Article 107** Where an unincorporated organization is dissolved, it shall undergo liquidation according to law.

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条　非法人组织除适用本章规定外，参照适用本编第三章第一节的有关规定。

**Article 108** In addition to the provisions of this Chapter, an unincorporated organization shall be governed mutatis mutandis by the relevant provisions of Section 1, Chapter 3 of this Part.

### 第五章　民事权利

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条　自然人的人身自由、人格尊严受法律保护。

**Article 109** The personal freedom and human dignity of a natural person shall be protected by law.

<a id="art-110"></a>第一百一十条　自然人享有生命权、身体权、健康权、姓名权、肖像权、名誉权、荣誉权、隐私权、婚姻自主权等权利。  
法人、非法人组织享有名称权、名誉权和荣誉权。

**Article 110** A natural person enjoys the rights to life, physical integrity, health, name, portrait, reputation, honor, privacy, and freedom of marriage, among others.  
A legal person or an unincorporated organization enjoys the rights to name, reputation, and honor.

<a id="art-111"></a>第一百一十一条　自然人的个人信息受法律保护。任何组织或者个人需要获取他人个人信息的，应当依法取得并确保信息安全，不得非法收集、使用、加工、传输他人个人信息，不得非法买卖、提供或者公开他人个人信息。

**Article 111** The personal information of a natural person shall be protected by law. Any organization or individual that needs to obtain personal information of others shall obtain it according to law and ensure its security, and shall not illegally collect, use, process, or transmit personal information of others, nor illegally trade, provide, or disclose personal information of others.

<a id="art-112"></a>第一百一十二条　自然人因婚姻家庭关系等产生的人身权利受法律保护。

**Article 112** The personal rights of a natural person arising from marital or family relationships, among others, shall be protected by law.

<a id="art-113"></a>第一百一十三条　民事主体的财产权利受法律平等保护。

**Article 113** The property rights of civil subjects shall be equally protected by law.

<a id="art-114"></a>第一百一十四条　民事主体依法享有物权。  
物权是权利人依法对特定的物享有直接支配和排他的权利，包括所有权、用益物权和担保物权。

**Article 114** A civil subject shall enjoy real rights according to law.  
A real right is the right of the right holder to directly control and exclude others with respect to a specific property according to law, including ownership, usufruct, and security interests.

<a id="art-115"></a>第一百一十五条　物包括不动产和动产。法律规定权利作为物权客体的，依照其规定。

**Article 115** Property includes immovable and movable property. Where a right is provided by law as the object of a real right, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-116"></a>第一百一十六条　物权的种类和内容，由法律规定。

**Article 116** The types and content of real rights shall be provided by law.

<a id="art-117"></a>第一百一十七条　为了公共利益的需要，依照法律规定的权限和程序征收、征用不动产或者动产的，应当给予公平、合理的补偿。

**Article 117** Where immovable or movable property is expropriated or requisitioned for the needs of the public interest, in accordance with the authority and procedures provided by law, fair and reasonable compensation shall be given.

<a id="art-118"></a>第一百一十八条　民事主体依法享有债权。  
债权是因合同、侵权行为、无因管理、不当得利以及法律的其他规定，权利人请求特定义务人为或者不为一定行为的权利。

**Article 118** A civil subject shall enjoy obligatory rights according to law.  
An obligatory right is the right of the right holder to request a specific obligor to perform or refrain from performing a certain act, arising from a contract, tort, negotiorum gestio, unjust enrichment, or other provisions of law.

<a id="art-119"></a>第一百一十九条　依法成立的合同，对当事人具有法律约束力。

**Article 119** A contract formed according to law shall be legally binding on the parties.

<a id="art-120"></a>第一百二十条　民事权益受到侵害的，被侵权人有权请求侵权人承担侵权责任。

**Article 120** Where a civil right or interest is infringed upon, the aggrieved party has the right to request the tortfeasor to bear tort liability.

<a id="art-121"></a>第一百二十一条　没有法定的或者约定的义务，为避免他人利益受损失而进行管理的人，有权请求受益人偿还由此支出的必要费用。

**Article 121** A person who, without a statutory or contractual obligation, manages the affairs of another to prevent harm to that other person's interests shall have the right to request the beneficiary to reimburse the necessary expenses incurred thereby.

<a id="art-122"></a>第一百二十二条　因他人没有法律根据，取得不当利益，受损失的人有权请求其返还不当利益。

**Article 122** Where a person acquires unjust benefits without any legal basis, the person who suffers loss shall have the right to request the return of such unjust benefits.

<a id="art-123"></a>第一百二十三条　民事主体依法享有知识产权。  
知识产权是权利人依法就下列客体享有的专有的权利：  
（一）作品；  
（二）发明、实用新型、外观设计；  
（三）商标；  
（四）地理标志；  
（五）商业秘密；  
（六）集成电路布图设计；  
（七）植物新品种；  
（八）法律规定的其他客体。

**Article 123** A civil subject shall enjoy intellectual property rights according to law. Intellectual property rights are the exclusive rights enjoyed by the right holder according to law in respect of the following subject matter:  
(1) works;  
(2) inventions, utility models, and designs;  
(3) trademarks;  
(4) geographical indications;  
(5) trade secrets;  
(6) layout designs of integrated circuits;  
(7) new plant varieties;  
(8) other subject matter as provided by law.

<a id="art-124"></a>第一百二十四条　自然人依法享有继承权。  
自然人合法的私有财产，可以依法继承。

**Article 124** A natural person shall enjoy the right of succession according to law.  
The lawful private property of a natural person may be inherited according to law.

<a id="art-125"></a>第一百二十五条　民事主体依法享有股权和其他投资性权利。

**Article 125** A civil subject shall enjoy equity interests and other investment rights according to law.

<a id="art-126"></a>第一百二十六条　民事主体享有法律规定的其他民事权利和利益。

**Article 126** A civil subject shall enjoy other civil rights and interests as provided by law.

<a id="art-127"></a>第一百二十七条　法律对数据、网络虚拟财产的保护有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 127** Where laws provide for the protection of data and network virtual property, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-128"></a>第一百二十八条　法律对未成年人、老年人、残疾人、妇女、消费者等的民事权利保护有特别规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 128** Where laws provide special provisions for the protection of the civil rights of minors, the elderly, persons with disabilities, women, consumers, and others, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-129"></a>第一百二十九条　民事权利可以依据民事法律行为、事实行为、法律规定的事件或者法律规定的其他方式取得。

**Article 129** Civil rights may be acquired through civil juristic acts, factual acts, events provided by law, or other means provided by law.

<a id="art-130"></a>第一百三十条　民事主体按照自己的意愿依法行使民事权利，不受干涉。

**Article 130** A civil subject shall exercise civil rights according to its own will and in accordance with law, and shall not be interfered with.

<a id="art-131"></a>第一百三十一条　民事主体行使权利时，应当履行法律规定的和当事人约定的义务。

**Article 131** When exercising rights, a civil subject shall perform the obligations provided by law and agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-132"></a>第一百三十二条　民事主体不得滥用民事权利损害国家利益、社会公共利益或者他人合法权益。

**Article 132** A civil subject shall not abuse civil rights to harm the national interest, the public interest, or the lawful rights and interests of others.

### 第六章　民事法律行为

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-133"></a>第一百三十三条　民事法律行为是民事主体通过意思表示设立、变更、终止民事法律关系的行为。

**Article 133** A civil juristic act is an act of a civil subject to establish, modify, or terminate a civil juristic relationship through a declaration of intent.

<a id="art-134"></a>第一百三十四条　民事法律行为可以基于双方或者多方的意思表示一致成立，也可以基于单方的意思表示成立。  
法人、非法人组织依照法律或者章程规定的议事方式和表决程序作出决议的，该决议行为成立。

**Article 134** A civil juristic act may be established based on the consensus of declarations of intent of two or more parties, and may also be established based on the declaration of intent of a single party.  
Where a legal person or an unincorporated organization makes a resolution in accordance with the deliberation methods and voting procedures provided by law or its articles of association, such act of making a resolution shall be established.

<a id="art-135"></a>第一百三十五条　民事法律行为可以采用书面形式、口头形式或者其他形式；法律、行政法规规定或者当事人约定采用特定形式的，应当采用特定形式。

**Article 135** A civil juristic act may be made in writing, orally, or in any other form; where a law or administrative regulation provides or the parties agree that a specific form shall be used, such specific form shall be used.

<a id="art-136"></a>第一百三十六条　民事法律行为自成立时生效，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。  
行为人非依法律规定或者未经对方同意，不得擅自变更或者解除民事法律行为。

**Article 136** A civil juristic act shall become effective upon its establishment, unless otherwise provided by law or agreed by the parties.  
No actor shall, without the basis of legal provisions or the consent of the other party, arbitrarily modify or rescind a civil juristic act.

#### 第二节　意思表示

<a id="art-137"></a>第一百三十七条　以对话方式作出的意思表示，相对人知道其内容时生效。  
以非对话方式作出的意思表示，到达相对人时生效。以非对话方式作出的采用数据电文形式的意思表示，相对人指定特定系统接收数据电文的，该数据电文进入该特定系统时生效；未指定特定系统的，相对人知道或者应当知道该数据电文进入其系统时生效。当事人对采用数据电文形式的意思表示的生效时间另有约定的，按照其约定。

**Article 137** A declaration of intent made in a dialogue shall become effective when the counterparty becomes aware of its content. A declaration of intent made otherwise than in a dialogue shall become effective when it reaches the counterparty. Where a declaration of intent is made otherwise than in a dialogue in the form of an electronic data message, and the counterparty has designated a specific system for receiving such data messages, it shall become effective when the data message enters that specific system; where no specific system is designated, it shall become effective when the counterparty knows or should know that the data message has entered its system. Where the parties have otherwise agreed on the effective time of a declaration of intent made in the form of an electronic data message, such agreement shall prevail.

<a id="art-138"></a>第一百三十八条　无相对人的意思表示，表示完成时生效。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 138** A declaration of intent made without a counterparty shall become effective upon completion of the declaration. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-139"></a>第一百三十九条　以公告方式作出的意思表示，公告发布时生效。

**Article 139** A declaration of intent made by public announcement shall become effective upon the issuance of the announcement.

<a id="art-140"></a>第一百四十条　行为人可以明示或者默示作出意思表示。  
沉默只有在有法律规定、当事人约定或者符合当事人之间的交易习惯时，才可以视为意思表示。

**Article 140** An actor may make a declaration of intent expressly or by implication.  
Silence shall be deemed a declaration of intent only where so provided by law, agreed by the parties, or in accordance with the transaction practices between the parties.

<a id="art-141"></a>第一百四十一条　行为人可以撤回意思表示。撤回意思表示的通知应当在意思表示到达相对人前或者与意思表示同时到达相对人。

**Article 141** A person may withdraw a declaration of intent. The notice of withdrawal of a declaration of intent shall reach the other party before or at the same time as the declaration of intent reaches the other party.

<a id="art-142"></a>第一百四十二条　有相对人的意思表示的解释，应当按照所使用的词句，结合相关条款、行为的性质和目的、习惯以及诚信原则，确定意思表示的含义。  
无相对人的意思表示的解释，不能完全拘泥于所使用的词句，而应当结合相关条款、行为的性质和目的、习惯以及诚信原则，确定行为人的真实意思。

**Article 142** The interpretation of a declaration of intent made to a counterparty shall be made in accordance with the wording used, and the meaning of the declaration of intent shall be determined by reference to the relevant provisions, the nature and purpose of the act, usages, and the principle of good faith.  
The interpretation of a declaration of intent not made to a counterparty shall not be strictly confined to the wording used, but the true intent of the person shall be determined by reference to the relevant provisions, the nature and purpose of the act, usages, and the principle of good faith.

#### 第三节　民事法律行为的效力

<a id="art-143"></a>第一百四十三条　具备下列条件的民事法律行为有效：  
（一）行为人具有相应的民事行为能力；  
（二）意思表示真实；  
（三）不违反法律、行政法规的强制性规定，不违背公序良俗。

**Article 143** A civil juristic act that meets the following conditions is valid:  
(1) the person has corresponding capacity for civil conduct;  
(2) the declaration of intent is genuine; and (3) it does not violate the mandatory provisions of any law or administrative regulation, nor contravene public order and good customs.

<a id="art-144"></a>第一百四十四条　无民事行为能力人实施的民事法律行为无效。

**Article 144** A civil juristic act performed by a person without capacity for civil conduct is void.

<a id="art-145"></a>第一百四十五条　限制民事行为能力人实施的纯获利益的民事法律行为或者与其年龄、智力、精神健康状况相适应的民事法律行为有效；实施的其他民事法律行为经法定代理人同意或者追认后有效。  
相对人可以催告法定代理人自收到通知之日起三十日内予以追认。法定代理人未作表示的，视为拒绝追认。民事法律行为被追认前，善意相对人有撤销的权利。撤销应当以通知的方式作出。

**Article 145** A civil juristic act performed by a person with limited capacity for civil conduct that is purely beneficial to such person, or that is appropriate to such person's age, intelligence, or mental health condition, is valid; any other civil juristic act performed by such person is valid only after being consented to or ratified by the legal representative. The counterparty may urge the legal representative to ratify the act within thirty days from receipt of the notice. If the legal representative fails to respond, ratification shall be deemed to have been refused. Before the civil juristic act is ratified, a bona fide counterparty has the right to revoke the act. Revocation shall be made by notice.

<a id="art-146"></a>第一百四十六条　行为人与相对人以虚假的意思表示实施的民事法律行为无效。  
以虚假的意思表示隐藏的民事法律行为的效力，依照有关法律规定处理。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-68" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第68条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 146** A civil juristic act performed by a person and the counterparty with a false declaration of intent is void.  
The validity of a civil juristic act concealed by a false declaration of intent shall be dealt with in accordance with the provisions of relevant laws.

<a id="art-147"></a>第一百四十七条　基于重大误解实施的民事法律行为，行为人有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。

**Article 147** A person who performs a civil juristic act based on a material mistake has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-148"></a>第一百四十八条　一方以欺诈手段，使对方在违背真实意思的情况下实施的民事法律行为，受欺诈方有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。

**Article 148** Where a party, by means of fraud, causes the other party to perform a civil juristic act contrary to its true intent, the defrauded party has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-149"></a>第一百四十九条　第三人实施欺诈行为，使一方在违背真实意思的情况下实施的民事法律行为，对方知道或者应当知道该欺诈行为的，受欺诈方有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。

**Article 149** Where a third party commits fraud, causing one party to perform a civil juristic act contrary to its true intent, and the other party knows or should have known of the fraud, the defrauded party has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-150"></a>第一百五十条　一方或者第三人以胁迫手段，使对方在违背真实意思的情况下实施的民事法律行为，受胁迫方有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。

**Article 150** Where a party or a third party, by means of duress, causes the other party to perform a civil juristic act contrary to its true intent, the coerced party has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-151"></a>第一百五十一条　一方利用对方处于危困状态、缺乏判断能力等情形，致使民事法律行为成立时显失公平的，受损害方有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。

**Article 151** Where one party takes advantage of the other party's predicament, lack of judgment, or other circumstances, causing the civil juristic act to be manifestly unfair when concluded, the aggrieved party has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-152"></a>第一百五十二条　有下列情形之一的，撤销权消灭：  
（一）当事人自知道或者应当知道撤销事由之日起一年内、重大误解的当事人自知道或者应当知道撤销事由之日起九十日内没有行使撤销权；  
（二）当事人受胁迫，自胁迫行为终止之日起一年内没有行使撤销权；  
（三）当事人知道撤销事由后明确表示或者以自己的行为表明放弃撤销权。  
当事人自民事法律行为发生之日起五年内没有行使撤销权的，撤销权消灭。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第19条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 152** The right to revocation is extinguished in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) a party fails to exercise the right to revocation within one year from the date it knows or should have known of the cause for revocation, or a party acting under a material mistake fails to exercise the right to revocation within ninety days from the date it knows or should have known of the cause for revocation;  
(2) a coerced party fails to exercise the right to revocation within one year from the date the duress ceases  
(3) a party, after learning of the cause for revocation, expressly states or indicates by its own conduct that it waives the right to revocation. The right to revocation is extinguished if a party fails to exercise it within five years from the date the civil juristic act took effect.

<a id="art-153"></a>第一百五十三条　违反法律、行政法规的强制性规定的民事法律行为无效。但是，该强制性规定不导致该民事法律行为无效的除外。  
违背公序良俗的民事法律行为无效。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 153** A civil juristic act that violates a mandatory provision of any law or administrative regulation is void, unless the mandatory provision does not render the civil juristic act void.  
A civil juristic act that contravenes public order and good customs is void.

<a id="art-154"></a>第一百五十四条　行为人与相对人恶意串通，损害他人合法权益的民事法律行为无效。

**Article 154** A civil juristic act performed by a person and the counterparty through malicious collusion to harm the lawful rights and interests of another person is void.

<a id="art-155"></a>第一百五十五条　无效的或者被撤销的民事法律行为自始没有法律约束力。

**Article 155** A civil juristic act that is void or has been revoked has no legally binding effect ab initio.

<a id="art-156"></a>第一百五十六条　民事法律行为部分无效，不影响其他部分效力的，其他部分仍然有效。

**Article 156** Where part of a civil juristic act is void without affecting the validity of other parts, the other parts shall remain valid.

<a id="art-157"></a>第一百五十七条　民事法律行为无效、被撤销或者确定不发生效力后，行为人因该行为取得的财产，应当予以返还；不能返还或者没有必要返还的，应当折价补偿。有过错的一方应当赔偿对方由此所受到的损失；各方都有过错的，应当各自承担相应的责任。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 157** After a civil juristic act is void, revoked, or confirmed to be ineffective, any property obtained by a person as a result of the act shall be returned; if it cannot be returned or return is unnecessary, compensation shall be made in monetary terms. The party at fault shall compensate the other party for any losses incurred thereby; if both parties are at fault, each party shall bear the corresponding liability. Where otherwise provided by law, such provisions shall prevail.

#### 第四节　民事法律行为的附条件和附期限

<a id="art-158"></a>第一百五十八条　民事法律行为可以附条件，但是根据其性质不得附条件的除外。附生效条件的民事法律行为，自条件成就时生效。附解除条件的民事法律行为，自条件成就时失效。

**Article 158** A civil juristic act may be subject to a condition, unless it cannot be subject to a condition by its nature. A civil juristic act subject to a condition precedent shall take effect when the condition is fulfilled. A civil juristic act subject to a condition subsequent shall cease to have effect when the condition is fulfilled.

<a id="art-159"></a>第一百五十九条　附条件的民事法律行为，当事人为自己的利益不正当地阻止条件成就的，视为条件已经成就；不正当地促成条件成就的，视为条件不成就。

**Article 159** Under a conditional civil juristic act, if a party improperly prevents the fulfillment of the condition for its own benefit, the condition shall be deemed to have been fulfilled; if a party improperly procures the fulfillment of the condition, the condition shall be deemed not to have been fulfilled.

<a id="art-160"></a>第一百六十条　民事法律行为可以附期限，但是根据其性质不得附期限的除外。附生效期限的民事法律行为，自期限届至时生效。附终止期限的民事法律行为，自期限届满时失效。

**Article 160** A civil juristic act may be subject to a time limit, unless it cannot be subject to a time limit by its nature. A civil juristic act subject to a time period for its commencement shall take effect when the time period arrives. A civil juristic act subject to a time period for its termination shall cease to have effect when the time period expires.

### 第七章　代理

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-161"></a>第一百六十一条　民事主体可以通过代理人实施民事法律行为。  
依照法律规定、当事人约定或者民事法律行为的性质，应当由本人亲自实施的民事法律行为，不得代理。

**Article 161** A civil subject may perform a civil juristic act through an agent.  
A civil juristic act that shall be performed personally by the principal according to the provisions of law, the agreement of the parties, or the nature of the civil juristic act may not be performed through an agent.

<a id="art-162"></a>第一百六十二条　代理人在代理权限内，以被代理人名义实施的民事法律行为，对被代理人发生效力。

**Article 162** A civil juristic act performed by an agent in the name of the principal within the scope of the power of agency shall be binding on the principal.

<a id="art-163"></a>第一百六十三条　代理包括委托代理和法定代理。  
委托代理人按照被代理人的委托行使代理权。法定代理人依照法律的规定行使代理权。

**Article 163** Agency includes mandated agency and statutory agency. A mandated agent shall exercise the power of agency in accordance with the mandate of the principal. A statutory agent shall exercise the power of agency in accordance with the provisions of law.

<a id="art-164"></a>第一百六十四条　代理人不履行或者不完全履行职责，造成被代理人损害的，应当承担民事责任。  
代理人和相对人恶意串通，损害被代理人合法权益的，代理人和相对人应当承担连带责任。

**Article 164** Where an agent fails to perform or fails to fully perform its duties, causing damage to the principal, it shall bear civil liability.  
Where the agent and the other party maliciously collude, causing damage to the lawful rights and interests of the principal, the agent and the other party shall bear joint and several liability.

#### 第二节　委托代理

<a id="art-165"></a>第一百六十五条　委托代理授权采用书面形式的，授权委托书应当载明代理人的姓名或者名称、代理事项、权限和期限，并由被代理人签名或者盖章。

**Article 165** Where a mandate for agency is made in written form, the power of attorney shall state the name of the agent, the matters of agency, the scope of authority and the term, and shall be signed or sealed by the principal.

<a id="art-166"></a>第一百六十六条　数人为同一代理事项的代理人的，应当共同行使代理权，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 166** Where several persons act as agents for the same matters of agency, they shall jointly exercise the power of agency, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-167"></a>第一百六十七条　代理人知道或者应当知道代理事项违法仍然实施代理行为，或者被代理人知道或者应当知道代理人的代理行为违法未作反对表示的，被代理人和代理人应当承担连带责任。

**Article 167** Where an agent knows or should have known that the matters of agency are illegal but still performs the agency act, or the principal knows or should have known that the agent's act is illegal but raises no objection, the principal and the agent shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-168"></a>第一百六十八条　代理人不得以被代理人的名义与自己实施民事法律行为，但是被代理人同意或者追认的除外。  
代理人不得以被代理人的名义与自己同时代理的其他人实施民事法律行为，但是被代理的双方同意或者追认的除外。

**Article 168** An agent may not perform a civil juristic act in the name of the principal with itself, unless the principal consents or ratifies it.  
An agent may not perform a civil juristic act in the name of the principal with another person whom the agent also represents at the same time, unless both principals consent or ratify it.

<a id="art-169"></a>第一百六十九条　代理人需要转委托第三人代理的，应当取得被代理人的同意或者追认。  
转委托代理经被代理人同意或者追认的，被代理人可以就代理事务直接指示转委托的第三人，代理人仅就第三人的选任以及对第三人的指示承担责任。  
转委托代理未经被代理人同意或者追认的，代理人应当对转委托的第三人的行为承担责任；但是，在紧急情况下代理人为了维护被代理人的利益需要转委托第三人代理的除外。

**Article 169** Where an agent needs to sub-entrust the agency to a third party, the agent shall obtain the consent or ratification of the principal.  
Where the sub-entrustment is consented to or ratified by the principal, the principal may directly give instructions to the third party regarding the agency matters, and the agent shall only be liable for the selection of the third party and for the instructions given to the third party.  
Where the sub-entrustment is not consented to or ratified by the principal, the agent shall be liable for the acts of the third party; except that, in an emergency situation, the agent needs to sub-entrust the agency to a third party in order to protect the interests of the principal.

<a id="art-170"></a>第一百七十条　执行法人或者非法人组织工作任务的人员，就其职权范围内的事项，以法人或者非法人组织的名义实施的民事法律行为，对法人或者非法人组织发生效力。  
法人或者非法人组织对执行其工作任务的人员职权范围的限制，不得对抗善意相对人。

**Article 170** A civil juristic act performed by a person who performs tasks for a legal person or an unincorporated organization, within the scope of their duties, in the name of the legal person or unincorporated organization, shall be binding on the legal person or unincorporated organization.  
Any restriction by the legal person or unincorporated organization on the scope of duties of the person performing tasks for it may not be asserted against a bona fide other party.

<a id="art-171"></a>第一百七十一条　行为人没有代理权、超越代理权或者代理权终止后，仍然实施代理行为，未经被代理人追认的，对被代理人不发生效力。  
相对人可以催告被代理人自收到通知之日起三十日内予以追认。被代理人未作表示的，视为拒绝追认。行为人实施的行为被追认前，善意相对人有撤销的权利。撤销应当以通知的方式作出。  
行为人实施的行为未被追认的，善意相对人有权请求行为人履行债务或者就其受到的损害请求行为人赔偿。但是，赔偿的范围不得超过被代理人追认时相对人所能获得的利益。  
相对人知道或者应当知道行为人无权代理的，相对人和行为人按照各自的过错承担责任。

**Article 171** Where an actor performs an agency act without the power of agency, beyond the scope of agency, or after the power of agency has terminated, and such act is not ratified by the principal, it shall not be binding on the principal. The other party may demand that the principal ratify the act within thirty days after receiving the notice. If the principal does not respond, it shall be deemed a refusal to ratify. Before the act performed by the actor is ratified, a bona fide other party has the right to revoke it. The revocation shall be made by notice. Where the act performed by the actor is not ratified, a bona fide other party has the right to demand that the actor perform the obligation or compensate for the damage suffered. However, the scope of compensation may not exceed the benefits that the other party could have obtained if the principal had ratified the act. Where the other party knows or should have known that the actor has no power of agency, the other party and the actor shall bear liability according to their respective fault.

<a id="art-172"></a>第一百七十二条　行为人没有代理权、超越代理权或者代理权终止后，仍然实施代理行为，相对人有理由相信行为人有代理权的，代理行为有效。

**Article 172** Where an actor performs an agency act without the power of agency, beyond the scope of agency, or after the power of agency has terminated, and the other party has reason to believe that the actor has the power of agency, the agency act shall be valid.

#### 第三节　代理终止

<a id="art-173"></a>第一百七十三条　有下列情形之一的，委托代理终止：  
（一）代理期限届满或者代理事务完成；  
（二）被代理人取消委托或者代理人辞去委托；  
（三）代理人丧失民事行为能力；  
（四）代理人或者被代理人死亡；  
（五）作为代理人或者被代理人的法人、非法人组织终止。

**Article 173** The mandated agency shall terminate under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the term of agency expires or the matters of agency are completed;  
(2) the principal revokes the mandate or the agent resigns from the mandate;  
(3) the agent loses capacity for civil conduct;  
(4) the agent or the principal dies  
(5) the legal person or unincorporated organization acting as the agent or the principal terminates.

<a id="art-174"></a>第一百七十四条　被代理人死亡后，有下列情形之一的，委托代理人实施的代理行为有效：  
（一）代理人不知道且不应当知道被代理人死亡；  
（二）被代理人的继承人予以承认；  
（三）授权中明确代理权在代理事务完成时终止；  
（四）被代理人死亡前已经实施，为了被代理人的继承人的利益继续代理。  
作为被代理人的法人、非法人组织终止的，参照适用前款规定。

**Article 174** After the death of the principal, the agency acts performed by the mandated agent shall be valid under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the agent neither knew nor should have known of the death of the principal;  
(2) the successor of the principal recognizes the acts;  
(3) the authorization clearly states that the power of agency terminates upon completion of the matters of agency  
(4) the act has been performed before the death of the principal, and the agent continues to act for the benefit of the successor of the principal.
