Where the legal person or unincorporated organization acting as the principal terminates, the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply by analogy.

<a id="art-175"></a>第一百七十五条　有下列情形之一的，法定代理终止：  
（一）被代理人取得或者恢复完全民事行为能力；  
（二）代理人丧失民事行为能力；  
（三）代理人或者被代理人死亡；  
（四）法律规定的其他情形。

**Article 175** The statutory agency shall terminate under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the principal acquires or regains full capacity for civil conduct;  
(2) the agent loses capacity for civil conduct;  
(3) the agent or the principal dies  
(4) other circumstances prescribed by law.

### 第八章　民事责任

<a id="art-176"></a>第一百七十六条　民事主体依照法律规定或者按照当事人约定，履行民事义务，承担民事责任。

**Article 176** A civil subject shall perform civil obligations and bear civil liability in accordance with the provisions of law or the agreement of the parties.

<a id="art-177"></a>第一百七十七条　二人以上依法承担按份责任，能够确定责任大小的，各自承担相应的责任；难以确定责任大小的，平均承担责任。

**Article 177** Where two or more persons bear several liability according to law, if the extent of liability can be determined, each shall bear the corresponding liability; if it is difficult to determine the extent of liability, they shall bear the liability in equal shares.

<a id="art-178"></a>第一百七十八条　二人以上依法承担连带责任的，权利人有权请求部分或者全部连带责任人承担责任。  
连带责任人的责任份额根据各自责任大小确定；难以确定责任大小的，平均承担责任。实际承担责任超过自己责任份额的连带责任人，有权向其他连带责任人追偿。  
连带责任，由法律规定或者当事人约定。

**Article 178** Where two or more persons bear joint and several liability according to law, the obligee has the right to demand that part or all of the jointly and severally liable persons bear the liability. The shares of liability of the jointly and severally liable persons shall be determined according to the extent of each person's liability; if it is difficult to determine the extent of liability, they shall bear the liability in equal shares. A jointly and severally liable person who actually bears liability exceeding its own share has the right to seek contribution from the other jointly and severally liable persons. Joint and several liability shall be provided for by law or agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-179"></a>第一百七十九条　承担民事责任的方式主要有：  
（一）停止侵害；  
（二）排除妨碍；  
（三）消除危险；  
（四）返还财产；  
（五）恢复原状；  
（六）修理、重作、更换；  
（七）继续履行；  
（八）赔偿损失；  
（九）支付违约金；  
（十）消除影响、恢复名誉；  
（十一）赔礼道歉。  
法律规定惩罚性赔偿的，依照其规定。  
本条规定的承担民事责任的方式，可以单独适用，也可以合并适用。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理侵害植物新品种权纠纷案件具体应用法律问题的若干规定.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理侵害植物新品种权纠纷案件具体应用法律问题的若干规定》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 179** The main methods of bearing civil liability include:  
(1) cessation of the infringement;  
(2) removal of the obstruction;  
(3) elimination of the danger;  
(4) return of property;  
(5) restoration to the original state;  
(6) repair, redoing, or replacement;  
(7) continued performance;  
(8) compensation for losses;  
(9) payment of liquidated damages;  
(10) elimination of adverse effects and restoration of reputation; and (11) extension of an apology. Where the law provides for punitive damages, such provisions shall apply. The methods of bearing civil liability provided for in this Article may be applied separately or in combination.

<a id="art-180"></a>第一百八十条　因不可抗力不能履行民事义务的，不承担民事责任。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。  
不可抗力是不能预见、不能避免且不能克服的客观情况。

**Article 180** Where a civil obligation cannot be performed due to force majeure, no civil liability shall be borne, unless otherwise provided by law.  
Force majeure means an objective circumstance that is unforeseeable, unavoidable, and insurmountable.

<a id="art-181"></a>第一百八十一条　因正当防卫造成损害的，不承担民事责任。  
正当防卫超过必要的限度，造成不应有的损害的，正当防卫人应当承担适当的民事责任。

**Article 181** If damage is caused by a justifiable defense, the person acting in such defense shall not bear civil liability.  
If the justifiable defense exceeds the necessary limit and causes undue damage, the person acting in such defense shall bear appropriate civil liability.

<a id="art-182"></a>第一百八十二条　因紧急避险造成损害的，由引起险情发生的人承担民事责任。  
危险由自然原因引起的，紧急避险人不承担民事责任，可以给予适当补偿。  
紧急避险采取措施不当或者超过必要的限度，造成不应有的损害的，紧急避险人应当承担适当的民事责任。

**Article 182** If damage is caused by an act of rescue, civil liability shall be borne by the person who gave rise to the peril.  
If the peril arises from natural causes, the person performing the act of rescue shall not bear civil liability, but may provide appropriate compensation.  
If the measures taken in the act of rescue are improper or exceed the necessary limit and cause undue damage, the person performing the act of rescue shall bear appropriate civil liability.

<a id="art-183"></a>第一百八十三条　因保护他人民事权益使自己受到损害的，由侵权人承担民事责任，受益人可以给予适当补偿。没有侵权人、侵权人逃逸或者无力承担民事责任，受害人请求补偿的，受益人应当给予适当补偿。

**Article 183** If a person suffers damage while protecting the civil rights and interests of another, civil liability shall be borne by the infringer, and the beneficiary may provide appropriate compensation. If there is no infringer, the infringer has fled, or the infringer is unable to bear civil liability, the beneficiary shall provide appropriate compensation if the victim requests compensation.

<a id="art-184"></a>第一百八十四条　因自愿实施紧急救助行为造成受助人损害的，救助人不承担民事责任。

**Article 184** If a person voluntarily renders emergency aid and causes damage to the aided person, the rescuer shall not bear civil liability.

<a id="art-185"></a>第一百八十五条　侵害英雄烈士等的姓名、肖像、名誉、荣誉，损害社会公共利益的，应当承担民事责任。

**Article 185** A person who infringes upon the name, likeness, reputation, or honor of heroes and martyrs, thereby harming the public interest, shall bear civil liability.

<a id="art-186"></a>第一百八十六条　因当事人一方的违约行为，损害对方人身权益、财产权益的，受损害方有权选择请求其承担违约责任或者侵权责任。

**Article 186** If a party's breach of contract damages the personal or property rights and interests of the other party, the damaged party has the right to choose to request the breaching party to bear liability for breach of contract or liability for tort.

<a id="art-187"></a>第一百八十七条　民事主体因同一行为应当承担民事责任、行政责任和刑事责任的，承担行政责任或者刑事责任不影响承担民事责任；民事主体的财产不足以支付的，优先用于承担民事责任。

**Article 187** If a civil subject shall bear civil liability, administrative liability, and criminal liability for the same act, the assumption of administrative liability or criminal liability shall not affect the assumption of civil liability; if the property of the civil subject is insufficient to pay all obligations, it shall first be used for assuming civil liability.

### 第九章　诉讼时效

<a id="art-188"></a>第一百八十八条　向人民法院请求保护民事权利的诉讼时效期间为三年。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。  
诉讼时效期间自权利人知道或者应当知道权利受到损害以及义务人之日起计算。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。但是，自权利受到损害之日起超过二十年的，人民法院不予保护，有特殊情况的，人民法院可以根据权利人的申请决定延长。

**Article 188** The limitation of action for requesting the People's Court to protect civil rights shall be three years. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail. The limitation of action shall commence on the date when the right holder knows or should have known that the right has been damaged and who the obligor is. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail. However, if more than twenty years have elapsed since the right was damaged, the People's Court shall not grant protection; in special circumstances, the People's Court may decide to extend the period upon the application of the right holder.

<a id="art-189"></a>第一百八十九条　当事人约定同一债务分期履行的，诉讼时效期间自最后一期履行期限届满之日起计算。

**Article 189** If the parties agree that the same debt shall be performed in installments, the limitation of action shall commence on the date on which the performance period for the last installment expires.

<a id="art-190"></a>第一百九十条　无民事行为能力人或者限制民事行为能力人对其法定代理人的请求权的诉讼时效期间，自该法定代理终止之日起计算。

**Article 190** The limitation of action for a claim of a person with no capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct against his or her statutory agent shall commence on the date on which such statutory agency terminates.

<a id="art-191"></a>第一百九十一条　未成年人遭受性侵害的损害赔偿请求权的诉讼时效期间，自受害人年满十八周岁之日起计算。

**Article 191** The limitation of action for a minor's claim for damages arising from sexual assault shall commence on the date on which the victim reaches the age of eighteen.

<a id="art-192"></a>第一百九十二条　诉讼时效期间届满的，义务人可以提出不履行义务的抗辩。  
诉讼时效期间届满后，义务人同意履行的，不得以诉讼时效期间届满为由抗辩；义务人已经自愿履行的，不得请求返还。

**Article 192** If the limitation of action has expired, the obligor may raise a defense of non-performance.  
After the limitation of action has expired, if the obligor agrees to perform, the obligor may not use the expiration of the limitation of action as a defense; if the obligor has already voluntarily performed, the obligor may not request the return of the performance.

<a id="art-193"></a>第一百九十三条　人民法院不得主动适用诉讼时效的规定。

**Article 193** The People's Court shall not apply the provisions on limitation of action ex officio.

<a id="art-194"></a>第一百九十四条　在诉讼时效期间的最后六个月内，因下列障碍，不能行使请求权的，诉讼时效中止：  
（一）不可抗力；  
（二）无民事行为能力人或者限制民事行为能力人没有法定代理人，或者法定代理人死亡、丧失民事行为能力、丧失代理权；  
（三）继承开始后未确定继承人或者遗产管理人；  
（四）权利人被义务人或者其他人控制；  
（五）其他导致权利人不能行使请求权的障碍。  
自中止时效的原因消除之日起满六个月，诉讼时效期间届满。

**Article 194** If, within the last six months of the limitation of action, a right holder is unable to exercise the right of claim due to the following obstacles, the limitation of action shall be suspended:  
(1) force majeure;  
(2) a person with no capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct has no statutory agent, or the statutory agent has died, lost capacity for civil conduct, or lost the power of agency;  
(3) no heir or estate administrator has been determined after the commencement of succession;  
(4) the right holder is controlled by the obligor or another person;  
(5) other obstacles that prevent the right holder from exercising the right of claim. The limitation of action shall expire six months after the date on which the cause of suspension is eliminated.

<a id="art-195"></a>第一百九十五条　有下列情形之一的，诉讼时效中断，从中断、有关程序终结时起，诉讼时效期间重新计算：  
（一）权利人向义务人提出履行请求；  
（二）义务人同意履行义务；  
（三）权利人提起诉讼或者申请仲裁；  
（四）与提起诉讼或者申请仲裁具有同等效力的其他情形。

**Article 195** The limitation of action shall be interrupted in any of the following circumstances, and the limitation period shall recommence from the time of the interruption or the conclusion of the relevant proceedings:  
(1) the right holder requests the obligor to perform;  
(2) the obligor agrees to perform the obligation;  
(3) the right holder files a lawsuit or applies for arbitration  
(4) other circumstances having equivalent effect to filing a lawsuit or applying for arbitration.

<a id="art-196"></a>第一百九十六条　下列请求权不适用诉讼时效的规定：  
（一）请求停止侵害、排除妨碍、消除危险；  
（二）不动产物权和登记的动产物权的权利人请求返还财产；  
（三）请求支付抚养费、赡养费或者扶养费；  
（四）依法不适用诉讼时效的其他请求权。

**Article 196** The following claims shall not be subject to the provisions on limitation of action:  
(1) claims for cessation of infringement, removal of obstruction, or elimination of danger;  
(2) claims by the holder of a real right in immovable property or a registered real right in movable property for the return of property;  
(3) claims for payment of child support, alimony, or maintenance; and (4) other claims that are not subject to the limitation of action according to law.

<a id="art-197"></a>第一百九十七条　诉讼时效的期间、计算方法以及中止、中断的事由由法律规定，当事人约定无效。  
当事人对诉讼时效利益的预先放弃无效。

**Article 197** The period of limitation of action, the method of calculation, and the grounds for suspension and interruption shall be provided by law; any agreement by the parties to the contrary shall be void.  
Any advance waiver by the parties of the benefits of the limitation of action shall be void.

<a id="art-198"></a>第一百九十八条　法律对仲裁时效有规定的，依照其规定；没有规定的，适用诉讼时效的规定。

**Article 198** Where the law provides for a limitation period for arbitration, such provisions shall prevail; if no such provision exists, the provisions on limitation of action shall apply.

<a id="art-199"></a>第一百九十九条　法律规定或者当事人约定的撤销权、解除权等权利的存续期间，除法律另有规定外，自权利人知道或者应当知道权利产生之日起计算，不适用有关诉讼时效中止、中断和延长的规定。存续期间届满，撤销权、解除权等权利消灭。

**Article 199** The period of existence of a right of avoidance, right of dissolution, or other such rights provided by law or agreed by the parties shall, unless the law provides otherwise, commence on the date when the right holder knows or should have known that the right has arisen, and the provisions on suspension, interruption, and extension of the limitation of action shall not apply to it. Upon the expiration of the period of existence, the right of avoidance, right of dissolution, or other such rights shall be extinguished.

### 第十章　期间计算

<a id="art-200"></a>第二百条　民法所称的期间按照公历年、月、日、小时计算。

**Article 200** Periods referred to in the Civil Code shall be calculated in accordance with the Gregorian calendar in years, months, days, and hours.

<a id="art-201"></a>第二百零一条　按照年、月、日计算期间的，开始的当日不计入，自下一日开始计算。  
按照小时计算期间的，自法律规定或者当事人约定的时间开始计算。

**Article 201** Where a time period is calculated by year, month, or day, the first day shall not be counted, and the calculation shall start from the next day.  
Where a time period is calculated by hour, the calculation shall start from the time specified by law or agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-202"></a>第二百零二条　按照年、月计算期间的，到期月的对应日为期间的最后一日；没有对应日的，月末日为期间的最后一日。

**Article 202** Where a time period is calculated by year or month, the corresponding day of the month of expiration shall be the last day of the time period; if there is no such corresponding day, the last day of that month shall be the last day of the time period.

<a id="art-203"></a>第二百零三条　期间的最后一日是法定休假日的，以法定休假日结束的次日为期间的最后一日。  
期间的最后一日的截止时间为二十四时；有业务时间的，停止业务活动的时间为截止时间。

**Article 203** If the last day of a time period falls on a legal holiday, the day after the end of such legal holiday shall be the last day of the time period.  
The deadline for the last day of a time period shall be twenty-four hours; if there is business operating time, the time at which business activities cease shall be the deadline.

<a id="art-204"></a>第二百零四条　期间的计算方法依照本法的规定，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 204** The method for calculating time periods shall be subject to the provisions of this Law, unless otherwise provided by law or otherwise agreed by the parties.

## 第二编　物权

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-205"></a>第二百零五条　本编调整因物的归属和利用产生的民事关系。

**Article 205** This Book regulates the civil relations arising from the attribution and use of things.

<a id="art-206"></a>第二百零六条　国家坚持和完善公有制为主体、多种所有制经济共同发展，按劳分配为主体、多种分配方式并存，社会主义市场经济体制等社会主义基本经济制度。  
国家巩固和发展公有制经济，鼓励、支持和引导非公有制经济的发展。  
国家实行社会主义市场经济，保障一切市场主体的平等法律地位和发展权利。

**Article 206** The State upholds and improves the basic socialist economic systems, including the system under which public ownership is the mainstay and multiple forms of ownership develop together, the system under which distribution according to work is the mainstay and multiple modes of distribution coexist, and the socialist market economy system.  
The State consolidates and develops the public sector of the economy, and encourages, supports, and guides the development of the non-public sector of the economy.  
The State practices a socialist market economy and guarantees the equal legal status and development rights of all market entities.

<a id="art-207"></a>第二百零七条　国家、集体、私人的物权和其他权利人的物权受法律平等保护，任何组织或者个人不得侵犯。

**Article 207** The property rights of the State, collectives, and private persons, as well as those of other right holders, shall be equally protected by law, and no organization or individual shall infringe upon them.

<a id="art-208"></a>第二百零八条　不动产物权的设立、变更、转让和消灭，应当依照法律规定登记。动产物权的设立和转让，应当依照法律规定交付。

**Article 208** The creation, alteration, transfer, and extinguishment of property rights in immovable property shall be registered in accordance with the provisions of law. The creation and transfer of property rights in movable property shall be delivered in accordance with the provisions of law.

### 第二章　物权的设立、变更、转让和消灭

#### 第一节　不动产登记

<a id="art-209"></a>第二百零九条　不动产物权的设立、变更、转让和消灭，经依法登记，发生效力；未经登记，不发生效力，但是法律另有规定的除外。  
依法属于国家所有的自然资源，所有权可以不登记。

**Article 209** The creation, alteration, transfer, and extinguishment of property rights in immovable property shall take effect upon registration in accordance with law; without registration, they shall not take effect, unless otherwise provided by law.  
The ownership of natural resources that are owned by the State according to law may be exempted from registration.

<a id="art-210"></a>第二百一十条　不动产登记，由不动产所在地的登记机构办理。  
国家对不动产实行统一登记制度。统一登记的范围、登记机构和登记办法，由法律、行政法规规定。

**Article 210** Registration of immovable property shall be handled by the registration authority at the place where the immovable property is located. The State practices a unified registration system for immovable property. The scope, registration authorities, and registration methods for such unified registration shall be provided by laws and administrative regulations.

<a id="art-211"></a>第二百一十一条　当事人申请登记，应当根据不同登记事项提供权属证明和不动产界址、面积等必要材料。

**Article 211** A party applying for registration shall, depending on the different registration matters, provide certificates of title and necessary materials such as the boundaries and area of the immovable property.

<a id="art-212"></a>第二百一十二条　登记机构应当履行下列职责：  
（一）查验申请人提供的权属证明和其他必要材料；  
（二）就有关登记事项询问申请人；  
（三）如实、及时登记有关事项；  
（四）法律、行政法规规定的其他职责。  
申请登记的不动产的有关情况需要进一步证明的，登记机构可以要求申请人补充材料，必要时可以实地查看。

**Article 212** The registration authority shall perform the following duties:  
(1) inspecting the certificates of title and other necessary materials provided by the applicant;  
(2) inquiring with the applicant about the relevant registration matters;  
(3) accurately and promptly registering the relevant matters; and (4) other duties provided by laws and administrative regulations. If the relevant circumstances of the immovable property for which registration is applied require further verification, the registration authority may request the applicant to supplement materials, and may conduct an on-site inspection when necessary.

<a id="art-213"></a>第二百一十三条　登记机构不得有下列行为：  
（一）要求对不动产进行评估；  
（二）以年检等名义进行重复登记；  
（三）超出登记职责范围的其他行为。

**Article 213** The registration authority shall not engage in the following acts:  
(1) requiring an appraisal of the immovable property;  
(2) conducting duplicate registration under the guise of annual inspection or the like  
(3) other acts exceeding the scope of its registration duties.

<a id="art-214"></a>第二百一十四条　不动产物权的设立、变更、转让和消灭，依照法律规定应当登记的，自记载于不动产登记簿时发生效力。

**Article 214** The creation, alteration, transfer, and extinguishment of a property right in immovable property, which shall be registered according to the provisions of law, shall take effect upon being entered in the immovable property register.

<a id="art-215"></a>第二百一十五条　当事人之间订立有关设立、变更、转让和消灭不动产物权的合同，除法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定外，自合同成立时生效；未办理物权登记的，不影响合同效力。

**Article 215** A contract concluded between parties concerning the creation, alteration, transfer, or extinguishment of a property right in immovable property shall take effect upon its formation, unless otherwise provided by law or otherwise agreed by the parties; failure to complete the registration of the property right shall not affect the validity of the contract.

<a id="art-216"></a>第二百一十六条　不动产登记簿是物权归属和内容的根据。  
不动产登记簿由登记机构管理。

**Article 216** The immovable property register shall be the basis for determining the attribution and content of property rights.  
The immovable property register shall be managed by the registration authority.

<a id="art-217"></a>第二百一十七条　不动产权属证书是权利人享有该不动产物权的证明。不动产权属证书记载的事项，应当与不动产登记簿一致；记载不一致的，除有证据证明不动产登记簿确有错误外，以不动产登记簿为准。

**Article 217** The certificate of title to immovable property shall be the proof that the right holder enjoys the property right in such immovable property. The matters recorded in the certificate of title to immovable property shall be consistent with the immovable property register; in case of any inconsistency, the immovable property register shall prevail, unless there is evidence proving that the immovable property register is indeed erroneous.

<a id="art-218"></a>第二百一十八条　权利人、利害关系人可以申请查询、复制不动产登记资料，登记机构应当提供。

**Article 218** The right holder or any interested party may apply to inspect or reproduce the immovable property registration materials, and the registration authority shall provide them.

<a id="art-219"></a>第二百一十九条　利害关系人不得公开、非法使用权利人的不动产登记资料。

**Article 219** An interested party shall not disclose or unlawfully use the immovable property registration materials of the right holder.

<a id="art-220"></a>第二百二十条　权利人、利害关系人认为不动产登记簿记载的事项错误的，可以申请更正登记。不动产登记簿记载的权利人书面同意更正或者有证据证明登记确有错误的，登记机构应当予以更正。  
不动产登记簿记载的权利人不同意更正的，利害关系人可以申请异议登记。登记机构予以异议登记，申请人自异议登记之日起十五日内不提起诉讼的，异议登记失效。异议登记不当，造成权利人损害的，权利人可以向申请人请求损害赔偿。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 220** If a right holder or an interested party believes that a matter recorded in the immovable property register is erroneous, that person may apply for a corrective registration. If the right holder recorded in the immovable property register consents in writing to the correction or if there is evidence proving that the registration is indeed erroneous, the registration authority shall make the correction. If the right holder recorded in the immovable property register does not consent to the correction, the interested party may apply for an objection registration. Where the registration authority makes the objection registration, the objection registration shall lapse if the applicant fails to bring a lawsuit within fifteen days from the date of the objection registration. If the objection registration is improper and causes damage to the right holder, the right holder may request the applicant to pay damages.

<a id="art-221"></a>第二百二十一条　当事人签订买卖房屋的协议或者签订其他不动产物权的协议，为保障将来实现物权，按照约定可以向登记机构申请预告登记。预告登记后，未经预告登记的权利人同意，处分该不动产的，不发生物权效力。  
预告登记后，债权消灭或者自能够进行不动产登记之日起九十日内未申请登记的，预告登记失效。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第5条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 221** Where the parties enter into an agreement for the sale of a house or any other agreement for a real right in immovable property, they may, in order to secure the realization of such real right in future, apply to the registration authority for advance notice registration in accordance with the agreement. After the advance notice registration is effected, any disposal of such immovable property without the consent of the right holder under the advance notice registration shall not have the effect of a real right. After the advance notice registration is effected, the advance notice registration shall cease to have effect if the obligatory claim is extinguished or if no application for registration of the immovable property is made within ninety days from the date on which such registration may be made.

<a id="art-222"></a>第二百二十二条　当事人提供虚假材料申请登记，造成他人损害的，应当承担赔偿责任。  
因登记错误，造成他人损害的，登记机构应当承担赔偿责任。登记机构赔偿后，可以向造成登记错误的人追偿。

**Article 222** Where a party applying for registration with false materials causes damage to another person, it shall bear compensation liability. Where damage is caused to another person due to a registration error, the registration authority shall bear compensation liability. The registration authority, after making compensation, shall have the right of recourse against the person who caused the registration error.

<a id="art-223"></a>第二百二十三条　不动产登记费按件收取，不得按照不动产的面积、体积或者价款的比例收取。

**Article 223** The fee for registration of immovable property shall be collected on a per-item basis and shall not be collected as a percentage of the area, volume, or price of the immovable property.

#### 第二节　动产交付

<a id="art-224"></a>第二百二十四条　动产物权的设立和转让，自交付时发生效力，但是法律另有规定的除外。

**Article 224** The creation and transfer of a real right in movable property shall take effect upon delivery, unless otherwise provided by law.

<a id="art-225"></a>第二百二十五条　船舶、航空器和机动车等的物权的设立、变更、转让和消灭，未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第19条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 225** The creation, modification, transfer, or extinction of a real right in a vessel, aircraft, motor vehicle, or the like, if not registered, may not be asserted against a bona fide third party.

<a id="art-226"></a>第二百二十六条　动产物权设立和转让前，权利人已经占有该动产的，物权自民事法律行为生效时发生效力。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第17条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 226** Where, before the creation or transfer of a real right in movable property, the right holder already possesses such movable property, the real right shall take effect at the time when the civil juristic act becomes effective.

<a id="art-227"></a>第二百二十七条　动产物权设立和转让前，第三人占有该动产的，负有交付义务的人可以通过转让请求第三人返还原物的权利代替交付。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第17条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 227** Where, before the creation or transfer of a real right in movable property, the movable property is possessed by a third party, the person obligated to deliver may, in lieu of delivery, transfer the right to claim the return of the original property from such third party.

<a id="art-228"></a>第二百二十八条　动产物权转让时，当事人又约定由出让人继续占有该动产的，物权自该约定生效时发生效力。

**Article 228** Where, upon the transfer of a real right in movable property, the parties further agree that the transferor shall continue to possess the movable property, the real right shall take effect at the time when such agreement becomes effective.

#### 第三节　其他规定

<a id="art-229"></a>第二百二十九条　因人民法院、仲裁机构的法律文书或者人民政府的征收决定等，导致物权设立、变更、转让或者消灭的，自法律文书或者征收决定等生效时发生效力。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第8条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 229** Where a real right is created, modified, transferred, or extinguished by reason of a legal document of a people's court or an arbitration authority, or an expropriation decision of the people's government, such real right shall take effect at the time when such legal document or expropriation decision becomes effective.

<a id="art-230"></a>第二百三十条　因继承取得物权的，自继承开始时发生效力。

**Article 230** Where a real right is acquired by succession, it shall take effect at the commencement of the succession.

<a id="art-231"></a>第二百三十一条　因合法建造、拆除房屋等事实行为设立或者消灭物权的，自事实行为成就时发生效力。

**Article 231** Where a real right is created or extinguished by a factual act such as lawful construction or demolition of a house, it shall take effect at the time when the factual act is accomplished.

<a id="art-232"></a>第二百三十二条　处分依照本节规定享有的不动产物权，依照法律规定需要办理登记的，未经登记，不发生物权效力。

**Article 232** Where a real right in immovable property enjoyed in accordance with the provisions of this Section is disposed of and such disposition requires registration according to law, the disposition shall not have the effect of a real right without registration.

### 第三章　物权的保护

<a id="art-233"></a>第二百三十三条　物权受到侵害的，权利人可以通过和解、调解、仲裁、诉讼等途径解决。

**Article 233** Where a real right is infringed upon, the right holder may seek resolution by means such as compromise, mediation, arbitration, or litigation.

<a id="art-234"></a>第二百三十四条　因物权的归属、内容发生争议的，利害关系人可以请求确认权利。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于审理执行异议之诉案件适用法律问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理执行异议之诉案件适用法律问题的解释》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 234** Where a dispute arises over the attribution or content of a real right, an interested person may request a confirmation of the right.

<a id="art-235"></a>第二百三十五条　无权占有不动产或者动产的，权利人可以请求返还原物。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第8条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 235** Where a person is in unauthorized possession of immovable or movable property, the right holder may demand the return of the original property.

<a id="art-236"></a>第二百三十六条　妨害物权或者可能妨害物权的，权利人可以请求排除妨害或者消除危险。

**Article 236** Where there is a nuisance to, or a possibility of nuisance to, a real right, the right holder may request the removal of the nuisance or the elimination of the danger.

<a id="art-237"></a>第二百三十七条　造成不动产或者动产毁损的，权利人可以依法请求修理、重作、更换或者恢复原状。

**Article 237** Where immovable or movable property is damaged, the right holder may, in accordance with law, request repair, re-making, replacement, or restoration to its original condition.

<a id="art-238"></a>第二百三十八条　侵害物权，造成权利人损害的，权利人可以依法请求损害赔偿，也可以依法请求承担其他民事责任。

**Article 238** Where a real right is infringed upon, causing damage to the right holder, the right holder may, in accordance with law, request compensation for damages or demand that the infringer bear other civil liability.

<a id="art-239"></a>第二百三十九条　本章规定的物权保护方式，可以单独适用，也可以根据权利被侵害的情形合并适用。  
第二分编　所有权

**Article 239** The means for protection of real rights provided in this Chapter may be applied separately or, depending on the circumstances of the infringement, cumulatively.  
Part Two Ownership

### 第四章　一般规定

<a id="art-240"></a>第二百四十条　所有权人对自己的不动产或者动产，依法享有占有、使用、收益和处分的权利。

**Article 240** An owner shall, in accordance with law, enjoy the right to possess, use, benefit from, and dispose of its own immovable or movable property.

<a id="art-241"></a>第二百四十一条　所有权人有权在自己的不动产或者动产上设立用益物权和担保物权。用益物权人、担保物权人行使权利，不得损害所有权人的权益。

**Article 241** The owner has the right to establish a usufruct or a security interest on its own immovable or movable property. When exercising their rights, the usufructuary or the holder of a security interest shall not harm the rights and interests of the owner.

<a id="art-242"></a>第二百四十二条　法律规定专属于国家所有的不动产和动产，任何组织或者个人不能取得所有权。

**Article 242** No organization or individual may acquire ownership of immovable or movable property that, according to law, is exclusively owned by the State.

<a id="art-243"></a>第二百四十三条　为了公共利益的需要，依照法律规定的权限和程序可以征收集体所有的土地和组织、个人的房屋以及其他不动产。  
征收集体所有的土地，应当依法及时足额支付土地补偿费、安置补助费以及农村村民住宅、其他地上附着物和青苗等的补偿费用，并安排被征地农民的社会保障费用，保障被征地农民的生活，维护被征地农民的合法权益。  
征收组织、个人的房屋以及其他不动产，应当依法给予征收补偿，维护被征收人的合法权益；征收个人住宅的，还应当保障被征收人的居住条件。  
任何组织或者个人不得贪污、挪用、私分、截留、拖欠征收补偿费等费用。

**Article 243** For the purpose of public interest, collectively-owned land and the houses and other immovable property owned by organizations or individuals may be expropriated in accordance with the scope of authority and procedures as provided by law.  
Where collectively-owned land is expropriated, land compensation fees, resettlement subsidies, and compensation for rural villagers' houses, other attachments on the land, and immature crops shall be paid in full and in a timely manner according to law, and social security expenses for the land-expropriated farmers shall be arranged, so as to ensure their livelihood and protect their lawful rights and interests.  
Where the houses or other immovable property of an organization or individual is expropriated, expropriation compensation shall be given according to law to protect the lawful rights and interests of the expropriated person; where a private residence is expropriated, the housing conditions of the expropriated person shall also be guaranteed.  
No organization or individual may embezzle, misappropriate, privately divide, withhold, or delay the payment of expropriation compensation fees or other such expenses.

<a id="art-244"></a>第二百四十四条　国家对耕地实行特殊保护，严格限制农用地转为建设用地，控制建设用地总量。不得违反法律规定的权限和程序征收集体所有的土地。

**Article 244** The State applies special protection to arable land, strictly restricts the conversion of agricultural land into construction land, and controls the total volume of construction land. Collectively-owned land shall not be expropriated in violation of the scope of authority and procedures as provided by law.

<a id="art-245"></a>第二百四十五条　因抢险救灾、疫情防控等紧急需要，依照法律规定的权限和程序可以征用组织、个人的不动产或者动产。被征用的不动产或者动产使用后，应当返还被征用人。组织、个人的不动产或者动产被征用或者征用后毁损、灭失的，应当给予补偿。

**Article 245** For emergency needs such as emergency rescue and disaster relief, or epidemic prevention and control, an organization's or individual's immovable or movable property may be requisitioned in accordance with the scope of authority and procedures as provided by law. After the requisitioned immovable or movable property is used, it shall be returned to the person from whom it was requisitioned. If an organization's or individual's immovable or movable property is requisitioned or is damaged or lost after requisition, compensation shall be given.

### 第五章　国家所有权和集体所有权、私人所有权

<a id="art-246"></a>第二百四十六条　法律规定属于国家所有的财产，属于国家所有即全民所有。  
国有财产由国务院代表国家行使所有权。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 246** Property that, according to law, is owned by the State is owned by the State, that is, by the whole people. The State Council shall exercise ownership of State-owned property on behalf of the State. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-247"></a>第二百四十七条　矿藏、水流、海域属于国家所有。

**Article 247** Mineral deposits, water flows, and sea areas are owned by the State.

<a id="art-248"></a>第二百四十八条　无居民海岛属于国家所有，国务院代表国家行使无居民海岛所有权。

**Article 248** Uninhabited sea islands are owned by the State, and the State Council shall exercise ownership of uninhabited sea islands on behalf of the State.

<a id="art-249"></a>第二百四十九条　城市的土地，属于国家所有。法律规定属于国家所有的农村和城市郊区的土地，属于国家所有。

**Article 249** Land in cities is owned by the State. Land in rural areas and suburban areas that, according to law, is owned by the State is owned by the State.

<a id="art-250"></a>第二百五十条　森林、山岭、草原、荒地、滩涂等自然资源，属于国家所有，但是法律规定属于集体所有的除外。

**Article 250** Natural resources such as forests, mountains, grasslands, wasteland, and tidal flats are owned by the State, except where the law provides that they are owned by the collectives.

<a id="art-251"></a>第二百五十一条　法律规定属于国家所有的野生动植物资源，属于国家所有。

**Article 251** Wildlife resources that, according to law, are owned by the State are owned by the State.

<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条　无线电频谱资源属于国家所有。

**Article 252** Radio spectrum resources are owned by the State.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条　法律规定属于国家所有的文物，属于国家所有。

**Article 253** Cultural relics that, according to law, are owned by the State are owned by the State.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条　国防资产属于国家所有。  
铁路、公路、电力设施、电信设施和油气管道等基础设施，依照法律规定为国家所有的，属于国家所有。

**Article 254** National defense assets are owned by the State.  
Infrastructure such as railways, highways, electric power facilities, telecommunications facilities, and oil and gas pipelines that, pursuant to law, are owned by the State shall be owned by the State.

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条　国家机关对其直接支配的不动产和动产，享有占有、使用以及依照法律和国务院的有关规定处分的权利。

**Article 255** State organs shall have the right to possess, use, and dispose of the immovable and movable property directly under their control in accordance with the law and the relevant regulations of the State Council.

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条　国家举办的事业单位对其直接支配的不动产和动产，享有占有、使用以及依照法律和国务院的有关规定收益、处分的权利。

**Article 256** Public institutions established by the State shall have the right to possess, use, and obtain benefits from and dispose of the immovable and movable property directly under their control in accordance with the law and the relevant regulations of the State Council.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条　国家出资的企业，由国务院、地方人民政府依照法律、行政法规规定分别代表国家履行出资人职责，享有出资人权益。

**Article 257** For enterprises in which the State has made capital contributions, the State Council and local people's governments shall, in accordance with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations, respectively perform the duties of contributors on behalf of the State and enjoy the rights and interests of contributors.

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条　国家所有的财产受法律保护，禁止任何组织或者个人侵占、哄抢、私分、截留、破坏。

**Article 258** State-owned property is protected by law, and no organization or individual may appropriate, plunder, privately divide, withhold, or damage it.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条　履行国有财产管理、监督职责的机构及其工作人员，应当依法加强对国有财产的管理、监督，促进国有财产保值增值，防止国有财产损失；滥用职权，玩忽职守，造成国有财产损失的，应当依法承担法律责任。  
违反国有财产管理规定，在企业改制、合并分立、关联交易等过程中，低价转让、合谋私分、擅自担保或者以其他方式造成国有财产损失的，应当依法承担法律责任。

**Article 259** The institutions and their staff members that perform the duties of managing and supervising State-owned property shall strengthen, according to law, the management and supervision of State-owned property, promote the preservation and appreciation of State-owned property, and prevent losses to State-owned property; where any person abuses power or neglects duty, causing losses to State-owned property, the person shall bear legal liability according to law.  
Where any person, in violation of the provisions on the administration of State-owned property, through measures such as transferring at a low price, conspiring to privately divide property, providing unauthorized guarantees, or other means during processes including enterprise restructuring, merger, division, or related-party transactions, causes losses to State-owned property, the person shall bear legal liability according to law.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条　集体所有的不动产和动产包括：  
（一）法律规定属于集体所有的土地和森林、山岭、草原、荒地、滩涂；  
（二）集体所有的建筑物、生产设施、农田水利设施；  
（三）集体所有的教育、科学、文化、卫生、体育等设施；  
（四）集体所有的其他不动产和动产。

**Article 260** Collectively-owned immovable and movable property includes:  
(1) land, forests, mountains, grasslands, wasteland, and tidal flats that, according to law, are owned by the collectives;  
(2) buildings, production facilities, and farmland water conservancy facilities owned by the collectives;  
(3) educational, scientific, cultural, health, and sports facilities owned by the collectives; and (4) other immovable and movable property owned by the collectives.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条　农民集体所有的不动产和动产，属于本集体成员集体所有。  
下列事项应当依照法定程序经本集体成员决定：  
（一）土地承包方案以及将土地发包给本集体以外的组织或者个人承包；  
（二）个别土地承包经营权人之间承包地的调整；  
（三）土地补偿费等费用的使用、分配办法；  
（四）集体出资的企业的所有权变动等事项；  
（五）法律规定的其他事项。

**Article 261** The immovables and movables owned by a farmers' collective shall be collectively owned by the members of the collective. The following matters shall be decided by the members of the collective in accordance with statutory procedures:  
(1) the plan for land contracting and the leasing of land to entities or individuals outside the collective for contracting;  
(2) the adjustment of the contracted land among individual holders of land contractual management rights;  
(3) the method for the use and distribution of costs such as land compensation fees;  
(4) matters such as the change of ownership of enterprises with collective investment; and (5) other matters provided for by law.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条　对于集体所有的土地和森林、山岭、草原、荒地、滩涂等，依照下列规定行使所有权：  
（一）属于村农民集体所有的，由村集体经济组织或者村民委员会依法代表集体行使所有权；  
（二）分别属于村内两个以上农民集体所有的，由村内各该集体经济组织或者村民小组依法代表集体行使所有权；  
（三）属于乡镇农民集体所有的，由乡镇集体经济组织代表集体行使所有权。

**Article 262** For land and forests, hills, grasslands, unreclaimed lands, tidelands, etc. owned by a collective, the ownership shall be exercised in accordance with the following provisions:  
(1) where they are owned by the village farmers' collective, the collective ownership shall be exercised on behalf of the collective by the village collective economic organization or the villagers' committee according to law;  
(2) where they are respectively owned by two or more farmers' collectives within the village, the collective ownership shall be exercised on behalf of each collective by the respective collective economic organization or the villagers' group within the village according to law; and (3) where they are owned by the township farmers' collective, the collective ownership shall be exercised on behalf of the collective by the township collective economic organization.

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条　城镇集体所有的不动产和动产，依照法律、行政法规的规定由本集体享有占有、使用、收益和处分的权利。

**Article 263** The immovables and movables owned by an urban collective shall be enjoyed by the collective with the rights to possess, use, benefit from, and dispose of them in accordance with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条　农村集体经济组织或者村民委员会、村民小组应当依照法律、行政法规以及章程、村规民约向本集体成员公布集体财产的状况。集体成员有权查阅、复制相关资料。

**Article 264** A rural collective economic organization, or a villagers' committee or villagers' group shall, in accordance with laws, administrative regulations, and its articles of association or village regulations and folk conventions, disclose the status of collective property to its members. The members of the collective shall have the right to consult and reproduce relevant materials.

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条　集体所有的财产受法律保护，禁止任何组织或者个人侵占、哄抢、私分、破坏。  
农村集体经济组织、村民委员会或者其负责人作出的决定侵害集体成员合法权益的，受侵害的集体成员可以请求人民法院予以撤销。

**Article 265** The property owned by a collective shall be protected by law, and no entity or individual may embezzle, loot, privately divide, or destroy it.  
Where a decision made by a rural collective economic organization, a villagers' committee, or the responsible person thereof infringes upon the lawful rights and interests of the members of the collective, the infringed members may request the People's Court to revoke the decision.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条　私人对其合法的收入、房屋、生活用品、生产工具、原材料等不动产和动产享有所有权。

**Article 266** A private individual shall enjoy the right of ownership over his or her lawful income, houses, articles of daily use, tools of production, raw materials, and other immovables and movables.

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条　私人的合法财产受法律保护，禁止任何组织或者个人侵占、哄抢、破坏。

**Article 267** The lawful property of a private individual shall be protected by law, and no entity or individual may embezzle, loot, or destroy it.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条　国家、集体和私人依法可以出资设立有限责任公司、股份有限公司或者其他企业。国家、集体和私人所有的不动产或者动产投到企业的，由出资人按照约定或者出资比例享有资产收益、重大决策以及选择经营管理者等权利并履行义务。

**Article 268** The State, a collective, and a private individual may, according to law, make capital contributions to establish a limited liability company, a company limited by shares, or another enterprise. Where immovables or movables owned by the State, a collective, or a private individual are invested in an enterprise, the investors shall, in accordance with the agreement or in proportion to their respective capital contributions, enjoy the rights to receive asset returns, make major decisions, and select business managers, among others, and shall perform the corresponding obligations.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条　营利法人对其不动产和动产依照法律、行政法规以及章程享有占有、使用、收益和处分的权利。  
营利法人以外的法人，对其不动产和动产的权利，适用有关法律、行政法规以及章程的规定。

**Article 269** A for-profit legal person shall, in accordance with laws, administrative regulations, and its articles of association, enjoy the rights to possess, use, benefit from, and dispose of its immovables and movables.  
For the rights of a legal person other than a for-profit legal person over its immovables and movables, the provisions of relevant laws, administrative regulations, and its articles of association shall apply.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条　社会团体法人、捐助法人依法所有的不动产和动产，受法律保护。

**Article 270** The immovables and movables lawfully owned by a social organization legal person or a donation legal person shall be protected by law.

### 第六章　业主的建筑物区分所有权

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条　业主对建筑物内的住宅、经营性用房等专有部分享有所有权，对专有部分以外的共有部分享有共有和共同管理的权利。

**Article 271** An owner shall have ownership over the exclusive parts of a building, such as residential and commercial units, and shall have the right of common ownership and joint management over the common parts other than the exclusive parts.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条　业主对其建筑物专有部分享有占有、使用、收益和处分的权利。业主行使权利不得危及建筑物的安全，不得损害其他业主的合法权益。

**Article 272** An owner shall have the right to possess, use, seek profits from, and dispose of the exclusive parts of the building. When exercising such rights, the owner shall not endanger the safety of the building or impair the lawful rights and interests of other owners.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条　业主对建筑物专有部分以外的共有部分，享有权利，承担义务；不得以放弃权利为由不履行义务。  
业主转让建筑物内的住宅、经营性用房，其对共有部分享有的共有和共同管理的权利一并转让。

**Article 273** An owner shall have rights and bear obligations in respect of the common parts of a building other than the exclusive parts; the owner shall not fail to perform obligations on the ground of waiving rights.  
Where an owner transfers the residential or commercial unit in the building, the right of common ownership and joint management over the common parts shall be transferred together.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条　建筑区划内的道路，属于业主共有，但是属于城镇公共道路的除外。建筑区划内的绿地，属于业主共有，但是属于城镇公共绿地或者明示属于个人的除外。建筑区划内的其他公共场所、公用设施和物业服务用房，属于业主共有。

**Article 274** Roads within the building zone shall be owned by the owners, except for those which are urban public roads. Green areas within the building zone shall be owned by the owners, except for those which are urban public green areas or expressly indicated as owned by an individual. Other public places, public facilities and premises for property management services within the building zone shall be owned by the owners.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条　建筑区划内，规划用于停放汽车的车位、车库的归属，由当事人通过出售、附赠或者出租等方式约定。  
占用业主共有的道路或者其他场地用于停放汽车的车位，属于业主共有。

**Article 275** The ownership of parking spaces and garages planned for parking motor vehicles within the building zone shall be agreed by the parties by means of sale, gift, or lease, among others.  
Parking spaces occupying the roads or other sites owned by the owners for parking motor vehicles shall be owned by the owners.

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条　建筑区划内，规划用于停放汽车的车位、车库应当首先满足业主的需要。

**Article 276** Parking spaces and garages planned for parking motor vehicles within the building zone shall first satisfy the needs of the owners.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条　业主可以设立业主大会，选举业主委员会。业主大会、业主委员会成立的具体条件和程序，依照法律、法规的规定。  
地方人民政府有关部门、居民委员会应当对设立业主大会和选举业主委员会给予指导和协助。

**Article 277** Owners may establish a owners' assembly and elect a owners' committee. The specific conditions and procedures for the formation of a owners' assembly and a owners' committee shall be governed by the provisions of laws and regulations. The relevant departments of the local people's government and the residents' committee shall provide guidance and assistance for the establishment of a owners' assembly and the election of a owners' committee.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条　下列事项由业主共同决定：  
（一）制定和修改业主大会议事规则；  
（二）制定和修改管理规约；  
（三）选举业主委员会或者更换业主委员会成员；  
（四）选聘和解聘物业服务企业或者其他管理人；  
（五）使用建筑物及其附属设施的维修资金；  
（六）筹集建筑物及其附属设施的维修资金；  
（七）改建、重建建筑物及其附属设施；  
（八）改变共有部分的用途或者利用共有部分从事经营活动；  
（九）有关共有和共同管理权利的其他重大事项。  
业主共同决定事项，应当由专有部分面积占比三分之二以上的业主且人数占比三分之二以上的业主参与表决。决定前款第六项至第八项规定的事项，应当经参与表决专有部分面积四分之三以上的业主且参与表决人数四分之三以上的业主同意。决定前款其他事项，应当经参与表决专有部分面积过半数的业主且参与表决人数过半数的业主同意。

**Article 278** The following matters shall be decided jointly by the owners:  
(1) formulating and amending the rules of procedure of the owners' assembly;  
(2) formulating and amending the management regulations;  
(3) electing or replacing members of the owners' committee;  
(4) selecting and dismissing a property service enterprise or other manager;  
(5) using the maintenance funds for the building and its ancillary facilities;  
(6) raising the maintenance funds for the building and its ancillary facilities;  
(7) altering or rebuilding the building and its ancillary facilities;  
(8) changing the use of the common parts or utilizing the common parts for business activities;  
(9) other significant matters concerning the right of common ownership and joint management. A matter to be decided jointly by the owners shall be subject to voting by owners whose exclusive parts account for not less than two-thirds of the total area and who account for not less than two-thirds of the total number of owners. Decisions on matters specified in items (6) through (8) of the preceding paragraph shall be approved by owners whose exclusive parts account for not less than three-fourths of the area of the participating voting owners and who account for not less than three-fourths of the number of the participating voting owners. Decisions on other matters specified in the preceding paragraph shall be approved by owners whose exclusive parts account for more than half of the area of the participating voting owners and who account for more than half of the number of the participating voting owners.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条　业主不得违反法律、法规以及管理规约，将住宅改变为经营性用房。业主将住宅改变为经营性用房的，除遵守法律、法规以及管理规约外，应当经有利害关系的业主一致同意。

**Article 279** An owner shall not, in violation of laws, regulations, and the management regulations, change a residential unit into a commercial unit. Where an owner changes a residential unit into a commercial unit, in addition to complying with laws, regulations, and the management regulations, the owner shall obtain the unanimous consent of the interested owners.

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条　业主大会或者业主委员会的决定，对业主具有法律约束力。  
业主大会或者业主委员会作出的决定侵害业主合法权益的，受侵害的业主可以请求人民法院予以撤销。

**Article 280** Decisions of the owners' assembly or the owners' committee shall be legally binding on the owners.  
Where a decision made by the owners' assembly or the owners' committee infringes upon the lawful rights and interests of an owner, the aggrieved owner may request the People's Court to revoke the decision.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条　建筑物及其附属设施的维修资金，属于业主共有。经业主共同决定，可以用于电梯、屋顶、外墙、无障碍设施等共有部分的维修、更新和改造。建筑物及其附属设施的维修资金的筹集、使用情况应当定期公布。  
紧急情况下需要维修建筑物及其附属设施的，业主大会或者业主委员会可以依法申请使用建筑物及其附属设施的维修资金。

**Article 281** The maintenance funds for a building and its ancillary facilities shall be jointly owned by the owners. Upon a joint decision of the owners, such funds may be used for the repair, renewal, and renovation of the common parts such as elevators, roofs, external walls, and barrier-free facilities. The raising and use of the maintenance funds for a building and its ancillary facilities shall be published periodically. Where a building and its ancillary facilities need to be repaired under emergency circumstances, the owners' assembly or the owners' committee may, according to law, apply for the use of the maintenance funds for the building and its ancillary facilities.

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条　建设单位、物业服务企业或者其他管理人等利用业主的共有部分产生的收入，在扣除合理成本之后，属于业主共有。

**Article 282** The income generated from the use of the common parts of the owners by the developer, the property service enterprise, or other managers, after deducting reasonable costs, shall be jointly owned by the owners.

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条　建筑物及其附属设施的费用分摊、收益分配等事项，有约定的，按照约定；没有约定或者约定不明确的，按照业主专有部分面积所占比例确定。

**Article 283** Matters such as the apportionment of expenses and the distribution of proceeds for a building and its ancillary facilities shall be handled in accordance with the agreement, if any; in the absence of an agreement or if the agreement is unclear, they shall be determined according to the proportion of the area of the owners' exclusive parts.

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条　业主可以自行管理建筑物及其附属设施，也可以委托物业服务企业或者其他管理人管理。  
对建设单位聘请的物业服务企业或者其他管理人，业主有权依法更换。

**Article 284** Owners may manage the building and its ancillary facilities by themselves, or may entrust a property service enterprise or other manager with the management thereof.  
With respect to the property service enterprise or other manager engaged by the developer, the owners shall have the right to replace it according to law.

<a id="art-285"></a>第二百八十五条　物业服务企业或者其他管理人根据业主的委托，依照本法第三编有关物业服务合同的规定管理建筑区划内的建筑物及其附属设施，接受业主的监督，并及时答复业主对物业服务情况提出的询问。  
物业服务企业或者其他管理人应当执行政府依法实施的应急处置措施和其他管理措施，积极配合开展相关工作。

**Article 285** A property service enterprise or other manager shall, based on the entrustment of the owners, manage the buildings and their ancillary facilities within the building zone in accordance with the provisions of Part Three of this Law concerning the property service contract, shall accept the supervision of the owners, and shall promptly reply to inquiries raised by the owners regarding the property service.  
A property service enterprise or other manager shall implement the emergency response measures and other management measures implemented by the government according to law, and shall actively cooperate in carrying out relevant work.

<a id="art-286"></a>第二百八十六条　业主应当遵守法律、法规以及管理规约，相关行为应当符合节约资源、保护生态环境的要求。对于物业服务企业或者其他管理人执行政府依法实施的应急处置措施和其他管理措施，业主应当依法予以配合。  
业主大会或者业主委员会，对任意弃置垃圾、排放污染物或者噪声、违反规定饲养动物、违章搭建、侵占通道、拒付物业费等损害他人合法权益的行为，有权依照法律、法规以及管理规约，请求行为人停止侵害、排除妨碍、消除危险、恢复原状、赔偿损失。  
业主或者其他行为人拒不履行相关义务的，有关当事人可以向有关行政主管部门报告或者投诉，有关行政主管部门应当依法处理。

**Article 286** Owners shall comply with laws, regulations, and the management covenant, and their relevant conduct shall meet the requirements of resource conservation and ecological environment protection. Owners shall, according to law, cooperate with the property service enterprise or other manager in implementing the emergency response measures and other management measures implemented by the government according to law. With respect to any act that harms the lawful rights and interests of others, such as arbitrarily discarding garbage, discharging pollutants or noise, raising animals in violation of regulations, constructing illegally, encroaching on passageways, or refusing to pay property management fees, the owners' assembly or the owners' committee shall have the right, in accordance with laws, regulations, and the management covenant, to request the actor to cease the infringement, remove the obstruction, eliminate the danger, restore the property to its original condition, or compensate for the losses. Where an owner or other actor refuses to perform the relevant obligations, the interested party may report or complain to the relevant administrative department, and the relevant administrative department shall handle the matter according to law.

<a id="art-287"></a>第二百八十七条　业主对建设单位、物业服务企业或者其他管理人以及其他业主侵害自己合法权益的行为，有权请求其承担民事责任。

**Article 287** Owners shall have the right to request the developer, the property service enterprise or other manager, or other owners that infringe upon their lawful rights and interests to bear civil liability.

### 第七章　相邻关系

<a id="art-288"></a>第二百八十八条　不动产的相邻权利人应当按照有利生产、方便生活、团结互助、公平合理的原则，正确处理相邻关系。

**Article 288** Neighboring right holders of immovable property shall correctly handle neighboring relations in accordance with the principles of facilitating production, making life convenient, promoting solidarity and mutual assistance, and ensuring fairness and reasonableness.

<a id="art-289"></a>第二百八十九条　法律、法规对处理相邻关系有规定的，依照其规定；法律、法规没有规定的，可以按照当地习惯。

**Article 289** Where there are provisions in laws and regulations on handling neighboring relations, such provisions shall be followed; where there are no such provisions in laws and regulations, local customs may be followed.

<a id="art-290"></a>第二百九十条　不动产权利人应当为相邻权利人用水、排水提供必要的便利。  
对自然流水的利用，应当在不动产的相邻权利人之间合理分配。对自然流水的排放，应当尊重自然流向。

**Article 290** A right holder of immovable property shall provide necessary convenience to neighboring right holders for using water and draining water. The use of natural flowing water shall be reasonably distributed among the neighboring right holders of immovable property. The drainage of natural flowing water shall respect the natural flow direction.

<a id="art-291"></a>第二百九十一条　不动产权利人对相邻权利人因通行等必须利用其土地的，应当提供必要的便利。

**Article 291** Where a neighboring right holder must use the land of a right holder of immovable property for passage or the like, the right holder of immovable property shall provide necessary convenience.

<a id="art-292"></a>第二百九十二条　不动产权利人因建造、修缮建筑物以及铺设电线、电缆、水管、暖气和燃气管线等必须利用相邻土地、建筑物的，该土地、建筑物的权利人应当提供必要的便利。

**Article 292** Where a right holder of immovable property must use neighboring land or buildings for purposes such as constructing or repairing a building, or laying wires, cables, water pipes, heating pipes, or gas pipes, the right holder of such land or buildings shall provide necessary convenience.

<a id="art-293"></a>第二百九十三条　建造建筑物，不得违反国家有关工程建设标准，不得妨碍相邻建筑物的通风、采光和日照。

**Article 293** The construction of a building shall not violate the relevant national engineering construction standards, and shall not obstruct the ventilation, lighting, or sunshine of neighboring buildings.

<a id="art-294"></a>第二百九十四条　不动产权利人不得违反国家规定弃置固体废物，排放大气污染物、水污染物、土壤污染物、噪声、光辐射、电磁辐射等有害物质。

**Article 294** A right holder of immovable property shall not, in violation of national regulations, discard solid waste or discharge harmful substances such as air pollutants, water pollutants, soil pollutants, noise, light radiation, or electromagnetic radiation.

<a id="art-295"></a>第二百九十五条　不动产权利人挖掘土地、建造建筑物、铺设管线以及安装设备等，不得危及相邻不动产的安全。

**Article 295** Where a right holder of immovable property excavates land, constructs a building, lays pipelines, or installs equipment, it shall not endanger the safety of the neighboring immovable property.

<a id="art-296"></a>第二百九十六条　不动产权利人因用水、排水、通行、铺设管线等利用相邻不动产的，应当尽量避免对相邻的不动产权利人造成损害。

**Article 296** Where a right holder of immovable property uses neighboring immovable property for purposes such as using water, draining water, passing, or laying pipelines, it shall, to the extent possible, avoid causing damage to the neighboring right holder of immovable property.

### 第八章　共有

<a id="art-297"></a>第二百九十七条　不动产或者动产可以由两个以上组织、个人共有。共有包括按份共有和共同共有。

**Article 297** Immovable or movable property may be jointly owned by two or more organizations or individuals. Joint ownership includes ownership in shares and joint ownership.

<a id="art-298"></a>第二百九十八条　按份共有人对共有的不动产或者动产按照其份额享有所有权。

**Article 298** A co-owner by shares shall enjoy ownership of the jointly owned immovable or movable property according to its share.

<a id="art-299"></a>第二百九十九条　共同共有人对共有的不动产或者动产共同享有所有权。

**Article 299** A joint co-owner shall jointly enjoy ownership of the jointly owned immovable or movable property.

<a id="art-300"></a>第三百条　共有人按照约定管理共有的不动产或者动产；没有约定或者约定不明确的，各共有人都有管理的权利和义务。

**Article 300** Co-owners shall manage the jointly owned immovable or movable property in accordance with the agreement; in the absence of an agreement or if the agreement is unclear, each co-owner shall have the right and obligation to manage it.

<a id="art-301"></a>第三百零一条　处分共有的不动产或者动产以及对共有的不动产或者动产作重大修缮、变更性质或者用途的，应当经占份额三分之二以上的按份共有人或者全体共同共有人同意，但是共有人之间另有约定的除外。

**Article 301** For the disposition of a jointly owned immovable or movable property, or for major repairs, change of nature or use of such property, the consent of the co-owners by shares holding two-thirds or more of the shares or all the joint co-owners shall be obtained, unless otherwise agreed upon among the co-owners.

<a id="art-302"></a>第三百零二条　共有人对共有物的管理费用以及其他负担，有约定的，按照其约定；没有约定或者约定不明确的，按份共有人按照其份额负担，共同共有人共同负担。

**Article 302** Where the co-owners have agreed on the management expenses and other burdens of the jointly owned property, such agreement shall prevail; where there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, the co-owners by shares shall bear the burdens in proportion to their shares, and the joint co-owners shall bear the burdens jointly.

<a id="art-303"></a>第三百零三条　共有人约定不得分割共有的不动产或者动产，以维持共有关系的，应当按照约定，但是共有人有重大理由需要分割的，可以请求分割；没有约定或者约定不明确的，按份共有人可以随时请求分割，共同共有人在共有的基础丧失或者有重大理由需要分割时可以请求分割。因分割造成其他共有人损害的，应当给予赔偿。

**Article 303** Where the co-owners have agreed not to partition the jointly owned immovable or movable property in order to maintain the co-ownership relationship, such agreement shall be followed; however, if a co-owner has a material reason requiring partition, he may request partition. Where there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, a co-owner by shares may request partition at any time, and a joint co-owner may request partition when the basis of joint ownership is lost or there is a material reason requiring partition. If the partition causes damage to other co-owners, compensation shall be made.

<a id="art-304"></a>第三百零四条　共有人可以协商确定分割方式。达不成协议，共有的不动产或者动产可以分割且不会因分割减损价值的，应当对实物予以分割；难以分割或者因分割会减损价值的，应当对折价或者拍卖、变卖取得的价款予以分割。  
共有人分割所得的不动产或者动产有瑕疵的，其他共有人应当分担损失。

**Article 304** The co-owners may determine the method of partition through consultation. If no agreement is reached, where the jointly owned immovable or movable property is partitionable and its value shall not be diminished by partition, the property in kind shall be partitioned; where partition is difficult or the value shall be diminished by partition, the proceeds obtained from the appraised value, auction or sale shall be partitioned. If the immovable or movable property obtained by a co-owner through partition is defective, the other co-owners shall share the losses.

<a id="art-305"></a>第三百零五条　按份共有人可以转让其享有的共有的不动产或者动产份额。其他共有人在同等条件下享有优先购买的权利。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第10条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-13" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第13条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 305** A co-owner by shares may transfer his share in the jointly owned immovable or movable property. The other co-owners shall have a preemptive right to purchase under equal conditions.

<a id="art-306"></a>第三百零六条　按份共有人转让其享有的共有的不动产或者动产份额的，应当将转让条件及时通知其他共有人。其他共有人应当在合理期限内行使优先购买权。  
两个以上其他共有人主张行使优先购买权的，协商确定各自的购买比例；协商不成的，按照转让时各自的共有份额比例行使优先购买权。

**Article 306** Where a co-owner by shares transfers his share in the jointly owned immovable or movable property, he shall promptly notify the other co-owners of the terms of transfer. The other co-owners shall exercise their preemptive right to purchase within a reasonable period. If two or more other co-owners claim to exercise the preemptive right to purchase, they shall determine their respective purchase proportions through consultation; if consultation fails, they shall exercise the preemptive right to purchase in proportion to their respective shares in the co-ownership at the time of transfer.

<a id="art-307"></a>第三百零七条　因共有的不动产或者动产产生的债权债务，在对外关系上，共有人享有连带债权、承担连带债务，但是法律另有规定或者第三人知道共有人不具有连带债权债务关系的除外；在共有人内部关系上，除共有人另有约定外，按份共有人按照份额享有债权、承担债务，共同共有人共同享有债权、承担债务。偿还债务超过自己应当承担份额的按份共有人，有权向其他共有人追偿。

**Article 307** With respect to claims and debts arising from the jointly owned immovable or movable property, in the external relationship, the co-owners shall enjoy joint claims and bear joint debts, unless otherwise provided by law or the third party knows that the co-owners do not have a joint claim-debt relationship; in the internal relationship among the co-owners, unless otherwise agreed by the co-owners, the co-owners by shares shall enjoy claims and bear debts in proportion to their shares, and the joint co-owners shall jointly enjoy claims and bear debts. A co-owner by shares who has repaid debts exceeding his own share shall have the right to claim recourse against the other co-owners.

<a id="art-308"></a>第三百零八条　共有人对共有的不动产或者动产没有约定为按份共有或者共同共有，或者约定不明确的，除共有人具有家庭关系等外，视为按份共有。

**Article 308** Where the co-owners have not agreed on whether the co-ownership of the immovable or movable property is co-ownership by shares or joint co-ownership, or the agreement is unclear, it shall be deemed as co-ownership by shares, unless the co-owners have a family relationship or similar.

<a id="art-309"></a>第三百零九条　按份共有人对共有的不动产或者动产享有的份额，没有约定或者约定不明确的，按照出资额确定；不能确定出资额的，视为等额享有。

**Article 309** Where the shares of co-owners by shares in the jointly owned immovable or movable property are not agreed upon or the agreement is unclear, the shares shall be determined according to the amount of capital contribution; if the capital contribution cannot be determined, the shares shall be deemed to be equal.

<a id="art-310"></a>第三百一十条　两个以上组织、个人共同享有用益物权、担保物权的，参照适用本章的有关规定。

**Article 310** Where two or more organizations or individuals jointly enjoy a usufruct or security interest, the relevant provisions of this Chapter shall apply mutatis mutandis.

### 第九章　所有权取得的特别规定

<a id="art-311"></a>第三百一十一条　无处分权人将不动产或者动产转让给受让人的，所有权人有权追回；除法律另有规定外，符合下列情形的，受让人取得该不动产或者动产的所有权：  
（一）受让人受让该不动产或者动产时是善意；  
（二）以合理的价格转让；  
（三）转让的不动产或者动产依照法律规定应当登记的已经登记，不需要登记的已经交付给受让人。  
受让人依据前款规定取得不动产或者动产的所有权的，原所有权人有权向无处分权人请求损害赔偿。  
当事人善意取得其他物权的，参照适用前两款规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第17条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-18" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第18条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第19条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）.md#art-20" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》物权编的解释（一）》第20条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-37" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第37条引用">[5]</a></sup>

**Article 311** Where a person without the right of disposition transfers an immovable or movable property to a transferee, the owner shall have the right to recover the property. Unless otherwise provided by law, where the following circumstances are met, the transferee shall acquire the ownership of the immovable or movable property:  
(1) the transferee is in good faith at the time of the transfer of the immovable or movable property;  
(2) the transfer is made at a reasonable price;  
(3) the transferred immovable or movable property that is required by law to be registered has been registered, and that which does not require registration has been delivered to the transferee. Where the transferee acquires the ownership of the immovable or movable property in accordance with the preceding paragraph, the original owner shall have the right to claim damages from the person without the right of disposition. Where a party acquires other real rights in good faith, the provisions of the preceding two paragraphs shall apply mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-312"></a>第三百一十二条　所有权人或者其他权利人有权追回遗失物。该遗失物通过转让被他人占有的，权利人有权向无处分权人请求损害赔偿，或者自知道或者应当知道受让人之日起二年内向受让人请求返还原物；但是，受让人通过拍卖或者向具有经营资格的经营者购得该遗失物的，权利人请求返还原物时应当支付受让人所付的费用。权利人向受让人支付所付费用后，有权向无处分权人追偿。

**Article 312** The owner or other right holder shall have the right to recover a lost property. Where the lost property is possessed by another person through transfer, the right holder shall have the right to claim damages from the person without the right of disposition, or claim the return of the original property from the transferee within two years from the date when he knows or should have known the transferee; however, if the transferee has purchased the lost property through auction or from a qualified business operator, the right holder shall, when claiming the return of the original property, pay the expenses incurred by the transferee. After paying the expenses to the transferee, the right holder shall have the right to claim recourse against the person without the right of disposition.

<a id="art-313"></a>第三百一十三条　善意受让人取得动产后，该动产上的原有权利消灭。但是，善意受让人在受让时知道或者应当知道该权利的除外。

**Article 313** After a transferee in good faith acquires a movable property, the original rights on such movable property shall extinguish, unless the transferee in good faith knew or should have known of such rights at the time of the transfer.

<a id="art-314"></a>第三百一十四条　拾得遗失物，应当返还权利人。拾得人应当及时通知权利人领取，或者送交公安等有关部门。

**Article 314** A person who finds a lost property shall return it to the right holder. The finder shall promptly notify the right holder to claim it, or deliver it to the public security organ or other relevant authorities.

<a id="art-315"></a>第三百一十五条　有关部门收到遗失物，知道权利人的，应当及时通知其领取；不知道的，应当及时发布招领公告。

**Article 315** Where the relevant authority receives a lost property, if it knows the right holder, it shall promptly notify him to claim it; if it does not know, it shall promptly publish a notice for claiming.

<a id="art-316"></a>第三百一十六条　拾得人在遗失物送交有关部门前，有关部门在遗失物被领取前，应当妥善保管遗失物。因故意或者重大过失致使遗失物毁损、灭失的，应当承担民事责任。

**Article 316** The finder shall take good care of the lost property before delivering it to the relevant authority, and the relevant authority shall take good care of it before it is claimed. If the lost property is damaged or lost due to intentional act or gross negligence, civil liability shall be borne.

<a id="art-317"></a>第三百一十七条　权利人领取遗失物时，应当向拾得人或者有关部门支付保管遗失物等支出的必要费用。  
权利人悬赏寻找遗失物的，领取遗失物时应当按照承诺履行义务。  
拾得人侵占遗失物的，无权请求保管遗失物等支出的费用，也无权请求权利人按照承诺履行义务。

**Article 317** When claiming a lost property, the right holder shall pay the finder or the relevant authority the necessary expenses incurred for keeping the lost property and the like.  
If the right holder has offered a reward for finding the lost property, he shall perform his obligation as promised when claiming the lost property.  
If the finder has misappropriated the lost property, he shall have no right to claim the expenses incurred for keeping the lost property, nor shall he have the right to request the right holder to perform the obligation as promised.

<a id="art-318"></a>第三百一十八条　遗失物自发布招领公告之日起一年内无人认领的，归国家所有。

**Article 318** A lost property that is not claimed by anyone within one year from the date of publishing the notice for claiming shall vest in the State.

<a id="art-319"></a>第三百一十九条　拾得漂流物、发现埋藏物或者隐藏物的，参照适用拾得遗失物的有关规定。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 319** For finding drifting objects, discovering buried objects or hidden objects, the relevant provisions on finding lost properties shall apply mutatis mutandis. Where there are other provisions in the law, those provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-320"></a>第三百二十条　主物转让的，从物随主物转让，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 320** Where the principal thing is transferred, the accessory thing shall be transferred together with the principal thing, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-321"></a>第三百二十一条　天然孳息，由所有权人取得；既有所有权人又有用益物权人的，由用益物权人取得。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。  
法定孳息，当事人有约定的，按照约定取得；没有约定或者约定不明确的，按照交易习惯取得。

**Article 321** Natural fruits shall be acquired by the owner; where there is both an owner and a usufructuary, the usufructuary shall acquire them. Where the parties have agreed otherwise, such agreement shall prevail. Legal fruits, where the parties have agreed on them, shall be acquired in accordance with the agreement; where there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, they shall be acquired in accordance with trade usage.

<a id="art-322"></a>第三百二十二条　因加工、附合、混合而产生的物的归属，有约定的，按照约定；没有约定或者约定不明确的，依照法律规定；法律没有规定的，按照充分发挥物的效用以及保护无过错当事人的原则确定。因一方当事人的过错或者确定物的归属造成另一方当事人损害的，应当给予赔偿或者补偿。  
第三分编　用益物权

**Article 322** The ownership of a thing arising from processing, attachment, or mixing shall, where there is an agreement, be determined in accordance with the agreement; where there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, it shall be determined in accordance with the law; where the law has no provisions, it shall be determined in accordance with the principle of giving full play to the utility of the thing and protecting the party without fault. Where damage is caused to another party due to the fault of one party or the determination of the ownership of the thing, compensation or indemnity shall be made. Part III Usufruct

### 第十章　一般规定

<a id="art-323"></a>第三百二十三条　用益物权人对他人所有的不动产或者动产，依法享有占有、使用和收益的权利。

**Article 323** A usufructuary shall have the right to possess, use, and benefit from the immovable or movable property owned by another person in accordance with law.

<a id="art-324"></a>第三百二十四条　国家所有或者国家所有由集体使用以及法律规定属于集体所有的自然资源，组织、个人依法可以占有、使用和收益。

**Article 324** An organization or individual may, in accordance with law, possess, use, and benefit from natural resources owned by the State, owned by the State but used by collectives, and those owned by collectives as provided by law.

<a id="art-325"></a>第三百二十五条　国家实行自然资源有偿使用制度，但是法律另有规定的除外。

**Article 325** The State shall implement a system of paid use of natural resources, unless otherwise provided by law.

<a id="art-326"></a>第三百二十六条　用益物权人行使权利，应当遵守法律有关保护和合理开发利用资源、保护生态环境的规定。所有权人不得干涉用益物权人行使权利。

**Article 326** A usufructuary, in exercising its rights, shall comply with the provisions of laws on the protection and rational development and utilization of resources and the protection of the ecological environment. The owner shall not interfere with the usufructuary's exercise of its rights.

<a id="art-327"></a>第三百二十七条　因不动产或者动产被征收、征用致使用益物权消灭或者影响用益物权行使的，用益物权人有权依据[本法第二百四十三条](#art-243)、第二百四十五条的规定获得相应补偿。

**Article 327** Where the usufruct is extinguished or its exercise is affected due to the expropriation or requisition of immovable or movable property, the usufructuary shall have the right to obtain corresponding compensation in accordance with Articles 243 and 245 of this Law.

<a id="art-328"></a>第三百二十八条　依法取得的海域使用权受法律保护。

**Article 328** The right to use sea areas obtained in accordance with law shall be protected by law.

<a id="art-329"></a>第三百二十九条　依法取得的探矿权、采矿权、取水权和使用水域、滩涂从事养殖、捕捞的权利受法律保护。

**Article 329** The rights to exploration, mining, water abstraction, and the right to use waters and tidal flats for aquaculture and fishing, obtained in accordance with law, shall be protected by law.

### 第十一章　土地承包经营权

<a id="art-330"></a>第三百三十条　农村集体经济组织实行家庭承包经营为基础、统分结合的双层经营体制。  
农民集体所有和国家所有由农民集体使用的耕地、林地、草地以及其他用于农业的土地，依法实行土地承包经营制度。

**Article 330** Rural collective economic organizations shall implement a two-tier management system combining unified and separate operations based on the household contract management system.  
The system of land contract management shall be implemented in accordance with law for arable land, forestland, grassland, and other agricultural land owned by the farmer collective or owned by the State but used by the farmer collective.

<a id="art-331"></a>第三百三十一条　土地承包经营权人依法对其承包经营的耕地、林地、草地等享有占有、使用和收益的权利，有权从事种植业、林业、畜牧业等农业生产。

**Article 331** Holders of the right to land contract management shall have the right to possess, use, and benefit from the arable land, forestland, grassland, etc., they contract to operate in accordance with law, and shall have the right to engage in agricultural production such as crop farming, forestry, and animal husbandry.

<a id="art-332"></a>第三百三十二条　耕地的承包期为三十年。草地的承包期为三十年至五十年。林地的承包期为三十年至七十年。  
前款规定的承包期限届满，由土地承包经营权人依照农村土地承包的法律规定继续承包。

**Article 332** The contract period for arable land shall be 30 years. The contract period for grassland shall be 30 to 50 years. The contract period for forestland shall be 30 to 70 years. Upon the expiration of the contract period specified in the preceding paragraph, the holder of the right to land contract management may continue the contract in accordance with the provisions of the law on rural land contracting.

<a id="art-333"></a>第三百三十三条　土地承包经营权自土地承包经营权合同生效时设立。  
登记机构应当向土地承包经营权人发放土地承包经营权证、林权证等证书，并登记造册，确认土地承包经营权。

**Article 333** The right to land contract management shall be established upon the entry into effect of the contract for land contract management.  
The registration authority shall issue certificates such as the certificate of the right to land contract management and the forest ownership certificate to the holder of the right to land contract management, and shall register them in a register to confirm the right to land contract management.

<a id="art-334"></a>第三百三十四条　土地承包经营权人依照法律规定，有权将土地承包经营权互换、转让。未经依法批准，不得将承包地用于非农建设。

**Article 334** A holder of the right to land contract management shall have the right to exchange or transfer the right to land contract management in accordance with law. Without approval in accordance with law, the contracted land shall not be used for non-agricultural construction.

<a id="art-335"></a>第三百三十五条　土地承包经营权互换、转让的，当事人可以向登记机构申请登记；未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。

**Article 335** Where the right to land contract management is exchanged or transferred, the parties may apply to the registration authority for registration; without registration, such right shall not be asserted against a bona fide third party.

<a id="art-336"></a>第三百三十六条　承包期内发包人不得调整承包地。  
因自然灾害严重毁损承包地等特殊情形，需要适当调整承包的耕地和草地的，应当依照农村土地承包的法律规定办理。

**Article 336** During the contract period, the party granting the contract shall not adjust the contracted land.  
Where, due to special circumstances such as severe damage to the contracted land caused by natural disasters, appropriate adjustment of the contracted arable land and grassland is necessary, it shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of the law on rural land contracting.

<a id="art-337"></a>第三百三十七条　承包期内发包人不得收回承包地。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 337** During the contract period, the party granting the contract shall not withdraw the contracted land. If the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-338"></a>第三百三十八条　承包地被征收的，土地承包经营权人有权依据[本法第二百四十三条](#art-243)的规定获得相应补偿。

**Article 338** Where the contracted land is expropriated, the holder of the right to land contract management shall have the right to obtain corresponding compensation in accordance with Article 243 of this Law.

<a id="art-339"></a>第三百三十九条　土地承包经营权人可以自主决定依法采取出租、入股或者其他方式向他人流转土地经营权。

**Article 339** A holder of the right to land contract management may, on its own initiative, decide to transfer the land management right to others by means of lease, shareholding, or other methods in accordance with law.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条　土地经营权人有权在合同约定的期限内占有农村土地，自主开展农业生产经营并取得收益。

**Article 340** A holder of the land management right shall have the right to possess the rural land within the period agreed in the contract, to independently carry out agricultural production and operation, and to obtain benefits.

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条　流转期限为五年以上的土地经营权，自流转合同生效时设立。当事人可以向登记机构申请土地经营权登记；未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。

**Article 341** The land management right with a transfer period of five years or more shall be established at the time when the transfer contract becomes effective. The parties may apply to the registration authority for the registration of the land management right; without registration, it shall not be invoked against a bona fide third party.

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条　通过招标、拍卖、公开协商等方式承包农村土地，经依法登记取得权属证书的，可以依法采取出租、入股、抵押或者其他方式流转土地经营权。

**Article 342** Where rural land is contracted through bidding, auction, public negotiation or other means, and the ownership certificate is obtained upon registration according to law, the land management right may be transferred by leasing, equity contribution, mortgage or other means according to law.

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条　国家所有的农用地实行承包经营的，参照适用本编的有关规定。

**Article 343** Where state-owned agricultural land is subject to contractual operation, the relevant provisions of this Part shall be applied mutatis mutandis.

### 第十二章　建设用地使用权

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条　建设用地使用权人依法对国家所有的土地享有占有、使用和收益的权利，有权利用该土地建造建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施。

**Article 344** The holder of the right to use construction land shall be entitled, according to law, to possess, use and benefit from the state-owned land, and shall have the right to use such land to construct buildings, structures and their auxiliary facilities.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条　建设用地使用权可以在土地的地表、地上或者地下分别设立。

**Article 345** The right to use construction land may be established separately on the surface, above the ground or underground of the land.

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条　设立建设用地使用权，应当符合节约资源、保护生态环境的要求，遵守法律、行政法规关于土地用途的规定，不得损害已经设立的用益物权。

**Article 346** The establishment of the right to use construction land shall comply with the requirements of conserving resources and protecting the ecological environment, abide by the provisions of laws and administrative regulations on the purposes of land use, and shall not harm the existing usufruct.

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条　设立建设用地使用权，可以采取出让或者划拨等方式。  
工业、商业、旅游、娱乐和商品住宅等经营性用地以及同一土地有两个以上意向用地者的，应当采取招标、拍卖等公开竞价的方式出让。  
严格限制以划拨方式设立建设用地使用权。

**Article 347** The right to use construction land may be established by means of grant or allocation.  
For commercial land such as industrial, commercial, tourism, entertainment and commercial residential land, and where there are two or more intended users for the same land, the grant shall be made by means of public bidding, auction or other public competitive pricing methods.  
The establishment of the right to use construction land by means of allocation shall be strictly restricted.

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条　通过招标、拍卖、协议等出让方式设立建设用地使用权的，当事人应当采用书面形式订立建设用地使用权出让合同。  
建设用地使用权出让合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）当事人的名称和住所；  
（二）土地界址、面积等；  
（三）建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施占用的空间；  
（四）土地用途、规划条件；  
（五）建设用地使用权期限；
