（六）出让金等费用及其支付方式；  
（七）解决争议的方法。

**Article 348** Where the right to use construction land is established by means of grant through bidding, auction, agreement or other means, the parties shall conclude a contract for the grant of the right to use construction land in writing. A contract for the grant of the right to use construction land shall generally include the following terms:  
(1) the names and domiciles of the parties;  
(2) the boundaries, area, etc. of the land;  
(3) the space occupied by the buildings, structures and their auxiliary facilities;  
(4) the land use purposes and planning conditions;  
(5) the term of the right to use construction land;  
(6) the fees such as the grant price and the method of payment thereof; and (7) the method for dispute resolution.

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条　设立建设用地使用权的，应当向登记机构申请建设用地使用权登记。建设用地使用权自登记时设立。登记机构应当向建设用地使用权人发放权属证书。

**Article 349** For the establishment of the right to use construction land, an application for the registration of the right to use construction land shall be made to the registration authority. The right to use construction land shall be established upon registration. The registration authority shall issue a title certificate to the holder of the right to use construction land.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条　建设用地使用权人应当合理利用土地，不得改变土地用途；需要改变土地用途的，应当依法经有关行政主管部门批准。

**Article 350** The holder of the right to use construction land shall make rational use of the land and shall not change the land use purpose; where it is necessary to change the land use purpose, approval shall be obtained from the relevant administrative department according to law.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条　建设用地使用权人应当依照法律规定以及合同约定支付出让金等费用。

**Article 351** The holder of the right to use construction land shall pay fees such as the grant price in accordance with the provisions of laws and the contractual agreement.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条　建设用地使用权人建造的建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施的所有权属于建设用地使用权人，但是有相反证据证明的除外。

**Article 352** The ownership of the buildings, structures and their auxiliary facilities constructed by the holder of the right to use construction land shall belong to the holder of the right to use construction land, unless there is evidence to the contrary.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条　建设用地使用权人有权将建设用地使用权转让、互换、出资、赠与或者抵押，但是法律另有规定的除外。

**Article 353** The holder of the right to use construction land shall have the right to transfer, exchange, make capital contribution with, donate or mortgage the right to use construction land, unless otherwise provided by law.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条　建设用地使用权转让、互换、出资、赠与或者抵押的，当事人应当采用书面形式订立相应的合同。使用期限由当事人约定，但是不得超过建设用地使用权的剩余期限。

**Article 354** Where the right to use construction land is transferred, exchanged, made as capital contribution, donated or mortgaged, the parties shall conclude a corresponding contract in writing. The term of use shall be agreed upon by the parties, but shall not exceed the remaining term of the right to use construction land.

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条　建设用地使用权转让、互换、出资或者赠与的，应当向登记机构申请变更登记。

**Article 355** Where the right to use construction land is transferred, exchanged, made as capital contribution or donated, an application for modification registration shall be made to the registration authority.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条　建设用地使用权转让、互换、出资或者赠与的，附着于该土地上的建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施一并处分。

**Article 356** Where the right to use construction land is transferred, exchanged, made as capital contribution or donated, the buildings, structures and their auxiliary facilities attached to the land shall be disposed of together therewith.

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条　建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施转让、互换、出资或者赠与的，该建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施占用范围内的建设用地使用权一并处分。

**Article 357** Where the buildings, structures and their auxiliary facilities are transferred, exchanged, made as capital contribution or donated, the right to use construction land within the area occupied by such buildings, structures and their auxiliary facilities shall be disposed of together therewith.

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条　建设用地使用权期限届满前，因公共利益需要提前收回该土地的，应当依据[本法第二百四十三条](#art-243)的规定对该土地上的房屋以及其他不动产给予补偿，并退还相应的出让金。

**Article 358** Where, before the expiration of the term of the right to use construction land, the land is required to be taken back in advance for the needs of the public interest, compensation shall be given for the houses and other immovable property on the land in accordance with the provisions of Article 243 of this Law, and the corresponding grant price shall be refunded.

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条　住宅建设用地使用权期限届满的，自动续期。续期费用的缴纳或者减免，依照法律、行政法规的规定办理。  
非住宅建设用地使用权期限届满后的续期，依照法律规定办理。该土地上的房屋以及其他不动产的归属，有约定的，按照约定；没有约定或者约定不明确的，依照法律、行政法规的规定办理。

**Article 359** Where the term of the right to use residential construction land expires, it shall be automatically renewed. The payment or reduction of and exemption from the renewal fees shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations. The renewal of the right to use non-residential construction land after the expiration of its term shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of laws. The ownership of the houses and other immovable property on the land shall, if there is an agreement, be subject to the agreement; if there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, it shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations.

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条　建设用地使用权消灭的，出让人应当及时办理注销登记。登记机构应当收回权属证书。

**Article 360** Where the right to use construction land is extinguished, the grantor shall promptly handle the deregistration. The registration authority shall withdraw the title certificate.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条　集体所有的土地作为建设用地的，应当依照土地管理的法律规定办理。

**Article 361** : Where collectively owned land is used as construction land, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of laws on land administration.

### 第十三章　宅基地使用权

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条　宅基地使用权人依法对集体所有的土地享有占有和使用的权利，有权依法利用该土地建造住宅及其附属设施。

**Article 362** : The holder of the right to use a residential site shall, in accordance with law, have the right to possess and use the collectively owned land, and shall have the right to use such land in accordance with law for the construction of a residence and its auxiliary installations.

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条　宅基地使用权的取得、行使和转让，适用土地管理的法律和国家有关规定。

**Article 363** : The acquisition, exercise, and transfer of the right to use a residential site shall be governed by the laws on land administration and the relevant state regulations.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条　宅基地因自然灾害等原因灭失的，宅基地使用权消灭。对失去宅基地的村民，应当依法重新分配宅基地。

**Article 364** : If a residential site is extinguished due to a natural disaster or other reason, the right to use that residential site shall be extinguished. For villagers who have lost their residential site, a new residential site shall be reallocated to them in accordance with law.

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条　已经登记的宅基地使用权转让或者消灭的，应当及时办理变更登记或者注销登记。

**Article 365** : Where the registered right to use a residential site is transferred or extinguished, the modification registration or cancellation registration shall be promptly processed.

### 第十四章　居住权

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条　居住权人有权按照合同约定，对他人的住宅享有占有、使用的用益物权，以满足生活居住的需要。

**Article 366** : The holder of a right of habitation shall, in accordance with the terms of the contract, have the right to possess and use the residence of another as a usufruct, in order to meet the needs of living and residence.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条　设立居住权，当事人应当采用书面形式订立居住权合同。  
居住权合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）当事人的姓名或者名称和住所；  
（二）住宅的位置；  
（三）居住的条件和要求；  
（四）居住权期限；  
（五）解决争议的方法。

**Article 367** : To establish a right of habitation, the parties shall conclude a contract for the right of habitation in writing. A contract for the right of habitation generally includes the following terms:  
(1) the name or title and domicile of the parties;  
(2) the location of the residence;  
(3) the conditions and requirements for habitation;  
(4) the term of the right of habitation; and (5) the method for dispute resolution.

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条　居住权无偿设立，但是当事人另有约定的除外。设立居住权的，应当向登记机构申请居住权登记。居住权自登记时设立。

**Article 368** : A right of habitation shall be established gratuitously, unless the parties agree otherwise. Where a right of habitation is established, an application for the registration of the right of habitation shall be made to the registration authority. The right of habitation shall be established upon registration.

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条　居住权不得转让、继承。设立居住权的住宅不得出租，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 369** : A right of habitation shall not be transferred or inherited. A residence on which a right of habitation is established shall not be leased, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条　居住权期限届满或者居住权人死亡的，居住权消灭。居住权消灭的，应当及时办理注销登记。

**Article 370** : The right of habitation shall be extinguished upon the expiration of its term or upon the death of the holder of the right of habitation. Where the right of habitation is extinguished, the cancellation registration shall be promptly processed.

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条　以遗嘱方式设立居住权的，参照适用本章的有关规定。

**Article 371** : Where a right of habitation is established by will, the relevant provisions of this Chapter shall be applied mutatis mutandis.

### 第十五章　地役权

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条　地役权人有权按照合同约定，利用他人的不动产，以提高自己的不动产的效益。  
前款所称他人的不动产为供役地，自己的不动产为需役地。

**Article 372** : The holder of an easement shall, in accordance with the terms of the contract, have the right to use the immovable property of another to improve the efficiency of his or her own immovable property.  
The immovable property of another referred to in the preceding paragraph is the servient tenement, and the party's own immovable property is the dominant tenement.

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条　设立地役权，当事人应当采用书面形式订立地役权合同。  
地役权合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）当事人的姓名或者名称和住所；  
（二）供役地和需役地的位置；  
（三）利用目的和方法；  
（四）地役权期限；  
（五）费用及其支付方式；  
（六）解决争议的方法。

**Article 373** : To establish an easement, the parties shall conclude a contract for the easement in writing. A contract for an easement generally includes the following terms:  
(1) the name or title and domicile of the parties;  
(2) the location of the servient and dominant tenements;  
(3) the purpose and method of use;  
(4) the term of the easement;  
(5) the fees and the method of payment; and (6) the method for dispute resolution.

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条　地役权自地役权合同生效时设立。当事人要求登记的，可以向登记机构申请地役权登记；未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。

**Article 374** : An easement shall be established when the contract for the easement takes effect. If the parties request registration, they may apply to the registration authority for the registration of the easement; without registration, it shall not be set up against a bona fide third party.

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条　供役地权利人应当按照合同约定，允许地役权人利用其不动产，不得妨害地役权人行使权利。

**Article 375** : The holder of the servient tenement shall, in accordance with the terms of the contract, allow the holder of the easement to use his or her immovable property, and shall not interfere with the exercise of the right by the holder of the easement.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条　地役权人应当按照合同约定的利用目的和方法利用供役地，尽量减少对供役地权利人物权的限制。

**Article 376** : The holder of the easement shall use the servient tenement in accordance with the purpose and method of use agreed upon in the contract, and shall minimize the restriction on the real right of the holder of the servient tenement.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条　地役权期限由当事人约定；但是，不得超过土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权等用益物权的剩余期限。

**Article 377** : The term of an easement shall be agreed upon by the parties; however, it shall not exceed the remaining term of the usufruct such as the right to the contractual operation of land, the right to the use of land for construction, and similar rights.

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条　土地所有权人享有地役权或者负担地役权的，设立土地承包经营权、宅基地使用权等用益物权时，该用益物权人继续享有或者负担已经设立的地役权。

**Article 378** : If the owner of the land enjoys an easement or bears an easement, when establishing a usufruct such as the right to the contractual operation of land or the right to use a residential site, the holder of such usufruct shall continue to enjoy or bear the easement already established.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条　土地上已经设立土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权、宅基地使用权等用益物权的，未经用益物权人同意，土地所有权人不得设立地役权。

**Article 379** : Where a usufruct such as the right to the contractual operation of land, the right to the use of land for construction, or the right to use a residential site has already been established on the land, the owner of the land shall not establish an easement without the consent of the holder of the usufruct.

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条　地役权不得单独转让。土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权等转让的，地役权一并转让，但是合同另有约定的除外。

**Article 380** : An easement shall not be transferred separately. Where the right to the contractual operation of land, the right to the use of land for construction, or any similar right is transferred, the easement shall be transferred together therewith, unless otherwise agreed in the contract.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条　地役权不得单独抵押。土地经营权、建设用地使用权等抵押的，在实现抵押权时，地役权一并转让。

**Article 381** An easement shall not be mortgaged separately. Where the right to land management, the right to use construction land, or the like is mortgaged, the easement shall be transferred together when the mortgage is enforced.

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条　需役地以及需役地上的土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权等部分转让时，转让部分涉及地役权的，受让人同时享有地役权。

**Article 382** Where the dominant tenement and the right to land contractual management, the right to use construction land, or the like on the dominant tenement are partially transferred, if the transferred part involves the easement, the transferee shall simultaneously enjoy the easement.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条　供役地以及供役地上的土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权等部分转让时，转让部分涉及地役权的，地役权对受让人具有法律约束力。

**Article 383** Where the servient tenement and the right to land contractual management, the right to use construction land, or the like on the servient tenement are partially transferred, if the transferred part involves the easement, the easement shall be legally binding on the transferee.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条　地役权人有下列情形之一的，供役地权利人有权解除地役权合同，地役权消灭：  
（一）违反法律规定或者合同约定，滥用地役权；  
（二）有偿利用供役地，约定的付款期限届满后在合理期限内经两次催告未支付费用。

**Article 384** Where the easement holder falls under any of the following circumstances, the servient tenement right holder may rescind the easement contract, and the easement shall be extinguished:  
(1) abusing the easement in violation of the provisions of the law or the contractual stipulations  
(2) in the case of exploiting the servient tenement with payment, failing to pay the fees within a reasonable period after the expiry of the agreed payment deadline upon two demands.

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条　已经登记的地役权变更、转让或者消灭的，应当及时办理变更登记或者注销登记。  
第四分编　担保物权

**Article 385** Where a registered easement is modified, transferred, or extinguished, modification registration or cancellation registration shall be handled in a timely manner.  
Part IV Security Interests

### 第十六章　一般规定

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条　担保物权人在债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现担保物权的情形，依法享有就担保财产优先受偿的权利，但是法律另有规定的除外。

**Article 386** Where the debtor fails to perform an obligation due, or any circumstance agreed upon by the parties for enforcing a security interest occurs, the security interest holder shall have the right to seek priority in satisfying its claim from the security property in accordance with the law, unless otherwise provided by the law.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条　债权人在借贷、买卖等民事活动中，为保障实现其债权，需要担保的，可以依照本法和其他法律的规定设立担保物权。  
第三人为债务人向债权人提供担保的，可以要求债务人提供反担保。反担保适用本法和其他法律的规定。

**Article 387** Where a creditor, in order to secure the satisfaction of its claim in civil activities such as lending or sale, needs a guarantee, it may create a security interest in accordance with the provisions of this Law and other laws. Where a third party provides security to a creditor for a debtor, the third party may require the debtor to provide counter-security. The provisions of this Law and other laws shall apply to counter-security.

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条　设立担保物权，应当依照本法和其他法律的规定订立担保合同。担保合同包括抵押合同、质押合同和其他具有担保功能的合同。担保合同是主债权债务合同的从合同。主债权债务合同无效的，担保合同无效，但是法律另有规定的除外。  
担保合同被确认无效后，债务人、担保人、债权人有过错的，应当根据其过错各自承担相应的民事责任。

**Article 388** To create a security interest, a security contract shall be concluded in accordance with the provisions of this Law and other laws. Security contracts include mortgage contracts, pledge contracts, and other contracts with security functions. A security contract is an ancillary contract to the principal claim and debt contract. If the principal claim and debt contract is void, the security contract shall be void, unless otherwise provided by the law. After a security contract is confirmed void, if the debtor, the guarantor, or the creditor is at fault, it shall bear the corresponding civil liability according to its fault.

<a id="art-389"></a>第三百八十九条　担保物权的担保范围包括主债权及其利息、违约金、损害赔偿金、保管担保财产和实现担保物权的费用。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。

**Article 389** The scope of security covered by a security interest shall include the principal claim and its interest, liquidated damages, compensation for damages, the expenses for keeping the security property, and the expenses for enforcing the security interest. If the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail.

<a id="art-390"></a>第三百九十条　担保期间，担保财产毁损、灭失或者被征收等，担保物权人可以就获得的保险金、赔偿金或者补偿金等优先受偿。被担保债权的履行期限未届满的，也可以提存该保险金、赔偿金或者补偿金等。

**Article 390** During the security period, if the security property is damaged, destroyed, or requisitioned, the security interest holder may seek priority in satisfying its claim from the insurance proceeds, compensation, or damages, etc. obtained. If the performance period for the secured claim has not expired, the said insurance proceeds, compensation, or damages, etc. may also be deposited.

<a id="art-391"></a>第三百九十一条　第三人提供担保，未经其书面同意，债权人允许债务人转移全部或者部分债务的，担保人不再承担相应的担保责任。

**Article 391** Where a third party provides security, if the creditor allows the debtor to transfer all or part of the debt without the third party's written consent, the guarantor shall no longer bear the corresponding security liability.

<a id="art-392"></a>第三百九十二条　被担保的债权既有物的担保又有人的担保的，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现担保物权的情形，债权人应当按照约定实现债权；没有约定或者约定不明确，债务人自己提供物的担保的，债权人应当先就该物的担保实现债权；第三人提供物的担保的，债权人可以就物的担保实现债权，也可以请求保证人承担保证责任。提供担保的第三人承担担保责任后，有权向债务人追偿。&thinsp;<sup>[1]</sup>

**Article 392** Where a secured claim has both a security interest and a guarantee, if the debtor fails to perform an obligation due, or any circumstance agreed upon by the parties for enforcing a security interest occurs, the creditor shall enforce the claim in accordance with the agreement. If there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and the debtor itself provides the security interest, the creditor shall first enforce the claim against the security interest. If a third party provides the security interest, the creditor may enforce the claim against the security interest or request the guarantor to bear the guarantee liability. After a third party who provides the security bears the security liability, it shall have the right of recourse against the debtor.

<a id="art-393"></a>第三百九十三条　有下列情形之一的，担保物权消灭：  
（一）主债权消灭；  
（二）担保物权实现；  
（三）债权人放弃担保物权；  
（四）法律规定担保物权消灭的其他情形。

**Article 393** A security interest shall be extinguished in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the principal claim is extinguished;  
(2) the security interest is enforced;  
(3) the creditor abandons the security interest  
(4) any other circumstance provided by the law for the extinction of a security interest.

### 第十七章　抵押权

#### 第一节　一般抵押权

<a id="art-394"></a>第三百九十四条　为担保债务的履行，债务人或者第三人不转移财产的占有，将该财产抵押给债权人的，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，债权人有权就该财产优先受偿。  
前款规定的债务人或者第三人为抵押人，债权人为抵押权人，提供担保的财产为抵押财产。

**Article 394** To secure the performance of a debt, where the debtor or a third party does not transfer the possession of a property but mortgages the property to the creditor, if the debtor fails to perform an obligation due, or any circumstance agreed upon by the parties for enforcing the mortgage occurs, the creditor shall have the right to seek priority in satisfying its claim from such property.  
The debtor or the third party as specified in the preceding paragraph is the mortgagor, the creditor is the mortgagee, and the property provided as security is the mortgaged property.

<a id="art-395"></a>第三百九十五条　债务人或者第三人有权处分的下列财产可以抵押：  
（一）建筑物和其他土地附着物；  
（二）建设用地使用权；  
（三）海域使用权；  
（四）生产设备、原材料、半成品、产品；  
（五）正在建造的建筑物、船舶、航空器；  
（六）交通运输工具；  
（七）法律、行政法规未禁止抵押的其他财产。  
抵押人可以将前款所列财产一并抵押。

**Article 395** The following property over which the debtor or a third party is entitled to dispose may be mortgaged:  
(1) buildings and other structures affixed to the land;  
(2) the right to use construction land;  
(3) the right to use sea areas;  
(4) production equipment, raw materials, semi-finished products, and finished products;  
(5) buildings, vessels, and aircraft under construction;  
(6) means of transport; and (7) other property that is not prohibited from being mortgaged by any law or administrative regulation. The mortgagor may mortgage all of the property listed in the preceding paragraph together.

<a id="art-396"></a>第三百九十六条　企业、个体工商户、农业生产经营者可以将现有的以及将有的生产设备、原材料、半成品、产品抵押，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，债权人有权就抵押财产确定时的动产优先受偿。

**Article 396** An enterprise, individual industrial or commercial household, or agricultural production operator may mortgage its existing and future-to-be-acquired production equipment, raw materials, semi-finished products, and finished products. If the debtor fails to perform an obligation due, or any circumstance agreed upon by the parties for enforcing the mortgage occurs, the creditor shall have the right to seek priority in satisfying its claim from the movable property as identified at the time the mortgage is enforced.

<a id="art-397"></a>第三百九十七条　以建筑物抵押的，该建筑物占用范围内的建设用地使用权一并抵押。以建设用地使用权抵押的，该土地上的建筑物一并抵押。  
抵押人未依据前款规定一并抵押的，未抵押的财产视为一并抵押。

**Article 397** Where a building is mortgaged, the right to use construction land within the area occupied by such building shall be mortgaged together. Where the right to use construction land is mortgaged, the building on the land shall be mortgaged together. If the mortgagor does not mortgage the property together in accordance with the preceding paragraph, the unmortgaged property shall be deemed to be mortgaged together.

<a id="art-398"></a>第三百九十八条　乡镇、村企业的建设用地使用权不得单独抵押。以乡镇、村企业的厂房等建筑物抵押的，其占用范围内的建设用地使用权一并抵押。

**Article 398** The right to use construction land of a township or village enterprise shall not be mortgaged separately. Where a building such as a factory building of a township or village enterprise is mortgaged, the right to use construction land within the area occupied by such building shall be mortgaged together.

<a id="art-399"></a>第三百九十九条　下列财产不得抵押：  
（一）土地所有权；  
（二）宅基地、自留地、自留山等集体所有土地的使用权，但是法律规定可以抵押的除外；  
（三）学校、幼儿园、医疗机构等为公益目的成立的非营利法人的教育设施、医疗卫生设施和其他公益设施；  
（四）所有权、使用权不明或者有争议的财产；  
（五）依法被查封、扣押、监管的财产；  
（六）法律、行政法规规定不得抵押的其他财产。

**Article 399** The following property shall not be mortgaged:  
(1) land ownership;  
(2) the right to use collectively owned land, such as residential land, private plots of cropland, and private plots of hilly land, except as otherwise provided by the law;  
(3) educational facilities, medical and health facilities, and other public welfare facilities of non-profit legal persons established for public welfare purposes, such as schools, kindergartens, and medical institutions;  
(4) property whose ownership or right to use is unclear or disputed;  
(5) property that is sealed up, detained, or placed under supervision in accordance with the law  
(6) other property that is prohibited from being mortgaged by any law or administrative regulation.

<a id="art-400"></a>第四百条　设立抵押权，当事人应当采用书面形式订立抵押合同。  
抵押合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）被担保债权的种类和数额；  
（二）债务人履行债务的期限；  
（三）抵押财产的名称、数量等情况；  
（四）担保的范围。

**Article 400** To create a mortgage, the parties shall enter into a mortgage contract in writing. A mortgage contract generally includes the following clauses:  
(1) the type and amount of the secured claim;  
(2) the time limit for the debtor to perform the debt;  
(3) the name, quantity, and other information of the mortgaged property; and (4) the scope of the security.

<a id="art-401"></a>第四百零一条　抵押权人在债务履行期限届满前，与抵押人约定债务人不履行到期债务时抵押财产归债权人所有的，只能依法就抵押财产优先受偿。

**Article 401** If a mortgagee and a mortgagor agree before the expiration of the performance period of the debt that the mortgaged property shall vest in the creditor if the debtor fails to perform the due debt, the mortgagee may only enjoy priority of compensation from the mortgaged property in accordance with law.

<a id="art-402"></a>第四百零二条　以[本法第三百九十五条](#art-395)第一款第一项至第三项规定的财产或者第五项规定的正在建造的建筑物抵押的，应当办理抵押登记。抵押权自登记时设立。

**Article 402** Where a mortgage is created on the property specified in Items (1) to (3) of the first paragraph of Article 395 of this Code or on a building under construction specified in Item (5), a mortgage registration shall be effected. The mortgage right shall be established upon registration.

<a id="art-403"></a>第四百零三条　以动产抵押的，抵押权自抵押合同生效时设立；未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。

**Article 403** Where a mortgage is created on chattels, the mortgage right shall be established upon the entry into effect of the mortgage contract; without registration, it may not be set up against a bona fide third party.

<a id="art-404"></a>第四百零四条　以动产抵押的，不得对抗正常经营活动中已经支付合理价款并取得抵押财产的买受人。

**Article 404** Where a mortgage is created on chattels, it may not be set up against a buyer who has paid a reasonable price and obtained the mortgaged property in the ordinary course of business.

<a id="art-405"></a>第四百零五条　抵押权设立前，抵押财产已经出租并转移占有的，原租赁关系不受该抵押权的影响。

**Article 405** If the mortgaged property has been leased and possession thereof has been transferred before the establishment of the mortgage right, the original lease relationship shall not be affected by such mortgage right.

<a id="art-406"></a>第四百零六条　抵押期间，抵押人可以转让抵押财产。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。抵押财产转让的，抵押权不受影响。  
抵押人转让抵押财产的，应当及时通知抵押权人。抵押权人能够证明抵押财产转让可能损害抵押权的，可以请求抵押人将转让所得的价款向抵押权人提前清偿债务或者提存。转让的价款超过债权数额的部分归抵押人所有，不足部分由债务人清偿。

**Article 406** During the mortgage period, the mortgagor may transfer the mortgaged property. If the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail. The mortgage right shall not be affected by the transfer of the mortgaged property. Where the mortgagor transfers the mortgaged property, the mortgagor shall promptly notify the mortgagee. If the mortgagee can prove that the transfer of the mortgaged property may prejudice the mortgage right, the mortgagee may request the mortgagor to apply the proceeds from the transfer to the advance repayment of the debt or to have them deposited with the mortgagee. The portion of the transfer proceeds exceeding the amount of the claim shall belong to the mortgagor, and the shortfall shall be paid by the debtor.

<a id="art-407"></a>第四百零七条　抵押权不得与债权分离而单独转让或者作为其他债权的担保。债权转让的，担保该债权的抵押权一并转让，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 407** A mortgage right shall not be transferred separately from the claim it secures, nor shall it be used as a security for another claim. Where the claim is assigned, the mortgage right securing such claim shall be assigned together therewith, unless otherwise provided by law or agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-408"></a>第四百零八条　抵押人的行为足以使抵押财产价值减少的，抵押权人有权请求抵押人停止其行为；抵押财产价值减少的，抵押权人有权请求恢复抵押财产的价值，或者提供与减少的价值相应的担保。抵押人不恢复抵押财产的价值，也不提供担保的，抵押权人有权请求债务人提前清偿债务。

**Article 408** If the mortgagor's conduct is sufficient to cause a decrease in the value of the mortgaged property, the mortgagee shall have the right to request the mortgagor to cease such conduct; if the value of the mortgaged property has decreased, the mortgagee shall have the right to request the restoration of the value of the mortgaged property or the provision of security corresponding to the decreased value. If the mortgagor neither restores the value of the mortgaged property nor provides such security, the mortgagee shall have the right to request the debtor to repay the debt before maturity.

<a id="art-409"></a>第四百零九条　抵押权人可以放弃抵押权或者抵押权的顺位。抵押权人与抵押人可以协议变更抵押权顺位以及被担保的债权数额等内容。但是，抵押权的变更未经其他抵押权人书面同意的，不得对其他抵押权人产生不利影响。  
债务人以自己的财产设定抵押，抵押权人放弃该抵押权、抵押权顺位或者变更抵押权的，其他担保人在抵押权人丧失优先受偿权益的范围内免除担保责任，但是其他担保人承诺仍然提供担保的除外。

**Article 409** A mortgagee may waive the mortgage right or the ranking of the mortgage right. The mortgagee and the mortgagor may, by agreement, modify the ranking of the mortgage right and the amount of the claim secured thereby, provided that any modification that has not obtained the written consent of other mortgagees shall not adversely affect such other mortgagees. Where a debtor creates a mortgage on its own property, and the mortgagee waives such mortgage right, the ranking thereof, or modifies the mortgage right, the guarantors other than the debtor shall be released from their guarantee liability to the extent of the priority right to compensation that the mortgagee has lost, unless such other guarantors undertake to continue to provide the guarantee.

<a id="art-410"></a>第四百一十条　债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，抵押权人可以与抵押人协议以抵押财产折价或者以拍卖、变卖该抵押财产所得的价款优先受偿。协议损害其他债权人利益的，其他债权人可以请求人民法院撤销该协议。  
抵押权人与抵押人未就抵押权实现方式达成协议的，抵押权人可以请求人民法院拍卖、变卖抵押财产。  
抵押财产折价或者变卖的，应当参照市场价格。

**Article 410** If the debtor fails to perform its due debt or a circumstance agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the mortgage right occurs, the mortgagee may reach an agreement with the mortgagor to obtain priority payment out of the proceeds from the conversion of the mortgaged property into money or from the auction or sale of such property. If such agreement prejudices the interests of other creditors, those other creditors may request the People's Court to revoke the agreement. If the mortgagee and the mortgagor fail to reach an agreement on the method for enforcing the mortgage right, the mortgagee may request the People's Court to auction or sell the mortgaged property. Where the mortgaged property is converted into money or sold, the market price shall be taken as a reference.

<a id="art-411"></a>第四百一十一条　依据[本法第三百九十六条](#art-396)规定设定抵押的，抵押财产自下列情形之一发生时确定：  
（一）债务履行期限届满，债权未实现；  
（二）抵押人被宣告破产或者解散；  
（三）当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形；  
（四）严重影响债权实现的其他情形。

**Article 411** Where a mortgage is created in accordance with Article 396 of this Code, the mortgaged property shall be determined upon the occurrence of any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the performance period for the debt has expired and the claim has not been satisfied;  
(2) the mortgagor has been declared bankrupt or dissolved;  
(3) a circumstance agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the mortgage right occurs  
(4) other circumstances that materially impair the satisfaction of the claim.

<a id="art-412"></a>第四百一十二条　债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，致使抵押财产被人民法院依法扣押的，自扣押之日起，抵押权人有权收取该抵押财产的天然孳息或者法定孳息，但是抵押权人未通知应当清偿法定孳息义务人的除外。  
前款规定的孳息应当先充抵收取孳息的费用。

**Article 412** Where the debtor fails to perform its due debt or a circumstance agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the mortgage right occurs, resulting in the mortgaged property being seized by the People's Court in accordance with law, the mortgagee shall, from the date of seizure, have the right to collect the natural fruits or legal fruits from the mortgaged property, unless the mortgagee has failed to notify the obligor who is obligated to satisfy the legal fruits.  
The fruits referred to in the preceding paragraph shall first be applied to cover the expenses incurred in collecting them.

<a id="art-413"></a>第四百一十三条　抵押财产折价或者拍卖、变卖后，其价款超过债权数额的部分归抵押人所有，不足部分由债务人清偿。

**Article 413** After the mortgaged property is converted into money, auctioned, or sold, the portion of the proceeds exceeding the amount of the claim shall belong to the mortgagor, and the shortfall shall be paid by the debtor.

<a id="art-414"></a>第四百一十四条　同一财产向两个以上债权人抵押的，拍卖、变卖抵押财产所得的价款依照下列规定清偿：  
（一）抵押权已经登记的，按照登记的时间先后确定清偿顺序；  
（二）抵押权已经登记的先于未登记的受偿；  
（三）抵押权未登记的，按照债权比例清偿。  
其他可以登记的担保物权，清偿顺序参照适用前款规定。

**Article 414** Where the same property is mortgaged to two or more creditors, the proceeds from the auction or sale of the mortgaged property shall be used for repayment in accordance with the following rules:  
(1) where the mortgage rights have been registered, the order of payment shall be determined according to the chronological order of registration;  
(2) a registered mortgage right shall be paid before an unregistered one; and (3) where the mortgage rights are not registered, payment shall be made in proportion to the amounts of the claims. The order of payment for other registrable security interests shall be determined by reference to the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-415"></a>第四百一十五条　同一财产既设立抵押权又设立质权的，拍卖、变卖该财产所得的价款按照登记、交付的时间先后确定清偿顺序。

**Article 415** Where both a mortgage right and a pledge right are created on the same property, the order of payment from the proceeds of the auction or sale of such property shall be determined according to the chronological order of registration and delivery.

<a id="art-416"></a>第四百一十六条　动产抵押担保的主债权是抵押物的价款，标的物交付后十日内办理抵押登记的，该抵押权人优先于抵押物买受人的其他担保物权人受偿，但是留置权人除外。

**Article 416** Where the principal claim secured by a chattel mortgage is the purchase price of the collateral, and the mortgage registration is effected within ten days after the delivery of the subject matter, the mortgagee shall be paid in priority over other secured creditors of the buyer of the collateral, except for a lien holder.

<a id="art-417"></a>第四百一十七条　建设用地使用权抵押后，该土地上新增的建筑物不属于抵押财产。该建设用地使用权实现抵押权时，应当将该土地上新增的建筑物与建设用地使用权一并处分。但是，新增建筑物所得的价款，抵押权人无权优先受偿。

**Article 417** After the mortgage of the right to use construction land is created, the buildings newly constructed on such land shall not form part of the mortgaged property. When the mortgage right is enforced on the right to use construction land, the newly constructed buildings on such land shall be disposed of together with the right to use construction land; however, the mortgagee shall have no right to obtain priority compensation from the proceeds of the newly constructed buildings.

<a id="art-418"></a>第四百一十八条　以集体所有土地的使用权依法抵押的，实现抵押权后，未经法定程序，不得改变土地所有权的性质和土地用途。

**Article 418** Where the right to use collectively-owned land is mortgaged in accordance with law, after the enforcement of the mortgage right, the nature of the land ownership and the purpose of the land shall not be altered without going through the statutory procedures.

<a id="art-419"></a>第四百一十九条　抵押权人应当在主债权诉讼时效期间行使抵押权；未行使的，人民法院不予保护。

**Article 419** A mortgagee shall exercise its mortgage right within the limitation period for the principal claim; if it fails to do so, the People's Court shall not provide protection.

#### 第二节　最高额抵押权

<a id="art-420"></a>第四百二十条　为担保债务的履行，债务人或者第三人对一定期间内将要连续发生的债权提供担保财产的，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，抵押权人有权在最高债权额限度内就该担保财产优先受偿。  
最高额抵押权设立前已经存在的债权，经当事人同意，可以转入最高额抵押担保的债权范围。

**Article 420** Where, to secure the performance of a debt, a debtor or a third party provides security property for claims to arise continuously within a certain period, and if the debtor fails to perform its due debt or a circumstance agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the mortgage right occurs, the mortgagee shall have the right to obtain priority payment from the security property within the maximum amount of the claims.  
Claims that already exist before the creation of the maximum mortgage right may, with the consent of the parties, be included within the scope of claims secured by the maximum mortgage right.

<a id="art-421"></a>第四百二十一条　最高额抵押担保的债权确定前，部分债权转让的，最高额抵押权不得转让，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 421** Prior to the determination of the claims secured by a maximum mortgage for floating claims, if part of the claims is assigned, the said maximum mortgage right shall not be assigned, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-422"></a>第四百二十二条　最高额抵押担保的债权确定前，抵押权人与抵押人可以通过协议变更债权确定的期间、债权范围以及最高债权额。但是，变更的内容不得对其他抵押权人产生不利影响。

**Article 422** Prior to the determination of the claims secured by a maximum mortgage for floating claims, the mortgagee and the mortgagor may, by agreement, change the period for the determination of the claims, the scope of the claims, and the maximum amount of the claims. However, such a change shall not adversely affect any other mortgagee.

<a id="art-423"></a>第四百二十三条　有下列情形之一的，抵押权人的债权确定：  
（一）约定的债权确定期间届满；  
（二）没有约定债权确定期间或者约定不明确，抵押权人或者抵押人自最高额抵押权设立之日起满二年后请求确定债权；  
（三）新的债权不可能发生；  
（四）抵押权人知道或者应当知道抵押财产被查封、扣押；  
（五）债务人、抵押人被宣告破产或者解散；  
（六）法律规定债权确定的其他情形。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-30" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第30条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 423** The claims of the mortgagee shall be determined under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the agreed period for determination of the claims expires;  
(2) if there is no agreement on a period for determination of the claims or the agreement is ambiguous, the mortgagee or mortgagor requests determination of the claims within two years from the date of creation of the maximum mortgage for floating claims;  
(3) the occurrence of new claims becomes impossible;  
(4) the mortgagee knows or should have known that the mortgaged property has been sealed up or distrained;  
(5) the debtor or mortgagor is declared bankrupt or dissolved  
(6) other circumstances provided by law for the determination of claims arise.

<a id="art-424"></a>第四百二十四条　最高额抵押权除适用本节规定外，适用本章第一节的有关规定。

**Article 424** In addition to the provisions of this Section, the relevant provisions of Section 1 of this Chapter shall apply to a maximum mortgage for floating claims.

### 第十八章　质权

#### 第一节　动产质权

<a id="art-425"></a>第四百二十五条　为担保债务的履行，债务人或者第三人将其动产出质给债权人占有的，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现质权的情形，债权人有权就该动产优先受偿。  
前款规定的债务人或者第三人为出质人，债权人为质权人，交付的动产为质押财产。

**Article 425** To secure the performance of an obligation, if a debtor or a third party pledges his or its chattel to the creditor for possession, and the debtor fails to perform the obligation as it falls due or if any circumstance upon which the pledge is to be realized as agreed by the parties occurs, the creditor shall be entitled to be paid in priority out of the proceeds of such chattel.  
The debtor or the third party mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be the pledgor, the creditor shall be the pledgee, and the delivered chattel shall be the pledged property.

<a id="art-426"></a>第四百二十六条　法律、行政法规禁止转让的动产不得出质。

**Article 426** Chattels whose transfer is prohibited by laws or administrative regulations shall not be pledged.

<a id="art-427"></a>第四百二十七条　设立质权，当事人应当采用书面形式订立质押合同。  
质押合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）被担保债权的种类和数额；  
（二）债务人履行债务的期限；  
（三）质押财产的名称、数量等情况；  
（四）担保的范围；  
（五）质押财产交付的时间、方式。

**Article 427** To create a pledge, the parties shall enter into a pledge contract in writing. A pledge contract generally contains the following terms:  
(1) the type and amount of the claim secured;  
(2) the time limit for the debtor to perform the obligation;  
(3) the name, quantity and other information of the pledged property;  
(4) the scope of the security; and (5) the time and manner of delivery of the pledged property.

<a id="art-428"></a>第四百二十八条　质权人在债务履行期限届满前，与出质人约定债务人不履行到期债务时质押财产归债权人所有的，只能依法就质押财产优先受偿。

**Article 428** If the pledgee and the pledgor agree, prior to the expiration of the time limit for the performance of the obligation, that the pledged property shall vest in the creditor if the debtor fails to perform the obligation as it falls due, the pledgee may only be entitled to be paid in priority out of the said pledged property in accordance with law.

<a id="art-429"></a>第四百二十九条　质权自出质人交付质押财产时设立。

**Article 429** A pledge shall be created upon delivery of the pledged property by the pledgor.

<a id="art-430"></a>第四百三十条　质权人有权收取质押财产的孳息，但是合同另有约定的除外。  
前款规定的孳息应当先充抵收取孳息的费用。

**Article 430** The pledgee shall have the right to collect the fruits of the pledged property, unless the contract provides otherwise.  
The fruits prescribed in the preceding paragraph shall first be used to offset the expenses incurred in collecting them.

<a id="art-431"></a>第四百三十一条　质权人在质权存续期间，未经出质人同意，擅自使用、处分质押财产，造成出质人损害的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 431** If the pledgee, during the existence of the pledge, without the consent of the pledgor, uses or disposes of the pledged property, causing damage to the pledgor, the pledgee shall be liable for compensation.

<a id="art-432"></a>第四百三十二条　质权人负有妥善保管质押财产的义务；因保管不善致使质押财产毁损、灭失的，应当承担赔偿责任。  
质权人的行为可能使质押财产毁损、灭失的，出质人可以请求质权人将质押财产提存，或者请求提前清偿债务并返还质押财产。

**Article 432** The pledgee shall be obligated to keep the pledged property in safe custody; if the pledged property is damaged or lost due to improper custody, the pledgee shall be liable for compensation.  
If the behavior of the pledgee may cause damage or loss to the pledged property, the pledgor may request the pledgee to deposit the pledged property, or request the advance payment of the obligation and the return of the pledged property.

<a id="art-433"></a>第四百三十三条　因不可归责于质权人的事由可能使质押财产毁损或者价值明显减少，足以危害质权人权利的，质权人有权请求出质人提供相应的担保；出质人不提供的，质权人可以拍卖、变卖质押财产，并与出质人协议将拍卖、变卖所得的价款提前清偿债务或者提存。

**Article 433** If the pledged property may be damaged or its value may obviously decrease due to a cause not attributable to the pledgee to such an extent as to endanger the rights of the pledgee, the pledgee shall have the right to request the pledgor to provide additional security; if the pledgor fails to do so, the pledgee may auction or sell off the pledged property, and agree with the pledgor to use the proceeds from the auction or sale for advance payment of the obligation or deposit them for safekeeping.

<a id="art-434"></a>第四百三十四条　质权人在质权存续期间，未经出质人同意转质，造成质押财产毁损、灭失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 434** If the pledgee, during the existence of the pledge, re-pledges the pledged property without the consent of the pledgor, causing damage or loss to the pledged property, the pledgee shall be liable for compensation.

<a id="art-435"></a>第四百三十五条　质权人可以放弃质权。债务人以自己的财产出质，质权人放弃该质权的，其他担保人在质权人丧失优先受偿权益的范围内免除担保责任，但是其他担保人承诺仍然提供担保的除外。

**Article 435** The pledgee may waive the pledge. If the debtor pledges his own property and the pledgee waives the pledge, the other sureties shall be exempted from the suretyship liability to the extent of the priority right to be paid that the pledgee loses, unless the other sureties promise to continue to provide the suretyship.

<a id="art-436"></a>第四百三十六条　债务人履行债务或者出质人提前清偿所担保的债权的，质权人应当返还质押财产。  
债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现质权的情形，质权人可以与出质人协议以质押财产折价，也可以就拍卖、变卖质押财产所得的价款优先受偿。  
质押财产折价或者变卖的，应当参照市场价格。

**Article 436** If the debtor performs his obligation or the pledgor pays in advance the secured claim, the pledgee shall return the pledged property.  
If the debtor fails to perform the obligation as it falls due or if any circumstance upon which the pledge is to be realized as agreed by the parties occurs, the pledgee may agree with the pledgor on the conversion of the pledged property into money, or be paid in priority out of the proceeds from the auction or sale of the pledged property.  
Where the pledged property is converted into money or sold, the market price shall be taken as a reference.

<a id="art-437"></a>第四百三十七条　出质人可以请求质权人在债务履行期限届满后及时行使质权；质权人不行使的，出质人可以请求人民法院拍卖、变卖质押财产。  
出质人请求质权人及时行使质权，因质权人怠于行使权利造成出质人损害的，由质权人承担赔偿责任。

**Article 437** The pledgor may request the pledgee, after the expiration of the time limit for the performance of the obligation, to exercise the pledge in a timely manner; if the pledgee fails to do so, the pledgor may request a people's court to auction or sell off the pledged property.  
If the pledgor requests the pledgee to exercise the pledge in a timely manner, and the pledgee causes damage to the pledgor due to his failure to do so, the pledgee shall be liable for compensation.

<a id="art-438"></a>第四百三十八条　质押财产折价或者拍卖、变卖后，其价款超过债权数额的部分归出质人所有，不足部分由债务人清偿。

**Article 438** After the pledged property has been converted into money, auctioned or sold, if the proceeds thereof exceed the amount of the claim, the balance shall be returned to the pledgor; if the proceeds thereof are insufficient, the debtor shall pay the balance.

<a id="art-439"></a>第四百三十九条　出质人与质权人可以协议设立最高额质权。  
最高额质权除适用本节有关规定外，参照适用本编第十七章第二节的有关规定。

**Article 439** The pledgor and the pledgee may agree to create a maximum pledge for floating claims.  
In addition to the relevant provisions of this Section, the relevant provisions of Section 2, Chapter 17 of this Part shall apply mutatis mutandis to a maximum pledge for floating claims.

#### 第二节　权利质权

<a id="art-440"></a>第四百四十条　债务人或者第三人有权处分的下列权利可以出质：  
（一）汇票、本票、支票；  
（二）债券、存款单；  
（三）仓单、提单；  
（四）可以转让的基金份额、股权；  
（五）可以转让的注册商标专用权、专利权、著作权等知识产权中的财产权；  
（六）现有的以及将有的应收账款；  
（七）法律、行政法规规定可以出质的其他财产权利。

**Article 440** The following rights that a debtor or a third party is entitled to dispose of may be pledged:  
(1) bills of exchange, promissory notes and cheques;  
(2) bonds and certificates of deposit;  
(3) warehouse receipts and bills of lading;  
(4) transferrable fund shares and equity interests;  
(5) property rights in intellectual property rights, such as the right to exclusive use of registered trademarks, patent rights, and copyrights, which are transferrable;  
(6) accounts receivable, existing and future; and (7) other property rights that may be pledged as provided by laws and administrative regulations.

<a id="art-441"></a>第四百四十一条　以汇票、本票、支票、债券、存款单、仓单、提单出质的，质权自权利凭证交付质权人时设立；没有权利凭证的，质权自办理出质登记时设立。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 441** Where a bill of exchange, promissory note, cheque, bond, certificate of deposit, warehouse receipt or bill of lading is pledged, the pledge right shall be established upon delivery of the title certificate to the pledgee; if there is no title certificate, the pledge right shall be established upon registration of the pledge. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-442"></a>第四百四十二条　汇票、本票、支票、债券、存款单、仓单、提单的兑现日期或者提货日期先于主债权到期的，质权人可以兑现或者提货，并与出质人协议将兑现的价款或者提取的货物提前清偿债务或者提存。

**Article 442** Where the date of payment or delivery of a bill of exchange, promissory note, cheque, bond, certificate of deposit, warehouse receipt or bill of lincoln falls prior to the maturity of the principal claim, the pledgee may cash or take delivery of the goods, and shall, through agreement with the pledgor, use the proceeds or the goods obtained to repay the debt in advance or deposit them with a competent authority.

<a id="art-443"></a>第四百四十三条　以基金份额、股权出质的，质权自办理出质登记时设立。  
基金份额、股权出质后，不得转让，但是出质人与质权人协商同意的除外。出质人转让基金份额、股权所得的价款，应当向质权人提前清偿债务或者提存。

**Article 443** Where fund units or shares are pledged, the pledge right shall be established upon registration of the pledge. After fund units or shares are pledged, they may not be transferred, except where the pledgor and the pledgee so agree through consultation. The proceeds obtained by the pledgor from the transfer of fund units or shares shall be used to repay the debt to the pledgee in advance or be deposited with a competent authority.

<a id="art-444"></a>第四百四十四条　以注册商标专用权、专利权、著作权等知识产权中的财产权出质的，质权自办理出质登记时设立。  
知识产权中的财产权出质后，出质人不得转让或者许可他人使用，但是出质人与质权人协商同意的除外。出质人转让或者许可他人使用出质的知识产权中的财产权所得的价款，应当向质权人提前清偿债务或者提存。

**Article 444** Where property rights in intellectual property such as the right to exclusive use of a registered trademark, patent right or copyright are pledged, the pledge right shall be established upon registration of the pledge. After property rights in intellectual property are pledged, the pledgor may not transfer or license the use thereof to others, except where the pledgor and the pledgee so agree through consultation. The proceeds obtained by the pledgor from the transfer or licensing of the pledged property rights in intellectual property shall be used to repay the debt to the pledgee in advance or be deposited with a competent authority.

<a id="art-445"></a>第四百四十五条　以应收账款出质的，质权自办理出质登记时设立。  
应收账款出质后，不得转让，但是出质人与质权人协商同意的除外。出质人转让应收账款所得的价款，应当向质权人提前清偿债务或者提存。

**Article 445** Where an account receivable is pledged, the pledge right shall be established upon registration of the pledge. After an account receivable is pledged, it may not be transferred, except where the pledgor and the pledgee so agree through consultation. The proceeds obtained by the pledgor from the transfer of the account receivable shall be used to repay the debt to the pledgee in advance or be deposited with a competent authority.

<a id="art-446"></a>第四百四十六条　权利质权除适用本节规定外，适用本章第一节的有关规定。

**Article 446** In addition to the provisions of this Section, the relevant provisions of Section 1 of this Chapter shall apply to the pledge of rights.

### 第十九章　留置权

<a id="art-447"></a>第四百四十七条　债务人不履行到期债务，债权人可以留置已经合法占有的债务人的动产，并有权就该动产优先受偿。  
前款规定的债权人为留置权人，占有的动产为留置财产。

**Article 447** Where the debtor fails to perform its obligations that have fallen due, the creditor may retain the debtor's movables which are already in the creditor's lawful possession, and shall be entitled to seek satisfaction in priority out of such movables.  
The creditor specified in the preceding paragraph shall be the lienholder, and the movables in possession shall be the retained property.

<a id="art-448"></a>第四百四十八条　债权人留置的动产，应当与债权属于同一法律关系，但是企业之间留置的除外。

**Article 448** The movables retained by the creditor shall be under the same legal relationship with the claim, except for retention between enterprises.

<a id="art-449"></a>第四百四十九条　法律规定或者当事人约定不得留置的动产，不得留置。

**Article 449** Movables which shall not be retained under the provisions of the law or the agreement of the parties may not be retained.

<a id="art-450"></a>第四百五十条　留置财产为可分物的，留置财产的价值应当相当于债务的金额。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-38" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第38条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 450** Where the retained property is divisible, the value of the retained property shall correspond to the amount of the debt.

<a id="art-451"></a>第四百五十一条　留置权人负有妥善保管留置财产的义务；因保管不善致使留置财产毁损、灭失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 451** The lienholder shall have the duty to properly keep the retained property, and shall bear the liability for compensation if the retained property is damaged, destroyed or lost due to improper keeping.

<a id="art-452"></a>第四百五十二条　留置权人有权收取留置财产的孳息。  
前款规定的孳息应当先充抵收取孳息的费用。

**Article 452** The lienholder shall be entitled to collect the fruits of the retained property.  
The fruits specified in the preceding paragraph shall first be applied to cover the expenses for their collection.

<a id="art-453"></a>第四百五十三条　留置权人与债务人应当约定留置财产后的债务履行期限；没有约定或者约定不明确的，留置权人应当给债务人六十日以上履行债务的期限，但是鲜活易腐等不易保管的动产除外。债务人逾期未履行的，留置权人可以与债务人协议以留置财产折价，也可以就拍卖、变卖留置财产所得的价款优先受偿。  
留置财产折价或者变卖的，应当参照市场价格。

**Article 453** The lienholder and the debtor shall agree on the period for performing the debt after the property has been retained; if there is no such agreement or the agreement is unclear, the lienholder shall give the debtor a period of not less than sixty days to perform the debt, except where it involves fresh, perishable or other movables that are difficult to keep. If the debtor still fails to perform at the expiry of such period, the lienholder may reach an agreement with the debtor on the conversion of the retained property into a sum of money as payment, or may seek satisfaction in priority out of the proceeds from the auction or sale of the retained property. Where the retained property is converted into a sum of money or sold, the market price shall be taken as a reference.

<a id="art-454"></a>第四百五十四条　债务人可以请求留置权人在债务履行期限届满后行使留置权；留置权人不行使的，债务人可以请求人民法院拍卖、变卖留置财产。

**Article 454** The debtor may request the lienholder to exercise the lien after the debt performance period has expired; if the lienholder fails to do so, the debtor may request the People's Court to auction or sell the retained property.

<a id="art-455"></a>第四百五十五条　留置财产折价或者拍卖、变卖后，其价款超过债权数额的部分归债务人所有，不足部分由债务人清偿。

**Article 455** After the retained property is converted into a sum of money or auctioned or sold, the portion of the proceeds exceeding the amount of the claim shall belong to the debtor, and any insufficiency shall be satisfied by the debtor.

<a id="art-456"></a>第四百五十六条　同一动产上已经设立抵押权或者质权，该动产又被留置的，留置权人优先受偿。

**Article 456** Where a mortgage or pledge has been established on the same movable, and the movable is retained afterwards, the lienholder shall have priority in satisfaction.

<a id="art-457"></a>第四百五十七条　留置权人对留置财产丧失占有或者留置权人接受债务人另行提供担保的，留置权消灭。  
第五分编　占　　有

**Article 457** Where the lienholder loses possession of the retained property or the lienholder accepts other security provided separately by the debtor, the lien shall extinguish.  
Part Five: Possession

### 第二十章　占有

<a id="art-458"></a>第四百五十八条　基于合同关系等产生的占有，有关不动产或者动产的使用、收益、违约责任等，按照合同约定；合同没有约定或者约定不明确的，依照有关法律规定。

**Article 458** For possession arising from a contractual relationship or other relationships, matters such as the use, earnings, and liability for breach of contract concerning immovable or movable property shall be governed by the contract; if there is no such provision in the contract or the provision is unclear, the relevant laws shall apply.

<a id="art-459"></a>第四百五十九条　占有人因使用占有的不动产或者动产，致使该不动产或者动产受到损害的，恶意占有人应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 459** Where the possessor, by using the immovable or movable property in its possession, causes damage to such property, a malicious possessor shall bear the liability for compensation.

<a id="art-460"></a>第四百六十条　不动产或者动产被占有人占有的，权利人可以请求返还原物及其孳息；但是，应当支付善意占有人因维护该不动产或者动产支出的必要费用。

**Article 460** Where immovable or movable property is possessed by a possessor, the right holder may request the return of the original property and its fruits; provided, however, that the right holder shall pay the necessary expenses incurred by the good-faith possessor in maintaining such immovable or movable property.

<a id="art-461"></a>第四百六十一条　占有的不动产或者动产毁损、灭失，该不动产或者动产的权利人请求赔偿的，占有人应当将因毁损、灭失取得的保险金、赔偿金或者补偿金等返还给权利人；权利人的损害未得到足够弥补的，恶意占有人还应当赔偿损失。

**Article 461** Where an immovable or movable property in possession is damaged or lost, and the right holder of such immovable or movable property claims compensation, the possessor shall return to the right holder any insurance proceeds, compensation or indemnity obtained due to the damage or loss. Where the damage to the right holder is not sufficiently compensated, the possessor in bad faith shall also compensate for the loss.

<a id="art-462"></a>第四百六十二条　占有的不动产或者动产被侵占的，占有人有权请求返还原物；对妨害占有的行为，占有人有权请求排除妨害或者消除危险；因侵占或者妨害造成损害的，占有人有权依法请求损害赔偿。  
占有人返还原物的请求权，自侵占发生之日起一年内未行使的，该请求权消灭。

**Article 462** Where an immovable or movable property in possession is encroached upon, the possessor shall have the right to request return of the original property. Where possession is obstructed, the possessor shall have the right to request removal of the obstruction or elimination of the danger. Where damage is caused by the encroachment or obstruction, the possessor shall have the right to request compensation for the damage according to law. Where the possessor's right to request return of the original property is not exercised within one year from the date of the encroachment, such right shall be extinguished.

## 第三编　合同

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-463"></a>第四百六十三条　本编调整因合同产生的民事关系。

**Article 463** This Part regulates civil relations arising from contracts.

<a id="art-464"></a>第四百六十四条　合同是民事主体之间设立、变更、终止民事法律关系的协议。  
婚姻、收养、监护等有关身份关系的协议，适用有关该身份关系的法律规定；没有规定的，可以根据其性质参照适用本编规定。

**Article 464** A contract is an agreement between civil subjects to establish, modify or terminate a civil legal relationship. Agreements concerning personal status relations, such as marriage, adoption and guardianship, shall be governed by the legal provisions concerning such personal status relations. In the absence of such provisions, the provisions of this Part may be applied by reference based on the nature of such agreements.

<a id="art-465"></a>第四百六十五条　依法成立的合同，受法律保护。  
依法成立的合同，仅对当事人具有法律约束力，但是法律另有规定的除外。

**Article 465** A contract lawfully formed shall be protected by law.  
A contract lawfully formed shall be legally binding only on the parties thereto, unless otherwise provided by law.

<a id="art-466"></a>第四百六十六条　当事人对合同条款的理解有争议的，应当依据[本法第一百四十二条](#art-142)第一款的规定，确定争议条款的含义。  
合同文本采用两种以上文字订立并约定具有同等效力的，对各文本使用的词句推定具有相同含义。各文本使用的词句不一致的，应当根据合同的相关条款、性质、目的以及诚信原则等予以解释。

**Article 466** Where the parties dispute the understanding of a contract clause, the meaning of the disputed clause shall be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 142, paragraph 1 of this Law. Where a contract is concluded in two or more languages and it is agreed that all versions are equally authentic, the words and sentences used in each version shall be presumed to have the same meaning. Where the words and sentences used in each version are inconsistent, the contract shall be interpreted in light of the relevant clauses, the nature and purpose of the contract, and the principle of good faith.

<a id="art-467"></a>第四百六十七条　本法或者其他法律没有明文规定的合同，适用本编通则的规定，并可以参照适用本编或者其他法律最相类似合同的规定。  
在中华人民共和国境内履行的中外合资经营企业合同、中外合作经营企业合同、中外合作勘探开发自然资源合同，适用中华人民共和国法律。

**Article 467** Contracts not expressly provided for in this Law or other laws shall be governed by the general provisions of this Part, and may be governed by reference to the provisions on the most similar type of contract under this Part or other laws.  
Contracts for Sino-foreign equity joint venture enterprises, contracts for Sino-foreign contractual joint venture enterprises, and contracts for Sino-foreign cooperative exploration and development of natural resources performed within the territory of the People's Republic of China shall be governed by the laws of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-468"></a>第四百六十八条　非因合同产生的债权债务关系，适用有关该债权债务关系的法律规定；没有规定的，适用本编通则的有关规定，但是根据其性质不能适用的除外。

**Article 468** Obligatory rights and obligations relations not arising from a contract shall be governed by the legal provisions concerning such obligatory rights and obligations relations. In the absence of such provisions, the relevant provisions of the general provisions of this Part shall apply, unless they cannot be applied due to the nature of such relations.

### 第二章　合同的订立

<a id="art-469"></a>第四百六十九条　当事人订立合同，可以采用书面形式、口头形式或者其他形式。  
书面形式是合同书、信件、电报、电传、传真等可以有形地表现所载内容的形式。  
以电子数据交换、电子邮件等方式能够有形地表现所载内容，并可以随时调取查用的数据电文，视为书面形式。

**Article 469** The parties may conclude a contract in written, oral or other forms.  
A written form refers to a form which can tangibly express the content contained therein, such as a contract instrument, letter, telegram, telex or facsimile.  
A data message in the form of electronic data interchange or email, among others, which can tangibly express the content contained therein and can be accessed for reference at any time, shall be deemed a written form.

<a id="art-470"></a>第四百七十条　合同的内容由当事人约定，一般包括下列条款：  
（一）当事人的姓名或者名称和住所；  
（二）标的；  
（三）数量；  
（四）质量；  
（五）价款或者报酬；  
（六）履行期限、地点和方式；  
（七）违约责任；  
（八）解决争议的方法。  
当事人可以参照各类合同的示范文本订立合同。

**Article 470** The content of a contract shall be agreed upon by the parties and generally includes the following clauses:  
(1) the names or titles and domiciles of the parties;  
(2) the subject matter;  
(3) the quantity;  
(4) the quality;  
(5) the price or remuneration;  
(6) the time limit, place and method of performance;  
(7) liability for breach of contract; and (8) the method for dispute resolution. The parties may conclude a contract by reference to model texts of various types of contracts.

<a id="art-471"></a>第四百七十一条　当事人订立合同，可以采取要约、承诺方式或者其他方式。

**Article 471** The parties may conclude a contract by means of an offer and acceptance or by other means.

<a id="art-472"></a>第四百七十二条　要约是希望与他人订立合同的意思表示，该意思表示应当符合下列条件：  
（一）内容具体确定；  
（二）表明经受要约人承诺，要约人即受该意思表示约束。

**Article 472** An offer is a manifestation of intent to conclude a contract with another person, and such manifestation of intent shall meet the following conditions:  
(1) the content is specific and definite; and (2) it indicates that the offeror shall be bound by the manifestation of intent upon acceptance by the offeree.

<a id="art-473"></a>第四百七十三条　要约邀请是希望他人向自己发出要约的表示。拍卖公告、招标公告、招股说明书、债券募集办法、基金招募说明书、商业广告和宣传、寄送的价目表等为要约邀请。  
商业广告和宣传的内容符合要约条件的，构成要约。

**Article 473** An invitation to offer is a manifestation by which a person invites another person to make an offer to him or her. Auction announcements, tender announcements, prospectuses, bond offering memoranda, fund prospectuses, commercial advertisements and promotions, and mailed price lists, among others, shall constitute invitations to offer. Where the content of a commercial advertisement or promotion meets the conditions for an offer, it shall constitute an offer.

<a id="art-474"></a>第四百七十四条　要约生效的时间适用[本法第一百三十七条](#art-137)的规定。

**Article 474** The time when an offer takes effect shall be subject to the provisions of Article 137 of this Law.

<a id="art-475"></a>第四百七十五条　要约可以撤回。要约的撤回适用[本法第一百四十一条](#art-141)的规定。

**Article 475** An offer may be withdrawn. The withdrawal of an offer shall be subject to the provisions of Article 141 of this Law.

<a id="art-476"></a>第四百七十六条　要约可以撤销，但是有下列情形之一的除外：  
（一）要约人以确定承诺期限或者其他形式明示要约不可撤销；  
（二）受要约人有理由认为要约是不可撤销的，并已经为履行合同做了合理准备工作。

**Article 476** An offer may be revoked, except under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the offeror expressly states, by fixing a time limit for acceptance or otherwise, that the offer is irrevocable  
(2) the offeree has reason to believe that the offer is irrevocable and has made reasonable preparations for performing the contract.

<a id="art-477"></a>第四百七十七条　撤销要约的意思表示以对话方式作出的，该意思表示的内容应当在受要约人作出承诺之前为受要约人所知道；撤销要约的意思表示以非对话方式作出的，应当在受要约人作出承诺之前到达受要约人。

**Article 477** Where the manifestation of intent to revoke an offer is made orally, the content of such manifestation of intent shall be known to the offeree before the offeree makes an acceptance. Where the manifestation of intent to revoke an offer is made by non-oral means, it shall reach the offeree before the offeree makes an acceptance.

<a id="art-478"></a>第四百七十八条　有下列情形之一的，要约失效：  
（一）要约被拒绝；  
（二）要约被依法撤销；  
（三）承诺期限届满，受要约人未作出承诺；  
（四）受要约人对要约的内容作出实质性变更。

**Article 478** An offer shall cease to be effective under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the offer is rejected;  
(2) the offer is revoked according to law;  
(3) the time limit for acceptance expires and the offeree has not made an acceptance  
(4) the offeree makes a material modification to the content of the offer.

<a id="art-479"></a>第四百七十九条　承诺是受要约人同意要约的意思表示。

**Article 479** An acceptance is a manifestation of intent by the offeree to assent to the offer.

<a id="art-480"></a>第四百八十条　承诺应当以通知的方式作出；但是，根据交易习惯或者要约表明可以通过行为作出承诺的除外。

**Article 480** An acceptance shall be made by means of a notice, unless, according to trade practices or the offer, the acceptance may be made by performing an act.

<a id="art-481"></a>第四百八十一条　承诺应当在要约确定的期限内到达要约人。  
要约没有确定承诺期限的，承诺应当依照下列规定到达：  
（一）要约以对话方式作出的，应当即时作出承诺；  
（二）要约以非对话方式作出的，承诺应当在合理期限内到达。

**Article 481** An acceptance shall reach the offeror within the time limit specified in the offer. If the offer does not specify a time limit for acceptance, the acceptance shall reach the offeror in accordance with the following provisions:  
(1) If the offer is made in the form of a dialogue, the acceptance shall be made immediately;  
(2) If the offer is made in a non-dialogue form, the acceptance shall reach the offeror within a reasonable time.

<a id="art-482"></a>第四百八十二条　要约以信件或者电报作出的，承诺期限自信件载明的日期或者电报交发之日开始计算。信件未载明日期的，自投寄该信件的邮戳日期开始计算。要约以电话、传真、电子邮件等快速通讯方式作出的，承诺期限自要约到达受要约人时开始计算。

**Article 482** If an offer is made by letter or telegram, the time limit for acceptance shall begin to run from the date shown in the letter or the date of dispatch of the telegram. If the letter does not show a date, the time limit shall run from the postmark date of the letter. If the offer is made by means of instantaneous communication such as telephone, facsimile, or electronic mail, the time limit for acceptance shall begin to run from the moment the offer reaches the offeree.

<a id="art-483"></a>第四百八十三条　承诺生效时合同成立，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 483** A contract is formed when the acceptance becomes effective, except as otherwise provided by law or agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-484"></a>第四百八十四条　以通知方式作出的承诺，生效的时间适用[本法第一百三十七条](#art-137)的规定。  
承诺不需要通知的，根据交易习惯或者要约的要求作出承诺的行为时生效。

**Article 484** The time when an acceptance made by means of a notice becomes effective shall be governed by the provisions of Article 137 of this Law.  
If an acceptance does not require a notice, it becomes effective when the act of acceptance is performed in accordance with trade usage or the requirements of the offer.

<a id="art-485"></a>第四百八十五条　承诺可以撤回。承诺的撤回适用[本法第一百四十一条](#art-141)的规定。

**Article 485** An acceptance may be withdrawn. The withdrawal of an acceptance shall be governed by the provisions of Article 141 of this Law.

<a id="art-486"></a>第四百八十六条　受要约人超过承诺期限发出承诺，或者在承诺期限内发出承诺，按照通常情形不能及时到达要约人的，为新要约；但是，要约人及时通知受要约人该承诺有效的除外。

**Article 486** If an offeree dispatches an acceptance after the time limit for acceptance has expired, or dispatches an acceptance within the time limit but under normal circumstances it would not reach the offeror in due time, such acceptance constitutes a new offer; unless the offeror promptly informs the offeree that the acceptance is effective.

<a id="art-487"></a>第四百八十七条　受要约人在承诺期限内发出承诺，按照通常情形能够及时到达要约人，但是因其他原因致使承诺到达要约人时超过承诺期限的，除要约人及时通知受要约人因承诺超过期限不接受该承诺外，该承诺有效。

**Article 487** If an offeree dispatches an acceptance within the time limit for acceptance, and under normal circumstances it would reach the offeror in due time, but the acceptance reaches the offeror after the time limit has expired due to other reasons, the acceptance shall be effective unless the offeror promptly informs the offeree that the acceptance is not accepted because it has exceeded the time limit.

<a id="art-488"></a>第四百八十八条　承诺的内容应当与要约的内容一致。受要约人对要约的内容作出实质性变更的，为新要约。有关合同标的、数量、质量、价款或者报酬、履行期限、履行地点和方式、违约责任和解决争议方法等的变更，是对要约内容的实质性变更。

**Article 488** The content of an acceptance shall be consistent with the content of the offer. If the offeree materially alters the content of the offer, such acceptance constitutes a new offer. A material alteration to the content of the offer includes, among others, a change in the subject matter, quantity, quality, price or remuneration, time limit for performance, place and method of performance, liability for breach of contract, and method of dispute resolution.

<a id="art-489"></a>第四百八十九条　承诺对要约的内容作出非实质性变更的，除要约人及时表示反对或者要约表明承诺不得对要约的内容作出任何变更外，该承诺有效，合同的内容以承诺的内容为准。

**Article 489** If an acceptance makes immaterial alterations to the content of the offer, the acceptance shall be effective and the content of the contract shall be the content of the acceptance, unless the offeror promptly objects to such alterations or the offer indicates that the acceptance shall not make any alteration to the content of the offer.

<a id="art-490"></a>第四百九十条　当事人采用合同书形式订立合同的，自当事人均签名、盖章或者按指印时合同成立。在签名、盖章或者按指印之前，当事人一方已经履行主要义务，对方接受时，该合同成立。  
法律、行政法规规定或者当事人约定合同应当采用书面形式订立，当事人未采用书面形式但是一方已经履行主要义务，对方接受时，该合同成立。

**Article 490** If the parties conclude a contract in the form of a written agreement, the contract is formed when all parties have signed, affixed their seals, or affixed their fingerprints. Before signing, affixing seals, or affixing fingerprints, if one party has already performed the principal obligation and the other party accepts it, the contract is formed. If laws or administrative regulations provide, or the parties agree, that a contract shall be concluded in written form, and the parties do not adopt the written form, but one party has already performed the principal obligation and the other party accepts it, the contract is formed.

<a id="art-491"></a>第四百九十一条　当事人采用信件、数据电文等形式订立合同要求签订确认书的，签订确认书时合同成立。  
当事人一方通过互联网等信息网络发布的商品或者服务信息符合要约条件的，对方选择该商品或者服务并提交订单成功时合同成立，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 491** If the parties conclude a contract in the form of letters, data messages, etc., and require a confirmation letter to be signed, the contract is formed when the confirmation letter is signed.  
If the information on goods or services published by one party through the Internet or other information networks satisfies the conditions for an offer, and the other party selects such goods or services and successfully submits the order, the contract is formed, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-492"></a>第四百九十二条　承诺生效的地点为合同成立的地点。  
采用数据电文形式订立合同的，收件人的主营业地为合同成立的地点；没有主营业地的，其住所地为合同成立的地点。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。

**Article 492** The place where the acceptance becomes effective is the place where the contract is formed. If the contract is concluded in the form of data messages, the principal place of business of the recipient is the place where the contract is formed; if there is no principal place of business, the domicile of the recipient is the place where the contract is formed. If the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail.

<a id="art-493"></a>第四百九十三条　当事人采用合同书形式订立合同的，最后签名、盖章或者按指印的地点为合同成立的地点，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 493** If the parties conclude a contract in the form of a written agreement, the place where the last party signs, affixes its seal, or affixes its fingerprint is the place where the contract is formed, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-494"></a>第四百九十四条　国家根据抢险救灾、疫情防控或者其他需要下达国家订货任务、指令性任务的，有关民事主体之间应当依照有关法律、行政法规规定的权利和义务订立合同。  
依照法律、行政法规的规定负有发出要约义务的当事人，应当及时发出合理的要约。  
依照法律、行政法规的规定负有作出承诺义务的当事人，不得拒绝对方合理的订立合同要求。

**Article 494** If the State issues national procurement orders or mandatory tasks for emergency rescue and disaster relief, epidemic prevention and control, or other needs, the relevant civil subjects shall conclude contracts in accordance with the rights and obligations stipulated by relevant laws and administrative regulations.  
A party that is obliged to make an offer in accordance with the provisions of laws or administrative regulations shall promptly dispatch a reasonable offer.  
A party that is obliged to accept an offer in accordance with the provisions of laws or administrative regulations shall not refuse the other party's reasonable request to conclude a contract.

<a id="art-495"></a>第四百九十五条　当事人约定在将来一定期限内订立合同的认购书、订购书、预订书等，构成预约合同。  
当事人一方不履行预约合同约定的订立合同义务的，对方可以请求其承担预约合同的违约责任。

**Article 495** A subscription letter, order letter, booking letter, or the like, in which the parties agree to enter into a contract within a certain period in the future, constitutes a preliminary contract.  
If one party fails to perform the obligation to enter into a contract as agreed in the preliminary contract, the other party may request that party bear the liability for breach of the preliminary contract.

<a id="art-496"></a>第四百九十六条　格式条款是当事人为了重复使用而预先拟定，并在订立合同时未与对方协商的条款。  
采用格式条款订立合同的，提供格式条款的一方应当遵循公平原则确定当事人之间的权利和义务，并采取合理的方式提示对方注意免除或者减轻其责任等与对方有重大利害关系的条款，按照对方的要求，对该条款予以说明。提供格式条款的一方未履行提示或者说明义务，致使对方没有注意或者理解与其有重大利害关系的条款的，对方可以主张该条款不成为合同的内容。

**Article 496** Standard terms are clauses that are prepared in advance by a party for repeated use and are not subject to negotiation with the other party at the time of concluding the contract. If a contract is concluded using standard terms, the party providing the standard terms shall determine the rights and obligations between the parties in accordance with the principle of fairness, and shall take reasonable means to draw the other party's attention to the clauses that have a significant bearing on the other party's interest, such as clauses that exclude or limit the providing party's liability, and shall explain such clauses upon the other party's request. If the party providing the standard terms fails to perform the obligation of drawing attention or explanation, thereby causing the other party not to notice or understand a clause that has a significant bearing on the other party's interest, the other party may claim that such clause does not become part of the contract.

<a id="art-497"></a>第四百九十七条　有下列情形之一的，该格式条款无效：  
（一）具有本法第一编第六章第三节和[本法第五百零六条](#art-506)规定的无效情形；  
（二）提供格式条款一方不合理地免除或者减轻其责任、加重对方责任、限制对方主要权利；  
（三）提供格式条款一方排除对方主要权利。

**Article 497** Standard terms are void under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) The circumstances for invalidity provided in Section 3 of Chapter 6 of Part One of this Law and Article 506 of this Law exist;  
(2) The party providing the standard terms unreasonably excludes or limits its own liability, increases the other party's liability, or restricts the other party's principal rights;  
(3) The party providing the standard terms excludes the other party's principal rights.

<a id="art-498"></a>第四百九十八条　对格式条款的理解发生争议的，应当按照通常理解予以解释。对格式条款有两种以上解释的，应当作出不利于提供格式条款一方的解释。格式条款和非格式条款不一致的，应当采用非格式条款。

**Article 498** If a dispute arises over the understanding of a standard term, such term shall be interpreted according to the ordinary understanding. If there are two or more interpretations of a standard term, an interpretation unfavorable to the party providing the standard terms shall be adopted. If a standard term is inconsistent with a non-standard term, the non-standard term shall prevail.

<a id="art-499"></a>第四百九十九条　悬赏人以公开方式声明对完成特定行为的人支付报酬的，完成该行为的人可以请求其支付。

**Article 499** If a reward offeror publicly declares that it shall pay a reward to a person who completes a specific act, the person who completes such act may request the reward.

<a id="art-500"></a>第五百条　当事人在订立合同过程中有下列情形之一，造成对方损失的，应当承担赔偿责任：  
（一）假借订立合同，恶意进行磋商；  
（二）故意隐瞒与订立合同有关的重要事实或者提供虚假情况；  
（三）有其他违背诚信原则的行为。

**Article 500** A party shall bear compensation liability if, during the process of concluding a contract, it causes losses to the other party under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) Negatively conducting negotiations under the pretext of concluding a contract;  
(2) Intentionally concealing material facts related to the conclusion of the contract or providing false information;  
(3) Having other acts that violate the principle of good faith.

<a id="art-501"></a>第五百零一条　当事人在订立合同过程中知悉的商业秘密或者其他应当保密的信息，无论合同是否成立，不得泄露或者不正当地使用；泄露、不正当地使用该商业秘密或者信息，造成对方损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 501** Where a party knows trade secrets or other information that shall be kept confidential during the process of concluding a contract, it shall not disclose or improperly use such trade secrets or information, regardless of whether the contract is established; if the disclosure or improper use of such trade secrets or information causes loss to the other party, it shall bear compensation liability.

### 第三章　合同的效力

<a id="art-502"></a>第五百零二条　依法成立的合同，自成立时生效，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。  
依照法律、行政法规的规定，合同应当办理批准等手续的，依照其规定。未办理批准等手续影响合同生效的，不影响合同中履行报批等义务条款以及相关条款的效力。应当办理申请批准等手续的当事人未履行义务的，对方可以请求其承担违反该义务的责任。  
依照法律、行政法规的规定，合同的变更、转让、解除等情形应当办理批准等手续的，适用前款规定。

**Article 502** A contract lawfully formed shall take effect upon its formation, unless otherwise provided by law or otherwise agreed by the parties. Where, according to the provisions of laws and administrative regulations, a contract shall undergo approval formalities or other such formalities, those provisions shall apply. The failure to undergo approval formalities that affects the contract's taking effect shall not affect the validity of the clauses on obligations to submit for approval and related clauses in the contract. If a party who shall undergo formalities such as applying for approval fails to perform its obligations, the other party may request it to bear liability for breaching such obligations. Where, according to the provisions of laws and administrative regulations, the modification, transfer, or dissolution of a contract, among other circumstances, shall undergo approval formalities or other such formalities, the preceding paragraph shall apply.

<a id="art-503"></a>第五百零三条　无权代理人以被代理人的名义订立合同，被代理人已经开始履行合同义务或者接受相对人履行的，视为对合同的追认。

**Article 503** Where an agent without authority concludes a contract in the name of the principal, and the principal has begun to perform the contractual obligations or has accepted the performance by the other party, the contract shall be deemed to have been ratified.

<a id="art-504"></a>第五百零四条　法人的法定代表人或者非法人组织的负责人超越权限订立的合同，除相对人知道或者应当知道其超越权限外，该代表行为有效，订立的合同对法人或者非法人组织发生效力。

**Article 504** Where a contract is concluded by the legal representative of a legal person or the responsible person of an unincorporated organization acting beyond their authority, such representational act shall be effective, and the contract concluded shall be binding on the legal person or unincorporated organization, unless the other party knew or should have known that they were acting beyond their authority.

<a id="art-505"></a>第五百零五条　当事人超越经营范围订立的合同的效力，应当依照本法第一编第六章第三节和本编的有关规定确定，不得仅以超越经营范围确认合同无效。

**Article 505** The validity of a contract concluded by a party beyond its scope of business shall be determined in accordance with the provisions of Section 3, Chapter VI, Part One of this Law and the relevant provisions of this Part; the contract shall not be deemed void solely on the ground of being beyond the scope of business.

<a id="art-506"></a>第五百零六条　合同中的下列免责条款无效：  
（一）造成对方人身损害的；  
（二）因故意或者重大过失造成对方财产损失的。

**Article 506** The following exculpatory clauses in a contract shall be void:  
(1) those causing personal injury to the other party  
(2) those causing property loss to the other party due to intentional act or gross negligence.

<a id="art-507"></a>第五百零七条　合同不生效、无效、被撤销或者终止的，不影响合同中有关解决争议方法的条款的效力。

**Article 507** The ineffectiveness, voidness, revocation, or termination of a contract shall not affect the validity of the clauses in the contract concerning the methods for dispute resolution.

<a id="art-508"></a>第五百零八条　本编对合同的效力没有规定的，适用本法第一编第六章的有关规定。

**Article 508** Where this Part has no provisions on the validity of a contract, the relevant provisions of Chapter VI, Part One of this Law shall apply.

### 第四章　合同的履行

<a id="art-509"></a>第五百零九条　当事人应当按照约定全面履行自己的义务。  
当事人应当遵循诚信原则，根据合同的性质、目的和交易习惯履行通知、协助、保密等义务。  
当事人在履行合同过程中，应当避免浪费资源、污染环境和破坏生态。

**Article 509** The parties shall fully perform their obligations in accordance with the agreement.  
The parties shall follow the principle of good faith and perform the obligations of notification, assistance, and confidentiality, among others, in accordance with the nature, purpose, and trade practices of the contract.  
During the performance of the contract, the parties shall avoid wasting resources, polluting the environment, and damaging the ecosystem.

<a id="art-510"></a>第五百一十条　合同生效后，当事人就质量、价款或者报酬、履行地点等内容没有约定或者约定不明确的，可以协议补充；不能达成补充协议的，按照合同相关条款或者交易习惯确定。

**Article 510** After the contract takes effect, if the parties have not agreed or have not clearly agreed on matters such as quality, price or remuneration, or place of performance, they may enter into a supplementary agreement; if a supplementary agreement cannot be reached, the contract shall be determined in accordance with the relevant clauses of the contract or trade practices.

<a id="art-511"></a>第五百一十一条　当事人就有关合同内容约定不明确，依据前条规定仍不能确定的，适用下列规定：  
（一）质量要求不明确的，按照强制性国家标准履行；没有强制性国家标准的，按照推荐性国家标准履行；没有推荐性国家标准的，按照行业标准履行；没有国家标准、行业标准的，按照通常标准或者符合合同目的的特定标准履行。  
（二）价款或者报酬不明确的，按照订立合同时履行地的市场价格履行；依法应当执行政府定价或者政府指导价的，依照规定履行。  
（三）履行地点不明确，给付货币的，在接受货币一方所在地履行；交付不动产的，在不动产所在地履行；其他标的，在履行义务一方所在地履行。  
（四）履行期限不明确的，债务人可以随时履行，债权人也可以随时请求履行，但是应当给对方必要的准备时间。  
（五）履行方式不明确的，按照有利于实现合同目的的方式履行。  
（六）履行费用的负担不明确的，由履行义务一方负担；因债权人原因增加的履行费用，由债权人负担。

**Article 511** Where the parties have not clearly agreed on the relevant contents of the contract and the matter cannot be determined in accordance with the preceding article, the following provisions shall apply:  
(1) if the quality requirement is unclear, performance shall be in accordance with mandatory national standards; in the absence of mandatory national standards, performance shall be in accordance with recommended national standards; in the absence of recommended national standards, performance shall be in accordance with industry standards; in the absence of national standards or industry standards, performance shall be in accordance with ordinary standards or specific standards consistent with the purpose of the contract;
