(2) if the price or remuneration is unclear, performance shall be in accordance with the market price at the place of performance at the time of conclusion of the contract; if government-set prices or government-guided prices are to be implemented according to law, performance shall be in accordance with such provisions;  
(3) if the place of performance is unclear, where the subject matter is payment of money, performance shall be at the place of the party receiving the money; where the subject matter is delivery of immovable property, performance shall be at the place where the immovable property is located; for any other subject matter, performance shall be at the place of the party performing the obligation;  
(4) if the time limit for performance is unclear, the debtor may perform at any time, and the creditor may demand performance at any time, but a reasonable period necessary for preparation shall be given to the other party;  
(5) if the method of performance is unclear, performance shall be in a manner conducive to achieving the purpose of the contract; and (6) if the burden of performance expenses is unclear, the party performing the obligation shall bear the expenses; expenses increased due to the cause of the creditor shall be borne by the creditor.

<a id="art-512"></a>第五百一十二条　通过互联网等信息网络订立的电子合同的标的为交付商品并采用快递物流方式交付的，收货人的签收时间为交付时间。电子合同的标的为提供服务的，生成的电子凭证或者实物凭证中载明的时间为提供服务时间；前述凭证没有载明时间或者载明时间与实际提供服务时间不一致的，以实际提供服务的时间为准。  
电子合同的标的物为采用在线传输方式交付的，合同标的物进入对方当事人指定的特定系统且能够检索识别的时间为交付时间。  
电子合同当事人对交付商品或者提供服务的方式、时间另有约定的，按照其约定。

**Article 512** Where the subject matter of an electronic contract concluded through the Internet or other information networks is the delivery of goods and is delivered by express logistics, the time of receipt by the consignee shall be the time of delivery. Where the subject matter of an electronic contract is the provision of services, the time indicated in the generated electronic voucher or physical voucher shall be the time of service provision; if the voucher does not indicate the time or the indicated time is inconsistent with the actual time of service provision, the actual time of service provision shall prevail. Where the subject matter of an electronic contract is delivered by online transmission, the time at which the subject matter of the contract enters the specific system designated by the other party and can be retrieved and identified shall be the time of delivery. Where the parties to an electronic contract have otherwise agreed on the method or time of delivery of goods or provision of services, such agreement shall be followed.

<a id="art-513"></a>第五百一十三条　执行政府定价或者政府指导价的，在合同约定的交付期限内政府价格调整时，按照交付时的价格计价。逾期交付标的物的，遇价格上涨时，按照原价格执行；价格下降时，按照新价格执行。逾期提取标的物或者逾期付款的，遇价格上涨时，按照新价格执行；价格下降时，按照原价格执行。

**Article 513** Where government-set prices or government-guided prices are implemented, if the government adjusts the prices within the delivery period agreed in the contract, the prices at the time of delivery shall be used for pricing. Where the subject matter is delivered late, if the price increases, the original price shall apply; if the price decreases, the new price shall apply. Where the subject matter is collected late or payment is made late, if the price increases, the new price shall apply; if the price decreases, the original price shall apply.

<a id="art-514"></a>第五百一十四条　以支付金钱为内容的债，除法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定外，债权人可以请求债务人以实际履行地的法定货币履行。

**Article 514** For an obligation with the content of paying money, unless otherwise provided by law or otherwise agreed by the parties, the creditor may request the debtor to perform in the legal tender of the place of actual performance.

<a id="art-515"></a>第五百一十五条　标的有多项而债务人只需履行其中一项的，债务人享有选择权；但是，法律另有规定、当事人另有约定或者另有交易习惯的除外。  
享有选择权的当事人在约定期限内或者履行期限届满未作选择，经催告后在合理期限内仍未选择的，选择权转移至对方。

**Article 515** Where there are multiple subject matters and the debtor only needs to perform one of them, the debtor shall have the right of choice; provided that, unless otherwise provided by law, otherwise agreed by the parties, or otherwise provided by trade practices.  
If the party with the right of choice fails to make a choice within the agreed period or before the expiration of the performance period, and fails to make a choice within a reasonable period after being urged, the right of choice shall shift to the other party.

<a id="art-516"></a>第五百一十六条　当事人行使选择权应当及时通知对方，通知到达对方时，标的确定。标的确定后不得变更，但是经对方同意的除外。  
可选择的标的发生不能履行情形的，享有选择权的当事人不得选择不能履行的标的，但是该不能履行的情形是由对方造成的除外。

**Article 516** When exercising the right of choice, a party shall promptly notify the other party; the subject matter shall be determined when the notice reaches the other party. The subject matter shall not be changed after it is determined, unless the other party consents. Where a circumstance of impossibility of performance occurs for an optional subject matter, the party with the right of choice may not select the subject matter that is impossible to perform, unless such impossibility was caused by the other party.

<a id="art-517"></a>第五百一十七条　债权人为二人以上，标的可分，按照份额各自享有债权的，为按份债权；债务人为二人以上，标的可分，按照份额各自负担债务的，为按份债务。  
按份债权人或者按份债务人的份额难以确定的，视为份额相同。

**Article 517** Where there are two or more creditors, and the subject matter is divisible, and each creditor has a claim in proportion to its share, it shall be a several claim; where there are two or more debtors, and the subject matter is divisible, and each debtor bears the obligation in proportion to its share, it shall be a several obligation.  
Where the shares of several creditors or several debtors are difficult to determine, the shares shall be deemed to be equal.

<a id="art-518"></a>第五百一十八条　债权人为二人以上，部分或者全部债权人均可以请求债务人履行债务的，为连带债权；债务人为二人以上，债权人可以请求部分或者全部债务人履行全部债务的，为连带债务。  
连带债权或者连带债务，由法律规定或者当事人约定。

**Article 518** Where there are two or more creditors, and all or part of the creditors may request the debtor to perform the obligation, it shall be a joint and several claim; where there are two or more debtors, and the creditor may request all or part of the debtors to perform the entire obligation, it shall be a joint and several obligation.  
A joint and several claim or joint and several obligation shall be provided by law or agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-519"></a>第五百一十九条　连带债务人之间的份额难以确定的，视为份额相同。  
实际承担债务超过自己份额的连带债务人，有权就超出部分在其他连带债务人未履行的份额范围内向其追偿，并相应地享有债权人的权利，但是不得损害债权人的利益。其他连带债务人对债权人的抗辩，可以向该债务人主张。  
被追偿的连带债务人不能履行其应分担份额的，其他连带债务人应当在相应范围内按比例分担。

**Article 519** Where the shares among joint and several debtors are difficult to determine, the shares shall be deemed to be equal. A joint and several debtor that has actually performed an obligation exceeding its own share shall have the right to seek recourse from the other joint and several debtors for the excess within the scope of their unperformed shares and shall correspondingly enjoy the rights of the creditor, provided that the creditor's interests are not prejudiced. Other joint and several debtors may raise defenses against the creditor against such debtor. If the joint and several debtor against whom recourse is sought cannot perform its share, the other joint and several debtors shall bear the share proportionally within the corresponding scope.

<a id="art-520"></a>第五百二十条　部分连带债务人履行、抵销债务或者提存标的物的，其他债务人对债权人的债务在相应范围内消灭；该债务人可以依据前条规定向其他债务人追偿。  
部分连带债务人的债务被债权人免除的，在该连带债务人应当承担的份额范围内，其他债务人对债权人的债务消灭。  
部分连带债务人的债务与债权人的债权同归于一人的，在扣除该债务人应当承担的份额后，债权人对其他债务人的债权继续存在。  
债权人对部分连带债务人的给付受领迟延的，对其他连带债务人发生效力。

**Article 520** Where some joint and several debtors perform, set off the obligation, or deposit the subject matter, the obligations of other debtors to the creditor shall be discharged to the corresponding extent; such debtors may seek recourse from the other debtors in accordance with the preceding article.  
Where the creditor discharges the obligation of some joint and several debtors, the obligations of the other debtors to the creditor shall be discharged within the scope of the share that such discharged debtors shall bear.  
Where the obligation of some joint and several debtors and the creditor's claim are merged in one person, the creditor's claim against the other debtors shall continue to exist after deducting the share that such debtors shall bear.  
Where the creditor is delayed in accepting performance from some joint and several debtors, it shall have effect on the other joint and several debtors.

<a id="art-521"></a>第五百二十一条　连带债权人之间的份额难以确定的，视为份额相同。  
实际受领债权的连带债权人，应当按比例向其他连带债权人返还。  
连带债权参照适用本章连带债务的有关规定。

**Article 521** Where the share of each joint creditor cannot be determined, it shall be deemed that they have equal shares.  
A joint creditor who has actually received performance of the obligatory right shall return the corresponding portion to the other joint creditors in proportion to their respective shares.  
Joint obligatory rights shall be governed by reference to the relevant provisions of this Chapter on joint obligations.

<a id="art-522"></a>第五百二十二条　当事人约定由债务人向第三人履行债务，债务人未向第三人履行债务或者履行债务不符合约定的，应当向债权人承担违约责任。  
法律规定或者当事人约定第三人可以直接请求债务人向其履行债务，第三人未在合理期限内明确拒绝，债务人未向第三人履行债务或者履行债务不符合约定的，第三人可以请求债务人承担违约责任；债务人对债权人的抗辩，可以向第三人主张。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-20" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第20条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 522** Where the parties agree that the debtor shall perform the obligation to a third party, and the debtor fails to perform the obligation to such third party or the performance is not in conformity with the agreement, the debtor shall bear the liability for breach of contract to the creditor.  
Where a law provides or the parties agree that the third party may directly request the debtor to perform the obligation to it, and the third party fails to explicitly refuse such performance within a reasonable period, but the debtor fails to perform the obligation to such third party or the performance is not in conformity with the agreement, the third party may request the debtor to bear the liability for breach of contract; and the defenses that the debtor has against the creditor may be asserted against the third party.

<a id="art-523"></a>第五百二十三条　当事人约定由第三人向债权人履行债务，第三人不履行债务或者履行债务不符合约定的，债务人应当向债权人承担违约责任。

**Article 523** Where the parties agree that a third party shall perform the obligation to the creditor, and the third party fails to perform the obligation or the performance is not in conformity with the agreement, the debtor shall bear the liability for breach of contract to the creditor.

<a id="art-524"></a>第五百二十四条　债务人不履行债务，第三人对履行该债务具有合法利益的，第三人有权向债权人代为履行；但是，根据债务性质、按照当事人约定或者依照法律规定只能由债务人履行的除外。  
债权人接受第三人履行后，其对债务人的债权转让给第三人，但是债务人和第三人另有约定的除外。

**Article 524** Where the debtor fails to perform its obligation, and a third party has a legitimate interest in performing such obligation, the third party shall be entitled to perform on behalf of the debtor to the creditor, unless such obligation may only be performed by the debtor by its nature, as agreed by the parties, or as provided by law.  
After the creditor accepts the performance by the third party, the creditor's obligatory right against the debtor shall be assigned to the third party, unless otherwise agreed by the debtor and the third party.

<a id="art-525"></a>第五百二十五条　当事人互负债务，没有先后履行顺序的，应当同时履行。一方在对方履行之前有权拒绝其履行请求。一方在对方履行债务不符合约定时，有权拒绝其相应的履行请求。

**Article 525** Where the parties owe obligations to each other and there is no order of priority for performance, they shall perform such obligations simultaneously. A party shall have the right to reject the other party's request for performance before the latter performs its own obligation. Where the other party's performance is not in conformity with the agreement, a party shall have the right to reject the other party's corresponding request for performance.

<a id="art-526"></a>第五百二十六条　当事人互负债务，有先后履行顺序，应当先履行债务一方未履行的，后履行一方有权拒绝其履行请求。先履行一方履行债务不符合约定的，后履行一方有权拒绝其相应的履行请求。

**Article 526** Where the parties owe obligations to each other, and there is an order of priority for performance, the party that is obligated to perform later shall have the right to reject the request for performance of the party that is obligated to perform first but fails to do so. Where the performance of the party that is obligated to perform first is not in conformity with the agreement, the party that is obligated to perform later shall have the right to reject the corresponding request for performance of the former party.

<a id="art-527"></a>第五百二十七条　应当先履行债务的当事人，有确切证据证明对方有下列情形之一的，可以中止履行：  
（一）经营状况严重恶化；  
（二）转移财产、抽逃资金，以逃避债务；  
（三）丧失商业信誉；  
（四）有丧失或者可能丧失履行债务能力的其他情形。  
当事人没有确切证据中止履行的，应当承担违约责任。

**Article 527** A party that is obligated to perform its obligation first may suspend its performance if it has conclusive evidence showing that the other party falls under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) its business operations are seriously deteriorating;  
(2) it is transferring property or withdrawing funds to evade obligations;  
(3) it has lost its business reputation  
(4) there are other circumstances where it has lost or may lose its ability to perform its obligation. Where a party suspends performance without conclusive evidence, it shall bear the liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-528"></a>第五百二十八条　当事人依据前条规定中止履行的，应当及时通知对方。对方提供适当担保的，应当恢复履行。中止履行后，对方在合理期限内未恢复履行能力且未提供适当担保的，视为以自己的行为表明不履行主要债务，中止履行的一方可以解除合同并可以请求对方承担违约责任。

**Article 528** Where a party suspends performance in accordance with the preceding article, it shall promptly notify the other party. If the other party provides appropriate security, the suspending party shall resume performance. After the suspension of performance, if the other party fails to restore its ability to perform and fails to provide appropriate security within a reasonable period, it shall be deemed as indicating by its own conduct that it shall not perform the main obligation. The party that suspended performance may dissolve the contract and may request the other party to bear the liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-529"></a>第五百二十九条　债权人分立、合并或者变更住所没有通知债务人，致使履行债务发生困难的，债务人可以中止履行或者将标的物提存。

**Article 529** Where a creditor is divided, merged, or changes its domicile without notifying the debtor, thereby making it difficult for the debtor to perform the obligation, the debtor may suspend performance or deposit the subject matter with a competent authority.

<a id="art-530"></a>第五百三十条　债权人可以拒绝债务人提前履行债务，但是提前履行不损害债权人利益的除外。  
债务人提前履行债务给债权人增加的费用，由债务人负担。

**Article 530** A creditor may reject the debtor's early performance of the obligation, unless such early performance does not harm the creditor's interests.  
Any additional costs incurred by the creditor due to the debtor's early performance shall be borne by the debtor.

<a id="art-531"></a>第五百三十一条　债权人可以拒绝债务人部分履行债务，但是部分履行不损害债权人利益的除外。  
债务人部分履行债务给债权人增加的费用，由债务人负担。

**Article 531** A creditor may reject the debtor's partial performance of the obligation, unless such partial performance does not harm the creditor's interests.  
Any additional costs incurred by the creditor due to the debtor's partial performance shall be borne by the debtor.

<a id="art-532"></a>第五百三十二条　合同生效后，当事人不得因姓名、名称的变更或者法定代表人、负责人、承办人的变动而不履行合同义务。

**Article 532** After a contract has taken effect, a party shall not refuse to perform its contractual obligations on the ground of a change in its name or the name of the party, or a change in its legal representative, the person in charge, or the handler.

<a id="art-533"></a>第五百三十三条　合同成立后，合同的基础条件发生了当事人在订立合同时无法预见的、不属于商业风险的重大变化，继续履行合同对于当事人一方明显不公平的，受不利影响的当事人可以与对方重新协商；在合理期限内协商不成的，当事人可以请求人民法院或者仲裁机构变更或者解除合同。  
人民法院或者仲裁机构应当结合案件的实际情况，根据公平原则变更或者解除合同。

**Article 533** After the conclusion of a contract, if a fundamental condition of the contract undergoes a material change that the parties could not have foreseen at the time of concluding the contract and that does not constitute a commercial risk, and the continued performance of the contract is manifestly unfair to one party, the party that is adversely affected may renegotiate with the other party. If the renegotiation fails within a reasonable period, the party may request the People's Court or an arbitration institution to modify or dissolve the contract. The People's Court or the arbitration institution shall, taking into account the actual circumstances of the case, modify or dissolve the contract in accordance with the principle of fairness.

<a id="art-534"></a>第五百三十四条　对当事人利用合同实施危害国家利益、社会公共利益行为的，市场监督管理和其他有关行政主管部门依照法律、行政法规的规定负责监督处理。

**Article 534** Where a party uses a contract to engage in any conduct that harms the interests of the State or the public interest, the market supervision and administration authorities and other relevant competent administrative departments shall, in accordance with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations, be responsible for supervising and handling the matter.

### 第五章　合同的保全

<a id="art-535"></a>第五百三十五条　因债务人怠于行使其债权或者与该债权有关的从权利，影响债权人的到期债权实现的，债权人可以向人民法院请求以自己的名义代位行使债务人对相对人的权利，但是该权利专属于债务人自身的除外。  
代位权的行使范围以债权人的到期债权为限。债权人行使代位权的必要费用，由债务人负担。  
相对人对债务人的抗辩，可以向债权人主张。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）.md#art-44" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）》第44条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 535** Where the debtor's failure to diligently exercise its obligatory right or any accessory right related thereto affects the realization of the creditor's due obligatory right, the creditor may apply to the People's Court to exercise by subrogation, in its own name, the debtor's rights against the counterparty, unless such rights exclusively belong to the debtor. The scope of exercising the right of subrogation shall be limited to the creditor's due obligatory right. The necessary expenses incurred by the creditor in exercising the right of subrogation shall be borne by the debtor. The defenses that the counterparty has against the debtor may be asserted against the creditor.

<a id="art-536"></a>第五百三十六条　债权人的债权到期前，债务人的债权或者与该债权有关的从权利存在诉讼时效期间即将届满或者未及时申报破产债权等情形，影响债权人的债权实现的，债权人可以代位向债务人的相对人请求其向债务人履行、向破产管理人申报或者作出其他必要的行为。

**Article 536** Before the creditor's obligatory right falls due, if the debtor's obligatory right or any accessory right related thereto is in a situation such as the limitation period for litigation is about to expire, or the creditor has failed to declare the obligatory right in bankruptcy in a timely manner, which affects the realization of the creditor's obligatory right, the creditor may by subrogation request the debtor's counterparty to perform to the debtor, declare it to the bankruptcy administrator, or take other necessary actions.

<a id="art-537"></a>第五百三十七条　人民法院认定代位权成立的，由债务人的相对人向债权人履行义务，债权人接受履行后，债权人与债务人、债务人与相对人之间相应的权利义务终止。债务人对相对人的债权或者与该债权有关的从权利被采取保全、执行措施，或者债务人破产的，依照相关法律的规定处理。

**Article 537** If the People's Court establishes the right of subrogation, the counterparty of the debtor shall perform its obligation to the creditor. After the creditor accepts the performance, the corresponding rights and obligations between the creditor and the debtor, and between the debtor and the counterparty, shall be terminated. If the debtor's obligatory right against the counterparty or any accessory right related thereto has been subjected to preservation or enforcement measures, or if the debtor has become bankrupt, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of relevant laws.

<a id="art-538"></a>第五百三十八条　债务人以放弃其债权、放弃债权担保、无偿转让财产等方式无偿处分财产权益，或者恶意延长其到期债权的履行期限，影响债权人的债权实现的，债权人可以请求人民法院撤销债务人的行为。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 538** If the debtor disposes of its property rights and interests without consideration by means of waiving its obligatory rights, waiving security for its obligatory rights, transferring property without consideration, or maliciously extends the period for performance of its due obligatory rights, thereby affecting the realization of the creditor's obligatory rights, the creditor may request the People's Court to revoke the debtor's act.

<a id="art-539"></a>第五百三十九条　债务人以明显不合理的低价转让财产、以明显不合理的高价受让他人财产或者为他人的债务提供担保，影响债权人的债权实现，债务人的相对人知道或者应当知道该情形的，债权人可以请求人民法院撤销债务人的行为。

**Article 539** If the debtor transfers property at a manifestly unreasonably low price, acquires another person's property at a manifestly unreasonably high price, or provides security for another person's obligations, thereby affecting the realization of the creditor's obligatory rights, and if the counterparty of the debtor knew or should have known such circumstances, the creditor may request the People's Court to revoke the debtor's act.

<a id="art-540"></a>第五百四十条　撤销权的行使范围以债权人的债权为限。债权人行使撤销权的必要费用，由债务人负担。

**Article 540** The scope of exercising the right of revocation shall be limited to the creditor's obligatory right. The necessary expenses incurred by the creditor in exercising the right of revocation shall be borne by the debtor.

<a id="art-541"></a>第五百四十一条　撤销权自债权人知道或者应当知道撤销事由之日起一年内行使。自债务人的行为发生之日起五年内没有行使撤销权的，该撤销权消灭。

**Article 541** The right of revocation shall be exercised within one year from the day when the creditor knows or should have known the cause for revocation. If the right of revocation has not been exercised within five years from the day when the debtor's act occurred, such right of revocation shall be extinguished.

<a id="art-542"></a>第五百四十二条　债务人影响债权人的债权实现的行为被撤销的，自始没有法律约束力。

**Article 542** Where an act of the debtor that affects the realization of the creditor's claim is revoked, such act shall have no legal binding force from the very beginning.

### 第六章　合同的变更和转让

<a id="art-543"></a>第五百四十三条　当事人协商一致，可以变更合同。

**Article 543** The parties may modify the contract upon mutual agreement.

<a id="art-544"></a>第五百四十四条　当事人对合同变更的内容约定不明确的，推定为未变更。

**Article 544** Where the parties do not agree clearly on the content of a modification of the contract, it shall be presumed that the contract has not been modified.

<a id="art-545"></a>第五百四十五条　债权人可以将债权的全部或者部分转让给第三人，但是有下列情形之一的除外：  
（一）根据债权性质不得转让；  
（二）按照当事人约定不得转让；  
（三）依照法律规定不得转让。  
当事人约定非金钱债权不得转让的，不得对抗善意第三人。当事人约定金钱债权不得转让的，不得对抗第三人。

**Article 545** A creditor may assign all or part of its claim to a third party, except in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the claim is not assignable by its nature;  
(2) the claim is not assignable according to the agreement between the parties  
(3) the claim is not assignable according to the provisions of law. Where the parties agree that a non-monetary claim shall not be assigned, such agreement may not be asserted against a bona fide third party. Where the parties agree that a monetary claim shall not be assigned, such agreement may not be asserted against any third party.

<a id="art-546"></a>第五百四十六条　债权人转让债权，未通知债务人的，该转让对债务人不发生效力。  
债权转让的通知不得撤销，但是经受让人同意的除外。

**Article 546** Where a creditor assigns a claim without notifying the debtor, such assignment shall not be binding on the debtor.  
A notice of assignment of a claim may not be revoked, except with the consent of the assignee.

<a id="art-547"></a>第五百四十七条　债权人转让债权的，受让人取得与债权有关的从权利，但是该从权利专属于债权人自身的除外。  
受让人取得从权利不因该从权利未办理转移登记手续或者未转移占有而受到影响。

**Article 547** Where a creditor assigns a claim, the assignee shall acquire the ancillary rights related to the claim, except where such ancillary rights are exclusively personal to the creditor.  
The acquisition of ancillary rights by the assignee shall not be affected by the failure to undergo registration of transfer or deliver possession of the ancillary rights.

<a id="art-548"></a>第五百四十八条　债务人接到债权转让通知后，债务人对让与人的抗辩，可以向受让人主张。

**Article 548** After receiving the notice of assignment of the claim, the debtor may assert against the assignee any defense that the debtor has against the assignor.

<a id="art-549"></a>第五百四十九条　有下列情形之一的，债务人可以向受让人主张抵销：  
（一）债务人接到债权转让通知时，债务人对让与人享有债权，且债务人的债权先于转让的债权到期或者同时到期；  
（二）债务人的债权与转让的债权是基于同一合同产生。

**Article 549** The debtor may set off against the assignee its claim in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) at the time the debtor receives the notice of assignment of the claim, the debtor has a claim against the assignor, and such claim of the debtor falls due before or at the same time as the assigned claim  
(2) the debtor's claim and the assigned claim arise from the same contract.

<a id="art-550"></a>第五百五十条　因债权转让增加的履行费用，由让与人负担。

**Article 550** The assignor shall bear the additional performance costs arising from the assignment of the claim.

<a id="art-551"></a>第五百五十一条　债务人将债务的全部或者部分转移给第三人的，应当经债权人同意。  
债务人或者第三人可以催告债权人在合理期限内予以同意，债权人未作表示的，视为不同意。

**Article 551** Where a debtor transfers all or part of its debt to a third party, it shall obtain the consent of the creditor.  
The debtor or the third party may urge the creditor to give consent within a reasonable period of time; if the creditor does not respond, it shall be deemed to have withheld consent.

<a id="art-552"></a>第五百五十二条　第三人与债务人约定加入债务并通知债权人，或者第三人向债权人表示愿意加入债务，债权人未在合理期限内明确拒绝的，债权人可以请求第三人在其愿意承担的债务范围内和债务人承担连带债务。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-12" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第12条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-36" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第36条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 552** Where a third party agrees with the debtor to join in the debt and notifies the creditor thereof, or where the third party indicates to the creditor that it is willing to join in the debt, and the creditor does not expressly refuse within a reasonable period of time, the creditor may request the third party to assume joint and several liability with the debtor within the scope of the debt that the third party is willing to assume.

<a id="art-553"></a>第五百五十三条　债务人转移债务的，新债务人可以主张原债务人对债权人的抗辩；原债务人对债权人享有债权的，新债务人不得向债权人主张抵销。

**Article 553** Where a debtor transfers its debt, the new debtor may assert any defense that the original debtor has against the creditor; however, if the original debtor has a claim against the creditor, the new debtor may not set off such claim against the creditor.

<a id="art-554"></a>第五百五十四条　债务人转移债务的，新债务人应当承担与主债务有关的从债务，但是该从债务专属于原债务人自身的除外。

**Article 554** Where a debtor transfers its debt, the new debtor shall assume the ancillary obligations related to the principal debt, except where such ancillary obligations are exclusively personal to the original debtor.

<a id="art-555"></a>第五百五十五条　当事人一方经对方同意，可以将自己在合同中的权利和义务一并转让给第三人。

**Article 555** A party may transfer its rights and obligations under the contract to a third party together, subject to the consent of the other party.

<a id="art-556"></a>第五百五十六条　合同的权利和义务一并转让的，适用债权转让、债务转移的有关规定。

**Article 556** Where the rights and obligations under a contract are transferred together, the relevant provisions on the assignment of claims and the transfer of debts shall apply.

### 第七章　合同的权利义务终止

<a id="art-557"></a>第五百五十七条　有下列情形之一的，债权债务终止：  
（一）债务已经履行；  
（二）债务相互抵销；  
（三）债务人依法将标的物提存；  
（四）债权人免除债务；  
（五）债权债务同归于一人；  
（六）法律规定或者当事人约定终止的其他情形。  
合同解除的，该合同的权利义务关系终止。

**Article 557** A claim and debt shall be extinguished in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the debt has been performed;  
(2) the debts are set off against each other;  
(3) the debtor has lawfully deposited the subject matter;  
(4) the creditor has released the debt;  
(5) the claim and debt are merged in the same person  
(6) any other circumstance in which the claim and debt are extinguished as provided by law or agreed by the parties. Upon rescission of a contract, the rights and obligations under such contract shall be extinguished.

<a id="art-558"></a>第五百五十八条　债权债务终止后，当事人应当遵循诚信等原则，根据交易习惯履行通知、协助、保密、旧物回收等义务。

**Article 558** After a claim and debt are extinguished, the parties shall, in accordance with the principle of good faith and other principles, and based on the trade practices, perform the obligations of notification, assistance, confidentiality, and recycling of used items, among others.

<a id="art-559"></a>第五百五十九条　债权债务终止时，债权的从权利同时消灭，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 559** When a claim and debt are extinguished, the ancillary rights of the claim shall also be extinguished simultaneously, except where the law provides otherwise or the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-560"></a>第五百六十条　债务人对同一债权人负担的数项债务种类相同，债务人的给付不足以清偿全部债务的，除当事人另有约定外，由债务人在清偿时指定其履行的债务。  
债务人未作指定的，应当优先履行已经到期的债务；数项债务均到期的，优先履行对债权人缺乏担保或者担保最少的债务；均无担保或者担保相等的，优先履行债务人负担较重的债务；负担相同的，按照债务到期的先后顺序履行；到期时间相同的，按照债务比例履行。

**Article 560** Where a debtor owes several debts of the same type to the same creditor and the debtor's performance is insufficient to discharge all the debts, the debtor shall, at the time of performance, designate which debt it is performing, except where the parties agree otherwise.  
If the debtor fails to make such designation, it shall first perform the debt that has fallen due; where all the debts have fallen due, it shall first perform the debt that is lacking security or is least secured; where none of the debts is secured or all are equally secured, it shall first perform the debt that is the most burdensome for the debtor; where the burdens are equal, the debts shall be performed in the order of their maturity; and where they fall due at the same time, they shall be performed in proportion to each debt.

<a id="art-561"></a>第五百六十一条　债务人在履行主债务外还应当支付利息和实现债权的有关费用，其给付不足以清偿全部债务的，除当事人另有约定外，应当按照下列顺序履行：  
（一）实现债权的有关费用；  
（二）利息；  
（三）主债务。

**Article 561** Where a debtor, in addition to performing the principal obligation, shall also pay interest and the relevant expenses for realizing the creditor's rights, and the debtor's performance is insufficient to discharge all obligations, unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the performance shall be made in the following order:  
(1) the relevant expenses for realizing the creditor's rights;  
(2) interest; and (3) the principal obligation.

<a id="art-562"></a>第五百六十二条　当事人协商一致，可以解除合同。  
当事人可以约定一方解除合同的事由。解除合同的事由发生时，解除权人可以解除合同。

**Article 562** The parties may dissolve the contract upon mutual agreement. The parties may agree on the grounds for one party to dissolve the contract. When the grounds for dissolution of the contract occur, the party entitled to dissolution may dissolve the contract.

<a id="art-563"></a>第五百六十三条　有下列情形之一的，当事人可以解除合同：  
（一）因不可抗力致使不能实现合同目的；  
（二）在履行期限届满前，当事人一方明确表示或者以自己的行为表明不履行主要债务；  
（三）当事人一方迟延履行主要债务，经催告后在合理期限内仍未履行；  
（四）当事人一方迟延履行债务或者有其他违约行为致使不能实现合同目的；  
（五）法律规定的其他情形。  
以持续履行的债务为内容的不定期合同，当事人可以随时解除合同，但是应当在合理期限之前通知对方。

**Article 563** A party may dissolve the contract under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the purpose of the contract cannot be achieved due to force majeure;  
(2) before the expiration of the performance period, the other party expressly states or indicates by its own conduct that it shall not perform the principal obligation;  
(3) the other party delays in performing the principal obligation and still fails to perform it within a reasonable period after being urged;  
(4) the other party delays in performing its obligations or commits other breaches of contract that render the purpose of the contract unachievable  
(5) other circumstances as provided by law. For a contract of indefinite duration that involves a continuous performance obligation, a party may dissolve the contract at any time, provided that it shall notify the other party within a reasonable period in advance.

<a id="art-564"></a>第五百六十四条　法律规定或者当事人约定解除权行使期限，期限届满当事人不行使的，该权利消灭。  
法律没有规定或者当事人没有约定解除权行使期限，自解除权人知道或者应当知道解除事由之日起一年内不行使，或者经对方催告后在合理期限内不行使的，该权利消灭。

**Article 564** Where the law provides or the parties agree on a period for exercising the right to dissolve the contract, and the party fails to exercise it upon the expiration of such period, that right shall lapse.  
Where the law does not provide or the parties have not agreed on a period for exercising the right to dissolve the contract, that right shall lapse if it is not exercised within one year from the date on which the person entitled to dissolution knew or should have known of the grounds for dissolution, or if it is not exercised within a reasonable period after being urged by the other party.

<a id="art-565"></a>第五百六十五条　当事人一方依法主张解除合同的，应当通知对方。合同自通知到达对方时解除；通知载明债务人在一定期限内不履行债务则合同自动解除，债务人在该期限内未履行债务的，合同自通知载明的期限届满时解除。对方对解除合同有异议的，任何一方当事人均可以请求人民法院或者仲裁机构确认解除行为的效力。  
当事人一方未通知对方，直接以提起诉讼或者申请仲裁的方式依法主张解除合同，人民法院或者仲裁机构确认该主张的，合同自起诉状副本或者仲裁申请书副本送达对方时解除。

**Article 565** Where one party claims dissolution of the contract according to law, it shall notify the other party. The contract shall be dissolved upon the receipt of the notice by the other party; if the notice states that the contract shall be automatically dissolved if the debtor fails to perform its obligations within a certain period, and the debtor fails to perform its obligations within that period, the contract shall be dissolved upon the expiration of the period specified in the notice. If the other party objects to the dissolution, either party may request the People's Court or an arbitration institution to confirm the validity of the dissolution. Where one party, without notifying the other party, claims dissolution of the contract according to law directly by filing a lawsuit or applying for arbitration, and the People's Court or arbitration institution confirms the claim, the contract shall be dissolved upon the service of a copy of the statement of claim or a copy of the arbitration application on the other party.

<a id="art-566"></a>第五百六十六条　合同解除后，尚未履行的，终止履行；已经履行的，根据履行情况和合同性质，当事人可以请求恢复原状或者采取其他补救措施，并有权请求赔偿损失。  
合同因违约解除的，解除权人可以请求违约方承担违约责任，但是当事人另有约定的除外。  
主合同解除后，担保人对债务人应当承担的民事责任仍应当承担担保责任，但是担保合同另有约定的除外。

**Article 566** After the dissolution of a contract, the performance of obligations not yet performed shall be terminated; for those already performed, a party may, based on the performance status and the nature of the contract, request the restoration of the original status or the adoption of other remedial measures, and shall have the right to claim damages.  
Where a contract is dissolved due to a breach of contract, the party entitled to dissolution may request the breaching party to bear the liability for breach of contract, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.  
After the dissolution of the principal contract, the guarantor shall still bear the guarantee liability for the civil liability that the debtor shall bear, unless otherwise agreed in the guarantee contract.

<a id="art-567"></a>第五百六十七条　合同的权利义务关系终止，不影响合同中结算和清理条款的效力。

**Article 567** The termination of the relationship of rights and obligations under a contract shall not affect the validity of the clauses in the contract concerning settlement and liquidation.

<a id="art-568"></a>第五百六十八条　当事人互负债务，该债务的标的物种类、品质相同的，任何一方可以将自己的债务与对方的到期债务抵销；但是，根据债务性质、按照当事人约定或者依照法律规定不得抵销的除外。  
当事人主张抵销的，应当通知对方。通知自到达对方时生效。抵销不得附条件或者附期限。

**Article 568** Where the parties owe obligations to each other, and the subject matter of such obligations is of the same type and quality, either party may set off its own obligation against the other party's due obligation; however, such set-off shall not apply where it is prohibited by the nature of the obligation, by agreement of the parties, or by law. Where a party claims a set-off, it shall notify the other party. The notice shall take effect upon its receipt by the other party. A set-off shall not be subject to any condition or time limit.

<a id="art-569"></a>第五百六十九条　当事人互负债务，标的物种类、品质不相同的，经协商一致，也可以抵销。

**Article 569** Where the parties owe obligations to each other, and the subject matter of such obligations is not of the same type and quality, they may also effect a set-off by mutual agreement.

<a id="art-570"></a>第五百七十条　有下列情形之一，难以履行债务的，债务人可以将标的物提存：  
（一）债权人无正当理由拒绝受领；  
（二）债权人下落不明；  
（三）债权人死亡未确定继承人、遗产管理人，或者丧失民事行为能力未确定监护人；  
（四）法律规定的其他情形。  
标的物不适于提存或者提存费用过高的，债务人依法可以拍卖或者变卖标的物，提存所得的价款。

**Article 570** Under any of the following circumstances, where performance of the obligation is difficult, the debtor may deposit the subject matter:  
(1) the creditor refuses to accept performance without just cause;  
(2) the creditor's whereabouts are unknown;  
(3) the creditor dies without a designated heir or estate administrator, or loses capacity for civil conduct without a designated guardian  
(4) other circumstances as provided by law. If the subject matter is unsuitable for deposit or the deposit costs are excessively high, the debtor may auction or sell the subject matter according to law and deposit the proceeds.

<a id="art-571"></a>第五百七十一条　债务人将标的物或者将标的物依法拍卖、变卖所得价款交付提存部门时，提存成立。  
提存成立的，视为债务人在其提存范围内已经交付标的物。

**Article 571** The deposit shall be established when the debtor delivers the subject matter or the proceeds from the auction or sale of the subject matter according to law to the deposit authority.  
Once the deposit is established, the debtor shall be deemed to have delivered the subject matter to the extent of the deposit.

<a id="art-572"></a>第五百七十二条　标的物提存后，债务人应当及时通知债权人或者债权人的继承人、遗产管理人、监护人、财产代管人。

**Article 572** After the subject matter is deposited, the debtor shall promptly notify the creditor or the creditor's heir, estate administrator, guardian, or property custodian.

<a id="art-573"></a>第五百七十三条　标的物提存后，毁损、灭失的风险由债权人承担。提存期间，标的物的孳息归债权人所有。提存费用由债权人负担。

**Article 573** After the subject matter is deposited, the risk of damage or loss shall be borne by the creditor. During the period of deposit, the fruits of the subject matter shall belong to the creditor. The deposit costs shall be borne by the creditor.

<a id="art-574"></a>第五百七十四条　债权人可以随时领取提存物。但是，债权人对债务人负有到期债务的，在债权人未履行债务或者提供担保之前，提存部门根据债务人的要求应当拒绝其领取提存物。  
债权人领取提存物的权利，自提存之日起五年内不行使而消灭，提存物扣除提存费用后归国家所有。但是，债权人未履行对债务人的到期债务，或者债权人向提存部门书面表示放弃领取提存物权利的，债务人负担提存费用后有权取回提存物。

**Article 574** The creditor may collect the deposited subject matter at any time. However, if the creditor owes a due obligation to the debtor, the deposit authority shall, upon the debtor's request, refuse the creditor's collection of the deposited subject matter until the creditor performs the obligation or provides security. The right of the creditor to collect the deposited subject matter shall lapse if it is not exercised within five years from the date of deposit, and the deposited subject matter, after deducting the deposit costs, shall belong to the State. However, if the creditor fails to perform its due obligation to the debtor, or the creditor indicates in writing to the deposit authority that it waives the right to collect the deposited subject matter, the debtor shall be entitled to take back the deposited subject matter after bearing the deposit costs.

<a id="art-575"></a>第五百七十五条　债权人免除债务人部分或者全部债务的，债权债务部分或者全部终止，但是债务人在合理期限内拒绝的除外。

**Article 575** Where the creditor remits part or all of the debtor's obligation, the creditor's rights and debtor's obligations shall be terminated in part or in whole, unless the debtor refuses such remission within a reasonable period.

<a id="art-576"></a>第五百七十六条　债权和债务同归于一人的，债权债务终止，但是损害第三人利益的除外。

**Article 576** Where the creditor's rights and debtor's obligations are vested in the same person, the creditor's rights and debtor's obligations shall be terminated, unless the interests of a third party are prejudiced.

### 第八章　违约责任

<a id="art-577"></a>第五百七十七条　当事人一方不履行合同义务或者履行合同义务不符合约定的，应当承担继续履行、采取补救措施或者赔偿损失等违约责任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）》第19条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 577** Where one party fails to perform its contractual obligations or performs them in a manner inconsistent with the agreement, it shall bear the liability for breach of contract, such as continuing performance, adopting remedial measures, or compensating for losses.

<a id="art-578"></a>第五百七十八条　当事人一方明确表示或者以自己的行为表明不履行合同义务的，对方可以在履行期限届满前请求其承担违约责任。

**Article 578** Where one party expressly states or indicates by its own conduct that it shall not perform its contractual obligations, the other party may request it to bear the liability for breach of contract before the expiration of the performance period.

<a id="art-579"></a>第五百七十九条　当事人一方未支付价款、报酬、租金、利息，或者不履行其他金钱债务的，对方可以请求其支付。

**Article 579** Where one party fails to pay the price, remuneration, rent, or interest, or fails to perform other monetary obligations, the other party may request payment.

<a id="art-580"></a>第五百八十条　当事人一方不履行非金钱债务或者履行非金钱债务不符合约定的，对方可以请求履行，但是有下列情形之一的除外：  
（一）法律上或者事实上不能履行；  
（二）债务的标的不适于强制履行或者履行费用过高；  
（三）债权人在合理期限内未请求履行。  
有前款规定的除外情形之一，致使不能实现合同目的的，人民法院或者仲裁机构可以根据当事人的请求终止合同权利义务关系，但是不影响违约责任的承担。

**Article 580** Where one party fails to perform a non-monetary obligation or performs it in a manner inconsistent with the agreement, the other party may request performance, except under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) performance is legally or factually impossible;  
(2) the subject matter of the obligation is unsuitable for compulsory performance or the cost of performance is excessively high  
(3) the creditor fails to request performance within a reasonable period. Where one of the excepted circumstances provided in the preceding paragraph renders the purpose of the contract unachievable, the People's Court or an arbitration institution may, upon a party's request, terminate the relationship of rights and obligations under the contract, but such termination shall not affect the liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-581"></a>第五百八十一条　当事人一方不履行债务或者履行债务不符合约定，根据债务的性质不得强制履行的，对方可以请求其负担由第三人替代履行的费用。

**Article 581** Where one party fails to perform an obligation, or the performance of an obligation does not conform to the agreement, and compulsory performance is not possible due to the nature of the obligation, the other party may request that the former bear the costs of performance by a third party in lieu thereof.

<a id="art-582"></a>第五百八十二条　履行不符合约定的，应当按照当事人的约定承担违约责任。对违约责任没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，受损害方根据标的的性质以及损失的大小，可以合理选择请求对方承担修理、重作、更换、退货、减少价款或者报酬等违约责任。

**Article 582** Where the performance does not conform to the agreement, the party in breach shall bear the liability for breach of contract in accordance with the agreement of the parties. If there is no agreement on liability for breach of contract or the agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the aggrieved party may, in light of the nature of the subject matter and the extent of the loss, reasonably elect to request that the other party bear the liability for breach of contract by way of repair, rework, replacement, return of the goods, reduction of the price or remuneration, or other such remedies.

<a id="art-583"></a>第五百八十三条　当事人一方不履行合同义务或者履行合同义务不符合约定的，在履行义务或者采取补救措施后，对方还有其他损失的，应当赔偿损失。

**Article 583** Where one party fails to perform its contractual obligations or performs them in a manner not in conformity with the agreement, and the other party suffers other losses after performance of the obligations or adoption of remedial measures, the former shall compensate for such losses.

<a id="art-584"></a>第五百八十四条　当事人一方不履行合同义务或者履行合同义务不符合约定，造成对方损失的，损失赔偿额应当相当于因违约所造成的损失，包括合同履行后可以获得的利益；但是，不得超过违约一方订立合同时预见到或者应当预见到的因违约可能造成的损失。

**Article 584** Where one party fails to perform its contractual obligations or performs them in a manner not in conformity with the agreement, thereby causing losses to the other party, the amount of compensation for losses shall be equivalent to the losses caused by the breach of contract, including the benefits that could have been obtained after the performance of the contract; provided, however, that it shall not exceed the losses that the party in breach foresaw or ought to have foreseen as possible consequences of the breach at the time of conclusion of the contract.

<a id="art-585"></a>第五百八十五条　当事人可以约定一方违约时应当根据违约情况向对方支付一定数额的违约金，也可以约定因违约产生的损失赔偿额的计算方法。  
约定的违约金低于造成的损失的，人民法院或者仲裁机构可以根据当事人的请求予以增加；约定的违约金过分高于造成的损失的，人民法院或者仲裁机构可以根据当事人的请求予以适当减少。  
当事人就迟延履行约定违约金的，违约方支付违约金后，还应当履行债务。

**Article 585** The parties may agree that, in the event of a breach of contract by one party, it shall pay a certain sum of liquidated damages to the other party in light of the circumstances of the breach, and may also agree on the method for calculating the amount of compensation for losses arising from the breach of contract.  
Where the agreed amount of liquidated damages is lower than the losses caused, the People's Court or an arbitration institution may, upon the request of a party, increase it; where the agreed amount of liquidated damages is excessively higher than the losses caused, the People's Court or an arbitration institution may, upon the request of a party, reduce it appropriately.  
Where the parties agree on liquidated damages for delay in performance, the defaulting party shall, after paying the liquidated damages, still perform its obligations.

<a id="art-586"></a>第五百八十六条　当事人可以约定一方向对方给付定金作为债权的担保。定金合同自实际交付定金时成立。  
定金的数额由当事人约定；但是，不得超过主合同标的额的百分之二十，超过部分不产生定金的效力。实际交付的定金数额多于或者少于约定数额的，视为变更约定的定金数额。

**Article 586** The parties may agree that one party pays a deposit to the other party as a guarantee for the performance of an obligation. A deposit contract shall be formed upon the actual payment of the deposit. The amount of the deposit shall be agreed upon by the parties; however, it shall not exceed twenty percent of the value of the subject matter of the principal contract, and any amount in excess shall not have the effect of a deposit. Where the amount of the deposit actually paid is more or less than the agreed amount, the agreed amount of deposit shall be deemed to have been modified.

<a id="art-587"></a>第五百八十七条　债务人履行债务的，定金应当抵作价款或者收回。给付定金的一方不履行债务或者履行债务不符合约定，致使不能实现合同目的的，无权请求返还定金；收受定金的一方不履行债务或者履行债务不符合约定，致使不能实现合同目的的，应当双倍返还定金。

**Article 587** Where the obligor performs its obligation, the deposit shall be applied towards the price or be returned. Where the party paying the deposit fails to perform the obligation or performs it in a manner not in conformity with the agreement, thereby defeating the purpose of the contract, it shall not be entitled to request the return of the deposit; where the party receiving the deposit fails to perform the obligation or performs it in a manner not in conformity with the agreement, thereby defeating the purpose of the contract, it shall return double the amount of the deposit.

<a id="art-588"></a>第五百八十八条　当事人既约定违约金，又约定定金的，一方违约时，对方可以选择适用违约金或者定金条款。  
定金不足以弥补一方违约造成的损失的，对方可以请求赔偿超过定金数额的损失。

**Article 588** Where the parties have agreed on both liquidated damages and a deposit, in the event of a breach of contract by one party, the other party may elect to apply either the liquidated damages clause or the deposit clause.  
Where the deposit is insufficient to compensate for the losses caused by the breach of one party, the other party may request compensation for losses in excess of the amount of the deposit.

<a id="art-589"></a>第五百八十九条　债务人按照约定履行债务，债权人无正当理由拒绝受领的，债务人可以请求债权人赔偿增加的费用。  
在债权人受领迟延期间，债务人无须支付利息。

**Article 589** Where the obligor performs its obligation in accordance with the agreement, but the obligee refuses to accept performance without justifiable reason, the obligor may request the obligee to compensate for the increased expenses incurred.  
During the period of delay by the obligee in accepting performance, the obligor shall not be required to pay interest.

<a id="art-590"></a>第五百九十条　当事人一方因不可抗力不能履行合同的，根据不可抗力的影响，部分或者全部免除责任，但是法律另有规定的除外。因不可抗力不能履行合同的，应当及时通知对方，以减轻可能给对方造成的损失，并应当在合理期限内提供证明。  
当事人迟延履行后发生不可抗力的，不免除其违约责任。

**Article 590** Where one party is unable to perform the contract due to force majeure, it shall be partially or wholly exempted from liability depending on the impact of the force majeure, unless otherwise provided by law. Where a party is unable to perform the contract due to force majeure, it shall promptly notify the other party to mitigate the potential losses that may be caused to the other party, and shall provide proof within a reasonable period. Where an event of force majeure occurs after one party has delayed in performance, the liability of the party for breach of contract shall not be exempted.

<a id="art-591"></a>第五百九十一条　当事人一方违约后，对方应当采取适当措施防止损失的扩大；没有采取适当措施致使损失扩大的，不得就扩大的损失请求赔偿。  
当事人因防止损失扩大而支出的合理费用，由违约方负担。

**Article 591** After one party breaches the contract, the other party shall take appropriate measures to prevent the aggravation of losses; if the other party fails to take appropriate measures resulting in the aggravation of losses, it shall not be entitled to claim compensation for the aggravated portion of the losses.  
The reasonable expenses incurred by a party in preventing the aggravation of losses shall be borne by the defaulting party.

<a id="art-592"></a>第五百九十二条　当事人都违反合同的，应当各自承担相应的责任。  
当事人一方违约造成对方损失，对方对损失的发生有过错的，可以减少相应的损失赔偿额。

**Article 592** Where both parties breach the contract, each party shall bear its respective liability.  
Where the breach of one party causes losses to the other party, and the other party is at fault for the occurrence of the losses, the amount of compensation for losses may be reduced accordingly.

<a id="art-593"></a>第五百九十三条　当事人一方因第三人的原因造成违约的，应当依法向对方承担违约责任。当事人一方和第三人之间的纠纷，依照法律规定或者按照约定处理。

**Article 593** Where one party breaches the contract due to the act of a third party, it shall bear liability for the breach of contract to the other party in accordance with law. Any disputes between that party and the third party shall be dealt with in accordance with legal provisions or the agreement between them.

<a id="art-594"></a>第五百九十四条　因国际货物买卖合同和技术进出口合同争议提起诉讼或者申请仲裁的时效期间为四年。  
第二分编　典型合同

**Article 594** The prescription period for bringing an action or applying for arbitration arising from disputes under a contract for the international sale of goods or a technology import and export contract shall be four years.  
Subsection II: Model Contracts

### 第九章　买卖合同

<a id="art-595"></a>第五百九十五条　买卖合同是出卖人转移标的物的所有权于买受人，买受人支付价款的合同。

**Article 595** A sales contract is a contract under which the seller transfers the ownership of the subject matter to the buyer, and the buyer pays the price.

<a id="art-596"></a>第五百九十六条　买卖合同的内容一般包括标的物的名称、数量、质量、价款、履行期限、履行地点和方式、包装方式、检验标准和方法、结算方式、合同使用的文字及其效力等条款。

**Article 596** The contents of a sales contract shall generally include terms such as the name, quantity, quality, and price of the subject matter, the performance period, place and method of performance, packaging method, inspection standards and methods, settlement method, the language used for the contract and its validity, and other such terms.

<a id="art-597"></a>第五百九十七条　因出卖人未取得处分权致使标的物所有权不能转移的，买受人可以解除合同并请求出卖人承担违约责任。  
法律、行政法规禁止或者限制转让的标的物，依照其规定。

**Article 597** Where the seller's failure to obtain the right of disposition results in the inability to transfer the ownership of the subject matter, the buyer may rescind the contract and request the seller to bear liability for breach of contract.  
In the case of subject matters the transfer of which is prohibited or restricted by laws or administrative regulations, the provisions thereof shall govern.

<a id="art-598"></a>第五百九十八条　出卖人应当履行向买受人交付标的物或者交付提取标的物的单证，并转移标的物所有权的义务。

**Article 598** The seller shall perform the obligations of delivering the subject matter or the documents for taking delivery of the subject matter to the buyer, and of transferring the ownership of the subject matter.

<a id="art-599"></a>第五百九十九条　出卖人应当按照约定或者交易习惯向买受人交付提取标的物单证以外的有关单证和资料。

**Article 599** The seller shall deliver to the buyer, in accordance with the agreement or trade usage, the relevant documents and materials other than the documents for taking delivery of the subject matter.

<a id="art-600"></a>第六百条　出卖具有知识产权的标的物的，除法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定外，该标的物的知识产权不属于买受人。

**Article 600** Where a subject matter with intellectual property rights is sold, unless otherwise provided by law or agreed by the parties, such intellectual property rights in the subject matter shall not belong to the buyer.

<a id="art-601"></a>第六百零一条　出卖人应当按照约定的时间交付标的物。约定交付期限的，出卖人可以在该交付期限内的任何时间交付。

**Article 601** The seller shall deliver the subject matter at the agreed time. If a period for delivery is agreed upon, the seller may deliver at any time within such period.

<a id="art-602"></a>第六百零二条　当事人没有约定标的物的交付期限或者约定不明确的，适用[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)、第五百一十一条第四项的规定。

**Article 602** If the parties have not agreed on a time limit for delivery of the subject matter or such agreement is unclear, the provisions of Articles 510 and 511(4) of this Law shall apply.

<a id="art-603"></a>第六百零三条　出卖人应当按照约定的地点交付标的物。  
当事人没有约定交付地点或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，适用下列规定：  
（一）标的物需要运输的，出卖人应当将标的物交付给第一承运人以运交给买受人；  
（二）标的物不需要运输，出卖人和买受人订立合同时知道标的物在某一地点的，出卖人应当在该地点交付标的物；不知道标的物在某一地点的，应当在出卖人订立合同时的营业地交付标的物。

**Article 603** The seller shall deliver the subject matter at the agreed place. If the parties have not agreed on the place of delivery or such agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the following provisions shall apply:  
(1) if the subject matter requires carriage, the seller shall deliver the subject matter to the first carrier for conveyance to the buyer;  
(2) if the subject matter does not require carriage, and the seller and the buyer, at the time of entering into the contract, know that the subject matter is at a certain place, the seller shall deliver the subject matter at that place; if they do not know that the subject matter is at a certain place, the seller shall deliver the subject matter at the seller's place of business at the time of entering into the contract.

<a id="art-604"></a>第六百零四条　标的物毁损、灭失的风险，在标的物交付之前由出卖人承担，交付之后由买受人承担，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 604** The risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter shall be borne by the seller before delivery and by the buyer after delivery, unless otherwise provided by law or agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-605"></a>第六百零五条　因买受人的原因致使标的物未按照约定的期限交付的，买受人应当自违反约定时起承担标的物毁损、灭失的风险。

**Article 605** If the subject matter is not delivered within the agreed time limit due to the buyer, the buyer shall bear the risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter from the time of such breach of agreement.

<a id="art-606"></a>第六百零六条　出卖人出卖交由承运人运输的在途标的物，除当事人另有约定外，毁损、灭失的风险自合同成立时起由买受人承担。

**Article 606** Where the seller sells the subject matter which is in transit and under carriage by a carrier, unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter shall be borne by the buyer from the time of formation of the contract.

<a id="art-607"></a>第六百零七条　出卖人按照约定将标的物运送至买受人指定地点并交付给承运人后，标的物毁损、灭失的风险由买受人承担。  
当事人没有约定交付地点或者约定不明确，依据[本法第六百零三条](#art-603)第二款第一项的规定标的物需要运输的，出卖人将标的物交付给第一承运人后，标的物毁损、灭失的风险由买受人承担。

**Article 607** After the seller transports the subject matter to the place designated by the buyer and delivers it to the carrier in accordance with the agreement, the risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter shall be borne by the buyer.  
If the parties have not agreed on the place of delivery or such agreement is unclear, and the subject matter requires carriage under Article 603(2)(1) of this Law, after the seller delivers the subject matter to the first carrier, the risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter shall be borne by the buyer.

<a id="art-608"></a>第六百零八条　出卖人按照约定或者依据[本法第六百零三条](#art-603)第二款第二项的规定将标的物置于交付地点，买受人违反约定没有收取的，标的物毁损、灭失的风险自违反约定时起由买受人承担。

**Article 608** If the seller places the subject matter at the place of delivery in accordance with the agreement or under Article 603(2)(2) of this Law, and the buyer, in breach of the agreement, fails to take delivery, the risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter shall be borne by the buyer from the time of such breach of agreement.

<a id="art-609"></a>第六百零九条　出卖人按照约定未交付有关标的物的单证和资料的，不影响标的物毁损、灭失风险的转移。

**Article 609** The seller's failure to deliver the documents and materials relating to the subject matter in accordance with the agreement shall not affect the transfer of the risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter.

<a id="art-610"></a>第六百一十条　因标的物不符合质量要求，致使不能实现合同目的的，买受人可以拒绝接受标的物或者解除合同。买受人拒绝接受标的物或者解除合同的，标的物毁损、灭失的风险由出卖人承担。

**Article 610** If the subject matter fails to meet the quality requirements, thereby preventing the realization of the purpose of the contract, the buyer may refuse to accept the subject matter or dissolve the contract. If the buyer refuses to accept the subject matter or dissolves the contract, the risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter shall be borne by the seller.

<a id="art-611"></a>第六百一十一条　标的物毁损、灭失的风险由买受人承担的，不影响因出卖人履行义务不符合约定，买受人请求其承担违约责任的权利。

**Article 611** Where the risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter is borne by the buyer, this shall not affect the buyer's right to demand that the seller bear liability for breach of contract due to the seller's failure to perform its obligations in accordance with the agreement.

<a id="art-612"></a>第六百一十二条　出卖人就交付的标的物，负有保证第三人对该标的物不享有任何权利的义务，但是法律另有规定的除外。

**Article 612** The seller shall have the obligation to warrant that no third party has any right to the delivered subject matter, unless otherwise provided by law.

<a id="art-613"></a>第六百一十三条　买受人订立合同时知道或者应当知道第三人对买卖的标的物享有权利的，出卖人不承担前条规定的义务。

**Article 613** If the buyer knew or should have known, at the time of entering into the contract, that a third party had rights to the subject matter of the sale, the seller shall not bear the obligation provided for in the preceding article.

<a id="art-614"></a>第六百一十四条　买受人有确切证据证明第三人对标的物享有权利的，可以中止支付相应的价款，但是出卖人提供适当担保的除外。

**Article 614** If the buyer has conclusive evidence proving that a third party has rights to the subject matter, the buyer may suspend payment of the corresponding price, unless the seller provides appropriate guarantee.

<a id="art-615"></a>第六百一十五条　出卖人应当按照约定的质量要求交付标的物。出卖人提供有关标的物质量说明的，交付的标的物应当符合该说明的质量要求。

**Article 615** The seller shall deliver the subject matter in accordance with the agreed quality requirements. If the seller has provided a description of the quality of the subject matter, the delivered subject matter shall meet the quality requirements set out in such description.

<a id="art-616"></a>第六百一十六条　当事人对标的物的质量要求没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，适用[本法第五百一十一条](#art-511)第一项的规定。

**Article 616** If the parties have not agreed on the quality requirements for the subject matter or such agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the provisions of Article 511(1) of this Law shall apply.

<a id="art-617"></a>第六百一十七条　出卖人交付的标的物不符合质量要求的，买受人可以依据[本法第五百八十二条](#art-582)至第五百八十四条的规定请求承担违约责任。

**Article 617** If the subject matter delivered by the seller does not meet the quality requirements, the buyer may, in accordance with the provisions of Articles 582 to 584 of this Law, demand that the seller bear liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-618"></a>第六百一十八条　当事人约定减轻或者免除出卖人对标的物瑕疵承担的责任，因出卖人故意或者重大过失不告知买受人标的物瑕疵的，出卖人无权主张减轻或者免除责任。

**Article 618** If the parties agree to mitigate or exempt the seller from liability for defects in the subject matter, but the seller, intentionally or by gross negligence, fails to inform the buyer of such defects, the seller shall have no right to claim mitigation or exemption of liability.

<a id="art-619"></a>第六百一十九条　出卖人应当按照约定的包装方式交付标的物。对包装方式没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，应当按照通用的方式包装；没有通用方式的，应当采取足以保护标的物且有利于节约资源、保护生态环境的包装方式。

**Article 619** The seller shall deliver the subject matter in the agreed packaging method. If there is no agreement on the packaging method or the agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the subject matter shall be packaged in a generally accepted manner; if there is no generally accepted manner, the packaging method shall be one that is sufficient to protect the subject matter and is conducive to resource conservation and ecological environment protection.

<a id="art-620"></a>第六百二十条　买受人收到标的物时应当在约定的检验期限内检验。没有约定检验期限的，应当及时检验。

**Article 620** Upon receipt of the subject matter, the buyer shall inspect it within the agreed inspection period. If no inspection period is agreed, the buyer shall inspect it promptly.

<a id="art-621"></a>第六百二十一条　当事人约定检验期限的，买受人应当在检验期限内将标的物的数量或者质量不符合约定的情形通知出卖人。买受人怠于通知的，视为标的物的数量或者质量符合约定。  
当事人没有约定检验期限的，买受人应当在发现或者应当发现标的物的数量或者质量不符合约定的合理期限内通知出卖人。买受人在合理期限内未通知或者自收到标的物之日起二年内未通知出卖人的，视为标的物的数量或者质量符合约定；但是，对标的物有质量保证期的，适用质量保证期，不适用该二年的规定。  
出卖人知道或者应当知道提供的标的物不符合约定的，买受人不受前两款规定的通知时间的限制。

**Article 621** Where the parties agree on an inspection period, the buyer shall notify the seller within the inspection period if the quantity or quality of the subject matter does not conform to the agreement. If the buyer is dilatory in giving such notice, the quantity or quality of the subject matter shall be deemed to conform to the agreement. Where the parties have not agreed on an inspection period, the buyer shall notify the seller within a reasonable period after discovering or should have discovered that the quantity or quality of the subject matter does not conform to the agreement. If the buyer fails to give notice within a reasonable period, or fails to give notice to the seller within two years from the date of receipt of the subject matter, the quantity or quality of the subject matter shall be deemed to conform to the agreement; provided that, if a quality guarantee period is applicable to the subject matter, the quality guarantee period shall apply instead of the said two-year period. Where the seller knew or should have known that the delivered subject matter did not conform to the agreement, the buyer shall not be subject to the time limits for notice prescribed in the preceding two paragraphs.

<a id="art-622"></a>第六百二十二条　当事人约定的检验期限过短，根据标的物的性质和交易习惯，买受人在检验期限内难以完成全面检验的，该期限仅视为买受人对标的物的外观瑕疵提出异议的期限。  
约定的检验期限或者质量保证期短于法律、行政法规规定期限的，应当以法律、行政法规规定的期限为准。

**Article 622** If the inspection period agreed upon by the parties is so short that, given the nature of the subject matter and trade practices, it is difficult for the buyer to complete a comprehensive inspection within such period, such period shall be deemed only as the period for the buyer to raise objections concerning the apparent defects of the subject matter.  
If the agreed inspection period or quality guarantee period is shorter than the period prescribed by laws or administrative regulations, the period prescribed by such laws or administrative regulations shall prevail.

<a id="art-623"></a>第六百二十三条　当事人对检验期限未作约定，买受人签收的送货单、确认单等载明标的物数量、型号、规格的，推定买受人已经对数量和外观瑕疵进行检验，但是有相关证据足以推翻的除外。

**Article 623** Where the parties have not agreed on an inspection period, and the delivery note, confirmation note or the like signed by the buyer specifies the quantity, model, and specification of the subject matter, it shall be presumed that the buyer has inspected the quantity and apparent defects of the subject matter, unless there is sufficient relevant evidence to the contrary.

<a id="art-624"></a>第六百二十四条　出卖人依照买受人的指示向第三人交付标的物，出卖人和买受人约定的检验标准与买受人和第三人约定的检验标准不一致的，以出卖人和买受人约定的检验标准为准。

**Article 624** Where the seller delivers the subject matter to a third party as instructed by the buyer, and the inspection standard agreed upon between the seller and the buyer is inconsistent with that agreed upon between the buyer and the third party, the inspection standard agreed upon between the seller and the buyer shall prevail.

<a id="art-625"></a>第六百二十五条　依照法律、行政法规的规定或者按照当事人的约定，标的物在有效使用年限届满后应予回收的，出卖人负有自行或者委托第三人对标的物予以回收的义务。

**Article 625** Where, according to laws or administrative regulations or the agreement of the parties, the subject matter shall be recycled after the expiration of its useful life, the seller shall bear the obligation to recycle the subject matter itself or to entrust a third party to do so.

<a id="art-626"></a>第六百二十六条　买受人应当按照约定的数额和支付方式支付价款。对价款的数额和支付方式没有约定或者约定不明确的，适用[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)、第五百一十一条第二项和第五项的规定。

**Article 626** The buyer shall pay the price in the agreed amount and by the agreed method of payment. If the amount or method of payment is not agreed upon or the agreement is unclear, the provisions of Articles 510, and subparagraphs (2) and (5) of Article 511 of this Law shall apply.

<a id="art-627"></a>第六百二十七条　买受人应当按照约定的地点支付价款。对支付地点没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，买受人应当在出卖人的营业地支付；但是，约定支付价款以交付标的物或者交付提取标的物单证为条件的，在交付标的物或者交付提取标的物单证的所在地支付。

**Article 627** The buyer shall pay the price at the agreed place. If the place of payment is not agreed upon or the agreement is unclear, and cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the buyer shall pay at the seller's place of business; provided that, if the payment of the price is conditional upon the delivery of the subject matter or the documents for taking delivery of the subject matter, payment shall be made at the place where the subject matter or the documents for taking delivery thereof are delivered.

<a id="art-628"></a>第六百二十八条　买受人应当按照约定的时间支付价款。对支付时间没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，买受人应当在收到标的物或者提取标的物单证的同时支付。

**Article 628** The buyer shall pay the price at the agreed time. If the time of payment is not agreed upon or the agreement is unclear, and cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the buyer shall pay simultaneously upon receipt of the subject matter or the documents for taking delivery of the subject matter.

<a id="art-629"></a>第六百二十九条　出卖人多交标的物的，买受人可以接收或者拒绝接收多交的部分。买受人接收多交部分的，按照约定的价格支付价款；买受人拒绝接收多交部分的，应当及时通知出卖人。

**Article 629** If the seller delivers an excess quantity of the subject matter, the buyer may accept or reject the excess part. If the buyer accepts the excess part, it shall pay for it at the agreed price; if the buyer rejects the excess part, it shall notify the seller promptly.

<a id="art-630"></a>第六百三十条　标的物在交付之前产生的孳息，归出卖人所有；交付之后产生的孳息，归买受人所有。但是，当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 630** Fruits generated from the subject matter before delivery shall belong to the seller; those generated after delivery shall belong to the buyer. However, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-631"></a>第六百三十一条　因标的物的主物不符合约定而解除合同的，解除合同的效力及于从物。因标的物的从物不符合约定被解除的，解除的效力不及于主物。

**Article 631** Where the contract is dissolved due to the non-conformity of the principal component of the subject matter, the dissolution shall extend to the accessory component. Where the contract is dissolved due to the non-conformity of the accessory component, the dissolution shall not extend to the principal component.

<a id="art-632"></a>第六百三十二条　标的物为数物，其中一物不符合约定的，买受人可以就该物解除。但是，该物与他物分离使标的物的价值显受损害的，买受人可以就数物解除合同。

**Article 632** Where the subject matter consists of several items, and one of them does not conform to the agreement, the buyer may dissolve the contract only with respect to that item. However, if the separation of that item from the others would materially impair the value of the subject matter, the buyer may dissolve the contract with respect to all the items.

<a id="art-633"></a>第六百三十三条　出卖人分批交付标的物的，出卖人对其中一批标的物不交付或者交付不符合约定，致使该批标的物不能实现合同目的的，买受人可以就该批标的物解除。  
出卖人不交付其中一批标的物或者交付不符合约定，致使之后其他各批标的物的交付不能实现合同目的的，买受人可以就该批以及之后其他各批标的物解除。  
买受人如果就其中一批标的物解除，该批标的物与其他各批标的物相互依存的，可以就已经交付和未交付的各批标的物解除。

**Article 633** Where the seller delivers the subject matter in installments, if the seller fails to deliver one installment or delivers it in a manner that does not conform to the agreement, thereby frustrating the purpose of the contract for that installment, the buyer may dissolve the contract with respect to that installment.  
If the seller fails to deliver one installment or delivers it in a manner that does not conform to the agreement, thereby frustrating the purpose of the contract for the subsequent installments, the buyer may dissolve the contract with respect to that installment and all subsequent installments.  
If the buyer dissolves the contract with respect to one installment, and if that installment is interdependent with the other installments, the buyer may dissolve the contract with respect to all installments, whether delivered or not.

<a id="art-634"></a>第六百三十四条　分期付款的买受人未支付到期价款的数额达到全部价款的五分之一，经催告后在合理期限内仍未支付到期价款的，出卖人可以请求买受人支付全部价款或者解除合同。  
出卖人解除合同的，可以向买受人请求支付该标的物的使用费。

**Article 634** If the buyer under an installment sale fails to pay an amount of the price due which reaches one-fifth of the total price, and still fails to pay the amount due within a reasonable period after receiving a demand for payment, the seller may request the buyer to pay the entire outstanding price or dissolve the contract.  
If the seller dissolves the contract, it may request the buyer to pay a fee for the use of the subject matter.

<a id="art-635"></a>第六百三十五条　凭样品买卖的当事人应当封存样品，并可以对样品质量予以说明。出卖人交付的标的物应当与样品及其说明的质量相同。

**Article 635** In a sale by sample, the parties shall seal the sample and may provide a description of the quality of the sample. The subject matter delivered by the seller shall conform to the quality of the sample and its description.

<a id="art-636"></a>第六百三十六条　凭样品买卖的买受人不知道样品有隐蔽瑕疵的，即使交付的标的物与样品相同，出卖人交付的标的物的质量仍然应当符合同种物的通常标准。

**Article 636** In a sale by sample, if the buyer is unaware of latent defects in the sample, even if the delivered subject matter is the same as the sample, the quality of the subject matter delivered by the seller shall still conform to the usual standards for goods of the same type.

<a id="art-637"></a>第六百三十七条　试用买卖的当事人可以约定标的物的试用期限。对试用期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，由出卖人确定。

**Article 637** The parties to a sale on trial may agree on a trial period for the subject matter. If the trial period is not agreed upon or the agreement is unclear, and cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, it shall be determined by the seller.

<a id="art-638"></a>第六百三十八条　试用买卖的买受人在试用期内可以购买标的物，也可以拒绝购买。试用期限届满，买受人对是否购买标的物未作表示的，视为购买。  
试用买卖的买受人在试用期内已经支付部分价款或者对标的物实施出卖、出租、设立担保物权等行为的，视为同意购买。

**Article 638** The buyer in a sale on trial may purchase the subject matter or refuse to purchase it during the trial period. Upon expiration of the trial period, if the buyer does not indicate whether to purchase the subject matter, it shall be deemed to have purchased it. If the buyer in a sale on trial has paid part of the price or has performed acts such as selling, leasing, or creating a security interest over the subject matter during the trial period, it shall be deemed to have agreed to purchase it.

<a id="art-639"></a>第六百三十九条　试用买卖的当事人对标的物使用费没有约定或者约定不明确的，出卖人无权请求买受人支付。

**Article 639** If the parties to a sale on trial have not agreed on a fee for the use of the subject matter, or the agreement is unclear, the seller shall have no right to request the buyer to pay such a fee.

<a id="art-640"></a>第六百四十条　标的物在试用期内毁损、灭失的风险由出卖人承担。

**Article 640** The risk of damage to or loss of the subject matter during the trial period shall be borne by the seller.

<a id="art-641"></a>第六百四十一条　当事人可以在买卖合同中约定买受人未履行支付价款或者其他义务的，标的物的所有权属于出卖人。  
出卖人对标的物保留的所有权，未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。

**Article 641** The parties may agree in a sales contract that the ownership of the subject matter shall remain with the seller if the buyer fails to perform the obligation to pay the price or other obligations.  
The seller's retained ownership of the subject matter may not be asserted against a bona fide third party unless it has been registered.

<a id="art-642"></a>第六百四十二条　当事人约定出卖人保留合同标的物的所有权，在标的物所有权转移前，买受人有下列情形之一，造成出卖人损害的，除当事人另有约定外，出卖人有权取回标的物：  
（一）未按照约定支付价款，经催告后在合理期限内仍未支付；  
（二）未按照约定完成特定条件；  
（三）将标的物出卖、出质或者作出其他不当处分。  
出卖人可以与买受人协商取回标的物；协商不成的，可以参照适用担保物权的实现程序。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-64" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第64条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 642** Where the parties agree that the seller shall retain ownership of the subject matter of the contract, and before the transfer of ownership of the subject matter, the buyer commits any of the following acts causing damage to the seller, unless the parties agree otherwise, the seller has the right to repossess the subject matter:  
(1) failing to pay the price as agreed and still failing to pay within a reasonable period after demand for payment;  
(2) failing to fulfill specific conditions as agreed;  
(3) selling, pledging, or otherwise improperly disposing of the subject matter. The seller may negotiate with the buyer for the repossession of the subject matter; if negotiation fails, the procedures for the realization of security interests may be applied mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-643"></a>第六百四十三条　出卖人依据前条第一款的规定取回标的物后，买受人在双方约定或者出卖人指定的合理回赎期限内，消除出卖人取回标的物的事由的，可以请求回赎标的物。  
买受人在回赎期限内没有回赎标的物，出卖人可以以合理价格将标的物出卖给第三人，出卖所得价款扣除买受人未支付的价款以及必要费用后仍有剩余的，应当返还买受人；不足部分由买受人清偿。

**Article 643** After the seller repossesses the subject matter in accordance with paragraph 1 of the preceding Article, if the buyer eliminates the grounds for the seller's repossession of the subject matter within the reasonable redemption period agreed by the parties or designated by the seller, the buyer may request redemption of the subject matter. If the buyer fails to redeem the subject matter within the redemption period, the seller may sell the subject matter to a third party at a reasonable price. If, after deducting the unpaid price and necessary expenses from the proceeds of the sale, there is a surplus, it shall be returned to the buyer; if there is a deficiency, the buyer shall make up the shortfall.

<a id="art-644"></a>第六百四十四条　招标投标买卖的当事人的权利和义务以及招标投标程序等，依照有关法律、行政法规的规定。

**Article 644** The rights and obligations of the parties to a sale by tender and the tender procedures, among others, shall be governed by the provisions of relevant laws and administrative regulations.

<a id="art-645"></a>第六百四十五条　拍卖的当事人的权利和义务以及拍卖程序等，依照有关法律、行政法规的规定。

**Article 645** The rights and obligations of the parties to an auction and the auction procedures, among others, shall be governed by the provisions of relevant laws and administrative regulations.

<a id="art-646"></a>第六百四十六条　法律对其他有偿合同有规定的，依照其规定；没有规定的，参照适用买卖合同的有关规定。

**Article 646** Where a law provides for other types of onerous contracts, such provisions shall apply; where there are no such provisions, the relevant provisions on sales contracts shall apply mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-647"></a>第六百四十七条　当事人约定易货交易，转移标的物的所有权的，参照适用买卖合同的有关规定。

**Article 647** Where the parties agree to a barter transaction for the transfer of ownership of the subject matter, the relevant provisions on sales contracts shall apply mutatis mutandis.

### 第十章　供用电、水、气、热力合同

<a id="art-648"></a>第六百四十八条　供用电合同是供电人向用电人供电，用电人支付电费的合同。  
向社会公众供电的供电人，不得拒绝用电人合理的订立合同要求。

**Article 648** A power supply contract is a contract under which a power supplier supplies electricity to a power consumer and the power consumer pays the electricity fee.  
A power supplier that supplies electricity to the public shall not reject a reasonable request of a power consumer to conclude a contract.

<a id="art-649"></a>第六百四十九条　供用电合同的内容一般包括供电的方式、质量、时间，用电容量、地址、性质，计量方式，电价、电费的结算方式，供用电设施的维护责任等条款。

**Article 649** The contents of a power supply contract generally include clauses on the method, quality, and time of power supply; the capacity, address, and nature of power consumption; the metering method; the electricity price and the method of settlement of electricity fees; and the maintenance responsibilities for power supply and consumption facilities.

<a id="art-650"></a>第六百五十条　供用电合同的履行地点，按照当事人约定；当事人没有约定或者约定不明确的，供电设施的产权分界处为履行地点。

**Article 650** The place of performance of a power supply contract shall be as agreed by the parties; if there is no agreement between the parties or the agreement is unclear, the boundary point of the property rights of the power supply facilities shall be the place of performance.

<a id="art-651"></a>第六百五十一条　供电人应当按照国家规定的供电质量标准和约定安全供电。供电人未按照国家规定的供电质量标准和约定安全供电，造成用电人损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 651** A power supplier shall supply electricity safely in accordance with the power supply quality standards prescribed by the State and the agreement. If the power supplier fails to supply electricity safely in accordance with the power supply quality standards prescribed by the State and the agreement, causing losses to the power consumer, the power supplier shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-652"></a>第六百五十二条　供电人因供电设施计划检修、临时检修、依法限电或者用电人违法用电等原因，需要中断供电时，应当按照国家有关规定事先通知用电人；未事先通知用电人中断供电，造成用电人损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 652** Where a power supplier needs to interrupt the power supply due to scheduled maintenance, temporary maintenance, lawful curtailment of power, or illegal use of electricity by the power consumer, among other reasons, the power supplier shall notify the power consumer in advance in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State; if the power supplier fails to notify the power consumer in advance of the interruption of power supply, causing losses to the power consumer, the power supplier shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-653"></a>第六百五十三条　因自然灾害等原因断电，供电人应当按照国家有关规定及时抢修；未及时抢修，造成用电人损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 653** Where a power outage occurs due to natural disasters or other reasons, the power supplier shall promptly carry out emergency repairs in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State; if the power supplier fails to carry out emergency repairs promptly, causing losses to the power consumer, the power supplier shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-654"></a>第六百五十四条　用电人应当按照国家有关规定和当事人的约定及时支付电费。用电人逾期不支付电费的，应当按照约定支付违约金。经催告用电人在合理期限内仍不支付电费和违约金的，供电人可以按照国家规定的程序中止供电。  
供电人依据前款规定中止供电的，应当事先通知用电人。

**Article 654** A power consumer shall pay the electricity fee promptly in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State and the agreement of the parties. If the power consumer fails to pay the electricity fee when due, the power consumer shall pay liquidated damages in accordance with the agreement. If, after demand for payment, the power consumer still fails to pay the electricity fee and liquidated damages within a reasonable period, the power supplier may suspend the power supply in accordance with the procedures prescribed by the State. If the power supplier suspends the power supply pursuant to the preceding paragraph, the power supplier shall notify the power consumer in advance.

<a id="art-655"></a>第六百五十五条　用电人应当按照国家有关规定和当事人的约定安全、节约和计划用电。用电人未按照国家有关规定和当事人的约定用电，造成供电人损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 655** A power consumer shall use electricity safely, economically, and as planned in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State and the agreement of the parties. If the power consumer fails to use electricity in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State and the agreement of the parties, causing losses to the power supplier, the power consumer shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-656"></a>第六百五十六条　供用水、供用气、供用热力合同，参照适用供用电合同的有关规定。

**Article 656** Contracts for the supply of water, gas, or heat shall apply mutatis mutandis the relevant provisions on power supply contracts.

### 第十一章　赠与合同

<a id="art-657"></a>第六百五十七条　赠与合同是赠与人将自己的财产无偿给予受赠人，受赠人表示接受赠与的合同。

**Article 657** A gift contract is a contract under which a donor gives the donor's own property to a donee gratuitously, and the donee expresses acceptance of the gift.

<a id="art-658"></a>第六百五十八条　赠与人在赠与财产的权利转移之前可以撤销赠与。  
经过公证的赠与合同或者依法不得撤销的具有救灾、扶贫、助残等公益、道德义务性质的赠与合同，不适用前款规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-32" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第32条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 658** The donor may revoke the gift before the transfer of the rights to the gifted property.  
The provisions of the preceding paragraph shall not apply to a notarized gift contract or a gift contract of a public welfare or moral obligation nature, such as for disaster relief, poverty alleviation, or assistance to the disabled, which shall not be revoked according to law.

<a id="art-659"></a>第六百五十九条　赠与的财产依法需要办理登记或者其他手续的，应当办理有关手续。

**Article 659** Where the gifted property is required by law to undergo registration or other procedures, the relevant procedures shall be completed.

<a id="art-660"></a>第六百六十条　经过公证的赠与合同或者依法不得撤销的具有救灾、扶贫、助残等公益、道德义务性质的赠与合同，赠与人不交付赠与财产的，受赠人可以请求交付。  
依据前款规定应当交付的赠与财产因赠与人故意或者重大过失致使毁损、灭失的，赠与人应当承担赔偿责任。
