However, the carrier shall not bear compensation liability if it proves that the damage to or loss of the goods is caused by force majeure, the inherent nature of the goods, reasonable depletion, or the fault of the consignor or the consignee.

<a id="art-833"></a>第八百三十三条　货物的毁损、灭失的赔偿额，当事人有约定的，按照其约定；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，按照交付或者应当交付时货物到达地的市场价格计算。法律、行政法规对赔偿额的计算方法和赔偿限额另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 833** The amount of compensation for the damage to or loss of goods shall be calculated in accordance with the agreement of the parties; if there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, it shall be calculated based on the market price at the place of destination at the time of delivery or when delivery should have been made. If laws or administrative regulations provide otherwise for the calculation method of the compensation amount or the limit of compensation, those provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-834"></a>第八百三十四条　两个以上承运人以同一运输方式联运的，与托运人订立合同的承运人应当对全程运输承担责任；损失发生在某一运输区段的，与托运人订立合同的承运人和该区段的承运人承担连带责任。

**Article 834** Where two or more carriers engage in successive carriage using the same mode of transport, the carrier that has entered into the contract with the consignor shall be liable for the entire carriage; if the loss occurs in a particular section of the transport, the carrier that has entered into the contract with the consignor and the carrier in that section shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-835"></a>第八百三十五条　货物在运输过程中因不可抗力灭失，未收取运费的，承运人不得请求支付运费；已经收取运费的，托运人可以请求返还。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 835** If the goods are lost due to force majeure during carriage and the freight has not been collected, the carrier shall not claim payment of the freight; if the freight has already been collected, the consignor may request its return. If the law provides otherwise, those provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-836"></a>第八百三十六条　托运人或者收货人不支付运费、保管费或者其他费用的，承运人对相应的运输货物享有留置权，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 836** If the consignor or the consignee fails to pay freight, storage fees, or other expenses, the carrier shall have a lien over the relevant goods in transport, unless the parties have agreed otherwise.

<a id="art-837"></a>第八百三十七条　收货人不明或者收货人无正当理由拒绝受领货物的，承运人依法可以提存货物。

**Article 837** If the consignee is unknown or if the consignee refuses to take delivery of the goods without just cause, the carrier may deposit the goods in accordance with law.

#### 第四节　多式联运合同

<a id="art-838"></a>第八百三十八条　多式联运经营人负责履行或者组织履行多式联运合同，对全程运输享有承运人的权利，承担承运人的义务。

**Article 838** The multimodal transport operator shall be responsible for performing or arranging the performance of the multimodal transport contract, and shall enjoy the rights and bear the obligations of a carrier with respect to the entire transport.

<a id="art-839"></a>第八百三十九条　多式联运经营人可以与参加多式联运的各区段承运人就多式联运合同的各区段运输约定相互之间的责任；但是，该约定不影响多式联运经营人对全程运输承担的义务。

**Article 839** The multimodal transport operator may agree with the carriers of the various sections participating in the multimodal transport on their respective responsibilities regarding the transport of each section under the multimodal transport contract; however, such agreement shall not affect the obligations of the multimodal transport operator in respect of the entire transport.

<a id="art-840"></a>第八百四十条　多式联运经营人收到托运人交付的货物时，应当签发多式联运单据。按照托运人的要求，多式联运单据可以是可转让单据，也可以是不可转让单据。

**Article 840** When the multimodal transport operator receives the goods delivered by the consignor, it shall issue a multimodal transport document. At the request of the consignor, the multimodal transport document may be a negotiable document or a non-negotiable document.

<a id="art-841"></a>第八百四十一条　因托运人托运货物时的过错造成多式联运经营人损失的，即使托运人已经转让多式联运单据，托运人仍然应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 841** Where the consignor causes losses to the multi-modal transport operator due to its fault in consigning the goods, the consignor shall still be liable for compensation even if the consignor has already transferred the multi-modal transport document.

<a id="art-842"></a>第八百四十二条　货物的毁损、灭失发生于多式联运的某一运输区段的，多式联运经营人的赔偿责任和责任限额，适用调整该区段运输方式的有关法律规定；货物毁损、灭失发生的运输区段不能确定的，依照本章规定承担赔偿责任。

**Article 842** Where the damage to or loss of the goods occurs during a particular stage of the multi-modal transport, the liability for compensation and the limitation of liability of the multi-modal transport operator shall be governed by the relevant laws regulating the mode of transport for that stage. Where the stage of transport during which the damage to or loss of the goods occurs cannot be determined, the liability for compensation shall be borne in accordance with the provisions of this Chapter.

### 第二十章　技术合同

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-843"></a>第八百四十三条　技术合同是当事人就技术开发、转让、许可、咨询或者服务订立的确立相互之间权利和义务的合同。

**Article 843** A technology contract is a contract concluded by parties to establish their mutual rights and obligations regarding technology development, transfer, licensing, consultation or services.

<a id="art-844"></a>第八百四十四条　订立技术合同，应当有利于知识产权的保护和科学技术的进步，促进科学技术成果的研发、转化、应用和推广。

**Article 844** The conclusion of a technology contract shall be conducive to the protection of intellectual property and the advancement of science and technology, and shall promote the research and development, transformation, application and dissemination of scientific and technological achievements.

<a id="art-845"></a>第八百四十五条　技术合同的内容一般包括项目的名称，标的的内容、范围和要求，履行的计划、地点和方式，技术信息和资料的保密，技术成果的归属和收益的分配办法，验收标准和方法，名词和术语的解释等条款。  
与履行合同有关的技术背景资料、可行性论证和技术评价报告、项目任务书和计划书、技术标准、技术规范、原始设计和工艺文件，以及其他技术文档，按照当事人的约定可以作为合同的组成部分。  
技术合同涉及专利的，应当注明发明创造的名称、专利申请人和专利权人、申请日期、申请号、专利号以及专利权的有效期限。

**Article 845** The contents of a technology contract generally include clauses such as the project name, the subject matter's content, scope and requirements, the plan, place and method of performance, the confidentiality of technical information and materials, the attribution of technical achievements and the method of distributing proceeds, the acceptance criteria and methods, and the interpretation of terms and expressions.  
Technical background materials, feasibility studies and technical evaluation reports, project task statements and plans, technical standards, technical specifications, original designs and process documents, as well as other technical documentation related to the performance of the contract, may be made part of the contract according to the agreement of the parties.  
Where a technology contract involves a patent, the name of the invention or creation, the patent applicant and patentee, the application date, the application number, the patent number, and the term of validity of the patent right shall be indicated.

<a id="art-846"></a>第八百四十六条　技术合同价款、报酬或者使用费的支付方式由当事人约定，可以采取一次总算、一次总付或者一次总算、分期支付，也可以采取提成支付或者提成支付附加预付入门费的方式。  
约定提成支付的，可以按照产品价格、实施专利和使用技术秘密后新增的产值、利润或者产品销售额的一定比例提成，也可以按照约定的其他方式计算。提成支付的比例可以采取固定比例、逐年递增比例或者逐年递减比例。  
约定提成支付的，当事人可以约定查阅有关会计账目的办法。

**Article 846** The method of payment for the price, remuneration or royalty under a technology contract shall be agreed upon by the parties; it may be in the form of a lump sum payment in full, a lump sum payment in installments, or a royalty payment or a royalty payment with an upfront fee. Where royalty payment is agreed upon, the calculation may be based on a certain percentage of the product price, or the newly increased output value, profit or product sales revenue resulting from the implementation of the patent or use of the technical know-how, or may be calculated according to other methods agreed upon. The percentage for royalty payment may be fixed, or increase or decrease on a yearly basis. Where royalty payment is agreed upon, the parties may agree on a method for inspecting the relevant accounting books.

<a id="art-847"></a>第八百四十七条　职务技术成果的使用权、转让权属于法人或者非法人组织的，法人或者非法人组织可以就该项职务技术成果订立技术合同。法人或者非法人组织订立技术合同转让职务技术成果时，职务技术成果的完成人享有以同等条件优先受让的权利。  
职务技术成果是执行法人或者非法人组织的工作任务，或者主要是利用法人或者非法人组织的物质技术条件所完成的技术成果。

**Article 847** Where the right to use and the right to transfer a service-related technological achievement belong to a legal person or unincorporated organization, such legal person or unincorporated organization may conclude a technology contract regarding such service-related technological achievement. Where a legal person or unincorporated organization concludes a technology contract to transfer a service-related technological achievement, the individual who has accomplished the service-related technological achievement shall have the right of first refusal to acquire it under the same conditions. A service-related technological achievement is a technological achievement accomplished in the course of performing the tasks of a legal person or unincorporated organization, or predominantly by utilizing the material and technical conditions of a legal person or unincorporated organization.

<a id="art-848"></a>第八百四十八条　非职务技术成果的使用权、转让权属于完成技术成果的个人，完成技术成果的个人可以就该项非职务技术成果订立技术合同。

**Article 848** The right to use and the right to transfer a non-service-related technological achievement belong to the individual who has accomplished the technological achievement, and such individual may conclude a technology contract regarding such non-service-related technological achievement.

<a id="art-849"></a>第八百四十九条　完成技术成果的个人享有在有关技术成果文件上写明自己是技术成果完成者的权利和取得荣誉证书、奖励的权利。

**Article 849** The individual who has accomplished a technological achievement shall have the right to be identified as the acclaimer of the technological achievement in the relevant documents on the technological achievement and the right to obtain a certificate of honor and a reward.

<a id="art-850"></a>第八百五十条　非法垄断技术或者侵害他人技术成果的技术合同无效。

**Article 850** A technology contract that illegally monopolizes technology or infringes upon the technological achievements of others is void.

#### 第二节　技术开发合同

<a id="art-851"></a>第八百五十一条　技术开发合同是当事人之间就新技术、新产品、新工艺、新品种或者新材料及其系统的研究开发所订立的合同。  
技术开发合同包括委托开发合同和合作开发合同。  
技术开发合同应当采用书面形式。  
当事人之间就具有实用价值的科技成果实施转化订立的合同，参照适用技术开发合同的有关规定。

**Article 851** A technology development contract is a contract concluded between parties for the research and development of new technologies, new products, new processes, new varieties or new materials and their systems.  
Technology development contracts include commissioned development contracts and cooperative development contracts.  
A technology development contract shall be in writing.  
A contract concluded between parties for the transformation and implementation of scientific and technological achievements with practical value shall be governed by reference to the relevant provisions on technology development contracts.

<a id="art-852"></a>第八百五十二条　委托开发合同的委托人应当按照约定支付研究开发经费和报酬，提供技术资料，提出研究开发要求，完成协作事项，接受研究开发成果。

**Article 852** The commissioning party under a commissioned development contract shall pay the research and development expenses and remuneration, provide technical information, put forward the research and development requirements, complete the matters of collaboration, and accept the research and development results in accordance with the agreement.

<a id="art-853"></a>第八百五十三条　委托开发合同的研究开发人应当按照约定制定和实施研究开发计划，合理使用研究开发经费，按期完成研究开发工作，交付研究开发成果，提供有关的技术资料和必要的技术指导，帮助委托人掌握研究开发成果。

**Article 853** The research and developer under a commissioned development contract shall formulate and implement the research and development plan, rationally use the research and development funds, complete the research and development work on schedule, deliver the research and development results, provide relevant technical information and necessary technical guidance, and assist the commissioning party in mastering the research and development results in accordance with the agreement.

<a id="art-854"></a>第八百五十四条　委托开发合同的当事人违反约定造成研究开发工作停滞、延误或者失败的，应当承担违约责任。

**Article 854** Where a party to a commissioned development contract violates the agreement, causing the research and development work to be stalled, delayed or fail, such party shall bear the liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-855"></a>第八百五十五条　合作开发合同的当事人应当按照约定进行投资，包括以技术进行投资，分工参与研究开发工作，协作配合研究开发工作。

**Article 855** The parties to a cooperative development contract shall make investments, including investment in the form of technology, participate in the research and development work with a division of labor, and collaborate and cooperate in the research and development work in accordance with the agreement.

<a id="art-856"></a>第八百五十六条　合作开发合同的当事人违反约定造成研究开发工作停滞、延误或者失败的，应当承担违约责任。

**Article 856** Where a party to a cooperative development contract violates the agreement, causing the research and development work to be stalled, delayed or fail, such party shall bear the liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-857"></a>第八百五十七条　作为技术开发合同标的的技术已经由他人公开，致使技术开发合同的履行没有意义的，当事人可以解除合同。

**Article 857** Where the technology that is the subject matter of a technology development contract has already been made public by another person, rendering the performance of the technology development contract meaningless, any party may rescind the contract.

<a id="art-858"></a>第八百五十八条　技术开发合同履行过程中，因出现无法克服的技术困难，致使研究开发失败或者部分失败的，该风险由当事人约定；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，风险由当事人合理分担。  
当事人一方发现前款规定的可能致使研究开发失败或者部分失败的情形时，应当及时通知另一方并采取适当措施减少损失；没有及时通知并采取适当措施，致使损失扩大的，应当就扩大的损失承担责任。

**Article 858** During the performance of a technology development contract, where the research and development fails or partially fails due to insurmountable technical difficulties, the risk shall be borne as agreed by the parties; where there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, andcannot be determined pursuant to the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, the risk shall be reasonably shared by the parties.  
Where one party discovers the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph that may lead to the failure or partial failure of the research and development, it shall promptly notify the other party and take appropriate measures to mitigate the losses; where it fails to promptly notify or take appropriate measures, causing the losses to be aggravated, it shall bear liability for the aggravated losses.

<a id="art-859"></a>第八百五十九条　委托开发完成的发明创造，除法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定外，申请专利的权利属于研究开发人。研究开发人取得专利权的，委托人可以依法实施该专利。  
研究开发人转让专利申请权的，委托人享有以同等条件优先受让的权利。

**Article 859** For an invention or creation accomplished under a commissioned development contract, unless otherwise provided by law or agreed by the parties, the right to apply for a patent belongs to the research and developer. Where the research and developer obtains the patent right, the commissioning party may exploit the patent according to law. Where the research and developer transfers the right to apply for a patent, the commissioning party shall have the right of first refusal to acquire it under the same conditions.

<a id="art-860"></a>第八百六十条　合作开发完成的发明创造，申请专利的权利属于合作开发的当事人共有；当事人一方转让其共有的专利申请权的，其他各方享有以同等条件优先受让的权利。但是，当事人另有约定的除外。  
合作开发的当事人一方声明放弃其共有的专利申请权的，除当事人另有约定外，可以由另一方单独申请或者由其他各方共同申请。申请人取得专利权的，放弃专利申请权的一方可以免费实施该专利。  
合作开发的当事人一方不同意申请专利的，另一方或者其他各方不得申请专利。

**Article 860** For an invention or creation accomplished under a cooperative development contract, the right to apply for a patent belongs to the parties to the cooperative development jointly. Where one party transfers its joint right to apply for a patent, the other parties shall have the right of first refusal to acquire it under the same conditions, unless otherwise agreed by the parties. Where one party to a cooperative development declares its waiver of its joint right to apply for a patent, the other party may apply alone or the other parties may jointly apply, unless otherwise agreed by the parties. Where the applicant obtains the patent right, the party that has waived its right to apply for a patent may exploit the patent free of charge. Where one party to a cooperative development does not agree to apply for a patent, the other party or parties may not apply for a patent.

<a id="art-861"></a>第八百六十一条　委托开发或者合作开发完成的技术秘密成果的使用权、转让权以及收益的分配办法，由当事人约定；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，在没有相同技术方案被授予专利权前，当事人均有使用和转让的权利。但是，委托开发的研究开发人不得在向委托人交付研究开发成果之前，将研究开发成果转让给第三人。

**Article 861** The parties shall agree on the right to use, the right to assign, and the method of distribution of proceeds from the results of technical secrets achieved through commissioned development or cooperative development. Where there is no such agreement or the agreement is unclear, and cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, before a patent right is granted for the same technical solution, each party shall have the right to use and assign such results. However, the developer in a commissioned development may not assign the research and development results to a third party before delivering the research and development results to the commissioning party.

#### 第三节　技术转让合同和技术许可合同

<a id="art-862"></a>第八百六十二条　技术转让合同是合法拥有技术的权利人，将现有特定的专利、专利申请、技术秘密的相关权利让与他人所订立的合同。  
技术许可合同是合法拥有技术的权利人，将现有特定的专利、技术秘密的相关权利许可他人实施、使用所订立的合同。  
技术转让合同和技术许可合同中关于提供实施技术的专用设备、原材料或者提供有关的技术咨询、技术服务的约定，属于合同的组成部分。

**Article 862** A technology transfer contract is a contract whereby a right holder who lawfully owns technology assigns to another party the relevant rights to an existing specific patent, patent application, or technical secret.  
A technology license contract is a contract whereby a right holder who lawfully owns technology licenses another party to exploit or use the relevant rights to an existing specific patent or technical secret.  
The stipulations in a technology transfer contract or a technology license contract regarding the provision of special-purpose equipment or raw materials for exploiting the technology, or the provision of related technical consultation or technical services, shall constitute an integral part of the contract.

<a id="art-863"></a>第八百六十三条　技术转让合同包括专利权转让、专利申请权转让、技术秘密转让等合同。  
技术许可合同包括专利实施许可、技术秘密使用许可等合同。  
技术转让合同和技术许可合同应当采用书面形式。

**Article 863** Technology transfer contracts include contracts for the assignment of patent rights, the assignment of patent application rights, and the assignment of technical secrets.  
Technology license contracts include contracts for the license to exploit a patent, the license to use a technical secret, and the like.  
A technology transfer contract or a technology license contract shall be in writing.

<a id="art-864"></a>第八百六十四条　技术转让合同和技术许可合同可以约定实施专利或者使用技术秘密的范围，但是不得限制技术竞争和技术发展。

**Article 864** A technology transfer contract or a technology license contract may stipulate the scope within which the patent is exploited or the technical secret is used, but shall not restrict technological competition or technological development.

<a id="art-865"></a>第八百六十五条　专利实施许可合同仅在该专利权的存续期限内有效。专利权有效期限届满或者专利权被宣告无效的，专利权人不得就该专利与他人订立专利实施许可合同。

**Article 865** A patent exploitation license contract shall only be valid during the term of the patent right. After the term of validity of the patent right expires or the patent right is declared invalid, the patentee may not enter into a patent exploitation license contract with any other person in respect of that patent.

<a id="art-866"></a>第八百六十六条　专利实施许可合同的许可人应当按照约定许可被许可人实施专利，交付实施专利有关的技术资料，提供必要的技术指导。

**Article 866** The licensor under a patent exploitation license contract shall, in accordance with the agreement, license the licensee to exploit the patent, deliver the technical materials related to the exploitation of the patent, and provide necessary technical guidance.

<a id="art-867"></a>第八百六十七条　专利实施许可合同的被许可人应当按照约定实施专利，不得许可约定以外的第三人实施该专利，并按照约定支付使用费。

**Article 867** The licensee under a patent exploitation license contract shall exploit the patent in accordance with the agreement, may not license any third party other than those agreed upon to exploit the patent, and shall pay the royalty in accordance with the agreement.

<a id="art-868"></a>第八百六十八条　技术秘密转让合同的让与人和技术秘密使用许可合同的许可人应当按照约定提供技术资料，进行技术指导，保证技术的实用性、可靠性，承担保密义务。  
前款规定的保密义务，不限制许可人申请专利，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 868** The assignor under a technical secret assignment contract and the licensor under a technical secret use license contract shall, in accordance with the agreement, provide technical materials, provide technical guidance, guarantee the practicality and reliability of the technology, and bear the obligation of confidentiality.  
The obligation of confidentiality stipulated in the preceding paragraph shall not restrict the licensor from applying for a patent, unless the parties have agreed otherwise.

<a id="art-869"></a>第八百六十九条　技术秘密转让合同的受让人和技术秘密使用许可合同的被许可人应当按照约定使用技术，支付转让费、使用费，承担保密义务。

**Article 869** The transferee under a technical secret assignment contract and the licensee under a technical secret use license contract shall, in accordance with the agreement, use the technology, pay the assignment fee or royalty, and bear the obligation of confidentiality.

<a id="art-870"></a>第八百七十条　技术转让合同的让与人和技术许可合同的许可人应当保证自己是所提供的技术的合法拥有者，并保证所提供的技术完整、无误、有效，能够达到约定的目标。

**Article 870** The assignor under a technology transfer contract and the licensor under a technology license contract shall guarantee that they are the lawful owner of the technology provided, and shall guarantee that the technology provided is complete, free from error, effective, and capable of achieving the agreed-upon objective.

<a id="art-871"></a>第八百七十一条　技术转让合同的受让人和技术许可合同的被许可人应当按照约定的范围和期限，对让与人、许可人提供的技术中尚未公开的秘密部分，承担保密义务。

**Article 871** The transferee under a technology transfer contract and the licensee under a technology license contract shall, within the agreed scope and period, bear the obligation of confidentiality with respect to the secret parts of the technology provided by the assignor or licensor that have not been disclosed.

<a id="art-872"></a>第八百七十二条　许可人未按照约定许可技术的，应当返还部分或者全部使用费，并应当承担违约责任；实施专利或者使用技术秘密超越约定的范围的，违反约定擅自许可第三人实施该项专利或者使用该项技术秘密的，应当停止违约行为，承担违约责任；违反约定的保密义务的，应当承担违约责任。  
让与人承担违约责任，参照适用前款规定。

**Article 872** Where the licensor fails to license the technology in accordance with the agreement, it shall refund part or all of the royalty and shall bear liability for breach of contract; where the licensor exploits the patent or uses the technical secret beyond the agreed scope, or in violation of the agreement, licenses a third party to exploit the patent or use the technical secret without authorization, it shall cease the breaching act and bear liability for breach of contract; where the licensor violates the agreed obligation of confidentiality, it shall bear liability for breach of contract.  
The provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply mutatis mutandis to the assignor's liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-873"></a>第八百七十三条　被许可人未按照约定支付使用费的，应当补交使用费并按照约定支付违约金；不补交使用费或者支付违约金的，应当停止实施专利或者使用技术秘密，交还技术资料，承担违约责任；实施专利或者使用技术秘密超越约定的范围的，未经许可人同意擅自许可第三人实施该专利或者使用该技术秘密的，应当停止违约行为，承担违约责任；违反约定的保密义务的，应当承担违约责任。  
受让人承担违约责任，参照适用前款规定。

**Article 873** Where the licensee fails to pay the royalty in accordance with the agreement, it shall make up the royalty and pay liquidated damages in accordance with the agreement; where it fails to make up the royalty or pay the liquidated damages, it shall cease exploiting the patent or using the technical secret, return the technical materials, and bear liability for breach of contract; where the licensee exploits the patent or uses the technical secret beyond the agreed scope, or, without the consent of the licensor, licenses a third party to exploit the patent or use the technical secret without authorization, it shall cease the breaching act and bear liability for breach of contract; where the licensee violates the agreed obligation of confidentiality, it shall bear liability for breach of contract.  
The provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply mutatis mutandis to the transferee's liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-874"></a>第八百七十四条　受让人或者被许可人按照约定实施专利、使用技术秘密侵害他人合法权益的，由让与人或者许可人承担责任，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 874** Where the transferee or licensee, in exploiting the patent or using the technical secret in accordance with the agreement, infringes upon the lawful rights and interests of another person, the assignor or licensor shall bear liability, unless the parties have agreed otherwise.

<a id="art-875"></a>第八百七十五条　当事人可以按照互利的原则，在合同中约定实施专利、使用技术秘密后续改进的技术成果的分享办法；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，一方后续改进的技术成果，其他各方无权分享。

**Article 875** The parties may stipulate in the contract the method of sharing the technical results subsequently improved upon the exploitation of the patent or the use of the technical secret in accordance with the principle of mutual benefit. Where there is no such agreement or the agreement is unclear, and cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the other parties shall have no right to share the technical results subsequently improved upon by one party.

<a id="art-876"></a>第八百七十六条　集成电路布图设计专有权、植物新品种权、计算机软件著作权等其他知识产权的转让和许可，参照适用本节的有关规定。

**Article 876** The assignment and licensing of other intellectual property rights, such as the exclusive right to layout designs of integrated circuits, the right to new plant varieties, and the copyright in computer software, shall be governed by the relevant provisions of this Section by reference.

<a id="art-877"></a>第八百七十七条　法律、行政法规对技术进出口合同或者专利、专利申请合同另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 877** Where laws or administrative regulations provide otherwise for technology import and export contracts or contracts for patents or patent applications, such provisions shall prevail.

#### 第四节　技术咨询合同和技术服务合同

<a id="art-878"></a>第八百七十八条　技术咨询合同是当事人一方以技术知识为对方就特定技术项目提供可行性论证、技术预测、专题技术调查、分析评价报告等所订立的合同。  
技术服务合同是当事人一方以技术知识为对方解决特定技术问题所订立的合同，不包括承揽合同和建设工程合同。

**Article 878** A technical consultation contract is a contract whereby one party uses its technical knowledge to provide the other party with reports on feasibility studies, technical forecasts, special technical investigations, analysis and evaluation, etc., in respect of a specific technical project.  
A technical service contract is a contract whereby one party uses its technical knowledge to solve a specific technical problem for the other party, but does not include a work contract or a construction project contract.

<a id="art-879"></a>第八百七十九条　技术咨询合同的委托人应当按照约定阐明咨询的问题，提供技术背景材料及有关技术资料，接受受托人的工作成果，支付报酬。

**Article 879** The commissioning party under a technical consultation contract shall, in accordance with the agreement, clarify the subject matter of the consultation, provide technical background materials and related technical data, accept the work product of the commissioned party, and pay the remuneration.

<a id="art-880"></a>第八百八十条　技术咨询合同的受托人应当按照约定的期限完成咨询报告或者解答问题，提出的咨询报告应当达到约定的要求。

**Article 880** The commissioned party under a technical consultation contract shall complete the consultation report or answer the questions within the agreed time period, and the consultation report submitted shall meet the agreed requirements.

<a id="art-881"></a>第八百八十一条　技术咨询合同的委托人未按照约定提供必要的资料，影响工作进度和质量，不接受或者逾期接受工作成果的，支付的报酬不得追回，未支付的报酬应当支付。  
技术咨询合同的受托人未按期提出咨询报告或者提出的咨询报告不符合约定的，应当承担减收或者免收报酬等违约责任。  
技术咨询合同的委托人按照受托人符合约定要求的咨询报告和意见作出决策所造成的损失，由委托人承担，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 881** If the client of a technology consulting contract fails to provide necessary materials as agreed, which affects the work progress and quality, or fails to accept or accept the work results beyond the agreed time limit, the remuneration paid shall not be recovered, and the unpaid remuneration shall be paid.  
If the受托人 of a technology consulting contract fails to submit a consulting report on time or submits a consulting report that does not conform to the agreement, it shall bear liability for breach of contract, such as reduction or exemption of remuneration.  
Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the losses caused by the client's decision made in accordance with the consulting report and opinions of the受托人 that comply with the agreed requirements shall be borne by the client.

<a id="art-882"></a>第八百八十二条　技术服务合同的委托人应当按照约定提供工作条件，完成配合事项，接受工作成果并支付报酬。

**Article 882** The client of a technical service contract shall provide working conditions as agreed, complete the matters of cooperation, accept the work results, and pay remuneration.

<a id="art-883"></a>第八百八十三条　技术服务合同的受托人应当按照约定完成服务项目，解决技术问题，保证工作质量，并传授解决技术问题的知识。

**Article 883** The受托人 of a technical service contract shall complete the service items as agreed, solve technical problems, ensure work quality, and impart the knowledge for solving technical problems.

<a id="art-884"></a>第八百八十四条　技术服务合同的委托人不履行合同义务或者履行合同义务不符合约定，影响工作进度和质量，不接受或者逾期接受工作成果的，支付的报酬不得追回，未支付的报酬应当支付。  
技术服务合同的受托人未按照约定完成服务工作的，应当承担免收报酬等违约责任。

**Article 884** If the client of a technical service contract fails to perform the contractual obligations or performs them in a manner inconsistent with the agreement, which affects the work progress and quality, or fails to accept or accept the work results beyond the agreed time limit, the remuneration paid shall not be recovered, and the unpaid remuneration shall be paid.  
If the受托人 of a technical service contract fails to complete the service work as agreed, it shall bear liability for breach of contract, such as exemption from remuneration.

<a id="art-885"></a>第八百八十五条　技术咨询合同、技术服务合同履行过程中，受托人利用委托人提供的技术资料和工作条件完成的新的技术成果，属于受托人。委托人利用受托人的工作成果完成的新的技术成果，属于委托人。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。

**Article 885** During the performance of a technology consulting contract or technical service contract, any new technological achievement completed by the受托人 using the technical materials and working conditions provided by the client shall belong to the受托人. Any new technological achievement completed by the client using the work results of the受托人 shall belong to the client. If the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail.

<a id="art-886"></a>第八百八十六条　技术咨询合同和技术服务合同对受托人正常开展工作所需费用的负担没有约定或者约定不明确的，由受托人负担。

**Article 886** If a technology consulting contract or technical service contract has no agreement or the agreement is unclear on the bearing of the expenses necessary for the受托人 to carry out its normal work, such expenses shall be borne by the受托人.

<a id="art-887"></a>第八百八十七条　法律、行政法规对技术中介合同、技术培训合同另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 887** If laws or administrative regulations have other provisions on technology intermediary contracts or technology training contracts, such provisions shall prevail.

### 第二十一章　保管合同

<a id="art-888"></a>第八百八十八条　保管合同是保管人保管寄存人交付的保管物，并返还该物的合同。  
寄存人到保管人处从事购物、就餐、住宿等活动，将物品存放在指定场所的，视为保管，但是当事人另有约定或者另有交易习惯的除外。

**Article 888** A deposit contract is a contract where the depositary keeps the deposited item delivered by the depositor and returns such item.  
If a depositor, while engaging in activities such as shopping, dining, or accommodation at the depositary's premises, places items in a designated area, it shall be deemed as a deposit, unless the parties have otherwise agreed or there is a different trade practice.

<a id="art-889"></a>第八百八十九条　寄存人应当按照约定向保管人支付保管费。  
当事人对保管费没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，视为无偿保管。

**Article 889** The depositor shall pay the deposit fee to the depositary as agreed.  
If the parties have no agreement or the agreement on the deposit fee is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, the deposit shall be deemed as gratuitous.

<a id="art-890"></a>第八百九十条　保管合同自保管物交付时成立，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 890** A deposit contract shall be formed upon the delivery of the deposited item, unless the parties have otherwise agreed.

<a id="art-891"></a>第八百九十一条　寄存人向保管人交付保管物的，保管人应当出具保管凭证，但是另有交易习惯的除外。

**Article 891** Where the depositor delivers the deposited item to the depositary, the depositary shall issue a deposit receipt, unless there is a different trade practice.

<a id="art-892"></a>第八百九十二条　保管人应当妥善保管保管物。  
当事人可以约定保管场所或者方法。除紧急情况或者为维护寄存人利益外，不得擅自改变保管场所或者方法。

**Article 892** The depositary shall properly keep the deposited item. The parties may agree on the place or method of deposit. Unless in an emergency or for the purpose of protecting the interests of the depositor, the depositary shall not change the place or method of deposit without authorization.

<a id="art-893"></a>第八百九十三条　寄存人交付的保管物有瑕疵或者根据保管物的性质需要采取特殊保管措施的，寄存人应当将有关情况告知保管人。寄存人未告知，致使保管物受损失的，保管人不承担赔偿责任；保管人因此受损失的，除保管人知道或者应当知道且未采取补救措施外，寄存人应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 893** If the deposited item delivered by the depositor has defects or requires special deposit measures due to its nature, the depositor shall inform the depositary of the relevant circumstances. If the depositor fails to inform, causing damage to the deposited item, the depositary shall not bear compensation liability. If the depositary suffers losses as a result, the depositor shall bear compensation liability unless the depositary knew or should have known of the situation and failed to take remedial measures.

<a id="art-894"></a>第八百九十四条　保管人不得将保管物转交第三人保管，但是当事人另有约定的除外。  
保管人违反前款规定，将保管物转交第三人保管，造成保管物损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 894** The depositary shall not transfer the deposited item to a third party for deposit, unless the parties have otherwise agreed.  
If the depositary, in violation of the provisions of the preceding paragraph, transfers the deposited item to a third party for deposit, causing damage to the deposited item, it shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-895"></a>第八百九十五条　保管人不得使用或者许可第三人使用保管物，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 895** The depositary shall not use or permit a third party to use the deposited item, unless the parties have otherwise agreed.

<a id="art-896"></a>第八百九十六条　第三人对保管物主张权利的，除依法对保管物采取保全或者执行措施外，保管人应当履行向寄存人返还保管物的义务。  
第三人对保管人提起诉讼或者对保管物申请扣押的，保管人应当及时通知寄存人。

**Article 896** If a third party claims a right over the deposited item, the depositary shall perform the obligation to return the deposited item to the depositor, except where property preservation or enforcement measures are taken against the deposited item in accordance with law.  
If a third party initiates a lawsuit against the depositary or applies for seizure of the deposited item, the depositary shall promptly notify the depositor.

<a id="art-897"></a>第八百九十七条　保管期内，因保管人保管不善造成保管物毁损、灭失的，保管人应当承担赔偿责任。但是，无偿保管人证明自己没有故意或者重大过失的，不承担赔偿责任。

**Article 897** Within the deposit period, if the deposited item is damaged or lost due to the depositary's failure to keep it properly, the depositary shall bear compensation liability. However, if a gratuitous depositary proves that it has no intention or gross negligence, it shall not bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-898"></a>第八百九十八条　寄存人寄存货币、有价证券或者其他贵重物品的，应当向保管人声明，由保管人验收或者封存；寄存人未声明的，该物品毁损、灭失后，保管人可以按照一般物品予以赔偿。

**Article 898** If a depositor deposits currency, negotiable securities, or other valuable items, it shall declare this to the depositary, and the depositary shall inspect or seal them. If the depositor fails to make such declaration, the depositary may compensate for the damage or loss of such items as ordinary items.

<a id="art-899"></a>第八百九十九条　寄存人可以随时领取保管物。  
当事人对保管期限没有约定或者约定不明确的，保管人可以随时请求寄存人领取保管物；约定保管期限的，保管人无特别事由，不得请求寄存人提前领取保管物。

**Article 899** The depositor may retrieve the deposited item at any time. If the parties have no agreement or the agreement on the deposit period is unclear, the depositary may request the depositor to retrieve the deposited item at any time. If a deposit period is agreed, the depositary shall not request the depositor to retrieve the deposited item early unless there are special reasons.

<a id="art-900"></a>第九百条　保管期限届满或者寄存人提前领取保管物的，保管人应当将原物及其孳息归还寄存人。

**Article 900** When the deposit period expires or the depositor retrieves the deposited item early, the depositary shall return the original item and its fruits to the depositor.

<a id="art-901"></a>第九百零一条　保管人保管货币的，可以返还相同种类、数量的货币；保管其他可替代物的，可以按照约定返还相同种类、品质、数量的物品。

**Article 901** Where a depositary keeps currency, it may return the currency of the same type and quantity; where it keeps other fungible items, it may return items of the same type, quality, and quantity in accordance with the agreement.

<a id="art-902"></a>第九百零二条　有偿的保管合同，寄存人应当按照约定的期限向保管人支付保管费。  
当事人对支付期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，应当在领取保管物的同时支付。

**Article 902** Under a contract of deposit for value, the depositor shall pay the storage fee to the depositary within the agreed time limit.  
If the time limit for payment is not agreed or the agreement is unclear and cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the payment shall be made at the same time as the deposited item is collected.

<a id="art-903"></a>第九百零三条　寄存人未按照约定支付保管费或者其他费用的，保管人对保管物享有留置权，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 903** If the depositor fails to pay the storage fee or other expenses in accordance with the agreement, the depositary shall have a lien over the deposited item, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.

### 第二十二章　仓储合同

<a id="art-904"></a>第九百零四条　仓储合同是保管人储存存货人交付的仓储物，存货人支付仓储费的合同。

**Article 904** A warehousing contract is a contract whereby the depositary stores the goods delivered by the depositor and the depositor pays the warehousing fee.

<a id="art-905"></a>第九百零五条　仓储合同自保管人和存货人意思表示一致时成立。

**Article 905** A warehousing contract shall be formed when the depositary and the depositor reach a consensus through their mutual expressions of intent.

<a id="art-906"></a>第九百零六条　储存易燃、易爆、有毒、有腐蚀性、有放射性等危险物品或者易变质物品的，存货人应当说明该物品的性质，提供有关资料。  
存货人违反前款规定的，保管人可以拒收仓储物，也可以采取相应措施以避免损失的发生，因此产生的费用由存货人负担。  
保管人储存易燃、易爆、有毒、有腐蚀性、有放射性等危险物品的，应当具备相应的保管条件。

**Article 906** Where dangerous goods such as inflammable, explosive, toxic, corrosive, or radioactive items or perishable goods are stored, the depositor shall state the nature of such goods and provide relevant information.  
Where the depositor violates the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the depositary may reject the goods or take corresponding measures to avoid the occurrence of loss, and the expenses incurred as a result thereof shall be borne by the depositor.  
Where a depositary stores dangerous goods such as inflammable, explosive, toxic, corrosive, or radioactive items, it shall have the corresponding storage conditions.

<a id="art-907"></a>第九百零七条　保管人应当按照约定对入库仓储物进行验收。保管人验收时发现入库仓储物与约定不符合的，应当及时通知存货人。保管人验收后，发生仓储物的品种、数量、质量不符合约定的，保管人应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 907** The depositary shall inspect the goods delivered into storage in accordance with the agreement. If the depositary, during inspection, discovers that the goods delivered into storage do not conform to the agreement, it shall promptly notify the depositor. If, after inspection by the depositary, the type, quantity, or quality of the goods fails to conform to the agreement, the depositary shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-908"></a>第九百零八条　存货人交付仓储物的，保管人应当出具仓单、入库单等凭证。

**Article 908** Upon delivery of the goods by the depositor, the depositary shall issue documents such as a warehouse receipt or a storage receipt.

<a id="art-909"></a>第九百零九条　保管人应当在仓单上签名或者盖章。仓单包括下列事项：  
（一）存货人的姓名或者名称和住所；  
（二）仓储物的品种、数量、质量、包装及其件数和标记；  
（三）仓储物的损耗标准；  
（四）储存场所；  
（五）储存期限；  
（六）仓储费；  
（七）仓储物已经办理保险的，其保险金额、期间以及保险人的名称；  
（八）填发人、填发地和填发日期。

**Article 909** The depositary shall sign or affix its seal on the warehouse receipt. The warehouse receipt shall include the following items:  
(1) the name or title and domicile of the depositor;  
(2) the type, quantity, quality, packaging, number of packages, and marks of the goods;  
(3) the standard for loss and damage of the goods;  
(4) the storage premises;  
(5) the term of storage;  
(6) the warehousing fee;  
(7) where the goods have been insured, the sum insured, the insurance period, and the name of the insurer; and (8) the issuer, place of issue, and date of issue.

<a id="art-910"></a>第九百一十条　仓单是提取仓储物的凭证。存货人或者仓单持有人在仓单上背书并经保管人签名或者盖章的，可以转让提取仓储物的权利。

**Article 910** The warehouse receipt is a document for taking delivery of the goods. The depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt may transfer the right to take delivery of the goods by endorsing the warehouse receipt and obtaining the signature or seal of the depositary.

<a id="art-911"></a>第九百一十一条　保管人根据存货人或者仓单持有人的要求，应当同意其检查仓储物或者提取样品。

**Article 911** The depositary shall, at the request of the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt, agree to allow such person to inspect the goods or take samples.

<a id="art-912"></a>第九百一十二条　保管人发现入库仓储物有变质或者其他损坏的，应当及时通知存货人或者仓单持有人。

**Article 912** If the depositary discovers that the goods delivered into storage have deteriorated or suffered other damage, it shall promptly notify the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt.

<a id="art-913"></a>第九百一十三条　保管人发现入库仓储物有变质或者其他损坏，危及其他仓储物的安全和正常保管的，应当催告存货人或者仓单持有人作出必要的处置。因情况紧急，保管人可以作出必要的处置；但是，事后应当将该情况及时通知存货人或者仓单持有人。

**Article 913** If the depositary discovers that the goods delivered into storage have deteriorated or suffered other damage, thereby endangering the safety and proper storage of other goods, it shall urge the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt to make necessary dispositions. In an emergency, the depositary may make necessary dispositions; provided, however, that it shall promptly notify the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt of such situation thereafter.

<a id="art-914"></a>第九百一十四条　当事人对储存期限没有约定或者约定不明确的，存货人或者仓单持有人可以随时提取仓储物，保管人也可以随时请求存货人或者仓单持有人提取仓储物，但是应当给予必要的准备时间。

**Article 914** Where the parties have not agreed on the term of storage or the agreement is unclear, the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt may take delivery of the goods at any time, and the depositary may also demand at any time that the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt take delivery of the goods, provided that a necessary period of preparation shall be given.

<a id="art-915"></a>第九百一十五条　储存期限届满，存货人或者仓单持有人应当凭仓单、入库单等提取仓储物。存货人或者仓单持有人逾期提取的，应当加收仓储费；提前提取的，不减收仓储费。

**Article 915** Upon expiry of the term of storage, the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt shall take delivery of the goods by presenting the warehouse receipt, storage receipt, or such other documents. If the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt takes delivery after the expiry of the term, an additional warehousing fee shall be charged; if delivery is taken before the expiry of the term, the warehousing fee shall not be reduced.

<a id="art-916"></a>第九百一十六条　储存期限届满，存货人或者仓单持有人不提取仓储物的，保管人可以催告其在合理期限内提取；逾期不提取的，保管人可以提存仓储物。

**Article 916** Upon expiry of the term of storage, if the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt fails to take delivery of the goods, the depositary may urge such person to take delivery within a reasonable period; if delivery is not taken after the expiry of such period, the depositary may deposit the goods with a competent authority.

<a id="art-917"></a>第九百一十七条　储存期内，因保管不善造成仓储物毁损、灭失的，保管人应当承担赔偿责任。因仓储物本身的自然性质、包装不符合约定或者超过有效储存期造成仓储物变质、损坏的，保管人不承担赔偿责任。

**Article 917** If, during the term of storage, the goods are damaged or lost due to improper storage, the depositary shall bear compensation liability. If the goods deteriorate or are damaged due to the natural nature of the goods themselves, defective packaging not conforming to the agreement, or exceeding the effective storage period, the depositary shall not bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-918"></a>第九百一十八条　本章没有规定的，适用保管合同的有关规定。

**Article 918** Matters not provided for in this Chapter shall be governed by the relevant provisions on contracts of deposit.

### 第二十三章　委托合同

<a id="art-919"></a>第九百一十九条　委托合同是委托人和受托人约定，由受托人处理委托人事务的合同。

**Article 919** A contract of mandate is a contract whereby the principal and the agent agree that the agent shall handle the affairs of the principal.

<a id="art-920"></a>第九百二十条　委托人可以特别委托受托人处理一项或者数项事务，也可以概括委托受托人处理一切事务。

**Article 920** The principal may specifically mandate the agent to handle one or several affairs, or may generally mandate the agent to handle all affairs.

<a id="art-921"></a>第九百二十一条　委托人应当预付处理委托事务的费用。受托人为处理委托事务垫付的必要费用，委托人应当偿还该费用并支付利息。

**Article 921** The principal shall advance the expenses for handling the entrusted affairs. For necessary expenses advanced by the agent in handling the entrusted affairs, the principal shall reimburse such expenses and pay interest thereon.

<a id="art-922"></a>第九百二十二条　受托人应当按照委托人的指示处理委托事务。需要变更委托人指示的，应当经委托人同意；因情况紧急，难以和委托人取得联系的，受托人应当妥善处理委托事务，但是事后应当将该情况及时报告委托人。

**Article 922** The agent shall handle the entrusted affairs in accordance with the instructions of the principal. Where any modification of the principal's instructions is necessary, the consent of the principal must be obtained; if under urgent circumstances it is difficult to contact the principal, the agent shall properly handle the entrusted affairs, but shall promptly report such circumstances to the principal afterwards.

<a id="art-923"></a>第九百二十三条　受托人应当亲自处理委托事务。经委托人同意，受托人可以转委托。转委托经同意或者追认的，委托人可以就委托事务直接指示转委托的第三人，受托人仅就第三人的选任及其对第三人的指示承担责任。转委托未经同意或者追认的，受托人应当对转委托的第三人的行为承担责任；但是，在紧急情况下受托人为了维护委托人的利益需要转委托第三人的除外。

**Article 923** The agent shall handle the entrusted affairs in person. With the consent of the principal, the agent may sub-entrust the affairs. Where the sub-entrustment is consented to or ratified, the principal may give direct instructions to the third party on the entrusted affairs, and the agent shall only be liable for the selection of the third party and for the instructions given to the third party. Where the sub-entrustment is not consented to or ratified, the agent shall be liable for the acts of the third party to whom the affairs are sub-entrusted; except that under urgent circumstances, the agent needs to sub-entrust the affairs to a third party in order to safeguard the interests of the principal.

<a id="art-924"></a>第九百二十四条　受托人应当按照委托人的要求，报告委托事务的处理情况。委托合同终止时，受托人应当报告委托事务的结果。

**Article 924** The agent shall, as requested by the principal, report on the handling of the entrusted affairs. Upon termination of the agency contract, the agent shall report the results of the entrusted affairs.

<a id="art-925"></a>第九百二十五条　受托人以自己的名义，在委托人的授权范围内与第三人订立的合同，第三人在订立合同时知道受托人与委托人之间的代理关系的，该合同直接约束委托人和第三人；但是，有确切证据证明该合同只约束受托人和第三人的除外。

**Article 925** Where an agent enters into a contract with a third party in its own name and within the scope of authority granted by the principal, and the third party knows at the time of entering into the contract of the agency relationship between the agent and the principal, such contract shall directly bind the principal and the third party; except where there is conclusive evidence to prove that the contract only binds the agent and the third party.

<a id="art-926"></a>第九百二十六条　受托人以自己的名义与第三人订立合同时，第三人不知道受托人与委托人之间的代理关系的，受托人因第三人的原因对委托人不履行义务，受托人应当向委托人披露第三人，委托人因此可以行使受托人对第三人的权利。但是，第三人与受托人订立合同时如果知道该委托人就不会订立合同的除外。  
受托人因委托人的原因对第三人不履行义务，受托人应当向第三人披露委托人，第三人因此可以选择受托人或者委托人作为相对人主张其权利，但是第三人不得变更选定的相对人。  
委托人行使受托人对第三人的权利的，第三人可以向委托人主张其对受托人的抗辩。第三人选定委托人作为其相对人的，委托人可以向第三人主张其对受托人的抗辩以及受托人对第三人的抗辩。

**Article 926** Where an agent enters into a contract with a third party in its own name, and the third party is unaware of the agency relationship between the agent and the principal, if the agent fails to perform its obligations to the principal due to the fault of the third party, the agent shall disclose the third party to the principal, and the principal may then exercise the rights of the agent against the third party. However, this shall not apply if the third party would not have entered into the contract had it known of the identity of the principal at the time of entering into the contract. If the agent fails to perform its obligations to the third party due to the fault of the principal, the agent shall disclose the principal to the third party. The third party may then choose either the agent or the principal as the counterparty to assert its rights, but the third party may not change the chosen counterparty. Where the principal exercises the rights of the agent against the third party, the third party may raise against the principal the defenses it has against the agent. Where the third party chooses the principal as its counterparty, the principal may raise against the third party the defenses it has against the agent, as well as the defenses the agent has against the third party.

<a id="art-927"></a>第九百二十七条　受托人处理委托事务取得的财产，应当转交给委托人。

**Article 927** Any property acquired by the agent in handling the entrusted affairs shall be transferred to the principal.

<a id="art-928"></a>第九百二十八条　受托人完成委托事务的，委托人应当按照约定向其支付报酬。  
因不可归责于受托人的事由，委托合同解除或者委托事务不能完成的，委托人应当向受托人支付相应的报酬。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。

**Article 928** Where the agent completes the entrusted affairs, the principal shall pay the remuneration to the agent as agreed. If the agency contract is dissolved or the entrusted affairs cannot be completed due to reasons not attributable to the agent, the principal shall pay the agent corresponding remuneration. If the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail.

<a id="art-929"></a>第九百二十九条　有偿的委托合同，因受托人的过错造成委托人损失的，委托人可以请求赔偿损失。无偿的委托合同，因受托人的故意或者重大过失造成委托人损失的，委托人可以请求赔偿损失。  
受托人超越权限造成委托人损失的，应当赔偿损失。

**Article 929** For a remunerated agency contract, the principal may claim compensation for losses caused to the principal by the agent's fault. For a gratuitous agency contract, the principal may claim compensation for losses caused to the principal by the agent's intentional act or gross negligence. Where the agent causes losses to the principal by acting beyond its authority, the agent shall compensate for such losses.

<a id="art-930"></a>第九百三十条　受托人处理委托事务时，因不可归责于自己的事由受到损失的，可以向委托人请求赔偿损失。

**Article 930** Where the agent, when handling the entrusted affairs, suffers losses due to reasons not attributable to itself, it may claim compensation for such losses from the principal.

<a id="art-931"></a>第九百三十一条　委托人经受托人同意，可以在受托人之外委托第三人处理委托事务。因此造成受托人损失的，受托人可以向委托人请求赔偿损失。

**Article 931** With the consent of the agent, the principal may entrust a third party other than the agent to handle the entrusted affairs. Where losses are thereby caused to the agent, the agent may claim compensation for such losses from the principal.

<a id="art-932"></a>第九百三十二条　两个以上的受托人共同处理委托事务的，对委托人承担连带责任。

**Article 932** Where two or more agents jointly handle the entrusted affairs, they shall bear joint and several liability to the principal.

<a id="art-933"></a>第九百三十三条　委托人或者受托人可以随时解除委托合同。因解除合同造成对方损失的，除不可归责于该当事人的事由外，无偿委托合同的解除方应当赔偿因解除时间不当造成的直接损失，有偿委托合同的解除方应当赔偿对方的直接损失和合同履行后可以获得的利益。

**Article 933** Either the principal or the agent may dissolve the agency contract at any time. Where losses are caused to the other party due to the dissolution, unless due to reasons not attributable to the dissolving party, the dissolving party of a gratuitous agency contract shall compensate for the direct losses caused by the improper timing of the dissolution, and the dissolving party of a remunerated agency contract shall compensate for the other party's direct losses and the benefits that could have been obtained after the performance of the contract.

<a id="art-934"></a>第九百三十四条　委托人死亡、终止或者受托人死亡、丧失民事行为能力、终止的，委托合同终止；但是，当事人另有约定或者根据委托事务的性质不宜终止的除外。

**Article 934** The agency contract shall terminate upon the death or termination of the principal, or upon the death, loss of civil capacity, or termination of the agent; except where the parties have otherwise agreed, or it is inappropriate to terminate based on the nature of the entrusted affairs.

<a id="art-935"></a>第九百三十五条　因委托人死亡或者被宣告破产、解散，致使委托合同终止将损害委托人利益的，在委托人的继承人、遗产管理人或者清算人承受委托事务之前，受托人应当继续处理委托事务。

**Article 935** Where the termination of the agency contract due to the death of the principal or its being declared bankrupt or dissolved would harm the interests of the principal, the agent shall continue to handle the entrusted affairs until the heir, estate administrator or liquidator of the principal takes over the entrusted affairs.

<a id="art-936"></a>第九百三十六条　因受托人死亡、丧失民事行为能力或者被宣告破产、解散，致使委托合同终止的，受托人的继承人、遗产管理人、法定代理人或者清算人应当及时通知委托人。因委托合同终止将损害委托人利益的，在委托人作出善后处理之前，受托人的继承人、遗产管理人、法定代理人或者清算人应当采取必要措施。

**Article 936** Where the agency contract terminates due to the death, loss of civil capacity, or being declared bankrupt or dissolved of the agent, the heir, estate administrator, legal representative or liquidator of the agent shall promptly notify the principal. Where the termination of the agency contract would harm the interests of the principal, the heir, estate administrator, legal representative or liquidator of the agent shall take necessary measures before the principal makes proper arrangements.

### 第二十四章　物业服务合同

<a id="art-937"></a>第九百三十七条　物业服务合同是物业服务人在物业服务区域内，为业主提供建筑物及其附属设施的维修养护、环境卫生和相关秩序的管理维护等物业服务，业主支付物业费的合同。  
物业服务人包括物业服务企业和其他管理人。

**Article 937** A property service contract is a contract under which a property service provider provides property services, including the repair and maintenance of buildings and their ancillary facilities, environmental sanitation, and management and maintenance of relevant order, within the property service area to the owners, and the owners pay property fees.  
Property service providers include property service enterprises and other managers.

<a id="art-938"></a>第九百三十八条　物业服务合同的内容一般包括服务事项、服务质量、服务费用的标准和收取办法、维修资金的使用、服务用房的管理和使用、服务期限、服务交接等条款。  
物业服务人公开作出的有利于业主的服务承诺，为物业服务合同的组成部分。  
物业服务合同应当采用书面形式。

**Article 938** The contents of a property service contract generally include clauses on service items, service quality, the standards and methods for collecting service fees, the use of maintenance funds, the management and use of service premises, the service term, and the handover of services.  
A service commitment publicly made by the property service provider that is beneficial to the owners shall be an integral part of the property service contract.  
A property service contract shall be in writing.

<a id="art-939"></a>第九百三十九条　建设单位依法与物业服务人订立的前期物业服务合同，以及业主委员会与业主大会依法选聘的物业服务人订立的物业服务合同，对业主具有法律约束力。

**Article 939** The preliminary property service contract entered into by the construction entity and the property service provider in accordance with the law, and the property service contract entered into by the owners' committee and the property service provider legally selected and appointed by the owners' assembly, shall have legal binding force on the owners.

<a id="art-940"></a>第九百四十条　建设单位依法与物业服务人订立的前期物业服务合同约定的服务期限届满前，业主委员会或者业主与新物业服务人订立的物业服务合同生效的，前期物业服务合同终止。

**Article 940** Where, prior to the expiration of the service term agreed in the preliminary property service contract entered into by the construction entity and the property service provider in accordance with the law, the property service contract entered into by the owners' committee or the owners with a new property service provider becomes effective, the preliminary property service contract shall terminate.

<a id="art-941"></a>第九百四十一条　物业服务人将物业服务区域内的部分专项服务事项委托给专业性服务组织或者其他第三人的，应当就该部分专项服务事项向业主负责。  
物业服务人不得将其应当提供的全部物业服务转委托给第三人，或者将全部物业服务支解后分别转委托给第三人。

**Article 941** Where a property service provider delegates certain specialized service matters within the property service area to a professional service organization or another third party, it shall be liable to the owners for such specialized service matters.  
A property service provider shall not subcontract the entire property services it shall provide to a third party, nor shall it split the entire property services into parts and subcontract them separately to third parties.

<a id="art-942"></a>第九百四十二条　物业服务人应当按照约定和物业的使用性质，妥善维修、养护、清洁、绿化和经营管理物业服务区域内的业主共有部分，维护物业服务区域内的基本秩序，采取合理措施保护业主的人身、财产安全。  
对物业服务区域内违反有关治安、环保、消防等法律法规的行为，物业服务人应当及时采取合理措施制止、向有关行政主管部门报告并协助处理。

**Article 942** A property service provider shall, in accordance with the agreement and the nature of the property's use, properly repair, maintain, clean, landscape, and manage the common areas of the owners within the property service area, maintain basic order within the property service area, and take reasonable measures to protect the personal and property safety of the owners.  
Where any act violating laws and regulations on public security, environmental protection, fire control, or the like occurs within the property service area, the property service provider shall promptly take reasonable measures to stop such act, report it to the relevant administrative department, and assist in handling it.

<a id="art-943"></a>第九百四十三条　物业服务人应当定期将服务的事项、负责人员、质量要求、收费项目、收费标准、履行情况，以及维修资金使用情况、业主共有部分的经营与收益情况等以合理方式向业主公开并向业主大会、业主委员会报告。

**Article 943** A property service provider shall periodically disclose to the owners, and report to the owners' assembly and the owners' committee, in a reasonable manner, the matters of service, responsible personnel, quality requirements, fee items, fee rates, performance status, as well as the use of maintenance funds and the operation and income of the common areas of the owners.

<a id="art-944"></a>第九百四十四条　业主应当按照约定向物业服务人支付物业费。物业服务人已经按照约定和有关规定提供服务的，业主不得以未接受或者无需接受相关物业服务为由拒绝支付物业费。  
业主违反约定逾期不支付物业费的，物业服务人可以催告其在合理期限内支付；合理期限届满仍不支付的，物业服务人可以提起诉讼或者申请仲裁。  
物业服务人不得采取停止供电、供水、供热、供燃气等方式催交物业费。

**Article 944** An owner shall pay the property service fee to the property service provider in accordance with the agreement. Where the property service provider has provided services in accordance with the agreement and relevant provisions, the owner shall not refuse to pay the property service fee on the grounds of not receiving or not needing the relevant property services. Where an owner fails to pay the property service fee within the agreed time limit in violation of the agreement, the property service provider may demand payment within a reasonable period; if the owner still fails to pay after the reasonable period expires, the property service provider may initiate a lawsuit or apply for arbitration. A property service provider shall not resort to measures such as cutting off power, water, heat, or gas supply to coerce payment of the property service fee.

<a id="art-945"></a>第九百四十五条　业主装饰装修房屋的，应当事先告知物业服务人，遵守物业服务人提示的合理注意事项，并配合其进行必要的现场检查。  
业主转让、出租物业专有部分、设立居住权或者依法改变共有部分用途的，应当及时将相关情况告知物业服务人。

**Article 945** Where an owner decorates or renovates the premises, he shall inform the property service provider in advance, comply with the reasonable precautions advised by the property service provider, and cooperate in any necessary on-site inspection.  
Where an owner transfers or leases the exclusive part of the property, establishes a right of habitation, or changes the use of the common area according to law, he shall promptly inform the property service provider of the relevant situation.

<a id="art-946"></a>第九百四十六条　业主依照法定程序共同决定解聘物业服务人的，可以解除物业服务合同。决定解聘的，应当提前六十日书面通知物业服务人，但是合同对通知期限另有约定的除外。  
依据前款规定解除合同造成物业服务人损失的，除不可归责于业主的事由外，业主应当赔偿损失。

**Article 946** Where the owners jointly decide to dismiss the property service provider in accordance with the statutory procedures, they may dissolve the property service contract. If they decide to dismiss, they shall notify the property service provider in writing sixty days in advance, unless the contract stipulates otherwise regarding the notice period. If the dissolution of the contract pursuant to the preceding paragraph causes losses to the property service provider, the owners shall compensate for the losses, unless the cause is not attributable to the owners.

<a id="art-947"></a>第九百四十七条　物业服务期限届满前，业主依法共同决定续聘的，应当与原物业服务人在合同期限届满前续订物业服务合同。  
物业服务期限届满前，物业服务人不同意续聘的，应当在合同期限届满前九十日书面通知业主或者业主委员会，但是合同对通知期限另有约定的除外。

**Article 947** Before the expiration of the property service term, if the owners jointly decide to renew the engagement in accordance with the law, they shall renew the property service contract with the original property service provider before the expiration of the contract term.  
Before the expiration of the property service term, if the property service provider does not agree to renew the engagement, it shall notify the owners or the owners' committee in writing ninety days before the expiration of the contract term, unless the contract stipulates otherwise regarding the notice period.

<a id="art-948"></a>第九百四十八条　物业服务期限届满后，业主没有依法作出续聘或者另聘物业服务人的决定，物业服务人继续提供物业服务的，原物业服务合同继续有效，但是服务期限为不定期。  
当事人可以随时解除不定期物业服务合同，但是应当提前六十日书面通知对方。

**Article 948** After the expiration of the property service term, if the owners have not made a decision according to law to renew the engagement or engage another property service provider, and the property service provider continues to provide property services, the original property service contract shall remain effective, but the service term shall be indefinite.  
A party may dissolve an indefinite property service contract at any time, but shall notify the other party in writing sixty days in advance.

<a id="art-949"></a>第九百四十九条　物业服务合同终止的，原物业服务人应当在约定期限或者合理期限内退出物业服务区域，将物业服务用房、相关设施、物业服务所必需的相关资料等交还给业主委员会、决定自行管理的业主或者其指定的人，配合新物业服务人做好交接工作，并如实告知物业的使用和管理状况。  
原物业服务人违反前款规定的，不得请求业主支付物业服务合同终止后的物业费；造成业主损失的，应当赔偿损失。

**Article 949** Upon termination of the property service contract, the original property service provider shall withdraw from the property service area within the agreed period or a reasonable period, return the property service premises, relevant facilities, and the relevant materials necessary for property services to the owners' committee, the owners who decide to self-manage, or the persons designated by them, cooperate with the new property service provider in the handover, and truthfully inform the status of the property's use and management.  
Where the original property service provider violates the provisions of the preceding paragraph, it shall not request the owners to pay the property service fee after the termination of the property service contract; if it causes losses to the owners, it shall compensate for the losses.

<a id="art-950"></a>第九百五十条　物业服务合同终止后，在业主或者业主大会选聘的新物业服务人或者决定自行管理的业主接管之前，原物业服务人应当继续处理物业服务事项，并可以请求业主支付该期间的物业费。

**Article 950** After the termination of the property service contract, before the new property service provider selected by the owners or the owners' assembly, or the owners who decide to self-manage, takes over, the original property service provider shall continue to handle property service matters and may request the owners to pay the property service fee for such period.

### 第二十五章　行纪合同

<a id="art-951"></a>第九百五十一条　行纪合同是行纪人以自己的名义为委托人从事贸易活动，委托人支付报酬的合同。

**Article 951** A commission contract is a contract under which a commission agent engages in trading activities in its own name for a principal, and the principal pays remuneration.

<a id="art-952"></a>第九百五十二条　行纪人处理委托事务支出的费用，由行纪人负担，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 952** The expenses incurred by a commission agent in handling the entrusted affairs shall be borne by the commission agent, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-953"></a>第九百五十三条　行纪人占有委托物的，应当妥善保管委托物。

**Article 953** Where a commission agent is in possession of the entrusted property, it shall keep it in safe custody.

<a id="art-954"></a>第九百五十四条　委托物交付给行纪人时有瑕疵或者容易腐烂、变质的，经委托人同意，行纪人可以处分该物；不能与委托人及时取得联系的，行纪人可以合理处分。

**Article 954** If the entrusted property, when delivered to the commission agent, is defective or is perishable or subject to deterioration, the commission agent may dispose of it with the consent of the principal; if the commission agent cannot contact the principal in a timely manner, it may dispose of it in a reasonable manner.

<a id="art-955"></a>第九百五十五条　行纪人低于委托人指定的价格卖出或者高于委托人指定的价格买入的，应当经委托人同意；未经委托人同意，行纪人补偿其差额的，该买卖对委托人发生效力。  
行纪人高于委托人指定的价格卖出或者低于委托人指定的价格买入的，可以按照约定增加报酬；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，该利益属于委托人。  
委托人对价格有特别指示的，行纪人不得违背该指示卖出或者买入。

**Article 955** If a commission agent sells at a price lower than the price designated by the principal or buys at a price higher than the price designated by the principal, it shall obtain the consent of the principal; if, without the consent of the principal, the commission agent compensates for the difference, the transaction shall be binding on the principal.  
If a commission agent sells at a price higher than the price designated by the principal or buys at a price lower than the price designated by the principal, it may claim additional remuneration in accordance with the agreement; if there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, such benefit shall belong to the principal.  
If the principal gives specific instructions regarding the price, the commission agent shall not sell or buy in violation of such instructions.

<a id="art-956"></a>第九百五十六条　行纪人卖出或者买入具有市场定价的商品，除委托人有相反的意思表示外，行纪人自己可以作为买受人或者出卖人。  
行纪人有前款规定情形的，仍然可以请求委托人支付报酬。

**Article 956** When a commission agent sells or buys commodities with a market price, unless the principal indicates a contrary intention, the commission agent may itself act as the buyer or seller.  
Where the commission agent falls under the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph, it may still request the principal to pay remuneration.

<a id="art-957"></a>第九百五十七条　行纪人按照约定买入委托物，委托人应当及时受领。经行纪人催告，委托人无正当理由拒绝受领的，行纪人依法可以提存委托物。  
委托物不能卖出或者委托人撤回出卖，经行纪人催告，委托人不取回或者不处分该物的，行纪人依法可以提存委托物。

**Article 957** Where a commission agent purchases the entrusted property in accordance with the agreement, the principal shall accept it in a timely manner. If, after the commission agent demands acceptance, the principal refuses to accept it without just cause, the commission agent may, according to law, deposit the entrusted property. If the entrusted property cannot be sold or the principal withdraws the sale, and after the commission agent demands action, the principal fails to recover or dispose of the property, the commission agent may, according to law, deposit the entrusted property.

<a id="art-958"></a>第九百五十八条　行纪人与第三人订立合同的，行纪人对该合同直接享有权利、承担义务。  
第三人不履行义务致使委托人受到损害的，行纪人应当承担赔偿责任，但是行纪人与委托人另有约定的除外。

**Article 958** Where a commission agent enters into a contract with a third party, the commission agent directly enjoys rights and assumes obligations under such contract.  
If the third party fails to perform its obligations, thereby causing damage to the principal, the commission agent shall bear compensation liability, unless the commission agent and the principal agree otherwise.

<a id="art-959"></a>第九百五十九条　行纪人完成或者部分完成委托事务的，委托人应当向其支付相应的报酬。委托人逾期不支付报酬的，行纪人对委托物享有留置权，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 959** Where a commission agent completes or partially completes the entrusted affairs, the principal shall pay corresponding remuneration to it. If the principal fails to pay the remuneration within the time limit, the commission agent has a lien over the entrusted property, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-960"></a>第九百六十条　本章没有规定的，参照适用委托合同的有关规定。

**Article 960** Matters not provided for in this Chapter shall be governed by the relevant provisions on commission contracts, mutatis mutandis.

### 第二十六章　中介合同

<a id="art-961"></a>第九百六十一条　中介合同是中介人向委托人报告订立合同的机会或者提供订立合同的媒介服务，委托人支付报酬的合同。

**Article 961** A intermediary contract is a contract under which the intermediary reports to the client on opportunities for concluding a contract or provides intermediary services for the conclusion of a contract, and the client pays remuneration therefor.

<a id="art-962"></a>第九百六十二条　中介人应当就有关订立合同的事项向委托人如实报告。  
中介人故意隐瞒与订立合同有关的重要事实或者提供虚假情况，损害委托人利益的，不得请求支付报酬并应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 962** The intermediary shall report truthfully to the client on matters pertaining to the conclusion of the contract.  
Where the intermediary intentionally conceals material facts relating to the conclusion of the contract or provides false information, thereby harming the interests of the client, it may not claim remuneration and shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-963"></a>第九百六十三条　中介人促成合同成立的，委托人应当按照约定支付报酬。对中介人的报酬没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，根据中介人的劳务合理确定。因中介人提供订立合同的媒介服务而促成合同成立的，由该合同的当事人平均负担中介人的报酬。  
中介人促成合同成立的，中介活动的费用，由中介人负担。

**Article 963** Where the intermediary facilitates the formation of the contract, the client shall pay remuneration as agreed. If the remuneration of the intermediary is not agreed upon or the agreement is unclear and cannot be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, it shall be reasonably determined based on the services provided by the intermediary. Where the formation of the contract is facilitated by the intermediary providing intermediary services for the conclusion of the contract, the parties to that contract shall equally bear the intermediary's remuneration. Where the intermediary facilitates the formation of the contract, the expenses of the intermediary activities shall be borne by the intermediary.

<a id="art-964"></a>第九百六十四条　中介人未促成合同成立的，不得请求支付报酬；但是，可以按照约定请求委托人支付从事中介活动支出的必要费用。

**Article 964** Where the intermediary does not facilitate the formation of the contract, it may not claim payment of remuneration; however, it may claim payment by the client of the necessary expenses incurred in carrying out the intermediary activities as agreed.

<a id="art-965"></a>第九百六十五条　委托人在接受中介人的服务后，利用中介人提供的交易机会或者媒介服务，绕开中介人直接订立合同的，应当向中介人支付报酬。

**Article 965** Where, after accepting the services of the intermediary, the client uses the transaction opportunities or intermediary services provided by the intermediary to bypass the intermediary and directly conclude a contract, it shall pay remuneration to the intermediary.

<a id="art-966"></a>第九百六十六条　本章没有规定的，参照适用委托合同的有关规定。

**Article 966** Matters not provided for in this Chapter shall be governed by applying mutatis mutandis the relevant provisions on the contract of mandate.

### 第二十七章　合伙合同

<a id="art-967"></a>第九百六十七条　合伙合同是两个以上合伙人为了共同的事业目的，订立的共享利益、共担风险的协议。

**Article 967** A partnership contract is an agreement concluded by two or more partners for a common business purpose to share interests and bear risks.

<a id="art-968"></a>第九百六十八条　合伙人应当按照约定的出资方式、数额和缴付期限，履行出资义务。

**Article 968** The partners shall perform their capital contribution obligations in accordance with the agreed method, amount, and time of payment.

<a id="art-969"></a>第九百六十九条　合伙人的出资、因合伙事务依法取得的收益和其他财产，属于合伙财产。  
合伙合同终止前，合伙人不得请求分割合伙财产。

**Article 969** The partners' capital contributions, returns obtained lawfully from partnership affairs, and other property shall constitute partnership property.  
Before the termination of the partnership contract, no partner may claim a division of the partnership property.

<a id="art-970"></a>第九百七十条　合伙人就合伙事务作出决定的，除合伙合同另有约定外，应当经全体合伙人一致同意。  
合伙事务由全体合伙人共同执行。按照合伙合同的约定或者全体合伙人的决定，可以委托一个或者数个合伙人执行合伙事务；其他合伙人不再执行合伙事务，但是有权监督执行情况。  
合伙人分别执行合伙事务的，执行事务合伙人可以对其他合伙人执行的事务提出异议；提出异议后，其他合伙人应当暂停该项事务的执行。

**Article 970** Decisions made by the partners on partnership affairs shall be subject to the unanimous consent of all partners, unless otherwise agreed in the partnership contract. Partnership affairs shall be jointly executed by all partners. In accordance with the provisions of the partnership contract or the decision of all partners, one or several partners may be appointed to execute the partnership affairs; the other partners shall no longer execute the partnership affairs, but shall have the right to supervise the execution thereof. Where partners execute the partnership affairs separately, the executing partner may raise objections to the affairs executed by other partners; after such objections are raised, the other partners shall suspend the execution of such affairs.

<a id="art-971"></a>第九百七十一条　合伙人不得因执行合伙事务而请求支付报酬，但是合伙合同另有约定的除外。

**Article 971** No partner may claim remuneration for the execution of partnership affairs, unless otherwise agreed in the partnership contract.

<a id="art-972"></a>第九百七十二条　合伙的利润分配和亏损分担，按照合伙合同的约定办理；合伙合同没有约定或者约定不明确的，由合伙人协商决定；协商不成的，由合伙人按照实缴出资比例分配、分担；无法确定出资比例的，由合伙人平均分配、分担。

**Article 972** The distribution of profits and the sharing of losses of the partnership shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of the partnership contract; if the partnership contract contains no such provisions or the provisions are unclear, the partners shall decide by consultation; if consultation fails, the partners shall distribute profits and share losses in proportion to their actually paid capital contributions; if the proportion of capital contributions cannot be determined, the partners shall distribute profits and share losses equally.

<a id="art-973"></a>第九百七十三条　合伙人对合伙债务承担连带责任。清偿合伙债务超过自己应当承担份额的合伙人，有权向其他合伙人追偿。

**Article 973** The partners shall bear joint and several liability for partnership debts. A partner who has discharged partnership debts in excess of its own share shall have the right to recover from the other partners.

<a id="art-974"></a>第九百七十四条　除合伙合同另有约定外，合伙人向合伙人以外的人转让其全部或者部分财产份额的，须经其他合伙人一致同意。

**Article 974** Unless otherwise agreed in the partnership contract, a partner transferring all or part of its property share to a person other than the partners shall require the unanimous consent of the other partners.

<a id="art-975"></a>第九百七十五条　合伙人的债权人不得代位行使合伙人依照本章规定和合伙合同享有的权利，但是合伙人享有的利益分配请求权除外。

**Article 975** A creditor of a partner may not exercise by subrogation the rights enjoyed by the partner under this Chapter and the partnership contract, except for the partner's right to claim distribution of interests.

<a id="art-976"></a>第九百七十六条　合伙人对合伙期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，视为不定期合伙。  
合伙期限届满，合伙人继续执行合伙事务，其他合伙人没有提出异议的，原合伙合同继续有效，但是合伙期限为不定期。  
合伙人可以随时解除不定期合伙合同，但是应当在合理期限之前通知其他合伙人。

**Article 976** Where the partners have not agreed on the term of the partnership or the agreement is unclear and cannot be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, the partnership shall be deemed to be a partnership for an indefinite term.  
Upon expiry of the partnership term, if the partners continue to execute partnership affairs and the other partners raise no objection, the original partnership contract shall continue to be valid, but the partnership term shall be indefinite.  
A partner may terminate the partnership contract for an indefinite term at any time, provided that it gives notice to the other partners within a reasonable period in advance.

<a id="art-977"></a>第九百七十七条　合伙人死亡、丧失民事行为能力或者终止的，合伙合同终止；但是，合伙合同另有约定或者根据合伙事务的性质不宜终止的除外。

**Article 977** Upon the death, loss of civil capacity, or termination of a partner, the partnership contract shall terminate; except where otherwise agreed in the partnership contract or where termination is not appropriate based on the nature of the partnership affairs.

<a id="art-978"></a>第九百七十八条　合伙合同终止后，合伙财产在支付因终止而产生的费用以及清偿合伙债务后有剩余的，依据[本法第九百七十二条](#art-972)的规定进行分配。  
第三分编　准合同

**Article 978** After the termination of the partnership contract, any partnership property remaining after payment of the expenses incurred as a result of the termination and satisfaction of the partnership debts shall be distributed in accordance with the provisions of Article 972 of this Law.  
Subdivision III Quasi-Contracts

### 第二十八章　无因管理

<a id="art-979"></a>第九百七十九条　管理人没有法定的或者约定的义务，为避免他人利益受损失而管理他人事务的，可以请求受益人偿还因管理事务而支出的必要费用；管理人因管理事务受到损失的，可以请求受益人给予适当补偿。  
管理事务不符合受益人真实意思的，管理人不享有前款规定的权利；但是，受益人的真实意思违反法律或者违背公序良俗的除外。

**Article 979** A manager who, without a legal or contractual obligation, manages the affairs of another person with the intention of preventing losses to the interests of that person may request the beneficiary to reimburse the necessary expenses incurred in managing the affairs; if the manager suffers losses as a result of managing the affairs, it may request the beneficiary to provide appropriate compensation.  
If the management of affairs is not in conformity with the true intention of the beneficiary, the manager shall not enjoy the rights provided for in the preceding paragraph; except where the true intention of the beneficiary violates the law or public order and good customs.

<a id="art-980"></a>第九百八十条　管理人管理事务不属于前条规定的情形，但是受益人享有管理利益的，受益人应当在其获得的利益范围内向管理人承担前条第一款规定的义务。

**Article 980** Where the management of affairs by the manager does not fall within the circumstances specified in the preceding article, but the beneficiary enjoys the benefits of the management, the beneficiary shall assume the obligations provided for in paragraph 1 of the preceding article to the manager to the extent of the benefits it obtains.

<a id="art-981"></a>第九百八十一条　管理人管理他人事务，应当采取有利于受益人的方法。中断管理对受益人不利的，无正当理由不得中断。

**Article 981** A manager who manages the affairs of another person shall adopt methods beneficial to the principal. If interrupting the management is detrimental to the principal, the manager shall not interrupt it without justifiable reasons.

<a id="art-982"></a>第九百八十二条　管理人管理他人事务，能够通知受益人的，应当及时通知受益人。管理的事务不需要紧急处理的，应当等待受益人的指示。

**Article 982** If a manager who manages the affairs of another person may notify the principal, he shall promptly notify the principal. If the matter managed does not require urgent handling, he shall await the principal's instructions.

<a id="art-983"></a>第九百八十三条　管理结束后，管理人应当向受益人报告管理事务的情况。管理人管理事务取得的财产，应当及时转交给受益人。

**Article 983** After the management concludes, the manager shall report to the principal on the situation of the managed affairs. Any property obtained by the manager from managing the affairs shall be promptly transferred to the principal.

<a id="art-984"></a>第九百八十四条　管理人管理事务经受益人事后追认的，从管理事务开始时起，适用委托合同的有关规定，但是管理人另有意思表示的除外。

**Article 984** If the principal subsequently ratifies the management of affairs by the manager, the relevant provisions on the entrustment contract shall apply from the commencement of the management, unless the manager expresses a contrary intention.

### 第二十九章　不当得利

<a id="art-985"></a>第九百八十五条　得利人没有法律根据取得不当利益的，受损失的人可以请求得利人返还取得的利益，但是有下列情形之一的除外：  
（一）为履行道德义务进行的给付；  
（二）债务到期之前的清偿；  
（三）明知无给付义务而进行的债务清偿。

**Article 985** Where a benefit recipient obtains unjust benefits without legal basis, the person who suffers loss may request the benefit recipient to return the benefits obtained, except under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) Performance made to fulfill a moral obligation;  
(2) Satisfaction of a debt before it becomes due;  
(3) Satisfaction of a debt while knowing that there is no obligation to perform.

<a id="art-986"></a>第九百八十六条　得利人不知道且不应当知道取得的利益没有法律根据，取得的利益已经不存在的，不承担返还该利益的义务。

**Article 986** Where a benefit recipient neither knows nor ought to have known that the benefits obtained have no legal basis, and the benefits obtained no longer exist, he shall not bear the obligation to return such benefits.

<a id="art-987"></a>第九百八十七条　得利人知道或者应当知道取得的利益没有法律根据的，受损失的人可以请求得利人返还其取得的利益并依法赔偿损失。

**Article 987** Where a benefit recipient knows or ought to have known that the benefits obtained have no legal basis, the person who suffers loss may request the benefit recipient to return the benefits obtained and to compensate for the losses according to law.

<a id="art-988"></a>第九百八十八条　得利人已经将取得的利益无偿转让给第三人的，受损失的人可以请求第三人在相应范围内承担返还义务。

**Article 988** Where a benefit recipient has transferred the benefits obtained to a third party without consideration, the person who suffers loss may request the third party to bear the obligation of return to the corresponding extent.

## 第四编　人格权

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-989"></a>第九百八十九条　本编调整因人格权的享有和保护产生的民事关系。

**Article 989** This Book regulates the civil relations arising from the enjoyment and protection of personality rights.

<a id="art-990"></a>第九百九十条　人格权是民事主体享有的生命权、身体权、健康权、姓名权、名称权、肖像权、名誉权、荣誉权、隐私权等权利。  
除前款规定的人格权外，自然人享有基于人身自由、人格尊严产生的其他人格权益。

**Article 990** Personality rights are the rights enjoyed by civil subjects, including the rights to life, body, health, name, entity name, likeness, reputation, honor, privacy, and other rights.  
In addition to the personality rights provided for in the preceding paragraph, natural persons enjoy other personality interests arising from personal freedom and human dignity.

<a id="art-991"></a>第九百九十一条　民事主体的人格权受法律保护，任何组织或者个人不得侵害。

**Article 991** The personality rights of civil subjects shall be protected by law, and no organization or individual may infringe upon them.

<a id="art-992"></a>第九百九十二条　人格权不得放弃、转让或者继承。

**Article 992** Personality rights may not be waived, transferred, or inherited.

<a id="art-993"></a>第九百九十三条　民事主体可以将自己的姓名、名称、肖像等许可他人使用，但是依照法律规定或者根据其性质不得许可的除外。
