**Article 993** A civil subject may license others to use his name, entity name, likeness, or the like, except where such licensing is prohibited by law or by the nature of the right.

<a id="art-994"></a>第九百九十四条　死者的姓名、肖像、名誉、荣誉、隐私、遗体等受到侵害的，其配偶、子女、父母有权依法请求行为人承担民事责任；死者没有配偶、子女且父母已经死亡的，其他近亲属有权依法请求行为人承担民事责任。

**Article 994** Where the name, likeness, reputation, honor, privacy, or remains of a deceased person are infringed upon, his spouse, children, and parents shall have the right to request, according to law, that the infringer bear civil liability. If the deceased person has no spouse or children, and his parents are deceased, his other close relatives shall have the right to request, according to law, that the infringer bear civil liability.

<a id="art-995"></a>第九百九十五条　人格权受到侵害的，受害人有权依照本法和其他法律的规定请求行为人承担民事责任。受害人的停止侵害、排除妨碍、消除危险、消除影响、恢复名誉、赔礼道歉请求权，不适用诉讼时效的规定。

**Article 995** Where a personality right is infringed upon, the victim shall have the right to request, in accordance with this Law and other laws, that the infringer bear civil liability. The victim's right to request cessation of the infringement, removal of the obstruction, elimination of the danger, elimination of adverse effects, restoration of reputation, or making an apology shall not be subject to the statute of limitations.

<a id="art-996"></a>第九百九十六条　因当事人一方的违约行为，损害对方人格权并造成严重精神损害，受损害方选择请求其承担违约责任的，不影响受损害方请求精神损害赔偿。

**Article 996** Where a party's breach of contract infringes upon the other party's personality right and causes serious mental distress, and the injured party elects to request the breaching party to bear liability for breach of contract, such election shall not preclude the injured party from claiming compensation for moral damages.

<a id="art-997"></a>第九百九十七条　民事主体有证据证明行为人正在实施或者即将实施侵害其人格权的违法行为，不及时制止将使其合法权益受到难以弥补的损害的，有权依法向人民法院申请采取责令行为人停止有关行为的措施。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-12" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第12条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 997** Where a civil subject has evidence proving that an infringer is committing or about to commit an unlawful act that infringes upon his personality right, and failure to promptly stop it shall cause irreparable damage to his lawful rights and interests, he shall have the right to apply to the People's Court according to law for measures ordering the infringer to cease the relevant acts.

<a id="art-998"></a>第九百九十八条　认定行为人承担侵害除生命权、身体权和健康权外的人格权的民事责任，应当考虑行为人和受害人的职业、影响范围、过错程度，以及行为的目的、方式、后果等因素。

**Article 998** In determining the civil liability of an infringer for infringement upon personality rights other than the rights to life, body, and health, factors such as the occupations of the infringer and the victim, the scope of impact, the degree of fault, and the purpose, method, and consequences of the act shall be considered.

<a id="art-999"></a>第九百九十九条　为公共利益实施新闻报道、舆论监督等行为的，可以合理使用民事主体的姓名、名称、肖像、个人信息等；使用不合理侵害民事主体人格权的，应当依法承担民事责任。

**Article 999** Where acts such as news reporting or supervision by public opinion are carried out for the public interest, the name, entity name, likeness, personal information, etc. of a civil subject may be used reasonably. If the use is unreasonable and infringes upon the personality rights of the civil subject, civil liability shall be borne according to law.

<a id="art-1000"></a>第一千条　行为人因侵害人格权承担消除影响、恢复名誉、赔礼道歉等民事责任的，应当与行为的具体方式和造成的影响范围相当。  
行为人拒不承担前款规定的民事责任的，人民法院可以采取在报刊、网络等媒体上发布公告或者公布生效裁判文书等方式执行，产生的费用由行为人负担。

**Article 1000** Where an infringer bears civil liability such as eliminating adverse effects, restoring reputation, or making an apology for infringing upon personality rights, such liability shall be commensurate with the specific manner of the act and the scope of the adverse impact caused.  
If the infringer refuses to bear the civil liability provided for in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court may enforce it by means such as publishing an announcement on media like newspapers or the internet, or publishing the effective judgment document, and the costs incurred shall be borne by the infringer.

<a id="art-1001"></a>第一千零一条　对自然人因婚姻家庭关系等产生的身份权利的保护，适用本法第一编、第五编和其他法律的相关规定；没有规定的，可以根据其性质参照适用本编人格权保护的有关规定。

**Article 1001** The protection of the right to status of a natural person arising from marital and family relations and the like shall be governed by the relevant provisions of Book One, Book Five and other laws of this Code; in the absence of such provisions, the relevant provisions on the protection of personality rights in this Book may be applied mutatis mutandis based on its nature.

### 第二章　生命权、身体权和健康权

<a id="art-1002"></a>第一千零二条　自然人享有生命权。自然人的生命安全和生命尊严受法律保护。任何组织或者个人不得侵害他人的生命权。

**Article 1002** A natural person shall enjoy the right to life. The life safety and dignity of a natural person shall be protected by law. No organization or individual shall infringe upon the right to life of another person.

<a id="art-1003"></a>第一千零三条　自然人享有身体权。自然人的身体完整和行动自由受法律保护。任何组织或者个人不得侵害他人的身体权。

**Article 1003** A natural person shall enjoy the right to physical integrity. The physical integrity and freedom of movement of a natural person shall be protected by law. No organization or individual shall infringe upon the right to physical integrity of another person.

<a id="art-1004"></a>第一千零四条　自然人享有健康权。自然人的身心健康受法律保护。任何组织或者个人不得侵害他人的健康权。

**Article 1004** A natural person shall enjoy the right to health. The physical and mental health of a natural person shall be protected by law. No organization or individual shall infringe upon the right to health of another person.

<a id="art-1005"></a>第一千零五条　自然人的生命权、身体权、健康权受到侵害或者处于其他危难情形的，负有法定救助义务的组织或者个人应当及时施救。

**Article 1005** Where a natural person's right to life, right to physical integrity or right to health is infringed upon, or such person is in other perilous circumstances, an organization or individual that has a legal obligation to provide rescue shall promptly render such rescue.

<a id="art-1006"></a>第一千零六条　完全民事行为能力人有权依法自主决定无偿捐献其人体细胞、人体组织、人体器官、遗体。任何组织或者个人不得强迫、欺骗、利诱其捐献。  
完全民事行为能力人依据前款规定同意捐献的，应当采用书面形式，也可以订立遗嘱。  
自然人生前未表示不同意捐献的，该自然人死亡后，其配偶、成年子女、父母可以共同决定捐献，决定捐献应当采用书面形式。

**Article 1006** A person with full capacity for civil conduct shall have the right to independently decide to donate his or her human cells, human tissues, human organs, or remains free of charge according to law. No organization or individual may coerce, deceive, or induce such person into making such donation. Where a person with full capacity for civil conduct consents to donation in accordance with the preceding paragraph, such consent shall be made in writing, or a will may be made. Where a natural person did not express disagreement with donation during his or her lifetime, after the death of such natural person, his or her spouse, adult children, and parents may jointly decide on the donation, and the decision to donate shall be made in writing.

<a id="art-1007"></a>第一千零七条　禁止以任何形式买卖人体细胞、人体组织、人体器官、遗体。  
违反前款规定的买卖行为无效。

**Article 1007** The sale of human cells, human tissues, human organs, or remains in any form shall be prohibited.  
Any act of sale in violation of the preceding paragraph shall be void.

<a id="art-1008"></a>第一千零八条　为研制新药、医疗器械或者发展新的预防和治疗方法，需要进行临床试验的，应当依法经相关主管部门批准并经伦理委员会审查同意，向受试者或者受试者的监护人告知试验目的、用途和可能产生的风险等详细情况，并经其书面同意。  
进行临床试验的，不得向受试者收取试验费用。

**Article 1008** Where a clinical trial is required for the development of new drugs, medical devices, or new methods for prevention and treatment, it shall be approved by the relevant competent authority and reviewed and approved by an ethics committee according to law. The subject or the subject's guardian shall be informed of detailed information such as the purpose, use, and potential risks of the trial, and their written consent shall be obtained. No fees shall be charged to the subject for conducting a clinical trial.

<a id="art-1009"></a>第一千零九条　从事与人体基因、人体胚胎等有关的医学和科研活动，应当遵守法律、行政法规和国家有关规定，不得危害人体健康，不得违背伦理道德，不得损害公共利益。

**Article 1009** Medical and scientific research activities involving human genes, human embryos, and the like shall comply with laws, administrative regulations, and relevant state provisions, and shall not harm human health, violate ethical morality, or damage the public interest.

<a id="art-1010"></a>第一千零一十条　违背他人意愿，以言语、文字、图像、肢体行为等方式对他人实施性骚扰的，受害人有权依法请求行为人承担民事责任。  
机关、企业、学校等单位应当采取合理的预防、受理投诉、调查处置等措施，防止和制止利用职权、从属关系等实施性骚扰。

**Article 1010** Where a person commits sexual harassment against another person by means of words, text, images, physical acts, or the like against such other person's will, the victim shall have the right to request the actor to bear civil liability according to law.  
State organs, enterprises, schools, and other entities shall take reasonable measures such as prevention, acceptance of complaints, investigation, and handling to prevent and stop sexual harassment committed through the abuse of authority, subordinate relationships, or the like.

<a id="art-1011"></a>第一千零一十一条　以非法拘禁等方式剥夺、限制他人的行动自由，或者非法搜查他人身体的，受害人有权依法请求行为人承担民事责任。

**Article 1011** Where a person deprives or restricts another person's freedom of action by means of unlawful detention or the like, or unlawfully searches another person's body, the victim shall have the right to request the actor to bear civil liability according to law.

### 第三章　姓名权和名称权

<a id="art-1012"></a>第一千零一十二条　自然人享有姓名权，有权依法决定、使用、变更或者许可他人使用自己的姓名，但是不得违背公序良俗。

**Article 1012** A natural person shall enjoy the right to name, and shall have the right to decide, use, change, or permit another person to use his or her name according to law, provided that such acts shall not contravene public order and good customs.

<a id="art-1013"></a>第一千零一十三条　法人、非法人组织享有名称权，有权依法决定、使用、变更、转让或者许可他人使用自己的名称。

**Article 1013** A legal person or an unincorporated organization shall enjoy the right to name, and shall have the right to decide, use, change, transfer, or permit another person to use its name according to law.

<a id="art-1014"></a>第一千零一十四条　任何组织或者个人不得以干涉、盗用、假冒等方式侵害他人的姓名权或者名称权。

**Article 1014** No organization or individual may infringe upon another person's right to name by means of interference, misappropriation, impersonation, or the like.

<a id="art-1015"></a>第一千零一十五条　自然人应当随父姓或者母姓，但是有下列情形之一的，可以在父姓和母姓之外选取姓氏：  
（一）选取其他直系长辈血亲的姓氏；  
（二）因由法定扶养人以外的人扶养而选取扶养人姓氏；  
（三）有不违背公序良俗的其他正当理由。  
少数民族自然人的姓氏可以遵从本民族的文化传统和风俗习惯。

**Article 1015** A natural person shall bear the surname of his or her father or mother, but may, under any of the following circumstances, choose a surname other than the father's or mother's surname:  
(1) choosing the surname of another direct lineal elder relative by blood;  
(2) choosing the surname of the person who has raised the natural person due to being raised by a person other than a legally designated supporter  
(3) having other justifiable reasons that do not contravene public order and good customs. The surname of a natural person of an ethnic minority may follow the cultural traditions and customs of that ethnic group.

<a id="art-1016"></a>第一千零一十六条　自然人决定、变更姓名，或者法人、非法人组织决定、变更、转让名称的，应当依法向有关机关办理登记手续，但是法律另有规定的除外。  
民事主体变更姓名、名称的，变更前实施的民事法律行为对其具有法律约束力。

**Article 1016** Where a natural person decides on or changes his or her name, or a legal person or an unincorporated organization decides on, changes, or transfers its name, it shall go through the registration formalities with the relevant authority according to law, except as otherwise provided by law.  
Where a civil subject changes his, her, or its name, the juridical acts performed before such change shall be legally binding on such subject.

<a id="art-1017"></a>第一千零一十七条　具有一定社会知名度，被他人使用足以造成公众混淆的笔名、艺名、网名、译名、字号、姓名和名称的简称等，参照适用姓名权和名称权保护的有关规定。

**Article 1017** Where a pen name, stage name, online pseudonym, translated name, business name, abbreviation of a name or designation, or the like has a certain degree of social recognition and its use by another person is sufficient to cause public confusion, the relevant provisions on the protection of the right to name shall apply mutatis mutandis.

### 第四章　肖像权

<a id="art-1018"></a>第一千零一十八条　自然人享有肖像权，有权依法制作、使用、公开或者许可他人使用自己的肖像。  
肖像是通过影像、雕塑、绘画等方式在一定载体上所反映的特定自然人可以被识别的外部形象。

**Article 1018** A natural person shall enjoy the right to portrait, and shall have the right to make, use, disclose, or permit another person to use his or her portrait according to law.  
A portrait refers to the recognizable external image of a specific natural person reflected on a certain medium through images, sculptures, paintings, or other means.

<a id="art-1019"></a>第一千零一十九条　任何组织或者个人不得以丑化、污损，或者利用信息技术手段伪造等方式侵害他人的肖像权。未经肖像权人同意，不得制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像，但是法律另有规定的除外。  
未经肖像权人同意，肖像作品权利人不得以发表、复制、发行、出租、展览等方式使用或者公开肖像权人的肖像。

**Article 1019** No organization or individual may infringe upon another person's right to portrait by means of defacing, tarnishing, or forging by using information technology, or the like. Without the consent of the right holder of the portrait, the portrait of the right holder shall not be made, used, or disclosed, except as otherwise provided by law. Without the consent of the right holder of the portrait, the right holder of a portrait work shall not use or disclose the portrait of the right holder of the portrait by means of publication, reproduction, distribution, rental, exhibition, or the like.

<a id="art-1020"></a>第一千零二十条　合理实施下列行为的，可以不经肖像权人同意：  
（一）为个人学习、艺术欣赏、课堂教学或者科学研究，在必要范围内使用肖像权人已经公开的肖像；  
（二）为实施新闻报道，不可避免地制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像；  
（三）为依法履行职责，国家机关在必要范围内制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像；  
（四）为展示特定公共环境，不可避免地制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像；  
（五）为维护公共利益或者肖像权人合法权益，制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像的其他行为。

**Article 1020** The following acts may be reasonably performed without the consent of the right holder of the portrait:  
(1) using the portrait of the right holder that has already been disclosed within the necessary scope for personal study, artistic appreciation, classroom teaching, or scientific research;  
(2) inevitably making, using, or disclosing the portrait of the right holder for the purpose of carrying out news reporting;  
(3) making, using, or disclosing the portrait of the right holder within the necessary scope by a state organ for the purpose of performing its duties according to law;  
(4) inevitably making, using, or disclosing the portrait of the right holder for the purpose of displaying a specific public environment  
(5) other acts of making, using, or disclosing the portrait of the right holder for the purpose of maintaining the public interest or the lawful rights and interests of the right holder of the portrait.

<a id="art-1021"></a>第一千零二十一条　当事人对肖像许可使用合同中关于肖像使用条款的理解有争议的，应当作出有利于肖像权人的解释。

**Article 1021** Where a dispute arises between the parties regarding the understanding of the terms concerning the use of a portrait in a portrait licensing contract, an interpretation favorable to the right holder of the portrait shall be adopted.

<a id="art-1022"></a>第一千零二十二条　当事人对肖像许可使用期限没有约定或者约定不明确的，任何一方当事人可以随时解除肖像许可使用合同，但是应当在合理期限之前通知对方。  
当事人对肖像许可使用期限有明确约定，肖像权人有正当理由的，可以解除肖像许可使用合同，但是应当在合理期限之前通知对方。因解除合同造成对方损失的，除不可归责于肖像权人的事由外，应当赔偿损失。

**Article 1022** Where the parties have not agreed on or have not clearly agreed on the term of portrait licensing, any party may dissolve the portrait licensing contract at any time, provided that the other party shall be notified within a reasonable period in advance. Where the parties have clearly agreed on the term of portrait licensing, the right holder of the portrait may, for justified reasons, dissolve the portrait licensing contract, provided that the other party shall be notified within a reasonable period in advance. If the dissolution of the contract causes losses to the other party, compensation shall be made, unless the cause is not attributable to the right holder of the portrait.

<a id="art-1023"></a>第一千零二十三条　对姓名等的许可使用，参照适用肖像许可使用的有关规定。  
对自然人声音的保护，参照适用肖像权保护的有关规定。

**Article 1023** The licensing of the use of names and other such matters shall be governed by mutatis mutandis the relevant provisions on the licensing of the use of portraits.  
The protection of a natural person's voice shall be governed by mutatis mutandis the relevant provisions on the protection of the right to a portrait.

### 第五章　名誉权和荣誉权

<a id="art-1024"></a>第一千零二十四条　民事主体享有名誉权。任何组织或者个人不得以侮辱、诽谤等方式侵害他人的名誉权。  
名誉是对民事主体的品德、声望、才能、信用等的社会评价。

**Article 1024** Civil subjects shall enjoy the right to reputation. No organization or individual may infringe upon the right to reputation of others by means of insult, defamation, or the like. Reputation refers to the social evaluation of a civil subject's moral character, prestige, talent, credit, and the like.

<a id="art-1025"></a>第一千零二十五条　行为人为公共利益实施新闻报道、舆论监督等行为，影响他人名誉的，不承担民事责任，但是有下列情形之一的除外：  
（一）捏造、歪曲事实；  
（二）对他人提供的严重失实内容未尽到合理核实义务；  
（三）使用侮辱性言辞等贬损他人名誉。

**Article 1025** Where a person engages in news reporting, supervision by public opinion, or similar acts for the public interest, which adversely affects another person's reputation, the person shall not bear civil liability unless any of the following circumstances exists:  
(1) the fabrication or distortion of facts;  
(2) failure to fulfill the reasonable duty of verification regarding seriously inaccurate content provided by others  
(3) the use of insulting language or other means to disparage another person's reputation.

<a id="art-1026"></a>第一千零二十六条　认定行为人是否尽到前条第二项规定的合理核实义务，应当考虑下列因素：  
（一）内容来源的可信度；  
（二）对明显可能引发争议的内容是否进行了必要的调查；  
（三）内容的时限性；  
（四）内容与公序良俗的关联性；  
（五）受害人名誉受贬损的可能性；  
（六）核实能力和核实成本。

**Article 1026** When determining whether a person has fulfilled the reasonable duty of verification as provided in subparagraph (2) of the preceding article, the following factors shall be taken into consideration:  
(1) the credibility of the source of the content;  
(2) whether necessary investigation has been conducted into content that is manifestly likely to give rise to controversy;  
(3) the timeliness of the content;  
(4) the relevance of the content to public order and good customs;  
(5) the likelihood of damage to the victim's reputation; and (6) the capability and cost of verification.

<a id="art-1027"></a>第一千零二十七条　行为人发表的文学、艺术作品以真人真事或者特定人为描述对象，含有侮辱、诽谤内容，侵害他人名誉权的，受害人有权依法请求该行为人承担民事责任。  
行为人发表的文学、艺术作品不以特定人为描述对象，仅其中的情节与该特定人的情况相似的，不承担民事责任。

**Article 1027** Where a literary or artistic work published by a person describes a real person, real event, or a specific person, contains insulting or defamatory content, and infringes upon another person's right to reputation, the victim shall have the right to request the person to bear civil liability in accordance with law.  
Where a literary or artistic work published by a person does not describe a specific person, and only the plot therein is similar to the circumstances of that specific person, the person shall not bear civil liability.

<a id="art-1028"></a>第一千零二十八条　民事主体有证据证明报刊、网络等媒体报道的内容失实，侵害其名誉权的，有权请求该媒体及时采取更正或者删除等必要措施。

**Article 1028** Where a civil subject has evidence proving that content reported by media such as newspapers or the internet is inaccurate and infringes upon his or her right to reputation, the civil subject shall have the right to request such media to promptly take necessary measures, such as correction or deletion.

<a id="art-1029"></a>第一千零二十九条　民事主体可以依法查询自己的信用评价；发现信用评价不当的，有权提出异议并请求采取更正、删除等必要措施。信用评价人应当及时核查，经核查属实的，应当及时采取必要措施。

**Article 1029** A civil subject may, in accordance with law, inquire about his or her credit evaluation; if the civil subject finds that the credit evaluation is improper, the civil subject shall have the right to raise an objection and request the adoption of necessary measures such as correction or deletion. The credit evaluator shall promptly verify such matters; if the verification confirms the issue, the credit evaluator shall promptly adopt necessary measures.

<a id="art-1030"></a>第一千零三十条　民事主体与征信机构等信用信息处理者之间的关系，适用本编有关个人信息保护的规定和其他法律、行政法规的有关规定。

**Article 1030** The relationship between a civil subject and a credit information processor such as a credit investigation agency shall be governed by the provisions of this Part on the protection of personal information and the relevant provisions of other laws and administrative regulations.

<a id="art-1031"></a>第一千零三十一条　民事主体享有荣誉权。任何组织或者个人不得非法剥夺他人的荣誉称号，不得诋毁、贬损他人的荣誉。  
获得的荣誉称号应当记载而没有记载的，民事主体可以请求记载；获得的荣誉称号记载错误的，民事主体可以请求更正。

**Article 1031** Civil subjects shall enjoy the right to honor. No organization or individual may unlawfully deprive another person of his or her honorary title, or slander or disparage another person's honor.  
Where an honorary title obtained by a civil subject should have been recorded but has not been recorded, the civil subject may request its recording; where the honorary title obtained by a civil subject is recorded incorrectly, the civil subject may request its correction.

### 第六章　隐私权和个人信息保护

<a id="art-1032"></a>第一千零三十二条　自然人享有隐私权。任何组织或者个人不得以刺探、侵扰、泄露、公开等方式侵害他人的隐私权。  
隐私是自然人的私人生活安宁和不愿为他人知晓的私密空间、私密活动、私密信息。

**Article 1032** Natural persons shall enjoy the right to privacy. No organization or individual may infringe upon another person's right to privacy by means of prying, intrusion, disclosure, publication, or the like.  
Privacy refers to a natural person's peace of private life and the private space, private activities, and private information that the natural person does not wish to be known by others.

<a id="art-1033"></a>第一千零三十三条　除法律另有规定或者权利人明确同意外，任何组织或者个人不得实施下列行为：  
（一）以电话、短信、即时通讯工具、电子邮件、传单等方式侵扰他人的私人生活安宁；  
（二）进入、拍摄、窥视他人的住宅、宾馆房间等私密空间；  
（三）拍摄、窥视、窃听、公开他人的私密活动；  
（四）拍摄、窥视他人身体的私密部位；  
（五）处理他人的私密信息；  
（六）以其他方式侵害他人的隐私权。

**Article 1033** Unless otherwise provided by law or expressly consented to by the right holder, no organization or individual may commit any of the following acts:  
(1) intruding upon another person's peace of private life by means of telephone, text messages, instant messaging tools, emails, leaflets, or the like;  
(2) entering, photographing, or peeping into another person's private spaces such as a residence or hotel room;  
(3) photographing, peeping at, eavesdropping on, or publishing another person's private activities;  
(4) photographing or peeping at the intimate body parts of another person;  
(5) processing another person's private information  
(6) infringing upon another person's right to privacy by other means.

<a id="art-1034"></a>第一千零三十四条　自然人的个人信息受法律保护。  
个人信息是以电子或者其他方式记录的能够单独或者与其他信息结合识别特定自然人的各种信息，包括自然人的姓名、出生日期、身份证件号码、生物识别信息、住址、电话号码、电子邮箱、健康信息、行踪信息等。  
个人信息中的私密信息，适用有关隐私权的规定；没有规定的，适用有关个人信息保护的规定。

**Article 1034** A natural person's personal information shall be protected by law.  
Personal information refers to all kinds of information recorded electronically or by other means that can identify a specific natural person either alone or in combination with other information, including a natural person's name, date of birth, identity document number, biometric information, address, telephone number, email address, health information, whereabouts information, and the like.  
For private information among personal information, the provisions on the right to privacy shall apply; where there are no such provisions, the provisions on the protection of personal information shall apply.

<a id="art-1035"></a>第一千零三十五条　处理个人信息的，应当遵循合法、正当、必要原则，不得过度处理，并符合下列条件：  
（一）征得该自然人或者其监护人同意，但是法律、行政法规另有规定的除外；  
（二）公开处理信息的规则；  
（三）明示处理信息的目的、方式和范围；  
（四）不违反法律、行政法规的规定和双方的约定。  
个人信息的处理包括个人信息的收集、存储、使用、加工、传输、提供、公开等。

**Article 1035** When processing personal information, the principles of legality, legitimacy, and necessity shall be followed, and excessive processing shall not be allowed, and the following conditions shall be met:  
(1) obtaining the consent of the natural person or his or her guardian, unless otherwise provided by law or administrative regulations;  
(2) disclosing the rules for processing the information;  
(3) expressly stating the purpose, method, and scope of processing the information; and (4) not violating the provisions of laws or administrative regulations or the agreement of the parties. Processing of personal information includes the collection, storage, use, processing, transmission, provision, publication, and the like of personal information.

<a id="art-1036"></a>第一千零三十六条　处理个人信息，有下列情形之一的，行为人不承担民事责任：  
（一）在该自然人或者其监护人同意的范围内合理实施的行为；  
（二）合理处理该自然人自行公开的或者其他已经合法公开的信息，但是该自然人明确拒绝或者处理该信息侵害其重大利益的除外；  
（三）为维护公共利益或者该自然人合法权益，合理实施的其他行为。

**Article 1036** Where personal information is processed under any of the following circumstances, the person shall not bear civil liability:  
(1) the act is reasonably carried out within the scope of consent of the natural person or his or her guardian;  
(2) the act reasonably processes information that has been voluntarily made public by the natural person or has otherwise been lawfully made public, unless the natural person expressly refuses or the processing of such information infringes upon his or her material interests  
(3) the act is reasonably carried out to safeguard the public interest or the lawful rights and interests of the natural person.

<a id="art-1037"></a>第一千零三十七条　自然人可以依法向信息处理者查阅或者复制其个人信息；发现信息有错误的，有权提出异议并请求及时采取更正等必要措施。  
自然人发现信息处理者违反法律、行政法规的规定或者双方的约定处理其个人信息的，有权请求信息处理者及时删除。

**Article 1037** A natural person may, in accordance with law, access or copy his or her personal information from an information processor; if the natural person finds errors in the information, the natural person shall have the right to raise an objection and request the timely adoption of necessary measures such as correction.  
If a natural person finds that an information processor processes his or her personal information in violation of the provisions of laws or administrative regulations or the agreement of the parties, the natural person shall have the right to request the information processor to promptly delete it.

<a id="art-1038"></a>第一千零三十八条　信息处理者不得泄露或者篡改其收集、存储的个人信息；未经自然人同意，不得向他人非法提供其个人信息，但是经过加工无法识别特定个人且不能复原的除外。  
信息处理者应当采取技术措施和其他必要措施，确保其收集、存储的个人信息安全，防止信息泄露、篡改、丢失；发生或者可能发生个人信息泄露、篡改、丢失的，应当及时采取补救措施，按照规定告知自然人并向有关主管部门报告。

**Article 1038** An information processor shall not disclose or tamper with the personal information it collects or stores; without the consent of the natural person, it shall not illegally provide the natural person's personal information to others, unless the information, after processing, cannot identify a specific individual and cannot be restored.  
An information processor shall adopt technical measures and other necessary measures to ensure the security of the personal information it collects and stores, and prevent such information from being disclosed, tampered with, or lost; where a disclosure, tampering, or loss of personal information has occurred or is likely to occur, the information processor shall promptly adopt remedial measures, inform the natural person in accordance with the provisions, and report to the relevant competent authority.

<a id="art-1039"></a>第一千零三十九条　国家机关、承担行政职能的法定机构及其工作人员对于履行职责过程中知悉的自然人的隐私和个人信息，应当予以保密，不得泄露或者向他人非法提供。

**Article 1039** State organs, statutory institutions with administrative functions, and their staff shall keep confidential the privacy and personal information of natural persons that they become aware of in the course of performing their duties, and shall not disclose or illegally provide such information to others.

## 第五编　婚姻家庭

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-1040"></a>第一千零四十条　本编调整因婚姻家庭产生的民事关系。

**Article 1040** This Part regulates civil relations arising from marriage and family.

<a id="art-1041"></a>第一千零四十一条　婚姻家庭受国家保护。  
实行婚姻自由、一夫一妻、男女平等的婚姻制度。  
保护妇女、未成年人、老年人、残疾人的合法权益。

**Article 1041** Marriage and family shall be protected by the State.  
A marriage system based on freedom of marriage, monogamy, and equality between men and women shall be implemented.  
The legitimate rights and interests of women, minors, the elderly, and persons with disabilities shall be protected.

<a id="art-1042"></a>第一千零四十二条　禁止包办、买卖婚姻和其他干涉婚姻自由的行为。禁止借婚姻索取财物。  
禁止重婚。禁止有配偶者与他人同居。  
禁止家庭暴力。禁止家庭成员间的虐待和遗弃。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 1042** Arranged marriage, mercenary marriage, and any other acts interfering with the freedom of marriage shall be prohibited. The exaction of money or gifts in connection with marriage shall be prohibited. Bigamy shall be prohibited. No person who has a spouse may cohabit with another person. Domestic violence shall be prohibited. Abuse of or desertion of family members shall be prohibited.

<a id="art-1043"></a>第一千零四十三条　家庭应当树立优良家风，弘扬家庭美德，重视家庭文明建设。  
夫妻应当互相忠实，互相尊重，互相关爱；家庭成员应当敬老爱幼，互相帮助，维护平等、和睦、文明的婚姻家庭关系。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1043** Families shall establish good family traditions, promote family virtues, and attach importance to the building of a civilized family. Spouses shall be faithful to, respect, and care for each other. Family members shall respect the aged and love the young, help each other, and maintain marriage and family relations that are equal, harmonious, and civilized.

<a id="art-1044"></a>第一千零四十四条　收养应当遵循最有利于被收养人的原则，保障被收养人和收养人的合法权益。  
禁止借收养名义买卖未成年人。

**Article 1044** Adoption shall follow the principle most beneficial to the adopted person, and the legitimate rights and interests of the adopted person and the adopter shall be guaranteed.  
The sale of minors under the guise of adoption shall be prohibited.

<a id="art-1045"></a>第一千零四十五条　亲属包括配偶、血亲和姻亲。  
配偶、父母、子女、兄弟姐妹、祖父母、外祖父母、孙子女、外孙子女为近亲属。  
配偶、父母、子女和其他共同生活的近亲属为家庭成员。

**Article 1045** Relatives include spouses, blood relatives, and relatives by marriage.  
Spouses, parents, children, siblings, paternal grandparents, maternal grandparents, grandchildren, and great-grandchildren are close relatives.  
Spouses, parents, children, and other close relatives living together are family members.

### 第二章　结婚

<a id="art-1046"></a>第一千零四十六条　结婚应当男女双方完全自愿，禁止任何一方对另一方加以强迫，禁止任何组织或者个人加以干涉。

**Article 1046** Marriage shall be based on the complete willingness of both a man and a woman. No party may compel the other party, and no organization or individual may interfere.

<a id="art-1047"></a>第一千零四十七条　结婚年龄，男不得早于二十二周岁，女不得早于二十周岁。

**Article 1047** For marriage, the man shall reach the age of twenty-two, and the woman shall reach the age of twenty.

<a id="art-1048"></a>第一千零四十八条　直系血亲或者三代以内的旁系血亲禁止结婚。

**Article 1048** No marriage may be contracted between lineal blood relatives or collateral blood relatives within three generations.

<a id="art-1049"></a>第一千零四十九条　要求结婚的男女双方应当亲自到婚姻登记机关申请结婚登记。符合本法规定的，予以登记，发给结婚证。完成结婚登记，即确立婚姻关系。未办理结婚登记的，应当补办登记。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第8条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 1049** Both a man and a woman requesting marriage shall appear in person before a marriage registration authority to apply for marriage registration. If the requirements set out in this Law are met, the marriage shall be registered and a marriage certificate shall be issued. Once the marriage is registered, the marital relationship is established. Where the marriage has not been registered, the parties shall go through the registration procedures subsequently.

<a id="art-1050"></a>第一千零五十条　登记结婚后，按照男女双方约定，女方可以成为男方家庭的成员，男方可以成为女方家庭的成员。

**Article 1050** After marriage registration, in accordance with the agreement between both a man and a woman, the woman may become a member of the man's family, and the man may become a member of the woman's family.

<a id="art-1051"></a>第一千零五十一条　有下列情形之一的，婚姻无效：  
（一）重婚；  
（二）有禁止结婚的亲属关系；  
（三）未到法定婚龄。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第9条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第10条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-14" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第14条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-15" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第15条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第17条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第1条引用">[6]</a></sup>

**Article 1051** A marriage shall be void under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) bigamy;  
(2) having a prohibited family relationship between the parties;  
(3) failing to meet the legal age for marriage.

<a id="art-1052"></a>第一千零五十二条　因胁迫结婚的，受胁迫的一方可以向人民法院请求撤销婚姻。  
请求撤销婚姻的，应当自胁迫行为终止之日起一年内提出。  
被非法限制人身自由的当事人请求撤销婚姻的，应当自恢复人身自由之日起一年内提出。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-18" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第18条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第19条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/婚姻、家庭与继承/婚姻登记条例.md#art-12" title="被《婚姻登记条例》第12条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 1052** Where a marriage is concluded by coercion, the coerced party may petition the People's Court for revocation of the marriage.  
A petition for revocation of marriage shall be filed within one year from the date on which the coercive act ceases.  
Where a party whose personal freedom is illegally restricted petitions for revocation of marriage, the petition shall be filed within one year from the date on which the party's personal freedom is restored.

<a id="art-1053"></a>第一千零五十三条　一方患有重大疾病的，应当在结婚登记前如实告知另一方；不如实告知的，另一方可以向人民法院请求撤销婚姻。  
请求撤销婚姻的，应当自知道或者应当知道撤销事由之日起一年内提出。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/婚姻、家庭与继承/婚姻登记条例.md#art-12" title="被《婚姻登记条例》第12条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1053** Where one party suffers from a serious disease, it shall truthfully inform the other party before marriage registration. If it fails to truthfully inform, the other party may petition the People's Court for revocation of the marriage. A petition for revocation of marriage shall be filed within one year from the date on which the party knows or should have known the cause for revocation.

<a id="art-1054"></a>第一千零五十四条　无效的或者被撤销的婚姻自始没有法律约束力，当事人不具有夫妻的权利和义务。同居期间所得的财产，由当事人协议处理；协议不成的，由人民法院根据照顾无过错方的原则判决。对重婚导致的无效婚姻的财产处理，不得侵害合法婚姻当事人的财产权益。当事人所生的子女，适用本法关于父母子女的规定。  
婚姻无效或者被撤销的，无过错方有权请求损害赔偿。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-20" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第20条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1054** A void or revoked marriage has no legal effect from its inception, and the parties shall not have the rights or obligations of husband and wife. Property acquired during cohabitation shall be disposed of by agreement between the parties. If no agreement is reached, the People's Court shall render a judgment based on the principle of protecting the innocent party. In the case of a void marriage due to bigamy, the disposal of property shall not infringe upon the property rights and interests of the party to the lawful marriage. The provisions of this Law on the relationship between parents and children shall apply to any children born of the parties. Where a marriage is void or revoked, the innocent party has the right to claim damages.

### 第三章　家庭关系

#### 第一节　夫妻关系

<a id="art-1055"></a>第一千零五十五条　夫妻在婚姻家庭中地位平等。

**Article 1055** The status of husband and wife shall be equal in marriage and family.

<a id="art-1056"></a>第一千零五十六条　夫妻双方都有各自使用自己姓名的权利。

**Article 1056** Both the husband and the wife have the right to use their own respective surnames.

<a id="art-1057"></a>第一千零五十七条　夫妻双方都有参加生产、工作、学习和社会活动的自由，一方不得对另一方加以限制或者干涉。

**Article 1057** Both the husband and the wife have the freedom to participate in production, work, study, and social activities. Neither party may restrict or interfere with the other.

<a id="art-1058"></a>第一千零五十八条　夫妻双方平等享有对未成年子女抚养、教育和保护的权利，共同承担对未成年子女抚养、教育和保护的义务。

**Article 1058** Both the husband and the wife enjoy equal rights to raise, educate, and protect minor children, and shall jointly bear the obligations to raise, educate, and protect minor children.

<a id="art-1059"></a>第一千零五十九条　夫妻有相互扶养的义务。  
需要扶养的一方，在另一方不履行扶养义务时，有要求其给付扶养费的权利。

**Article 1059** The husband and wife have the duty to support each other.  
Where one party in need of support fails to receive support from the other party, it has the right to demand the other party to pay support.

<a id="art-1060"></a>第一千零六十条　夫妻一方因家庭日常生活需要而实施的民事法律行为，对夫妻双方发生效力，但是夫妻一方与相对人另有约定的除外。  
夫妻之间对一方可以实施的民事法律行为范围的限制，不得对抗善意相对人。

**Article 1060** A civil juristic act carried out by one spouse for the needs of daily family life shall have effect on both spouses, except where the spouse and the counterparty have otherwise agreed.  
Any restriction agreed between the spouses on the scope of civil juristic acts that one spouse may carry out shall not be set up against a bona fide counterparty.

<a id="art-1061"></a>第一千零六十一条　夫妻有相互继承遗产的权利。

**Article 1061** Spouses shall have the right to inherit each other's estate.

<a id="art-1062"></a>第一千零六十二条　夫妻在婚姻关系存续期间所得的下列财产，为夫妻的共同财产，归夫妻共同所有：  
（一）工资、奖金、劳务报酬；  
（二）生产、经营、投资的收益；  
（三）知识产权的收益；  
（四）继承或者受赠的财产，但是[本法第一千零六十三条](#art-1063)第三项规定的除外；  
（五）其他应当归共同所有的财产。  
夫妻对共同财产，有平等的处理权。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-24" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第24条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-25" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第25条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-29" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第29条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 1062** The following property acquired during the existence of the marital relationship shall be the common property of the spouses and shall be jointly owned by them:  
(1) wages, bonuses, and labor remuneration;  
(2) proceeds from production, business operations, and investments;  
(3) proceeds from intellectual property rights;  
(4) property inherited or donated, except as provided in Item 3 of Article 1063 of this Code; and (5) other property that shall be jointly owned. Spouses shall have equal rights to manage the common property.

<a id="art-1063"></a>第一千零六十三条　下列财产为夫妻一方的个人财产：  
（一）一方的婚前财产；  
（二）一方因受到人身损害获得的赔偿或者补偿；  
（三）遗嘱或者赠与合同中确定只归一方的财产；  
（四）一方专用的生活用品；  
（五）其他应当归一方的财产。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-31" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第31条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1063** The following property shall be the separate property of one spouse:  
(1) pre-marital property of one spouse;  
(2) compensation or damages received by one spouse for personal injury;  
(3) property designated in a will or gift contract to be owned solely by one spouse;  
(4) articles for daily use exclusively for one spouse; and (5) other property that shall belong to one spouse.

<a id="art-1064"></a>第一千零六十四条　夫妻双方共同签名或者夫妻一方事后追认等共同意思表示所负的债务，以及夫妻一方在婚姻关系存续期间以个人名义为家庭日常生活需要所负的债务，属于夫妻共同债务。  
夫妻一方在婚姻关系存续期间以个人名义超出家庭日常生活需要所负的债务，不属于夫妻共同债务；但是，债权人能够证明该债务用于夫妻共同生活、共同生产经营或者基于夫妻双方共同意思表示的除外。

**Article 1064** Debts incurred through a joint expression of intent, such as joint signature by both spouses or subsequent ratification by one spouse, and debts incurred by one spouse in his or her own name during the existence of the marital relationship for the needs of daily family life, shall be community debts of the spouses.  
Debts incurred by one spouse in his or her own name during the existence of the marital relationship beyond the needs of daily family life shall not be community debts of the spouses; unless the creditor can prove that such debts were used for the spouses' community life, community production or business operations, or were based on the joint expression of intent of both spouses.

<a id="art-1065"></a>第一千零六十五条　男女双方可以约定婚姻关系存续期间所得的财产以及婚前财产归各自所有、共同所有或者部分各自所有、部分共同所有。约定应当采用书面形式。没有约定或者约定不明确的，适用[本法第一千零六十二条](#art-1062)、第一千零六十三条的规定。  
夫妻对婚姻关系存续期间所得的财产以及婚前财产的约定，对双方具有法律约束力。  
夫妻对婚姻关系存续期间所得的财产约定归各自所有，夫或者妻一方对外所负的债务，相对人知道该约定的，以夫或者妻一方的个人财产清偿。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-37" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第37条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1065** A man and a woman may agree that the property acquired during the existence of the marital relationship and pre-marital property shall be owned separately, jointly, or partially separately and partially jointly. Such an agreement shall be in writing. Where there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, the provisions of Articles 1062 and 1063 of this Code shall apply. An agreement between the spouses concerning property acquired during the existence of the marital relationship and pre-marital property shall be legally binding on both parties. Where the spouses agree that property acquired during the existence of the marital relationship shall be owned separately, and one spouse incurs a debt to a third party, if the third party is aware of such agreement, the debt shall be satisfied from the separate property of that spouse.

<a id="art-1066"></a>第一千零六十六条　婚姻关系存续期间，有下列情形之一的，夫妻一方可以向人民法院请求分割共同财产：  
（一）一方有隐藏、转移、变卖、毁损、挥霍夫妻共同财产或者伪造夫妻共同债务等严重损害夫妻共同财产利益的行为；  
（二）一方负有法定扶养义务的人患重大疾病需要医治，另一方不同意支付相关医疗费用。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-38" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第38条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第6条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第7条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 1066** During the existence of the marital relationship, under any of the following circumstances, one spouse may petition the People's Court for the partition of the common property:  
(1) One spouse commits acts that seriously impair the interests of the common property, such as concealing, transferring, selling, damaging, wasting the common property, or fabricating community debts  
(2) A person to whom one spouse has a statutory duty of support suffers from a serious illness and requires medical treatment, and the other spouse refuses to pay the relevant medical expenses.

#### 第二节　父母子女关系和其他近亲属关系

<a id="art-1067"></a>第一千零六十七条　父母不履行抚养义务的，未成年子女或者不能独立生活的成年子女，有要求父母给付抚养费的权利。  
成年子女不履行赡养义务的，缺乏劳动能力或者生活困难的父母，有要求成年子女给付赡养费的权利。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-41" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第41条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-42" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第42条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 1067** Where parents fail to perform their duty of support, minor children or adult children who are unable to live independently shall have the right to claim child support from their parents.  
Where adult children fail to perform their duty of maintenance, parents who lack the capacity to work or are experiencing financial hardship shall have the right to claim maintenance from their adult children.

<a id="art-1068"></a>第一千零六十八条　父母有教育、保护未成年子女的权利和义务。未成年子女造成他人损害的，父母应当依法承担民事责任。

**Article 1068** Parents shall have the right and duty to educate and protect their minor children. Where minor children cause damage to others, the parents shall bear civil liability according to law.

<a id="art-1069"></a>第一千零六十九条　子女应当尊重父母的婚姻权利，不得干涉父母离婚、再婚以及婚后的生活。子女对父母的赡养义务，不因父母的婚姻关系变化而终止。

**Article 1069** Children shall respect their parents' marital rights and shall not interfere with their parents' divorce, remarriage, or post-marital life. The duty of children to maintain their parents shall not terminate due to any change in the parents' marital relationship.

<a id="art-1070"></a>第一千零七十条　父母和子女有相互继承遗产的权利。

**Article 1070** Parents and children shall have the right to inherit each other's estate.

<a id="art-1071"></a>第一千零七十一条　非婚生子女享有与婚生子女同等的权利，任何组织或者个人不得加以危害和歧视。  
不直接抚养非婚生子女的生父或者生母，应当负担未成年子女或者不能独立生活的成年子女的抚养费。

**Article 1071** Children born out of wedlock shall enjoy the same rights as children born in wedlock, and no organization or individual may harm or discriminate against them.  
The biological father or mother who does not directly raise a child born out of wedlock shall bear the child support for the minor child or the adult child who is unable to live independently.

<a id="art-1072"></a>第一千零七十二条　继父母与继子女间，不得虐待或者歧视。  
继父或者继母和受其抚养教育的继子女间的权利义务关系，适用本法关于父母子女关系的规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-18" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第18条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1072** Stepparents and stepchildren shall not abuse or discriminate against each other.  
The rights and obligations between a stepfather or stepmother and stepchildren raised and educated by him or her shall be governed by the provisions of this Code concerning the relationship between parents and children.

<a id="art-1073"></a>第一千零七十三条　对亲子关系有异议且有正当理由的，父或者母可以向人民法院提起诉讼，请求确认或者否认亲子关系。  
对亲子关系有异议且有正当理由的，成年子女可以向人民法院提起诉讼，请求确认亲子关系。

**Article 1073** Where there is a dispute over a parent-child relationship and there is a legitimate reason, the father or mother may institute an action in the People's Court to confirm or deny the parent-child relationship.  
Where there is a dispute over a parent-child relationship and there is a legitimate reason, an adult child may institute an action in the People's Court to confirm the parent-child relationship.

<a id="art-1074"></a>第一千零七十四条　有负担能力的祖父母、外祖父母，对于父母已经死亡或者父母无力抚养的未成年孙子女、外孙子女，有抚养的义务。  
有负担能力的孙子女、外孙子女，对于子女已经死亡或者子女无力赡养的祖父母、外祖父母，有赡养的义务。

**Article 1074** Grandparents and maternal grandparents who have the ability to bear the burden shall have the duty to support their minor grandchildren or maternal grandchildren whose parents have died or are unable to support them.  
Grandchildren and maternal grandchildren who have the ability to bear the burden shall have the duty to maintain their grandparents or maternal grandparents whose children have died or are unable to maintain them.

<a id="art-1075"></a>第一千零七十五条　有负担能力的兄、姐，对于父母已经死亡或者父母无力抚养的未成年弟、妹，有扶养的义务。  
由兄、姐扶养长大的有负担能力的弟、妹，对于缺乏劳动能力又缺乏生活来源的兄、姐，有扶养的义务。

**Article 1075** Elder brothers and sisters who have the ability to bear the burden shall have the duty to support their minor younger brothers and sisters whose parents have died or are unable to support them.  
Younger brothers and sisters who have been raised by their elder brothers or sisters and have the ability to bear the burden shall have the duty to support their elder brothers or sisters who lack the capacity to work and have no source of livelihood.

### 第四章　离婚

<a id="art-1076"></a>第一千零七十六条　夫妻双方自愿离婚的，应当签订书面离婚协议，并亲自到婚姻登记机关申请离婚登记。  
离婚协议应当载明双方自愿离婚的意思表示和对子女抚养、财产以及债务处理等事项协商一致的意见。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-69" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第69条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1076** Where both spouses voluntarily divorce, they shall conclude a written divorce agreement and personally apply to the marriage registration authority for divorce registration.  
The divorce agreement shall state the expression of intent of both parties to voluntarily divorce and the mutually agreed opinions on matters such as the custody of children, property, and debt settlement.

<a id="art-1077"></a>第一千零七十七条　自婚姻登记机关收到离婚登记申请之日起三十日内，任何一方不愿意离婚的，可以向婚姻登记机关撤回离婚登记申请。  
前款规定期限届满后三十日内，双方应当亲自到婚姻登记机关申请发给离婚证；未申请的，视为撤回离婚登记申请。

**Article 1077** Within 30 days from the date on which the marriage registration authority receives the application for divorce registration, if either party is unwilling to divorce, that party may withdraw the application for divorce registration with the marriage registration authority.  
Within 30 days after the expiration of the period specified in the preceding paragraph, both parties shall personally appear before the marriage registration authority to apply for the issuance of a divorce certificate; if no application is made, the application for divorce registration shall be deemed to have been withdrawn.

<a id="art-1078"></a>第一千零七十八条　婚姻登记机关查明双方确实是自愿离婚，并已经对子女抚养、财产以及债务处理等事项协商一致的，予以登记，发给离婚证。

**Article 1078** Where the marriage registration authority ascertains that both parties are indeed voluntarily divorcing and have reached a consensus on matters such as the custody of children, property, and debt settlement, it shall register the divorce and issue a divorce certificate.

<a id="art-1079"></a>第一千零七十九条　夫妻一方要求离婚的，可以由有关组织进行调解或者直接向人民法院提起离婚诉讼。  
人民法院审理离婚案件，应当进行调解；如果感情确已破裂，调解无效的，应当准予离婚。  
有下列情形之一，调解无效的，应当准予离婚：  
（一）重婚或者与他人同居；  
（二）实施家庭暴力或者虐待、遗弃家庭成员；  
（三）有赌博、吸毒等恶习屡教不改；  
（四）因感情不和分居满二年；  
（五）其他导致夫妻感情破裂的情形。  
一方被宣告失踪，另一方提起离婚诉讼的，应当准予离婚。  
经人民法院判决不准离婚后，双方又分居满一年，一方再次提起离婚诉讼的，应当准予离婚。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-23" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第23条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-63" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第63条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-64" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第64条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 1079** Where one spouse demands a divorce, the relevant organization may conduct mediation, or the spouse may directly file a divorce action in the People's Court. When adjudicating a divorce case, the People's Court shall conduct mediation; if the mutual affection is indeed broken and mediation fails, the divorce shall be granted. A divorce shall be granted if mediation fails under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) bigamy or cohabitation with another person;  
(2) domestic violence or maltreatment, abandonment of family members;  
(3) repeated engagement in vices such as gambling or drug addiction despite repeated admonitions;  
(4) separation for at least two years due to incompatibility in affection;  
(5) other circumstances leading to the breakdown of mutual affection. Where one spouse has been declared missing, and the other spouse files a divorce action, the divorce shall be granted. After a judgment by the People's Court refusing a divorce, if the parties have been separated for another full year, and one party again files a divorce action, the divorce shall be granted.

<a id="art-1080"></a>第一千零八十条　完成离婚登记，或者离婚判决书、调解书生效，即解除婚姻关系。

**Article 1080** The marital relationship shall be dissolved upon the completion of divorce registration or upon the effectiveness of a divorce judgment or mediation statement.

<a id="art-1081"></a>第一千零八十一条　现役军人的配偶要求离婚，应当征得军人同意，但是军人一方有重大过错的除外。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-64" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第64条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1081** Where the spouse of a serviceperson on active duty requests a divorce, the consent of the serviceperson shall be obtained, except where the serviceperson has committed a major fault.

<a id="art-1082"></a>第一千零八十二条　女方在怀孕期间、分娩后一年内或者终止妊娠后六个月内，男方不得提出离婚；但是，女方提出离婚或者人民法院认为确有必要受理男方离婚请求的除外。

**Article 1082** The husband may not initiate divorce proceedings while his wife is pregnant, within one year after childbirth, or within six months after the termination of pregnancy; however, this restriction does not apply where the wife initiates the divorce proceedings or where the People's Court deems it necessary to accept the husband's divorce request.

<a id="art-1083"></a>第一千零八十三条　离婚后，男女双方自愿恢复婚姻关系的，应当到婚姻登记机关重新进行结婚登记。

**Article 1083** After divorce, if both the man and the woman voluntarily wish to restore the marital relationship, they shall undergo anew marriage registration at the marriage registration authority.

<a id="art-1084"></a>第一千零八十四条　父母与子女间的关系，不因父母离婚而消除。离婚后，子女无论由父或者母直接抚养，仍是父母双方的子女。  
离婚后，父母对于子女仍有抚养、教育、保护的权利和义务。  
离婚后，不满两周岁的子女，以由母亲直接抚养为原则。已满两周岁的子女，父母双方对抚养问题协议不成的，由人民法院根据双方的具体情况，按照最有利于未成年子女的原则判决。子女已满八周岁的，应当尊重其真实意愿。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-44" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第44条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-13" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第13条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-16" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第16条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 1084** The relationship between parents and children shall not be terminated by the divorce of the parents. After divorce, regardless of whether the child is directly raised by the father or the mother, the child remains the child of both parents. After divorce, parents shall still have the rights and obligations to raise, educate, and protect their children. After divorce, a child under the age of two shall be raised directly by the mother as a principle. For a child who has reached the age of two, if the parents fail to reach an agreement on custody, the People's Court shall render a judgment in accordance with the principle of the best interests of the minor child based on the specific circumstances of both parties. If the child has reached the age of eight, the child's true wishes shall be respected.

<a id="art-1085"></a>第一千零八十五条　离婚后，子女由一方直接抚养的，另一方应当负担部分或者全部抚养费。负担费用的多少和期限的长短，由双方协议；协议不成的，由人民法院判决。  
前款规定的协议或者判决，不妨碍子女在必要时向父母任何一方提出超过协议或者判决原定数额的合理要求。

**Article 1085** After divorce, where a child is directly raised by one party, the other party shall bear part or all of the child support. The amount and duration of such child support shall be agreed upon by both parties; where an agreement cannot be reached, the People's Court shall render a judgment. The agreement or judgment as provided in the preceding paragraph shall not preclude the child from making reasonable requests to either parent for an amount exceeding the originally agreed or adjudicated sum when necessary.

<a id="art-1086"></a>第一千零八十六条　离婚后，不直接抚养子女的父或者母，有探望子女的权利，另一方有协助的义务。  
行使探望权利的方式、时间由当事人协议；协议不成的，由人民法院判决。  
父或者母探望子女，不利于子女身心健康的，由人民法院依法中止探望；中止的事由消失后，应当恢复探望。

**Article 1086** After divorce, the parent who does not directly raise the child shall have the right to visit the child, and the other party shall have the obligation to provide assistance.  
The manner and time for exercising the visitation right shall be agreed upon by the parties; where an agreement cannot be reached, the People's Court shall render a judgment.  
If the visitation by the father or mother is detrimental to the physical or mental health of the child, the People's Court shall suspend the visitation in accordance with law; after the grounds for suspension cease to exist, visitation shall be resumed.

<a id="art-1087"></a>第一千零八十七条　离婚时，夫妻的共同财产由双方协议处理；协议不成的，由人民法院根据财产的具体情况，按照照顾子女、女方和无过错方权益的原则判决。  
对夫或者妻在家庭土地承包经营中享有的权益等，应当依法予以保护。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-69" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第69条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-78" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第78条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第10条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-20" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第20条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 1087** Upon divorce, the common property of the husband and wife shall be disposed by agreement between the parties; where an agreement cannot be reached, the People's Court shall render a judgment based on the specific circumstances of the property and in accordance with the principle of protecting the interests of the child, wife, and the non-fault party.  
The rights and interests enjoyed by the husband or wife in the operation of household land contracts, among others, shall be protected in accordance with law.

<a id="art-1088"></a>第一千零八十八条　夫妻一方因抚育子女、照料老年人、协助另一方工作等负担较多义务的，离婚时有权向另一方请求补偿，另一方应当给予补偿。具体办法由双方协议；协议不成的，由人民法院判决。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-21" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第21条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1088** Where one spouse has undertaken more obligations, such as raising children, caring for the elderly, or assisting the other spouse in work, that spouse shall have the right to request compensation from the other spouse upon divorce, and the other spouse shall make such compensation. The specific method shall be agreed upon by both parties; where an agreement cannot be reached, the People's Court shall render a judgment.

<a id="art-1089"></a>第一千零八十九条　离婚时，夫妻共同债务应当共同偿还。共同财产不足清偿或者财产归各自所有的，由双方协议清偿；协议不成的，由人民法院判决。

**Article 1089** Upon divorce, the joint debts of the husband and wife shall be repaid jointly. Where the common property is insufficient to repay the debts or where the property is owned by each party separately, the parties shall agree on the method of repayment; where an agreement cannot be reached, the People's Court shall render a judgment.

<a id="art-1090"></a>第一千零九十条　离婚时，如果一方生活困难，有负担能力的另一方应当给予适当帮助。具体办法由双方协议；协议不成的，由人民法院判决。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-22" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第22条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1090** Upon divorce, if one party is living in difficulty, the other party who has the ability to bear the burden shall provide appropriate assistance. The specific method shall be agreed upon by both parties; where an agreement cannot be reached, the People's Court shall render a judgment.

<a id="art-1091"></a>第一千零九十一条　有下列情形之一，导致离婚的，无过错方有权请求损害赔偿：  
（一）重婚；  
（二）与他人同居；  
（三）实施家庭暴力；  
（四）虐待、遗弃家庭成员；  
（五）有其他重大过错。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-86" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第86条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-87" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第87条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-88" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第88条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-89" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第89条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-90" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第90条引用">[5]</a></sup>

**Article 1091** Where a divorce occurs due to any of the following circumstances, the non-fault party shall have the right to claim damages:  
(1) bigamy;  
(2) cohabitation with another person;  
(3) committing domestic violence;  
(4) maltreatment or abandonment of family members;  
(5) having other major faults.

<a id="art-1092"></a>第一千零九十二条　夫妻一方隐藏、转移、变卖、毁损、挥霍夫妻共同财产，或者伪造夫妻共同债务企图侵占另一方财产的，在离婚分割夫妻共同财产时，对该方可以少分或者不分。离婚后，另一方发现有上述行为的，可以向人民法院提起诉讼，请求再次分割夫妻共同财产。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-84" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第84条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 1092** Where one spouse conceals, transfers, sells, damages, or squanders the common property of the husband and wife, or fabricates joint debts of the husband and wife in an attempt to encroach upon the property of the other spouse, that spouse may receive a reduced share or no share upon the division of the common property upon divorce. After divorce, if the other spouse discovers the aforesaid conduct, that spouse may file a lawsuit with the People's Court to request the redivision of the common property of the husband and wife.

### 第五章　收养

#### 第一节　收养关系的成立

<a id="art-1093"></a>第一千零九十三条　下列未成年人，可以被收养：  
（一）丧失父母的孤儿；  
（二）查找不到生父母的未成年人；  
（三）生父母有特殊困难无力抚养的子女。

**Article 1093** The following minors may be adopted:  
(1) orphans who have lost their parents;  
(2) minors whose biological parents cannot be located;  
(3) children whose biological parents are unable to raise them due to special difficulties.

<a id="art-1094"></a>第一千零九十四条　下列个人、组织可以作送养人：  
（一）孤儿的监护人；  
（二）儿童福利机构；  
（三）有特殊困难无力抚养子女的生父母。

**Article 1094** The following individuals or organizations may serve as the persons giving the child for adoption:  
(1) the guardians of an orphan;  
(2) child welfare institutions;  
(3) biological parents who, due to special difficulties, are unable to raise their children.

<a id="art-1095"></a>第一千零九十五条　未成年人的父母均不具备完全民事行为能力且可能严重危害该未成年人的，该未成年人的监护人可以将其送养。

**Article 1095** Where both parents of a minor lack full capacity for civil conduct and may seriously harm the minor, the guardian of the minor may give the minor for adoption.

<a id="art-1096"></a>第一千零九十六条　监护人送养孤儿的，应当征得有抚养义务的人同意。有抚养义务的人不同意送养、监护人不愿意继续履行监护职责的，应当依照本法第一编的规定另行确定监护人。

**Article 1096** Where a guardian gives an orphan for adoption, the guardian shall obtain the consent of the person obligated to raise the orphan. If the person obligated to raise the orphan does not consent to the adoption, and the guardian is unwilling to continue performing the guardianship duties, a guardian shall be otherwise designated in accordance with the provisions of Part One of this Law.

<a id="art-1097"></a>第一千零九十七条　生父母送养子女，应当双方共同送养。生父母一方不明或者查找不到的，可以单方送养。

**Article 1097** Where biological parents give their child for adoption, both biological parents shall act jointly. Where one biological parent is unknown or cannot be located, the other biological parent may give the child for adoption unilaterally.

<a id="art-1098"></a>第一千零九十八条　收养人应当同时具备下列条件：  
（一）无子女或者只有一名子女；  
（二）有抚养、教育和保护被收养人的能力；  
（三）未患有在医学上认为不应当收养子女的疾病；  
（四）无不利于被收养人健康成长的违法犯罪记录；  
（五）年满三十周岁。

**Article 1098** An adopter shall satisfy all of the following conditions:  
(1) having no children or having only one child;  
(2) possessing the ability to raise, educate, and protect the adoptee;  
(3) not suffering from a disease that, according to medical opinion, renders the person unsuitable for adopting a child;  
(4) having no criminal or illegal record that may be detrimental to the healthy growth of the adoptee;  
(5) having reached the age of thirty.

<a id="art-1099"></a>第一千零九十九条　收养三代以内旁系同辈血亲的子女，可以不受[本法第一千零九十三条](#art-1093)第三项、第一千零九十四条第三项和第一千一百零二条规定的限制。  
华侨收养三代以内旁系同辈血亲的子女，还可以不受[本法第一千零九十八条](#art-1098)第一项规定的限制。

**Article 1099** For the adoption of children of collateral relatives by blood of the same generation within three degrees of kinship, the restrictions provided in Item (3) of Article 1093, Item (3) of Article 1094, and Article 1102 of this Law shall not apply.  
Where an overseas Chinese adopts children of collateral relatives by blood of the same generation within three degrees of kinship, the restriction provided in Item (1) of Article 1098 of this Law shall also not apply.

<a id="art-1100"></a>第一千一百条　无子女的收养人可以收养两名子女；有子女的收养人只能收养一名子女。  
收养孤儿、残疾未成年人或者儿童福利机构抚养的查找不到生父母的未成年人，可以不受前款和[本法第一千零九十八条](#art-1098)第一项规定的限制。

**Article 1100** An adopter without children may adopt two children; an adopter with children may only adopt one child.  
Where the adoption involves an orphan, a minor with disabilities, or a minor raised by a child welfare institution whose biological parents cannot be located, the restrictions provided in the preceding paragraph and Item (1) of Article 1098 of this Law shall not apply.

<a id="art-1101"></a>第一千一百零一条　有配偶者收养子女，应当夫妻共同收养。

**Article 1101** Where a person with a spouse adopts a child, the adoption shall be jointly made by the husband and wife.

<a id="art-1102"></a>第一千一百零二条　无配偶者收养异性子女的，收养人与被收养人的年龄应当相差四十周岁以上。

**Article 1102** Where a person without a spouse adopts a child of the opposite sex, the age difference between the adopter and the adoptee shall be no less than 40 years.

<a id="art-1103"></a>第一千一百零三条　继父或者继母经继子女的生父母同意，可以收养继子女，并可以不受[本法第一千零九十三条](#art-1093)第三项、第一千零九十四条第三项、第一千零九十八条和第一千一百条第一款规定的限制。

**Article 1103** A stepfather or stepmother may, with the consent of the natural parents of the stepchild, adopt the stepchild and shall not be subject to the restrictions provided in subparagraph (3) of Article 1093, subparagraph (3) of Article 1094, Article 1098, and paragraph 1 of Article 1100 of this Law.

<a id="art-1104"></a>第一千一百零四条　收养人收养与送养人送养，应当双方自愿。收养八周岁以上未成年人的，应当征得被收养人的同意。

**Article 1104** The adoption by an adopter and the placement of a child for adoption by a person placing out a child for adoption shall be voluntary. Where an adoptee is a minor over the age of eight, the adoptee's consent shall be obtained.

<a id="art-1105"></a>第一千一百零五条　收养应当向县级以上人民政府民政部门登记。收养关系自登记之日起成立。  
收养查找不到生父母的未成年人的，办理登记的民政部门应当在登记前予以公告。  
收养关系当事人愿意签订收养协议的，可以签订收养协议。  
收养关系当事人各方或者一方要求办理收养公证的，应当办理收养公证。  
县级以上人民政府民政部门应当依法进行收养评估。

**Article 1105** Adoption shall be registered with the civil affairs department of the people's government at or above the county level. The adoptive relationship shall be established from the date of registration. Where an adoptee is a minor whose natural parents cannot be found, the civil affairs department handling the registration shall make a public announcement before registration. Where the parties to the adoptive relationship are willing to enter into an adoption agreement, they may do so. Where all parties or one party to the adoptive relationship request notarization of the adoption, the adoption shall be notarized. The civil affairs department of the people's government at or above the county level shall conduct an adoption assessment in accordance with law.

<a id="art-1106"></a>第一千一百零六条　收养关系成立后，公安机关应当按照国家有关规定为被收养人办理户口登记。

**Article 1106** After an adoptive relationship is established, the public security organ shall, in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State, register the household registration for the adoptee.

<a id="art-1107"></a>第一千一百零七条　孤儿或者生父母无力抚养的子女，可以由生父母的亲属、朋友抚养；抚养人与被抚养人的关系不适用本章规定。

**Article 1107** Orphans or children whose natural parents are unable to raise them may be raised by the relatives or friends of the natural parents; the provisions of this Chapter shall not apply to the relationship between the person raising the child and the child being raised.

<a id="art-1108"></a>第一千一百零八条　配偶一方死亡，另一方送养未成年子女的，死亡一方的父母有优先抚养的权利。

**Article 1108** Where one spouse dies and the other spouse places a minor child out for adoption, the parents of the deceased spouse shall have the priority right to raise the child.

<a id="art-1109"></a>第一千一百零九条　外国人依法可以在中华人民共和国收养子女。  
外国人在中华人民共和国收养子女，应当经其所在国主管机关依照该国法律审查同意。收养人应当提供由其所在国有权机构出具的有关其年龄、婚姻、职业、财产、健康、有无受过刑事处罚等状况的证明材料，并与送养人签订书面协议，亲自向省、自治区、直辖市人民政府民政部门登记。  
前款规定的证明材料应当经收养人所在国外交机关或者外交机关授权的机构认证，并经中华人民共和国驻该国使领馆认证，但是国家另有规定的除外。

**Article 1109** Aliens may adopt children in the People's Republic of China in accordance with law. Where an alien adopts a child in the People's Republic of China, the adoption shall be subject to the examination and approval of the competent authority of the alien's home country in accordance with the laws of that country. The adopter shall provide supporting materials issued by the competent authority of the alien's home country concerning the adopter's age, marital status, occupation, property, health status, and whether the adopter has been subject to criminal punishment, etc., and shall enter into a written agreement with the person placing out the child for adoption, and shall personally register with the civil affairs department of the people's government of a province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government. The supporting materials provided in the preceding paragraph shall be authenticated by the diplomatic authorities or an agency authorized by the diplomatic authorities of the alien's home country, and by the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in that country, unless otherwise provided by the State.

<a id="art-1110"></a>第一千一百一十条　收养人、送养人要求保守收养秘密的，其他人应当尊重其意愿，不得泄露。

**Article 1110** Where an adopter or a person placing out a child for adoption requests that the adoption details be kept confidential, other persons shall respect such request and shall not disclose them.

#### 第二节　收养的效力

<a id="art-1111"></a>第一千一百一十一条　自收养关系成立之日起，养父母与养子女间的权利义务关系，适用本法关于父母子女关系的规定；养子女与养父母的近亲属间的权利义务关系，适用本法关于子女与父母的近亲属关系的规定。  
养子女与生父母以及其他近亲属间的权利义务关系，因收养关系的成立而消除。

**Article 1111** From the date of establishment of the adoptive relationship, the provisions of this Law on the relationship between parents and children shall apply to the rights and obligations between adoptive parents and adopted children; the provisions of this Law on the relationship between children and the close relatives of their parents shall apply to the rights and obligations between adopted children and the close relatives of the adoptive parents.  
The rights and obligations between an adopted child and the natural parents and other close relatives shall be extinguished upon the establishment of the adoptive relationship.

<a id="art-1112"></a>第一千一百一十二条　养子女可以随养父或者养母的姓氏，经当事人协商一致，也可以保留原姓氏。

**Article 1112** An adopted child may adopt the surname of the adoptive father or the adoptive mother, or, subject to the unanimous agreement of the parties, may retain the original surname.

<a id="art-1113"></a>第一千一百一十三条　有本法第一编关于民事法律行为无效规定情形或者违反本编规定的收养行为无效。  
无效的收养行为自始没有法律约束力。

**Article 1113** An adoption act that falls under the circumstances provided in Book One of this Law for the invalidity of a civil juristic act or that violates the provisions of this Book shall be null and void.  
A void adoption act shall have no legally binding effect from the very beginning.

#### 第三节　收养关系的解除

<a id="art-1114"></a>第一千一百一十四条　收养人在被收养人成年以前，不得解除收养关系，但是收养人、送养人双方协议解除的除外。养子女八周岁以上的，应当征得本人同意。  
收养人不履行抚养义务，有虐待、遗弃等侵害未成年养子女合法权益行为的，送养人有权要求解除养父母与养子女间的收养关系。送养人、收养人不能达成解除收养关系协议的，可以向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 1114** An adopter shall not dissolve the adoptive relationship before the adoptee reaches majority, except where the adopter and the person placing out the child for adoption agree by mutual agreement to dissolve the relationship. Where the adopted child has reached the age of eight, the child's consent shall be obtained. Where the adopter fails to perform the duty of raising the child and commits acts such as abuse or abandonment that infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of the minor adopted child, the person placing out the child for adoption shall have the right to request the dissolution of the adoptive relationship between the adoptive parents and the adopted child. Where the person placing out the child for adoption and the adopter fail to reach an agreement on the dissolution of the adoptive relationship, they may file a lawsuit with the People's Court.

<a id="art-1115"></a>第一千一百一十五条　养父母与成年养子女关系恶化、无法共同生活的，可以协议解除收养关系。不能达成协议的，可以向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 1115** Where the relationship between adoptive parents and their adult adopted child deteriorates so that they cannot live together, the adoptive relationship may be dissolved by agreement. If no agreement can be reached, they may file a lawsuit with the People's Court.

<a id="art-1116"></a>第一千一百一十六条　当事人协议解除收养关系的，应当到民政部门办理解除收养关系登记。

**Article 1116** Where the parties agree to dissolve the adoptive relationship, they shall go through the registration of dissolution of the adoptive relationship with the civil affairs department.

<a id="art-1117"></a>第一千一百一十七条　收养关系解除后，养子女与养父母以及其他近亲属间的权利义务关系即行消除，与生父母以及其他近亲属间的权利义务关系自行恢复。但是，成年养子女与生父母以及其他近亲属间的权利义务关系是否恢复，可以协商确定。

**Article 1117** Upon dissolution of the adoptive relationship, the rights and obligations between the adopted child and the adoptive parents and other close relatives shall be terminated, and the rights and obligations between the adopted child and the natural parents and other close relatives shall be automatically restored. However, whether the rights and obligations between an adult adopted child and the natural parents and other close relatives are restored may be determined by negotiation.

<a id="art-1118"></a>第一千一百一十八条　收养关系解除后，经养父母抚养的成年养子女，对缺乏劳动能力又缺乏生活来源的养父母，应当给付生活费。因养子女成年后虐待、遗弃养父母而解除收养关系的，养父母可以要求养子女补偿收养期间支出的抚养费。  
生父母要求解除收养关系的，养父母可以要求生父母适当补偿收养期间支出的抚养费；但是，因养父母虐待、遗弃养子女而解除收养关系的除外。

**Article 1118** After dissolution of the adoptive relationship, an adult adopted child who was raised by the adoptive parents shall pay maintenance to the adoptive parents who are unable to work and lack a source of income. If the adoptive relationship is dissolved due to the adult adopted child's abuse or abandonment of the adoptive parents, the adoptive parents may request the adopted child to compensate for the maintenance expenses incurred during the adoption period. If the natural parents request dissolution of the adoptive relationship, the adoptive parents may request them to make appropriate compensation for the maintenance expenses incurred during the adoption period; however, this shall not apply if the adoptive relationship is dissolved due to the adoptive parents' abuse or abandonment of the adopted child.

## 第六编　继承

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-1119"></a>第一千一百一十九条　本编调整因继承产生的民事关系。

**Article 1119** This Book regulates the civil relations arising from succession.

<a id="art-1120"></a>第一千一百二十条　国家保护自然人的继承权。

**Article 1120** The State protects the right of succession of natural persons.

<a id="art-1121"></a>第一千一百二十一条　继承从被继承人死亡时开始。  
相互有继承关系的数人在同一事件中死亡，难以确定死亡时间的，推定没有其他继承人的人先死亡。都有其他继承人，辈份不同的，推定长辈先死亡；辈份相同的，推定同时死亡，相互不发生继承。

**Article 1121** Succession shall commence upon the death of the decedent. Where several persons with mutual succession relationship die in the same event and it is difficult to determine the time of death, the person without any other successor shall be presumed to have died first. Where each of them has other successors, if they are of different generations, the senior shall be presumed to have died first; if they are of the same generation, they shall be presumed to have died simultaneously, and no succession shall occur between them.

<a id="art-1122"></a>第一千一百二十二条　遗产是自然人死亡时遗留的个人合法财产。  
依照法律规定或者根据其性质不得继承的遗产，不得继承。

**Article 1122** An estate comprises the lawful personal property left by a natural person upon death.  
Any estate which is non-inheritable according to legal provisions or by its nature shall not be inherited.

<a id="art-1123"></a>第一千一百二十三条　继承开始后，按照法定继承办理；有遗嘱的，按照遗嘱继承或者遗赠办理；有遗赠扶养协议的，按照协议办理。

**Article 1123** After the commencement of succession, it shall be handled in accordance with statutory succession; where a will exists, it shall be handled in accordance with testamentary succession or legacy; where a legacy-support agreement exists, it shall be handled in accordance with such agreement.

<a id="art-1124"></a>第一千一百二十四条　继承开始后，继承人放弃继承的，应当在遗产处理前，以书面形式作出放弃继承的表示；没有表示的，视为接受继承。  
受遗赠人应当在知道受遗赠后六十日内，作出接受或者放弃受遗赠的表示；到期没有表示的，视为放弃受遗赠。

**Article 1124** After the commencement of succession, if an heir disclaims the inheritance, he or she shall, before the disposition of the estate, make a written statement of disclaimer; if no such statement is made, he or she shall be deemed to have accepted the inheritance.  
A legatee shall, within sixty days after becoming aware of the legacy, make a statement of acceptance or disclaimer; if no such statement is made upon expiration of the time limit, he or she shall be deemed to have disclaimed the legacy.

<a id="art-1125"></a>第一千一百二十五条　继承人有下列行为之一的，丧失继承权：  
（一）故意杀害被继承人；  
（二）为争夺遗产而杀害其他继承人；  
（三）遗弃被继承人，或者虐待被继承人情节严重；  
（四）伪造、篡改、隐匿或者销毁遗嘱，情节严重；  
（五）以欺诈、胁迫手段迫使或者妨碍被继承人设立、变更或者撤回遗嘱，情节严重。  
继承人有前款第三项至第五项行为，确有悔改表现，被继承人表示宽恕或者事后在遗嘱中将其列为继承人的，该继承人不丧失继承权。  
受遗赠人有本条第一款规定行为的，丧失受遗赠权。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第5条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第6条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第8条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第9条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 1125** An heir shall be disqualified from inheritance upon committing any of the following acts:  
(1) intentionally killing the decedent;  
(2) killing another heir for the purpose of vying for the estate;  
(3) abandoning the decedent or treating the decedent with gross maltreatment;  
(4) forging, altering, concealing, or destroying a will with gross circumstances  
(5) inducing or preventing the decedent to make, modify, or revoke a will by means of fraud or duress with gross circumstances. If an heir commits any of the acts specified in Items (3) to (5) of the preceding paragraph but shows genuine repentance, and the decedent expresses forgiveness or later designates the heir as a successor in a will, such heir shall not be disqualified from inheritance. If a legatee commits any of the acts specified in the first paragraph of this Article, he or she shall be disqualified from accepting the legacy.

### 第二章　法定继承

<a id="art-1126"></a>第一千一百二十六条　继承权男女平等。

**Article 1126** Male and female successors shall enjoy equal rights of succession.

<a id="art-1127"></a>第一千一百二十七条　遗产按照下列顺序继承：  
（一）第一顺序：配偶、子女、父母；  
（二）第二顺序：兄弟姐妹、祖父母、外祖父母。  
继承开始后，由第一顺序继承人继承，第二顺序继承人不继承；没有第一顺序继承人继承的，由第二顺序继承人继承。  
本编所称子女，包括婚生子女、非婚生子女、养子女和有扶养关系的继子女。  
本编所称父母，包括生父母、养父母和有扶养关系的继父母。  
本编所称兄弟姐妹，包括同父母的兄弟姐妹、同父异母或者同母异父的兄弟姐妹、养兄弟姐妹、有扶养关系的继兄弟姐妹。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第10条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1127** An estate shall be inherited in the following order:  
(1) first order: spouse, children, and parents;  
(2) second order: siblings, grandparents, and maternal grandparents. After the commencement of succession, the estate shall be inherited by the heirs of the first order, while the heirs of the second order shall not inherit; where there are no heirs of the first order, the estate shall be inherited by the heirs of the second order. The term "children" as used in this Book includes children born in wedlock, children born out of wedlock, adopted children, and stepchildren who have a support relationship. The term "parents" as used in this Book includes biological parents, adoptive parents, and stepparents who have a support relationship. The term "siblings" as used in this Book includes full siblings, half-siblings, adopted siblings, and stepsiblings who have a support relationship.

<a id="art-1128"></a>第一千一百二十八条　被继承人的子女先于被继承人死亡的，由被继承人的子女的直系晚辈血亲代位继承。  
被继承人的兄弟姐妹先于被继承人死亡的，由被继承人的兄弟姐妹的子女代位继承。  
代位继承人一般只能继承被代位继承人有权继承的遗产份额。

**Article 1128** Where a child of the decedent predeceases the decedent, the direct lineal descendants of such child shall inherit by subrogation.  
Where a sibling of the decedent predeceases the decedent, the children of such sibling shall inherit by subrogation.  
A subrogated successor shall generally only inherit the share of the estate which the subrogated successor would have been entitled to inherit.

<a id="art-1129"></a>第一千一百二十九条　丧偶儿媳对公婆，丧偶女婿对岳父母，尽了主要赡养义务的，作为第一顺序继承人。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-18" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第18条引用">[1]</a></sup>
