**Article 1129** A widowed daughter-in-law who has rendered the primary obligation of support to her parents-in-law, or a widowed son-in-law who has rendered the primary obligation of support to his parents-in-law, shall be deemed an heir of the first order.

<a id="art-1130"></a>第一千一百三十条　同一顺序继承人继承遗产的份额，一般应当均等。  
对生活有特殊困难又缺乏劳动能力的继承人，分配遗产时，应当予以照顾。  
对被继承人尽了主要扶养义务或者与被继承人共同生活的继承人，分配遗产时，可以多分。  
有扶养能力和有扶养条件的继承人，不尽扶养义务的，分配遗产时，应当不分或者少分。  
继承人协商同意的，也可以不均等。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1130** The share of the estate inherited by the heirs of the same order shall generally be equal.  
Where an heir has special difficulties in living and lacks the ability to work, such heir shall receive due consideration in the distribution of the estate.  
An heir who has rendered the primary obligation of support to the decedent or who has lived with the decedent may receive a larger share in the distribution of the estate.  
An heir who has the ability and conditions to provide support but fails to perform the obligation of support shall receive no or a smaller share in the distribution of the estate.  
The heirs may also distribute the estate in unequal shares if they so agree through negotiation.

<a id="art-1131"></a>第一千一百三十一条　对继承人以外的依靠被继承人扶养的人，或者继承人以外的对被继承人扶养较多的人，可以分给适当的遗产。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第10条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-20" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第20条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-21" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第21条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-41" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第41条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 1131** A person, other than an heir, who was dependent on the support of the decedent, or a person, other than an heir, who has contributed considerable support to the decedent, may receive an appropriate share of the estate.

<a id="art-1132"></a>第一千一百三十二条　继承人应当本着互谅互让、和睦团结的精神，协商处理继承问题。遗产分割的时间、办法和份额，由继承人协商确定；协商不成的，可以由人民调解委员会调解或者向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 1132** Heirs shall, in the spirit of mutual understanding, mutual accommodation, harmony, and unity, negotiate and resolve succession matters. The time, method, and shares for the partition of the estate shall be determined by the heirs through negotiation; where negotiation fails, the matter may be mediated by a People's Mediation Committee or submitted to a People's Court for litigation.

### 第三章　遗嘱继承和遗赠

<a id="art-1133"></a>第一千一百三十三条　自然人可以依照本法规定立遗嘱处分个人财产，并可以指定遗嘱执行人。  
自然人可以立遗嘱将个人财产指定由法定继承人中的一人或者数人继承。  
自然人可以立遗嘱将个人财产赠与国家、集体或者法定继承人以外的组织、个人。  
自然人可以依法设立遗嘱信托。

**Article 1133** A natural person may, in accordance with the provisions of this Law, make a will to dispose of his or her personal property and may appoint an executor of the will.  
A natural person may make a will to designate that his or her personal property be inherited by one or more of the statutory heirs.  
A natural person may make a will to bequeath his or her personal property to the State, a collective, or an organization or individual other than the statutory heirs.  
A natural person may establish a testamentary trust according to law.

<a id="art-1134"></a>第一千一百三十四条　自书遗嘱由遗嘱人亲笔书写，签名，注明年、月、日。

**Article 1134** A holographic will shall be written by the testator in his or her own handwriting, signed by the testator, and dated with the year, month, and day.

<a id="art-1135"></a>第一千一百三十五条　代书遗嘱应当有两个以上见证人在场见证，由其中一人代书，并由遗嘱人、代书人和其他见证人签名，注明年、月、日。

**Article 1135** A will made through a scribe shall, with at least two witnesses present, be written by one of the witnesses on behalf of the testator, and shall be signed by the testator, the scribe, and the other witnesses, and dated with the year, month, and day.

<a id="art-1136"></a>第一千一百三十六条　打印遗嘱应当有两个以上见证人在场见证。遗嘱人和见证人应当在遗嘱每一页签名，注明年、月、日。

**Article 1136** A printed will shall require the presence of at least two witnesses. The testator and the witnesses shall sign each page of the will and date it with the year, month, and day.

<a id="art-1137"></a>第一千一百三十七条　以录音录像形式立的遗嘱，应当有两个以上见证人在场见证。遗嘱人和见证人应当在录音录像中记录其姓名或者肖像，以及年、月、日。

**Article 1137** A will made in the form of audio or video recording shall require the presence of at least two witnesses. The testator and the witnesses shall record their names or images, as well as the year, month, and day, in the audio or video recording.

<a id="art-1138"></a>第一千一百三十八条　遗嘱人在危急情况下，可以立口头遗嘱。口头遗嘱应当有两个以上见证人在场见证。危急情况消除后，遗嘱人能够以书面或者录音录像形式立遗嘱的，所立的口头遗嘱无效。

**Article 1138** A testator may make an oral will in an emergency situation. An oral will shall require the presence of at least two witnesses. After the emergency situation lifts, if the testator is able to make a will in writing or by audio or video recording, the oral will shall be null and void.

<a id="art-1139"></a>第一千一百三十九条　公证遗嘱由遗嘱人经公证机构办理。

**Article 1139** A notarized will shall be handled by the testator through a notarization institution.

<a id="art-1140"></a>第一千一百四十条　下列人员不能作为遗嘱见证人：  
（一）无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人以及其他不具有见证能力的人；  
（二）继承人、受遗赠人；  
（三）与继承人、受遗赠人有利害关系的人。

**Article 1140** The following persons shall not act as witnesses to a will:  
(1) persons with no civil capacity, persons with limited civil capacity, or other persons lacking the capacity to witness;  
(2) heirs and legatees; and (3) persons having an interest in the heirs or legatees.

<a id="art-1141"></a>第一千一百四十一条　遗嘱应当为缺乏劳动能力又没有生活来源的继承人保留必要的遗产份额。

**Article 1141** A will shall preserve the necessary share of the estate for an heir lacking the capacity to work and without a source of livelihood.

<a id="art-1142"></a>第一千一百四十二条　遗嘱人可以撤回、变更自己所立的遗嘱。  
立遗嘱后，遗嘱人实施与遗嘱内容相反的民事法律行为的，视为对遗嘱相关内容的撤回。  
立有数份遗嘱，内容相抵触的，以最后的遗嘱为准。

**Article 1142** The testator may revoke or amend the will made by himself or herself.  
If, after making a will, the testator performs civil juristic acts contrary to the contents of the will, such acts shall be deemed as the revocation of the relevant contents of the will.  
If multiple wills made are inconsistent in terms of contents, the will that was made most recently shall prevail.

<a id="art-1143"></a>第一千一百四十三条　无民事行为能力人或者限制民事行为能力人所立的遗嘱无效。  
遗嘱必须表示遗嘱人的真实意思，受欺诈、胁迫所立的遗嘱无效。  
伪造的遗嘱无效。  
遗嘱被篡改的，篡改的内容无效。

**Article 1143** A will made by a person who has no civil capacity or a person with limited civil capacity is invalid.  
A will must manifest the genuine intention of the testator; a will made under fraud or duress is invalid.  
A forged will is invalid.  
If a will has been altered, the altered contents are invalid.

<a id="art-1144"></a>第一千一百四十四条　遗嘱继承或者遗赠附有义务的，继承人或者受遗赠人应当履行义务。没有正当理由不履行义务的，经利害关系人或者有关组织请求，人民法院可以取消其接受附义务部分遗产的权利。

**Article 1144** If testamentary succession or legacy is subject to an obligation, the heir or legatee shall perform such obligation. If the heir or legatee fails to perform the obligation without reasonable cause, upon the request of an interested person or the relevant organization, the People's Court may revoke his or her right to receive the part of the estate subject to the obligation.

### 第四章　遗产的处理

<a id="art-1145"></a>第一千一百四十五条　继承开始后，遗嘱执行人为遗产管理人；没有遗嘱执行人的，继承人应当及时推选遗产管理人；继承人未推选的，由继承人共同担任遗产管理人；没有继承人或者继承人均放弃继承的，由被继承人生前住所地的民政部门或者村民委员会担任遗产管理人。

**Article 1145** After the commencement of succession, the executor of the will shall serve as the estate administrator. If there is no executor of the will, the heirs shall promptly select an estate administrator. If the heirs fail to select one, all heirs shall jointly serve as the estate administrator. If there are no heirs or all heirs disclaim the succession, the civil affairs department or the villagers committee of the place of the deceased's domicile shall serve as the estate administrator.

<a id="art-1146"></a>第一千一百四十六条　对遗产管理人的确定有争议的，利害关系人可以向人民法院申请指定遗产管理人。

**Article 1146** If there is a dispute over the determination of an estate administrator, an interested person may apply to the People's Court for the appointment of an estate administrator.

<a id="art-1147"></a>第一千一百四十七条　遗产管理人应当履行下列职责：  
（一）清理遗产并制作遗产清单；  
（二）向继承人报告遗产情况；  
（三）采取必要措施防止遗产毁损、灭失；  
（四）处理被继承人的债权债务；  
（五）按照遗嘱或者依照法律规定分割遗产；  
（六）实施与管理遗产有关的其他必要行为。

**Article 1147** The estate administrator shall perform the following duties:  
(1) To take inventory of the estate and make a list thereof;  
(2) To report the estate to the heirs;  
(3) To take necessary measures to prevent the estate from being damaged or lost;  
(4) To settle the claims and debts of the deceased;  
(5) To distribute the estate in accordance with the will or the provisions of the law; and (6) To perform other necessary actions related to the administration of the estate.

<a id="art-1148"></a>第一千一百四十八条　遗产管理人应当依法履行职责，因故意或者重大过失造成继承人、受遗赠人、债权人损害的，应当承担民事责任。

**Article 1148** An estate administrator shall perform his or her duties according to law. If a loss is caused to an heir, a legatee, or a creditor due to the administrator's intention or gross negligence, the administrator shall bear civil liability.

<a id="art-1149"></a>第一千一百四十九条　遗产管理人可以依照法律规定或者按照约定获得报酬。

**Article 1149** An estate administrator may receive remuneration according to the provisions of the law or as agreed.

<a id="art-1150"></a>第一千一百五十条　继承开始后，知道被继承人死亡的继承人应当及时通知其他继承人和遗嘱执行人。继承人中无人知道被继承人死亡或者知道被继承人死亡而不能通知的，由被继承人生前所在单位或者住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会负责通知。

**Article 1150** After the commencement of succession, the heir who knows of the death of the deceased shall promptly notify the other heirs and the executor of the will. If none of the heirs knows of the death, or if they know of it but are unable to give notice, the employer of the deceased during his or her lifetime, or the residents committee or villagers committee of the place of the deceased's domicile, shall be responsible for giving the notice.

<a id="art-1151"></a>第一千一百五十一条　存有遗产的人，应当妥善保管遗产，任何组织或者个人不得侵吞或者争抢。

**Article 1151** The person in possession of the estate shall properly keep the estate, and no organization or individual may misappropriate or seize it.

<a id="art-1152"></a>第一千一百五十二条　继承开始后，继承人于遗产分割前死亡，并没有放弃继承的，该继承人应当继承的遗产转给其继承人，但是遗嘱另有安排的除外。

**Article 1152** After the commencement of succession, if an heir dies before the distribution of the estate and has not disclaimed the succession, the estate that the heir should have inherited shall pass to his or her successors, unless otherwise provided in the will.

<a id="art-1153"></a>第一千一百五十三条　夫妻共同所有的财产，除有约定的外，遗产分割时，应当先将共同所有的财产的一半分出为配偶所有，其余的为被继承人的遗产。  
遗产在家庭共有财产之中的，遗产分割时，应当先分出他人的财产。

**Article 1153** For property jointly owned by a couple, unless otherwise agreed, upon distribution of the estate, one-half of the jointly owned property shall first be allocated to the spouse as his or her own, and the remainder shall constitute the estate of the deceased.  
If the estate is part of the property jointly owned by a family, upon distribution of the estate, the property of others shall first be separated.

<a id="art-1154"></a>第一千一百五十四条　有下列情形之一的，遗产中的有关部分按照法定继承办理：  
（一）遗嘱继承人放弃继承或者受遗赠人放弃受遗赠；  
（二）遗嘱继承人丧失继承权或者受遗赠人丧失受遗赠权；  
（三）遗嘱继承人、受遗赠人先于遗嘱人死亡或者终止；  
（四）遗嘱无效部分所涉及的遗产；  
（五）遗嘱未处分的遗产。

**Article 1154** In any of the following circumstances, the relevant part of the estate shall be dealt with in accordance with the rules of statutory succession:  
(1) The testamentary heir disclaims the succession or the legatee disclaims the legacy;  
(2) The testamentary heir forfeits the right of succession or the legatee forfeits the right to receive the legacy;  
(3) The testamentary heir or the legatee predeceases the testator or terminates before him or her;  
(4) The part of the estate affected by the invalid portion of the will  
(5) The part of the estate not disposed of by the will.

<a id="art-1155"></a>第一千一百五十五条　遗产分割时，应当保留胎儿的继承份额。胎儿娩出时是死体的，保留的份额按照法定继承办理。

**Article 1155** Upon the distribution of the estate, a share of the succession shall be reserved for the fetus. If the fetus is stillborn at birth, the reserved share shall be dealt with in accordance with the rules of statutory succession.

<a id="art-1156"></a>第一千一百五十六条　遗产分割应当有利于生产和生活需要，不损害遗产的效用。  
不宜分割的遗产，可以采取折价、适当补偿或者共有等方法处理。

**Article 1156** The distribution of the estate shall facilitate the needs of production and livelihood and shall not impair the utility of the estate.  
For the estate that is unsuitable for division, methods such as conversion into monetary value, appropriate compensation, or co-ownership may be adopted.

<a id="art-1157"></a>第一千一百五十七条　夫妻一方死亡后另一方再婚的，有权处分所继承的财产，任何组织或者个人不得干涉。

**Article 1157** If the surviving spouse remarries after the death of the other spouse, he or she shall have the right to dispose of the property inherited, and no organization or individual may interfere.

<a id="art-1158"></a>第一千一百五十八条　自然人可以与继承人以外的组织或者个人签订遗赠扶养协议。按照协议，该组织或者个人承担该自然人生养死葬的义务，享有受遗赠的权利。

**Article 1158** A natural person may enter into a legacy-for-support agreement with an organization or an individual other than an heir. In accordance with the agreement, such organization or individual shall assume the obligation to support the natural person during his or her lifetime and to attend to his or her funeral and burial, and shall be entitled to receive the legacy.

<a id="art-1159"></a>第一千一百五十九条　分割遗产，应当清偿被继承人依法应当缴纳的税款和债务；但是，应当为缺乏劳动能力又没有生活来源的继承人保留必要的遗产。

**Article 1159** Upon distributing the estate, the taxes and debts that the deceased shall pay and settle according to law shall be satisfied; however, the necessary share of the estate shall be reserved for an heir who lacks the capacity to work and without a source of livelihood.

<a id="art-1160"></a>第一千一百六十条　无人继承又无人受遗赠的遗产，归国家所有，用于公益事业；死者生前是集体所有制组织成员的，归所在集体所有制组织所有。

**Article 1160** An estate with no successor to accept, nor a legatee to receive it, shall escheat to the State for use in public welfare undertakings; if the deceased was, during his or her lifetime, a member of a collective-owned organization, the estate shall escheat to such collective-owned organization.

<a id="art-1161"></a>第一千一百六十一条　继承人以所得遗产实际价值为限清偿被继承人依法应当缴纳的税款和债务。超过遗产实际价值部分，继承人自愿偿还的不在此限。  
继承人放弃继承的，对被继承人依法应当缴纳的税款和债务可以不负清偿责任。

**Article 1161** A successor shall pay off the taxes and debts that the decedent shall pay according to law within the limit of the actual value of the estate that the successor receives. If the taxes and debts exceed the actual value of the estate, the successor's voluntary repayment of the excess part shall not be subject to this limitation. Where a successor waives succession, he may be exempted from the liability for paying off the taxes and debts that the decedent shall pay according to law.

<a id="art-1162"></a>第一千一百六十二条　执行遗赠不得妨碍清偿遗赠人依法应当缴纳的税款和债务。

**Article 1162** The execution of a legacy shall not hinder the payment of taxes and debts that the testator shall pay according to law.

<a id="art-1163"></a>第一千一百六十三条　既有法定继承又有遗嘱继承、遗赠的，由法定继承人清偿被继承人依法应当缴纳的税款和债务；超过法定继承遗产实际价值部分，由遗嘱继承人和受遗赠人按比例以所得遗产清偿。

**Article 1163** Where there are both statutory succession and testate succession or legacy, the statutory successors shall pay off the taxes and debts that the decedent shall pay according to law; the part exceeding the actual value of the estate acquired by statutory succession shall be paid off by the testate successors and legatees in proportion to the estate they receive.

## 第七编　侵权责任

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-1164"></a>第一千一百六十四条　本编调整因侵害民事权益产生的民事关系。

**Article 1164** This Book regulates civil relations arising from the infringement of civil rights and interests.

<a id="art-1165"></a>第一千一百六十五条　行为人因过错侵害他人民事权益造成损害的，应当承担侵权责任。  
依照法律规定推定行为人有过错，其不能证明自己没有过错的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1165** Where a person infringes upon another person's civil rights and interests through fault and causes damage, he shall bear tort liability.  
Where a person is presumed to be at fault according to the provisions of the law, and he cannot prove that he is not at fault, he shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1166"></a>第一千一百六十六条　行为人造成他人民事权益损害，不论行为人有无过错，法律规定应当承担侵权责任的，依照其规定。

**Article 1166** Where a person causes damage to another person's civil rights and interests, regardless of whether the person is at fault, if the law provides that he shall bear tort liability, such provision shall apply.

<a id="art-1167"></a>第一千一百六十七条　侵权行为危及他人人身、财产安全的，被侵权人有权请求侵权人承担停止侵害、排除妨碍、消除危险等侵权责任。

**Article 1167** Where a tortious act endangers the personal or property safety of another person, the aggrieved person has the right to request the tortfeasor to bear tort liability such as cessation of the infringement, removal of the obstruction, and elimination of the danger.

<a id="art-1168"></a>第一千一百六十八条　二人以上共同实施侵权行为，造成他人损害的，应当承担连带责任。

**Article 1168** Where two or more persons jointly commit a tortious act and cause damage to another person, they shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-1169"></a>第一千一百六十九条　教唆、帮助他人实施侵权行为的，应当与行为人承担连带责任。  
教唆、帮助无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人实施侵权行为的，应当承担侵权责任；该无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人的监护人未尽到监护职责的，应当承担相应的责任。

**Article 1169** A person who instigates or assists another person in committing a tortious act shall bear joint and several liability with the actor.  
A person who instigates or assists a person without civil capacity or a person with limited civil capacity in committing a tortious act shall bear tort liability; if the guardian of such person without civil capacity or person with limited civil capacity fails to fulfill the guardian duties, the guardian shall bear corresponding liability.

<a id="art-1170"></a>第一千一百七十条　二人以上实施危及他人人身、财产安全的行为，其中一人或者数人的行为造成他人损害，能够确定具体侵权人的，由侵权人承担责任；不能确定具体侵权人的，行为人承担连带责任。

**Article 1170** Where two or more persons engage in acts that endanger the personal or property safety of another person, and the act of one or several of them causes damage to another person, if the specific tortfeasor can be identified, the tortfeasor shall bear liability; if the specific tortfeasor cannot be identified, all actors shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-1171"></a>第一千一百七十一条　二人以上分别实施侵权行为造成同一损害，每个人的侵权行为都足以造成全部损害的，行为人承担连带责任。

**Article 1171** Where two or more persons separately commit tortious acts and cause the same damage, and each person's tortious act is sufficient to cause the entire damage, the actors shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-1172"></a>第一千一百七十二条　二人以上分别实施侵权行为造成同一损害，能够确定责任大小的，各自承担相应的责任；难以确定责任大小的，平均承担责任。

**Article 1172** Where two or more persons separately commit tortious acts and cause the same damage, if the extent of liability can be determined, each person shall bear corresponding liability; if the extent of liability is difficult to determine, each person shall bear liability equally.

<a id="art-1173"></a>第一千一百七十三条　被侵权人对同一损害的发生或者扩大有过错的，可以减轻侵权人的责任。

**Article 1173** Where the aggrieved person is at fault for the occurrence or aggravation of the same damage, the tortfeasor's liability may be mitigated.

<a id="art-1174"></a>第一千一百七十四条　损害是因受害人故意造成的，行为人不承担责任。

**Article 1174** Where the damage is intentionally caused by the victim, the actor shall not bear liability.

<a id="art-1175"></a>第一千一百七十五条　损害是因第三人造成的，第三人应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1175** Where the damage is caused by a third party, the third party shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1176"></a>第一千一百七十六条　自愿参加具有一定风险的文体活动，因其他参加者的行为受到损害的，受害人不得请求其他参加者承担侵权责任；但是，其他参加者对损害的发生有故意或者重大过失的除外。  
活动组织者的责任适用[本法第一千一百九十八条](#art-1198)至第一千二百零一条的规定。

**Article 1176** Where a person voluntarily participates in cultural or sports activities with certain risks, and is damaged by the act of another participant, the victim may not request the other participant to bear tort liability, unless the other participant had intentional or gross negligence regarding the occurrence of the damage.  
The liability of the activity organizer shall be governed by the provisions of Articles 1198 to 1201 of this Law.

<a id="art-1177"></a>第一千一百七十七条　合法权益受到侵害，情况紧迫且不能及时获得国家机关保护，不立即采取措施将使其合法权益受到难以弥补的损害的，受害人可以在保护自己合法权益的必要范围内采取扣留侵权人的财物等合理措施；但是，应当立即请求有关国家机关处理。  
受害人采取的措施不当造成他人损害的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1177** Where a person's lawful rights and interests are infringed upon, the situation is urgent and timely protection from a state organ cannot be obtained, and failure to take immediate measures will cause irreparable damage to his lawful rights and interests, the victim may take reasonable measures such as detaining the tortfeasor's property within the scope necessary to protect his own lawful rights and interests; however, he shall immediately request the relevant state organ to handle the matter.  
Where the victim takes improper measures and causes damage to another person, he shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1178"></a>第一千一百七十八条　本法和其他法律对不承担责任或者减轻责任的情形另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 1178** Where this Law or other laws provide otherwise on the circumstances of non-liability or mitigation of liability, such provisions shall apply.

### 第二章　损害赔偿

<a id="art-1179"></a>第一千一百七十九条　侵害他人造成人身损害的，应当赔偿医疗费、护理费、交通费、营养费、住院伙食补助费等为治疗和康复支出的合理费用，以及因误工减少的收入。造成残疾的，还应当赔偿辅助器具费和残疾赔偿金；造成死亡的，还应当赔偿丧葬费和死亡赔偿金。

**Article 1179** Where a person infringes upon another person and causes personal injury, he shall compensate for the reasonable expenses incurred for treatment and rehabilitation, such as medical expenses, nursing fees, transportation fees, nutrition fees, and hospital meal subsidies, as well as the reduced income from loss of work. Where disability is caused, he shall also compensate for the cost of assistive devices and disability damages; where death is caused, he shall also compensate for funeral expenses and death damages.

<a id="art-1180"></a>第一千一百八十条　因同一侵权行为造成多人死亡的，可以以相同数额确定死亡赔偿金。

**Article 1180** Where the death of multiple persons is caused by the same tortious act, the death damages may be determined at the same amount.

<a id="art-1181"></a>第一千一百八十一条　被侵权人死亡的，其近亲属有权请求侵权人承担侵权责任。被侵权人为组织，该组织分立、合并的，承继权利的组织有权请求侵权人承担侵权责任。  
被侵权人死亡的，支付被侵权人医疗费、丧葬费等合理费用的人有权请求侵权人赔偿费用，但是侵权人已经支付该费用的除外。

**Article 1181** : Where an infringed person dies, his or her close relatives shall have the right to request the tortfeasor to bear tort liability. Where the infringed person is an organization that has been divided or merged, the organization that succeeds to its rights shall have the right to request the tortfeasor to bear tort liability. Where the infringed person dies, any person who has paid reasonable expenses such as medical expenses and funeral expenses for the infringed person shall have the right to request the tortfeasor to compensate such expenses, unless the tortfeasor has already paid such expenses.

<a id="art-1182"></a>第一千一百八十二条　侵害他人人身权益造成财产损失的，按照被侵权人因此受到的损失或者侵权人因此获得的利益赔偿；被侵权人因此受到的损失以及侵权人因此获得的利益难以确定，被侵权人和侵权人就赔偿数额协商不一致，向人民法院提起诉讼的，由人民法院根据实际情况确定赔偿数额。

**Article 1182** : Where infringement upon the personal rights and interests of another person causes property losses, compensation shall be made according to the losses suffered by the infringed person thereby or the benefits gained by the tortfeasor thereby; where the losses suffered by the infringed person thereby and the benefits gained by the tortfeasor thereby are difficult to determine, and the infringed person and the tortfeasor fail to reach an agreement on the amount of compensation through consultation and initiate an action in a People's Court, the People's Court shall determine the amount of compensation according to the actual circumstances.

<a id="art-1183"></a>第一千一百八十三条　侵害自然人人身权益造成严重精神损害的，被侵权人有权请求精神损害赔偿。  
因故意或者重大过失侵害自然人具有人身意义的特定物造成严重精神损害的，被侵权人有权请求精神损害赔偿。

**Article 1183** : Where infringement upon the personal rights and interests of a natural person causes serious mental harm, the infringed person shall have the right to claim compensation for mental harm.  
Where infringement, committed intentionally or through gross negligence, upon a specific item of a natural person that has personal significance for the natural person causes serious mental harm, the infringed person shall have the right to claim compensation for mental harm.

<a id="art-1184"></a>第一千一百八十四条　侵害他人财产的，财产损失按照损失发生时的市场价格或者其他合理方式计算。

**Article 1184** : Where property of another person is infringed upon, property losses shall be calculated according to the market price at the time of occurrence of the loss or by other reasonable means.

<a id="art-1185"></a>第一千一百八十五条　故意侵害他人知识产权，情节严重的，被侵权人有权请求相应的惩罚性赔偿。

**Article 1185** : Where intellectual property rights of another person are intentionally infringed upon under serious circumstances, the infringed person shall have the right to claim corresponding punitive damages.

<a id="art-1186"></a>第一千一百八十六条　受害人和行为人对损害的发生都没有过错的，依照法律的规定由双方分担损失。

**Article 1186** : Where neither the victim nor the actor is at fault for the occurrence of the damage, the losses shall be shared by both parties in accordance with the provisions of law.

<a id="art-1187"></a>第一千一百八十七条　损害发生后，当事人可以协商赔偿费用的支付方式。协商不一致的，赔偿费用应当一次性支付；一次性支付确有困难的，可以分期支付，但是被侵权人有权请求提供相应的担保。

**Article 1187** : After the occurrence of damage, the parties may negotiate the method of payment of compensation. If negotiation fails, the compensation shall be paid in a lump sum; if payment in a lump sum is genuinely difficult, it may be paid in installments, provided that the infringed person shall have the right to request the provision of corresponding security.

### 第三章　责任主体的特殊规定

<a id="art-1188"></a>第一千一百八十八条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人造成他人损害的，由监护人承担侵权责任。监护人尽到监护职责的，可以减轻其侵权责任。  
有财产的无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人造成他人损害的，从本人财产中支付赔偿费用；不足部分，由监护人赔偿。

**Article 1188** : Where a person without civil capacity or a person with limited civil capacity causes damage to another person, the guardian shall bear tort liability. If the guardian has fulfilled the duties of guardianship, his or her tort liability may be mitigated. Where a person without civil capacity or a person with limited civil capacity who has property causes damage to another person, the compensation expenses shall be paid from his or her own property; the shortfall shall be compensated by the guardian.

<a id="art-1189"></a>第一千一百八十九条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人造成他人损害，监护人将监护职责委托给他人的，监护人应当承担侵权责任；受托人有过错的，承担相应的责任。

**Article 1189** : Where a person without civil capacity or a person with limited civil capacity causes damage to another person, and the guardian has entrusted the guardianship duties to another person, the guardian shall bear tort liability; if the trustee is at fault, the trustee shall bear corresponding liability.

<a id="art-1190"></a>第一千一百九十条　完全民事行为能力人对自己的行为暂时没有意识或者失去控制造成他人损害有过错的，应当承担侵权责任；没有过错的，根据行为人的经济状况对受害人适当补偿。  
完全民事行为能力人因醉酒、滥用麻醉药品或者精神药品对自己的行为暂时没有意识或者失去控制造成他人损害的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1190** : Where a person with full civil capacity has temporary unconsciousness or loss of control over his or her own conduct causing damage to another person and is at fault, he or she shall bear tort liability; if not at fault, he or she shall make appropriate compensation to the victim based on the actor's economic circumstances.  
Where a person with full civil capacity causes damage to another person due to temporary unconsciousness or loss of control over his or her own conduct resulting from drunkenness or abuse of narcotic or psychotropic substances, he or she shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1191"></a>第一千一百九十一条　用人单位的工作人员因执行工作任务造成他人损害的，由用人单位承担侵权责任。用人单位承担侵权责任后，可以向有故意或者重大过失的工作人员追偿。  
劳务派遣期间，被派遣的工作人员因执行工作任务造成他人损害的，由接受劳务派遣的用工单位承担侵权责任；劳务派遣单位有过错的，承担相应的责任。

**Article 1191** : Where an employee of an employer causes damage to another person in the performance of work tasks, the employer shall bear tort liability. After bearing tort liability, the employer may seek recourse against the employee who has acted intentionally or with gross negligence. During the period of labor dispatch, where a dispatched employee causes damage to another person in the performance of work tasks, the accepting work unit shall bear tort liability; if the labor dispatch unit is at fault, it shall bear corresponding liability.

<a id="art-1192"></a>第一千一百九十二条　个人之间形成劳务关系，提供劳务一方因劳务造成他人损害的，由接受劳务一方承担侵权责任。接受劳务一方承担侵权责任后，可以向有故意或者重大过失的提供劳务一方追偿。提供劳务一方因劳务受到损害的，根据双方各自的过错承担相应的责任。  
提供劳务期间，因第三人的行为造成提供劳务一方损害的，提供劳务一方有权请求第三人承担侵权责任，也有权请求接受劳务一方给予补偿。接受劳务一方补偿后，可以向第三人追偿。

**Article 1192** : Where a personal labor service relationship is formed between individuals, and the party providing the labor service causes damage to another person in the course of providing the labor service, the party receiving the labor service shall bear tort liability. After bearing tort liability, the party receiving the labor service may seek recourse against the party providing the labor service who has acted intentionally or with gross negligence. Where the party providing the labor service suffers damage in the course of providing the labor service, each party shall bear corresponding liability according to its respective fault. During the period of providing labor service, where the party providing the labor service suffers damage due to the conduct of a third party, the party providing the labor service shall have the right to request the third party to bear tort liability and also have the right to request the party receiving the labor service to provide compensation. After providing compensation, the party receiving the labor service may seek recourse against the third party.

<a id="art-1193"></a>第一千一百九十三条　承揽人在完成工作过程中造成第三人损害或者自己损害的，定作人不承担侵权责任。但是，定作人对定作、指示或者选任有过错的，应当承担相应的责任。

**Article 1193** : Where a contractor causes damage to a third party or damage to itself in the process of completing the work, the hirer shall not bear tort liability. However, if the hirer is at fault in the ordering, instruction, or selection, the hirer shall bear corresponding liability.

<a id="art-1194"></a>第一千一百九十四条　网络用户、网络服务提供者利用网络侵害他人民事权益的，应当承担侵权责任。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 1194** : Where a network user or a network service provider infringes upon the civil rights and interests of another person using the network, it shall bear tort liability. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-1195"></a>第一千一百九十五条　网络用户利用网络服务实施侵权行为的，权利人有权通知网络服务提供者采取删除、屏蔽、断开链接等必要措施。通知应当包括构成侵权的初步证据及权利人的真实身份信息。  
网络服务提供者接到通知后，应当及时将该通知转送相关网络用户，并根据构成侵权的初步证据和服务类型采取必要措施；未及时采取必要措施的，对损害的扩大部分与该网络用户承担连带责任。  
权利人因错误通知造成网络用户或者网络服务提供者损害的，应当承担侵权责任。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 1195** : Where a network user commits an infringing act using network services, the right holder shall have the right to notify the network service provider to take necessary measures such as deletion, blocking, and disconnection of links. The notice shall include preliminary evidence of the infringement and the true identity information of the right holder. After receiving the notice, the network service provider shall promptly forward the notice to the relevant network user and take necessary measures based on the preliminary evidence of the infringement and the type of service; if it fails to take necessary measures promptly, it shall bear joint and several liability with the network user for the expanded part of the damage. Where the right holder causes damage to the network user or the network service provider due to an erroneous notice, the right holder shall bear tort liability. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-1196"></a>第一千一百九十六条　网络用户接到转送的通知后，可以向网络服务提供者提交不存在侵权行为的声明。声明应当包括不存在侵权行为的初步证据及网络用户的真实身份信息。  
网络服务提供者接到声明后，应当将该声明转送发出通知的权利人，并告知其可以向有关部门投诉或者向人民法院提起诉讼。网络服务提供者在转送声明到达权利人后的合理期限内，未收到权利人已经投诉或者提起诉讼通知的，应当及时终止所采取的措施。

**Article 1196** : After receiving the forwarded notice, the network user may submit to the network service provider a statement that no infringing act exists. The statement shall include preliminary evidence that no infringing act exists and the true identity information of the network user. After receiving the statement, the network service provider shall forward the statement to the right holder who issued the notice and inform the right holder that it may file a complaint with the relevant authority or initiate an action in a People's Court. If the network service provider does not receive a notice from the right holder that a complaint has been filed or an action has been initiated within a reasonable period after the forwarded statement reaches the right holder, the network service provider shall promptly terminate the measures taken.

<a id="art-1197"></a>第一千一百九十七条　网络服务提供者知道或者应当知道网络用户利用其网络服务侵害他人民事权益，未采取必要措施的，与该网络用户承担连带责任。

**Article 1197** : Where a network service provider knows or should know that a network user is infringing upon the civil rights and interests of another person using its network services and fails to take necessary measures, it shall bear joint and several liability with the network user.

<a id="art-1198"></a>第一千一百九十八条　宾馆、商场、银行、车站、机场、体育场馆、娱乐场所等经营场所、公共场所的经营者、管理者或者群众性活动的组织者，未尽到安全保障义务，造成他人损害的，应当承担侵权责任。  
因第三人的行为造成他人损害的，由第三人承担侵权责任；经营者、管理者或者组织者未尽到安全保障义务的，承担相应的补充责任。经营者、管理者或者组织者承担补充责任后，可以向第三人追偿。

**Article 1198** : Where the operator, manager of business premises or public places, such as hotels, shopping malls, banks, stations, airports, sports venues, and entertainment venues, or the organizer of mass activities fails to fulfill the duty of safety guarantee, causing damage to another person, such operator, manager, or organizer shall bear tort liability. Where damage to another person is caused by the conduct of a third party, the third party shall bear tort liability; if the operator, manager, or organizer fails to fulfill the duty of safety guarantee, it shall bear corresponding supplementary liability. After bearing supplementary liability, the operator, manager, or organizer may seek recourse against the third party.

<a id="art-1199"></a>第一千一百九十九条　无民事行为能力人在幼儿园、学校或者其他教育机构学习、生活期间受到人身损害的，幼儿园、学校或者其他教育机构应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明尽到教育、管理职责的，不承担侵权责任。

**Article 1199** : Where a person without civil capacity suffers personal injury while studying or living in a kindergarten, school, or other educational institution, the kindergarten, school, or other educational institution shall bear tort liability; however, if it can prove that it has fulfilled its duties of education and management, it shall not bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1200"></a>第一千二百条　限制民事行为能力人在学校或者其他教育机构学习、生活期间受到人身损害，学校或者其他教育机构未尽到教育、管理职责的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1200** : Where a person with limited civil capacity suffers personal injury while studying or living in a school or other educational institution, and the school or other educational institution has failed to fulfill its duties of education and management, it shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1201"></a>第一千二百零一条　无民事行为能力人或者限制民事行为能力人在幼儿园、学校或者其他教育机构学习、生活期间，受到幼儿园、学校或者其他教育机构以外的第三人人身损害的，由第三人承担侵权责任；幼儿园、学校或者其他教育机构未尽到管理职责的，承担相应的补充责任。幼儿园、学校或者其他教育机构承担补充责任后，可以向第三人追偿。

**Article 1201** Where a person without capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct suffers personal injury during the period of study or living in a kindergarten, school or other educational institution from a third party other than the kindergarten, school or other educational institution, the third party shall bear the tort liability; where the kindergarten, school or other educational institution fails to fulfill its management duties, it shall bear corresponding supplementary liability. After the kindergarten, school or other educational institution bears the supplementary liability, it may claim reimbursement from the third party.

### 第四章　产品责任

<a id="art-1202"></a>第一千二百零二条　因产品存在缺陷造成他人损害的，生产者应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1202** Where a defect in a product causes damage to another person, the manufacturer shall bear the tort liability.

<a id="art-1203"></a>第一千二百零三条　因产品存在缺陷造成他人损害的，被侵权人可以向产品的生产者请求赔偿，也可以向产品的销售者请求赔偿。  
产品缺陷由生产者造成的，销售者赔偿后，有权向生产者追偿。因销售者的过错使产品存在缺陷的，生产者赔偿后，有权向销售者追偿。

**Article 1203** Where a defect in a product causes damage to another person, the infringed party may claim compensation from the manufacturer of the product or from the seller of the product. Where the product defect is caused by the manufacturer, the seller, after compensating, has the right to claim reimbursement from the manufacturer. Where the product defect is caused by the fault of the seller, the manufacturer, after compensating, has the right to claim reimbursement from the seller.

<a id="art-1204"></a>第一千二百零四条　因运输者、仓储者等第三人的过错使产品存在缺陷，造成他人损害的，产品的生产者、销售者赔偿后，有权向第三人追偿。

**Article 1204** Where a product defect caused by the fault of a third party such as a carrier or warehouseman results in damage to another person, the manufacturer or seller of the product, after compensating, has the right to claim reimbursement from the third party.

<a id="art-1205"></a>第一千二百零五条　因产品缺陷危及他人人身、财产安全的，被侵权人有权请求生产者、销售者承担停止侵害、排除妨碍、消除危险等侵权责任。

**Article 1205** Where a product defect endangers the personal or property safety of another person, the infringed party has the right to request the manufacturer or seller to bear tort liabilities such as cessation of infringement, removal of obstruction, and elimination of danger.

<a id="art-1206"></a>第一千二百零六条　产品投入流通后发现存在缺陷的，生产者、销售者应当及时采取停止销售、警示、召回等补救措施；未及时采取补救措施或者补救措施不力造成损害扩大的，对扩大的损害也应当承担侵权责任。  
依据前款规定采取召回措施的，生产者、销售者应当负担被侵权人因此支出的必要费用。

**Article 1206** Where a defect is discovered after a product has been placed into circulation, the manufacturer or seller shall promptly take remedial measures such as ceasing sales, issuing warnings, and carrying out recalls; if they fail to take remedial measures in a timely manner or the remedial measures are ineffective, causing the damage to enlarge, they shall also bear tort liability for the enlarged damage.  
Where a recall measure is taken in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the manufacturer or seller shall bear the necessary expenses incurred by the infringed party as a result thereof.

<a id="art-1207"></a>第一千二百零七条　明知产品存在缺陷仍然生产、销售，或者没有依据前条规定采取有效补救措施，造成他人死亡或者健康严重损害的，被侵权人有权请求相应的惩罚性赔偿。

**Article 1207** Where a person knowingly manufactures or sells a product with a defect, or fails to take effective remedial measures in accordance with the provisions of the preceding article, thereby causing the death of another person or serious damage to the health of another person, the infringed party has the right to claim corresponding punitive damages.

### 第五章　机动车交通事故责任

<a id="art-1208"></a>第一千二百零八条　机动车发生交通事故造成损害的，依照道路交通安全法律和本法的有关规定承担赔偿责任。

**Article 1208** Where a motor vehicle traffic accident causes damage, the liability for compensation shall be borne in accordance with the provisions of the road traffic safety law and the relevant provisions of this Law.

<a id="art-1209"></a>第一千二百零九条　因租赁、借用等情形机动车所有人、管理人与使用人不是同一人时，发生交通事故造成损害，属于该机动车一方责任的，由机动车使用人承担赔偿责任；机动车所有人、管理人对损害的发生有过错的，承担相应的赔偿责任。

**Article 1209** Where, under circumstances such as lease or loan, the owner or manager of a motor vehicle is not the same person as the user, and a traffic accident causes damage, if the liability lies with that motor vehicle, the user of the motor vehicle shall bear the compensation liability; if the owner or manager of the motor vehicle is at fault for the occurrence of the damage, they shall bear corresponding compensation liability.

<a id="art-1210"></a>第一千二百一十条　当事人之间已经以买卖或者其他方式转让并交付机动车但是未办理登记，发生交通事故造成损害，属于该机动车一方责任的，由受让人承担赔偿责任。

**Article 1210** Where a motor vehicle has been transferred and delivered between parties by way of sale or other means but has not been registered, and a traffic accident causes damage, if the liability lies with that motor vehicle, the transferee shall bear the compensation liability.

<a id="art-1211"></a>第一千二百一十一条　以挂靠形式从事道路运输经营活动的机动车，发生交通事故造成损害，属于该机动车一方责任的，由挂靠人和被挂靠人承担连带责任。

**Article 1211** Where a motor vehicle engaging in road transport business operations in the form of affiliation causes damage in a traffic accident, if the liability lies with that motor vehicle, the affiliated party and the affiliated entity shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-1212"></a>第一千二百一十二条　未经允许驾驶他人机动车，发生交通事故造成损害，属于该机动车一方责任的，由机动车使用人承担赔偿责任；机动车所有人、管理人对损害的发生有过错的，承担相应的赔偿责任，但是本章另有规定的除外。

**Article 1212** Where a person drives another's motor vehicle without permission, and a traffic accident causes damage, if the liability lies with that motor vehicle, the user of the motor vehicle shall bear the compensation liability; if the owner or manager of the motor vehicle is at fault for the occurrence of the damage, they shall bear corresponding compensation liability, except as otherwise provided in this Chapter.

<a id="art-1213"></a>第一千二百一十三条　机动车发生交通事故造成损害，属于该机动车一方责任的，先由承保机动车强制保险的保险人在强制保险责任限额范围内予以赔偿；不足部分，由承保机动车商业保险的保险人按照保险合同的约定予以赔偿；仍然不足或者没有投保机动车商业保险的，由侵权人赔偿。

**Article 1213** Where a motor vehicle traffic accident causes damage, if the liability lies with that motor vehicle, the insurer of the compulsory motor vehicle insurance shall first compensate within the liability limit of the compulsory insurance; any deficiency shall be compensated by the insurer of the commercial motor vehicle insurance in accordance with the terms of the insurance contract; if there is still a deficiency or if commercial motor vehicle insurance has not been purchased, the tortfeasor shall compensate.

<a id="art-1214"></a>第一千二百一十四条　以买卖或者其他方式转让拼装或者已经达到报废标准的机动车，发生交通事故造成损害的，由转让人和受让人承担连带责任。

**Article 1214** Where a motor vehicle that is assembled or has reached the scrapping standard is transferred by way of sale or other means, and a traffic accident causes damage, the transferor and the transferee shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-1215"></a>第一千二百一十五条　盗窃、抢劫或者抢夺的机动车发生交通事故造成损害的，由盗窃人、抢劫人或者抢夺人承担赔偿责任。盗窃人、抢劫人或者抢夺人与机动车使用人不是同一人，发生交通事故造成损害，属于该机动车一方责任的，由盗窃人、抢劫人或者抢夺人与机动车使用人承担连带责任。  
保险人在机动车强制保险责任限额范围内垫付抢救费用的，有权向交通事故责任人追偿。

**Article 1215** Where a motor vehicle that is stolen, robbed or forcibly taken causes damage in a traffic accident, the thief, robber or forcible taker shall bear the compensation liability. Where the thief, robber or forcible taker is not the same person as the user of the motor vehicle, and a traffic accident causes damage, if the liability lies with that motor vehicle, the thief, robber or forcible taker and the user of the motor vehicle shall bear joint and several liability. Where the insurer advances rescue expenses within the liability limit of the compulsory motor vehicle insurance, it has the right to claim reimbursement from the person liable for the traffic accident.

<a id="art-1216"></a>第一千二百一十六条　机动车驾驶人发生交通事故后逃逸，该机动车参加强制保险的，由保险人在机动车强制保险责任限额范围内予以赔偿；机动车不明、该机动车未参加强制保险或者抢救费用超过机动车强制保险责任限额，需要支付被侵权人人身伤亡的抢救、丧葬等费用的，由道路交通事故社会救助基金垫付。道路交通事故社会救助基金垫付后，其管理机构有权向交通事故责任人追偿。

**Article 1216** Where the driver of a motor vehicle flees after a traffic accident, and the motor vehicle is covered by compulsory insurance, the insurer shall compensate within the liability limit of the compulsory motor vehicle insurance; if the motor vehicle is unknown, the motor vehicle is not covered by compulsory insurance, or the rescue expenses exceed the liability limit of the compulsory motor vehicle insurance, and costs for rescue, funeral, etc., of the personal injury or death of the infringed party need to be paid, the Road Traffic Accident Social Assistance Fund shall advance such expenses. After the Road Traffic Accident Social Assistance Fund advances such expenses, its administrative authority has the right to claim reimbursement from the person liable for the traffic accident.

<a id="art-1217"></a>第一千二百一十七条　非营运机动车发生交通事故造成无偿搭乘人损害，属于该机动车一方责任的，应当减轻其赔偿责任，但是机动车使用人有故意或者重大过失的除外。

**Article 1217** Where a non-commercial motor vehicle has a traffic accident causing damage to a gratuitous passenger, if the liability lies with that motor vehicle, the compensation liability shall be mitigated, except where the user of the motor vehicle has acted with intent or gross negligence.

### 第六章　医疗损害责任

<a id="art-1218"></a>第一千二百一十八条　患者在诊疗活动中受到损害，医疗机构或者其医务人员有过错的，由医疗机构承担赔偿责任。

**Article 1218** Where a patient sustains damage during diagnosis and treatment activities, and the medical institution or its medical personnel are at fault, the medical institution shall bear the compensation liability.

<a id="art-1219"></a>第一千二百一十九条　医务人员在诊疗活动中应当向患者说明病情和医疗措施。需要实施手术、特殊检查、特殊治疗的，医务人员应当及时向患者具体说明医疗风险、替代医疗方案等情况，并取得其明确同意；不能或者不宜向患者说明的，应当向患者的近亲属说明，并取得其明确同意。  
医务人员未尽到前款义务，造成患者损害的，医疗机构应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 1219** Medical personnel shall, during diagnosis and treatment activities, explain the patient's condition and medical measures to the patient. Where surgery, special examination or special treatment is required, medical personnel shall promptly and specifically explain the medical risks, alternative treatment plans, and other relevant information to the patient and obtain their explicit consent; if it is impossible or inappropriate to explain to the patient, they shall explain to the patient's near relatives and obtain their explicit consent. Where medical personnel fail to fulfill the obligations set forth in the preceding paragraph, thereby causing damage to the patient, the medical institution shall bear the compensation liability.

<a id="art-1220"></a>第一千二百二十条　因抢救生命垂危的患者等紧急情况，不能取得患者或者其近亲属意见的，经医疗机构负责人或者授权的负责人批准，可以立即实施相应的医疗措施。

**Article 1220** In emergency situations such as rescuing a patient whose life is in critical danger, where consent cannot be obtained from the patient or their near relatives, corresponding medical measures may be implemented immediately upon approval by the person in charge of the medical institution or an authorized person in charge.

<a id="art-1221"></a>第一千二百二十一条　医务人员在诊疗活动中未尽到与当时的医疗水平相应的诊疗义务，造成患者损害的，医疗机构应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 1221** Where a medical practitioner fails to fulfill the duty of care corresponding to the prevailing medical standard during diagnostic and treatment activities, causing damage to a patient, the medical institution shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-1222"></a>第一千二百二十二条　患者在诊疗活动中受到损害，有下列情形之一的，推定医疗机构有过错：  
（一）违反法律、行政法规、规章以及其他有关诊疗规范的规定；  
（二）隐匿或者拒绝提供与纠纷有关的病历资料；  
（三）遗失、伪造、篡改或者违法销毁病历资料。

**Article 1222** Where a patient sustains damage during diagnostic and treatment activities, and any of the following circumstances exists, fault of the medical institution shall be presumed:  
(1) the medical institution violates provisions of laws, administrative regulations, rules, or other relevant diagnostic and treatment standards;  
(2) the medical institution conceals or refuses to provide medical records related to the dispute  
(3) the medical institution loses, forges, alters, or unlawfully destroys medical records.

<a id="art-1223"></a>第一千二百二十三条　因药品、消毒产品、医疗器械的缺陷，或者输入不合格的血液造成患者损害的，患者可以向药品上市许可持有人、生产者、血液提供机构请求赔偿，也可以向医疗机构请求赔偿。患者向医疗机构请求赔偿的，医疗机构赔偿后，有权向负有责任的药品上市许可持有人、生产者、血液提供机构追偿。

**Article 1223** Where a patient sustains damage due to defects in a drug, disinfection product, or medical device, or due to the transfusion of unqualified blood, the patient may claim compensation from the marketing authorization holder of the drug, the manufacturer, or the blood supply institution, and may also claim compensation from the medical institution. Where a patient claims compensation from the medical institution, after making compensation, the medical institution shall have the right to recover the compensation from the responsible marketing authorization holder of the drug, manufacturer, or blood supply institution.

<a id="art-1224"></a>第一千二百二十四条　患者在诊疗活动中受到损害，有下列情形之一的，医疗机构不承担赔偿责任：  
（一）患者或者其近亲属不配合医疗机构进行符合诊疗规范的诊疗；  
（二）医务人员在抢救生命垂危的患者等紧急情况下已经尽到合理诊疗义务；  
（三）限于当时的医疗水平难以诊疗。  
前款第一项情形中，医疗机构或者其医务人员也有过错的，应当承担相应的赔偿责任。

**Article 1224** Where a patient sustains damage during diagnostic and treatment activities, and any of the following circumstances exists, the medical institution shall not bear compensation liability:  
(1) the patient or his or her close relatives fail to cooperate with the medical institution in providing diagnostic and treatment services that comply with the diagnostic and treatment standards;  
(2) the medical practitioners have fulfilled their reasonable duty of care in emergency circumstances such as rescuing a patient in critical condition;  
(3) the diagnostic or treatment is difficult due to the prevailing medical standard. Where, in the circumstance specified in subparagraph (1) of the preceding paragraph, the medical institution or its medical practitioners are also at fault, they shall bear corresponding compensation liability.

<a id="art-1225"></a>第一千二百二十五条　医疗机构及其医务人员应当按照规定填写并妥善保管住院志、医嘱单、检验报告、手术及麻醉记录、病理资料、护理记录等病历资料。  
患者要求查阅、复制前款规定的病历资料的，医疗机构应当及时提供。

**Article 1225** Medical institutions and their medical practitioners shall, in accordance with the provisions, fill in and properly keep medical records such as the inpatient record, order sheet, test reports, surgical and anesthesia records, pathological data, and nursing records.  
Where a patient requests to review or reproduce the medical records specified in the preceding paragraph, the medical institution shall provide them promptly.

<a id="art-1226"></a>第一千二百二十六条　医疗机构及其医务人员应当对患者的隐私和个人信息保密。泄露患者的隐私和个人信息，或者未经患者同意公开其病历资料的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1226** Medical institutions and their medical practitioners shall keep confidential the privacy and personal information of patients. Where a medical institution or its medical practitioner discloses the privacy or personal information of a patient, or makes public the patient's medical records without the patient's consent, the medical institution or medical practitioner shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1227"></a>第一千二百二十七条　医疗机构及其医务人员不得违反诊疗规范实施不必要的检查。

**Article 1227** Medical institutions and their medical practitioners shall not perform unnecessary examinations in violation of diagnostic and treatment standards.

<a id="art-1228"></a>第一千二百二十八条　医疗机构及其医务人员的合法权益受法律保护。  
干扰医疗秩序，妨碍医务人员工作、生活，侵害医务人员合法权益的，应当依法承担法律责任。

**Article 1228** The legitimate rights and interests of medical institutions and their medical practitioners shall be protected by law.  
Any party that disrupts medical order, impedes the work or life of medical practitioners, or infringes upon the legitimate rights and interests of medical practitioners shall bear legal liability according to law.

### 第七章　环境污染和生态破坏责任

<a id="art-1229"></a>第一千二百二十九条　因污染环境、破坏生态造成他人损害的，侵权人应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1229** Where a tortfeasor causes damage to another person by polluting the environment or damaging the ecology, the tortfeasor shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1230"></a>第一千二百三十条　因污染环境、破坏生态发生纠纷，行为人应当就法律规定的不承担责任或者减轻责任的情形及其行为与损害之间不存在因果关系承担举证责任。

**Article 1230** In a dispute arising from environmental pollution or ecological damage, the actor shall bear the burden of adducing evidence for the circumstances exempting or mitigating liability as provided by law, and for the absence of a causal relationship between the act and the damage.

<a id="art-1231"></a>第一千二百三十一条　两个以上侵权人污染环境、破坏生态的，承担责任的大小，根据污染物的种类、浓度、排放量，破坏生态的方式、范围、程度，以及行为对损害后果所起的作用等因素确定。

**Article 1231** Where two or more tortfeasors pollute the environment or damage the ecology, the extent of their respective liabilities shall be determined based on factors such as the type, concentration, and volume of pollutants emitted, the manner, scope, and degree of ecological damage, and the role of each act in causing the harmful consequences.

<a id="art-1232"></a>第一千二百三十二条　侵权人违反法律规定故意污染环境、破坏生态造成严重后果的，被侵权人有权请求相应的惩罚性赔偿。

**Article 1232** Where a tortfeasor intentionally pollutes the environment or damages the ecology in violation of the provisions of law and causes serious consequences, the aggrieved party shall have the right to claim corresponding punitive damages.

<a id="art-1233"></a>第一千二百三十三条　因第三人的过错污染环境、破坏生态的，被侵权人可以向侵权人请求赔偿，也可以向第三人请求赔偿。侵权人赔偿后，有权向第三人追偿。

**Article 1233** Where environmental pollution or ecological damage is caused by a third party's fault, the aggrieved party may claim compensation from the tortfeasor and may also claim compensation from the third party. After making compensation, the tortfeasor shall have the right to recover the compensation from the third party.

<a id="art-1234"></a>第一千二百三十四条　违反国家规定造成生态环境损害，生态环境能够修复的，国家规定的机关或者法律规定的组织有权请求侵权人在合理期限内承担修复责任。侵权人在期限内未修复的，国家规定的机关或者法律规定的组织可以自行或者委托他人进行修复，所需费用由侵权人负担。

**Article 1234** Where a tortfeasor, in violation of state provisions, causes damage to the ecological environment, and the ecological environment can be restored, the authority designated by the state or the organization provided for by law shall have the right to request the tortfeasor to assume restoration liability within a reasonable period. Where the tortfeasor fails to restore within such period, the authority designated by the state or the organization provided for by law may carry out the restoration itself or entrust another party to do so, and the necessary expenses shall be borne by the tortfeasor.

<a id="art-1235"></a>第一千二百三十五条　违反国家规定造成生态环境损害的，国家规定的机关或者法律规定的组织有权请求侵权人赔偿下列损失和费用：  
（一）生态环境受到损害至修复完成期间服务功能丧失导致的损失；  
（二）生态环境功能永久性损害造成的损失；  
（三）生态环境损害调查、鉴定评估等费用；  
（四）清除污染、修复生态环境费用；  
（五）防止损害的发生和扩大所支出的合理费用。

**Article 1235** Where a tortfeasor, in violation of state provisions, causes damage to the ecological environment, the authority designated by the state or the organization provided for by law shall have the right to request the tortfeasor to compensate for the following losses and expenses:  
(1) the losses resulting from the loss of service functions during the period from the occurrence of ecological damage to its restoration;  
(2) the losses caused by permanent damage to the ecological functions;  
(3) the expenses for investigation, expert evaluation, and assessment of the ecological damage;  
(4) the expenses for pollution removal and ecological restoration; and (5) the reasonable expenses incurred in preventing the occurrence and expansion of the damage.

### 第八章　高度危险责任

<a id="art-1236"></a>第一千二百三十六条　从事高度危险作业造成他人损害的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1236** Any person engaged in a highly hazardous activity who causes damage to another person shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1237"></a>第一千二百三十七条　民用核设施或者运入运出核设施的核材料发生核事故造成他人损害的，民用核设施的营运单位应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因战争、武装冲突、暴乱等情形或者受害人故意造成的，不承担责任。

**Article 1237** Where a nuclear accident occurs in a civilian nuclear facility or in nuclear materials transported into or out of such a facility and causes damage to another person, the operator of the civilian nuclear facility shall bear tort liability. However, if the operator can prove that the damage is caused by circumstances such as war, armed conflict, riot, or the victim's intentional conduct, the operator shall not bear liability.

<a id="art-1238"></a>第一千二百三十八条　民用航空器造成他人损害的，民用航空器的经营者应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因受害人故意造成的，不承担责任。

**Article 1238** Where a civil aircraft causes damage to another person, the operator of the civil aircraft shall bear tort liability. However, if the operator can prove that the damage is caused by the victim's intentional conduct, the operator shall not bear liability.

<a id="art-1239"></a>第一千二百三十九条　占有或者使用易燃、易爆、剧毒、高放射性、强腐蚀性、高致病性等高度危险物造成他人损害的，占有人或者使用人应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因受害人故意或者不可抗力造成的，不承担责任。被侵权人对损害的发生有重大过失的，可以减轻占有人或者使用人的责任。

**Article 1239** Where a person in possession or use of highly hazardous materials such as inflammable, explosive, highly toxic, highly radioactive, strongly corrosive, or highly pathogenic substances causes damage to another person, the possessor or user shall bear tort liability. However, if the possessor or user can prove that the damage is caused by the victim's intentional conduct or force majeure, the possessor or user shall not bear liability. Where the aggrieved party is grossly negligent with respect to the occurrence of the damage, the liability of the possessor or user may be mitigated.

<a id="art-1240"></a>第一千二百四十条　从事高空、高压、地下挖掘活动或者使用高速轨道运输工具造成他人损害的，经营者应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因受害人故意或者不可抗力造成的，不承担责任。被侵权人对损害的发生有重大过失的，可以减轻经营者的责任。

**Article 1240** Where a person engaged in activities involving high altitude, high voltage, underground excavation, or the use of high-speed rail transport causes damage to another person, the operator shall bear tort liability. However, if the operator can prove that the damage is caused by the victim's intentional conduct or force majeure, the operator shall not bear liability. Where the aggrieved party is grossly negligent with respect to the occurrence of the damage, the liability of the operator may be mitigated.

<a id="art-1241"></a>第一千二百四十一条　遗失、抛弃高度危险物造成他人损害的，由所有人承担侵权责任。所有人将高度危险物交由他人管理的，由管理人承担侵权责任；所有人有过错的，与管理人承担连带责任。

**Article 1241** Where a highly dangerous object is lost or abandoned and causes harm to another person, the owner shall bear the tort liability. Where the owner entrusts the highly dangerous object to another person for management, the manager shall bear the tort liability; if the owner is at fault, the owner and the manager shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-1242"></a>第一千二百四十二条　非法占有高度危险物造成他人损害的，由非法占有人承担侵权责任。所有人、管理人不能证明对防止非法占有尽到高度注意义务的，与非法占有人承担连带责任。

**Article 1242** Where a person illegally possesses a highly dangerous object and causes harm to another person, the illegal possessor shall bear the tort liability. If the owner or manager cannot prove that it has exercised a high degree of care in preventing the illegal possession, it shall bear joint and several liability with the illegal possessor.

<a id="art-1243"></a>第一千二百四十三条　未经许可进入高度危险活动区域或者高度危险物存放区域受到损害，管理人能够证明已经采取足够安全措施并尽到充分警示义务的，可以减轻或者不承担责任。

**Article 1243** Where a person enters an area of highly dangerous activities or an area where highly dangerous objects are stored without permission and suffers harm, if the manager can prove that it has taken sufficient safety measures and fulfilled its duty of adequate warning, the manager may be relieved of liability or have its liability mitigated.

<a id="art-1244"></a>第一千二百四十四条　承担高度危险责任，法律规定赔偿限额的，依照其规定，但是行为人有故意或者重大过失的除外。

**Article 1244** Where the law provides a limitation on compensation for bearing a high-risk liability, such provision shall apply, except where the actor has acted with intent or gross negligence.

### 第九章　饲养动物损害责任

<a id="art-1245"></a>第一千二百四十五条　饲养的动物造成他人损害的，动物饲养人或者管理人应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因被侵权人故意或者重大过失造成的，可以不承担或者减轻责任。

**Article 1245** Where a domesticated animal causes harm to another person, the animal keeper or manager shall bear the tort liability; however, if it can prove that the harm was caused by the intentional act or gross negligence of the infringed party, it may be relieved of liability or have its liability mitigated.

<a id="art-1246"></a>第一千二百四十六条　违反管理规定，未对动物采取安全措施造成他人损害的，动物饲养人或者管理人应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因被侵权人故意造成的，可以减轻责任。

**Article 1246** Where a person violates administrative regulations by failing to take safety measures with respect to an animal and causes harm to another person, the animal keeper or manager shall bear the tort liability; however, if it can prove that the harm was caused by the intentional act of the infringed party, its liability may be mitigated.

<a id="art-1247"></a>第一千二百四十七条　禁止饲养的烈性犬等危险动物造成他人损害的，动物饲养人或者管理人应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1247** Where a dangerous animal, such as a ferocious dog, the keeping of which is prohibited, causes harm to another person, the animal keeper or manager shall bear the tort liability.

<a id="art-1248"></a>第一千二百四十八条　动物园的动物造成他人损害的，动物园应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明尽到管理职责的，不承担侵权责任。

**Article 1248** Where an animal in a zoo causes harm to another person, the zoo shall bear the tort liability; however, if it can prove that it has fulfilled its management responsibilities, it shall not bear the tort liability.

<a id="art-1249"></a>第一千二百四十九条　遗弃、逃逸的动物在遗弃、逃逸期间造成他人损害的，由动物原饲养人或者管理人承担侵权责任。

**Article 1249** Where an abandoned or escaped animal causes harm to another person during the period of its abandonment or escape, the original keeper or manager of the animal shall bear the tort liability.

<a id="art-1250"></a>第一千二百五十条　因第三人的过错致使动物造成他人损害的，被侵权人可以向动物饲养人或者管理人请求赔偿，也可以向第三人请求赔偿。动物饲养人或者管理人赔偿后，有权向第三人追偿。

**Article 1250** Where an animal causes harm to another person due to the fault of a third party, the infringed party may claim compensation from the animal keeper or manager or from the third party. After the animal keeper or manager pays compensation, it shall have the right to seek recourse from the third party.

<a id="art-1251"></a>第一千二百五十一条　饲养动物应当遵守法律法规，尊重社会公德，不得妨碍他人生活。

**Article 1251** The keeping of animals shall comply with laws and regulations, respect social morality, and shall not interfere with the life of others.

### 第十章　建筑物和物件损害责任

<a id="art-1252"></a>第一千二百五十二条　建筑物、构筑物或者其他设施倒塌、塌陷造成他人损害的，由建设单位与施工单位承担连带责任，但是建设单位与施工单位能够证明不存在质量缺陷的除外。建设单位、施工单位赔偿后，有其他责任人的，有权向其他责任人追偿。  
因所有人、管理人、使用人或者第三人的原因，建筑物、构筑物或者其他设施倒塌、塌陷造成他人损害的，由所有人、管理人、使用人或者第三人承担侵权责任。

**Article 1252** Where a building, structure, or other facility collapses or subsides and causes harm to another person, the construction owner and the construction contractor shall bear joint and several liability, except where the construction owner and the construction contractor can prove that there is no quality defect. After the construction owner and the construction contractor pay compensation, if there are other responsible persons, they shall have the right to seek recourse from such other responsible persons. Where the collapse or subsidence of a building, structure, or other facility is caused by the owner, manager, user, or a third party, the owner, manager, user, or third party shall bear the tort liability.

<a id="art-1253"></a>第一千二百五十三条　建筑物、构筑物或者其他设施及其搁置物、悬挂物发生脱落、坠落造成他人损害，所有人、管理人或者使用人不能证明自己没有过错的，应当承担侵权责任。所有人、管理人或者使用人赔偿后，有其他责任人的，有权向其他责任人追偿。

**Article 1253** Where an object that is detached or falls from a building, structure, or other facility or from its placed or hanging object causes harm to another person, the owner, manager, or user shall bear the tort liability if it cannot prove that it is not at fault. After the owner, manager, or user pays compensation, if there are other responsible persons, it shall have the right to seek recourse from such other responsible persons.

<a id="art-1254"></a>第一千二百五十四条　禁止从建筑物中抛掷物品。从建筑物中抛掷物品或者从建筑物上坠落的物品造成他人损害的，由侵权人依法承担侵权责任；经调查难以确定具体侵权人的，除能够证明自己不是侵权人的外，由可能加害的建筑物使用人给予补偿。可能加害的建筑物使用人补偿后，有权向侵权人追偿。  
物业服务企业等建筑物管理人应当采取必要的安全保障措施防止前款规定情形的发生；未采取必要的安全保障措施的，应当依法承担未履行安全保障义务的侵权责任。  
发生本条第一款规定的情形的，公安等机关应当依法及时调查，查清责任人。

**Article 1254** Throwing objects from a building is prohibited. Where an object thrown from a building or an object falling from a building causes harm to another person, the tortfeasor shall bear the tort liability according to law. If, after investigation, it is difficult to identify the specific tortfeasor, unless a person can prove that it is not a tortfeasor, compensation shall be made by the building users who may have caused the harm. After the building users who may have caused the harm pay compensation, they shall have the right to seek recourse from the tortfeasor. Property service enterprises or other building managers shall take necessary safety measures to prevent the occurrence of the circumstances prescribed in the preceding paragraph; if they fail to take necessary safety measures, they shall bear the tort liability according to law for failing to fulfill the duty of safety protection. Where the circumstances prescribed in paragraph 1 occur, the public security organs or other authorities shall investigate promptly according to law to identify the responsible person.

<a id="art-1255"></a>第一千二百五十五条　堆放物倒塌、滚落或者滑落造成他人损害，堆放人不能证明自己没有过错的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1255** Where stacked objects collapse, roll down, or slide down and cause harm to another person, the person who stacked the objects shall bear the tort liability if it cannot prove that it is not at fault.

<a id="art-1256"></a>第一千二百五十六条　在公共道路上堆放、倾倒、遗撒妨碍通行的物品造成他人损害的，由行为人承担侵权责任。公共道路管理人不能证明已经尽到清理、防护、警示等义务的，应当承担相应的责任。

**Article 1256** Where a person piles, dumps, or spills objects that obstruct passage on a public road and causes harm to another person, the actor shall bear the tort liability. The manager of the public road shall bear the corresponding liability if it cannot prove that it has fulfilled its obligations of cleaning, protection, and warning.

<a id="art-1257"></a>第一千二百五十七条　因林木折断、倾倒或者果实坠落等造成他人损害，林木的所有人或者管理人不能证明自己没有过错的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1257** Where the breakage or toppling of trees or the falling of fruits causes harm to another person, the owner or manager of the trees shall bear the tort liability if it cannot prove that it is not at fault.

<a id="art-1258"></a>第一千二百五十八条　在公共场所或者道路上挖掘、修缮安装地下设施等造成他人损害，施工人不能证明已经设置明显标志和采取安全措施的，应当承担侵权责任。  
窨井等地下设施造成他人损害，管理人不能证明尽到管理职责的，应当承担侵权责任。  
附　　则

**Article 1258** Where a person excavates, repairs, or installs underground facilities in a public place or on a road and causes harm to another person, the constructor shall bear the tort liability if it cannot prove that it has set up clear signs and taken safety measures.  
Where a manhole or other underground facility causes harm to another person, the manager shall bear the tort liability if it cannot prove that it has fulfilled its management responsibilities.  
Supplement

<a id="art-1259"></a>第一千二百五十九条　民法所称的“以上”、“以下”、“以内”、“届满”，包括本数；所称的“不满”、“超过”、“以外”，不包括本数。

**Article 1259** In the Civil Law, the words "or more," "or less," "within," and "expiration of" include the number itself; the words "less than," "exceeding," and "beyond" exclude the number itself.

<a id="art-1260"></a>第一千二百六十条　本法自2021年1月1日起施行。《中华人民共和国婚姻法》、《中华人民共和国继承法》、《中华人民共和国民法通则》、《中华人民共和国收养法》、《中华人民共和国担保法》、《中华人民共和国合同法》、《中华人民共和国物权法》、《中华人民共和国侵权责任法》、《中华人民共和国民法总则》同时废止。

**Article 1260** This Law shall come into force on January 1, 2021. The Marriage Law of the People's Republic of China, the Inheritance Law of the People's Republic of China, the General Principles of the Civil Law of the People's Republic of China, the Adoption Law of the People's Republic of China, the Security Law of the People's Republic of China, the Contract Law of the People's Republic of China, the Property Law of the People's Republic of China, the Tort Liability Law of the People's Republic of China, and the General Provisions of the Civil Law of the People's Republic of China shall be abolished simultaneously.
